TL;DR: 本文提出两种可组合安全构造，证明在大多数量子密码协议中，传统上需要可信量子比特制备或测量的参与方可以将这些任务委托给不可信提供商，仅依赖可信单量子比特旋转设备即可完成协议。第一个构造实现单量子比特测量，普适适用；第二个构造处理量子比特制备，虽依赖特定假设，但这些假设天然满足大多数常见量子密码协议。结果正式验证了Qline架构支持多种单量子比特协议的通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子通信密码协议中，用户通常需要访问可信的量子比特源或探测器。近期研究表明，在Qline架构上，仅具备单量子比特旋转能力的参与方也能等价执行若干协议。本文旨在进一步推广这一思路，使传统上需要可信量子比特制备或测量的参与方能够将任务委托给不可信提供商。

Method: 提出两种可组合安全构造：构造一实现单量子比特测量，适用于任何上下文；构造二处理量子比特制备，依赖于底层协议的具体假设，并证明这些假设在大多数常见量子密码协议中天然成立。

Result: 两个构造在可组合安全框架下得到证明，并正式验证了Qline架构作为一种通用架构能够支持广泛的单量子比特协议。

Conclusion: 大多数量子密码协议中，原本需要可信量子比特制备或测量的参与方可以将这些任务委托给不可信提供商，仅需依赖可信的单量子比特旋转设备，从而降低了对硬件可信度的要求，拓展了Qline架构的适用性。

Abstract: In the domain of quantum communication, cryptographic protocols often require users to have access to trusted qubit sources or detectors. Recently, it was shown that on an architecture called the Qline, several protocols can equivalently be performed by parties capable only of single-qubit rotations. In this work, we introduce two composably secure constructions that together show how in most quantum cryptographic protocols, parties traditionally required to perform trusted qubit preparation or measurement can delegate these tasks to an untrusted provider and instead rely on a trusted single-qubit rotation device. Our first construction implements single-qubit measurement and is universally applicable across any context. In contrast, our second construction, which addresses qubit preparation, relies on specific assumptions regarding the underlying protocol. We show, however, that these assumptions are inherently satisfied by the vast majority of common quantum cryptographic protocols. A notable consequence of our results is the formal validation of the Qline as a versatile architecture capable of supporting a wide range of single-qubit protocols.

</details>


### [96] [Quantum Convolutional Neural Networks for Groundwater Heat Plume Prediction: A Surrogate Modeling Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23411)
*Danyal Maheshwari,Julia Pelzer,Miriam Schulte*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探索了量子卷积神经网络（QCNN）作为慕尼黑地下水热泵温度预测的替代模型，通过降维、参数化量子电路和哈密顿量特征编码，在模拟器、噪声模拟器、127量子比特Kyiv处理器及纠错增强硬件上评估性能。结果表明，尽管经典神经网络预测精度仍最高，但QCNN在模拟器上表现稳定，且在纠错硬件条件下性能显著提升，显示出量子增强替代建模的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子机器学习方法（特别是QCNN）用于复杂环境系统（如地下水热羽流动态）的建模，以应对当前量子硬件的可扩展性限制，并评估其在真实噪声环境下的可行性。

Method: 采用量子卷积神经网络（QCNN），包含量子卷积层、量子池化层和全连接量子读出阶段。卷积和池化通过基于旋转门的参数化量子电路和测量驱动解码实现；采用哈密顿量启发的特征编码方案制备输入态。原始高维模拟输出降维为紧凑参数集作为训练目标。在理想态矢量模拟器、噪声模拟器、IBM 127量子比特Kyiv处理器及纠错增强硬件上评估。

Result: 经典神经网络预测精度最高；QCNN在模拟器上表现一致且具有竞争力；在纠错增强的硬件条件下性能显著改善。

Conclusion: 量子增强替代建模是地下水温度预测的有前景方向，随着量子硬件和纠错技术的成熟，QCNN有望在实际应用中展现出更大优势。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning methods are increasingly explored for modeling complex environmental systems, including groundwater heat plume dynamics. In this work, we explore a Quantum Convolutional Neural Network (QCNN) as a surrogate model for predicting temperature variations in groundwater induced by geothermal heat pumps in the city of Munich. To comply with the scalability constraints of current quantum hardware, the original high-dimensional simulation output is reduced to a compact set of representative parameters that serve as training targets for the surrogate. The proposed QCNN architecture consists of a quantum convolutional layer, a quantum pooling layer, and a fully connected quantum readout stage. Convolution and pooling operations are realized via parameterized quantum circuits based on rotational gates and measurement-driven decoding, while a Hamiltonian-inspired feature-encoding scheme is used to prepare informative input states on the quantum device. We evaluate the QCNN across multiple execution backends, including an ideal statevector simulator, a noisy simulator, IBM's 127-qubit Kyiv quantum processor, and the same hardware augmented with advanced error-mitigation techniques. Realistic noise models are employed to approximate device behavior and to assess the impact of mitigation strategies. Model performance is benchmarked using mean squared error (MSE) on both training and testing sets. The results show that, although classical neural networks still achieve the highest predictive accuracy, the QCNN attains competitive and consistent performance on simulators and exhibits noticeable improvement under error-mitigated hardware conditions. These findings indicate that quantum-enhanced surrogate modeling is a promising direction for future groundwater temperature prediction as quantum hardware and error-mitigation techniques continue to mature.

</details>


### [97] [Dissipative preparation of Laughlin-like states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23451)
*Tong Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于局域损耗和泵浦通道的纯耗散协议，用于制备填充因子为1/3的Laughlin-like分数量子霍尔态，可推广至其他1/M填充态，并证明该态是Lindblad方程的精确稳态，且具有随系统尺寸增大而下界有界的Lindblad能隙，同时展示了通过慢调制泵浦通道实现绝热泵浦。工作为在近期量子模拟器上制备和操控FQH态提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔（FQH）态是强关联量子物质的核心范式，也是拓扑量子计算的关键平台，但现有制备方案复杂且难以在近期量子模拟器上实现，因此需要一种更可行的制备和操控方案。

Method: 提出纯耗散协议，利用局域损耗和泵浦通道构建Lindblad主方程，使Laughlin-like态成为其精确稳态；通过分析Lindblad能隙的下界保证可扩展性；通过慢调制泵浦通道实现绝热泵浦。

Result: Laughlin-like态是Lindblad方程的精确稳态且可从任意初态到达；Lindblad能隙随系统尺寸增大有下界；成功演示了通过慢调制泵浦通道的绝热泵浦过程。

Conclusion: 该工作为在近期量子模拟器上制备和操控分数量子霍尔态开辟了可行路线，有望推动FQH态在拓扑量子计算等领域的应用。

Abstract: Fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states are a central paradigm of strongly correlated quantum matter and a key platform for topological quantum computation. Here, we propose a purely dissipative protocol based on local loss and pump channels for preparing Laughlin-like states at filling $1/3$, with a possible extension to other $1/M$ filling states. We show that Laughlin-like states are the exact steady states of the Lindbladian and can be reached from arbitrary initial states. We find that the Lindbladian gap bounded from below with increasing system size. We further demonstrate adiabatic pumping of a Laughlin-like state through slow modulation of the pump channels during the evolution. Our work opens a feasible route to preparing and manipulating FQH states on near-term quantum simulators.

</details>


### [98] [Improved State Readout in NV Centers using Regression Models and Rabi Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23454)
*Fritz Haltenberger,Manpreet Singh Jattana*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的回归方法替代传统的对比度校准方法，通过利用荧光信号（特别是拉比振荡期间的动态信号）和回归模型（如线性岭回归）来更准确地读取氮-空位（NV）中心的自旋态布居数，在室温条件下实现了比传统方法更高的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于对比度校准的NV中心自旋态读取方法存在两方面的局限性：一是将动态荧光行为简化为标量值，丢失了时间动态信息；二是独立计算每个可能态的布居数，忽略了态之间的关联。这些因素限制了读取保真度。

Method: 使用回归模型（线性岭回归、非线性核模型等）在实验数据上训练，将荧光信号映射到理想的模拟布居数上。同时，通过在诱导拉比振荡期间进行测量来增强荧光信号的信息含量，利用时间分辨的动态信号而非单一对比度值进行态读取。

Result: 包含动态信号显著降低了多个测试模型的状态读取误差；线性岭回归的表现几乎与非线性核模型相当，表明简单模型已能捕捉增强荧光信号与态布居数之间的映射关系；该方法在实验中获得了比传统校准更高的保真度。

Conclusion: 这种数据驱动方法为固态量子寄存器中的高保真度自旋态读取提供了一种稳健且更优的替代方案，尤其在室温条件下的NV中心系统中展现出实用性。

Abstract: Readout of state populations in nitrogen-vacancy centers from fluorescence measurements at room-temperature is routinely achieved via contrast-based calibration. The fidelities achieved by this conventional approach are limited by reducing the dynamical fluorescence behaviour of the NV center to a scalar value, and calculating the population of each possible state independently. To address these limitations, we use regression models trained on experimental data to map the fluorescence signals onto ideal simulated populations. Additionally, we enhance the informational content of the fluorescence signals by performing measurements during induced Rabi oscillations. Our results demonstrate that including these dynamical signals significantly reduces state readout errors across multiple tested models. Notably, linear ridge regression performs nearly on par with a non-linear kernel-based model, showing that simple models already capture the relevant mapping between the enhanced fluorescence signals and the underlying state populations. This data-driven approach provides a robust alternative that achieves higher fidelities than conventional calibration in our setting, paving the way for high-fidelity state readout in solid-state quantum registers.

</details>


### [99] [Prethermal rotating-frame solid echo in a dipolar nuclear-spin network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23467)
*Quentin Reynard-Feytis,William Beatrez,Leo Joon Il Moon,Emanuel Druga,Ashok Ajoy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在金刚石中偶极耦合的¹³C核自旋网络驱动的脉冲自旋锁定系统中，观察到了旋转框架下的预热化固体回波现象，该回波源于Floquet微运动将相干性在不同算子子空间间转移，仅部分多体退相动力学被y脉冲反转，回波包络以拉伸指数衰减，特征时间T₂'≈13 ms。该工作将经典回波物理推广至预热化旋转框架，为高通量光谱学、哈密顿工程和长时量子 sensing 提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Floquet预热化能够赋予相互作用的量子固体长寿命、近似守恒的量，从而实现哈密顿工程和新的动力学探测手段。本文旨在实验上验证预热化旋转框架下的回波现象，并揭示其背后的物理机制。

Method: 利用超极化偶极耦合¹³C核自旋网络（金刚石中），通过脉冲自旋锁定驱动系统进入旋转框架预热化平台，获得准守恒的横向磁化并实现周期分辨的感应读出。在预热化流形内施加单次(α)_y脉冲，观测旋转框架自由感应信号在延迟τ后衰减、在2τ处复兴的回波现象。

Result: 观察到旋转框架固体回波：延迟τ后信号表观衰减，在2τ处磁化复兴，最大振幅出现在α≈π/2附近；回波包络呈拉伸指数衰减，特征时间T₂'≈13 ms；通过解析论证和玩具模型模拟将复兴归因于Floquet微运动在不同算子子空间间转移相干性。

Conclusion: 经典回波物理被成功翻译到预热化旋转框架中，表明连续询问的预热化自旋系综可作为高通量光谱学、哈密顿工程和长时量子传感的多功能平台。

Abstract: Floquet prethermalization can endow interacting quantum solids with long-lived, approximately conserved quantities, enabling Hamiltonian engineering and new dynamical probes. Using a hyperpolarized network of dipolar-coupled $^{13}$C nuclear spins in diamond driven by pulsed spin-locking, we access a rotating-frame prethermal plateau with quasi-conserved transverse magnetization and cycle-resolved inductive readout. Within this prethermal manifold we observe a robust \emph{rotating-frame solid echo}: after an apparent decay of the rotating-frame free-induction signal over a delay $τ$, the magnetization revives at time $2τ$ following a single $(α)_y$ pulse, with maximum amplitude near $α\simeqπ/2$. The echo envelope decays as a stretched exponential with characteristic time $T_2'\approx 13\,$ms. Analytical arguments and toy-model simulations attribute the revival to Floquet micromotion that transfers coherences between operator subspaces, so that only a subset of the many-body dephasing dynamics is inverted by the $y$ pulse. These results translate classic echo physics into the prethermal rotating frame and point to continuously interrogated prethermal spin ensembles as a versatile platform for high-throughput spectroscopy, Hamiltonian engineering, and long-duration quantum sensing.

</details>


### [100] [How Stark units enter SIC overlaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23535)
*Ingemar Bengtsson,Gary McConnell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文发现SIC-POVM向量的标量积由代数单位给出，特别是Stark单位的平方根，并指出重叠单位始终是来自射线类域的Stark单位平方根的整数幂乘积，在非最小情况下涉及多个射线类域，且每第二维度中某些重叠单位等于±1，这一现象源于射线类域的特殊性质，并与Shintani-Faddeev模余循环直接计算结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索SIC-POVM中向量标量积的代数结构，揭示其与Stark单位及射线类域之间的深层联系，并解释非最小情况下重叠单位的特殊性质。

Method: 结合精确代数推导与数值证据，分析SIC-POVM重叠单位在射线类域中的分解结构，考察非最小情形下的格点结构，并利用射线类域的特殊性质解释维度相关的±1现象。

Result: 重叠单位始终是来自基础域最大整数环所附射线类域中Stark单位平方根的整数幂乘积；非最小情形下涉及多个射线类域构成的格点结构；每第二维度中部分重叠单位等于±1，这由射线类域的特殊性质决定；发现与Shintani-Faddeev模余循环计算结果互补且一致。

Conclusion: SIC-POVM的重叠单位具有系统性的代数数论结构，可由射线类域中的Stark单位平方根完全刻画，非最小情形和维度奇偶性对重叠单位的影响均源于射线类域的深层性质。

Abstract: It has been observed that the mutual scalar products of the vectors in a SIC-POVM are given by algebraic units, and at least in some cases by square roots of Stark units. The full picture is somewhat more complicated, especially if non-minimal SIC-POVMs are considered. We present a mixture of exact and numerical evidence suggesting that the overlap units are always products of integral powers of square roots of Stark units from ray class fields all of which are attached to the maximal ring of integers in the base field. In the non-minimal case a lattice of such ray class fields is involved. In every second dimension (counted in a certain way) some of the overlap units equal $\pm 1$, and we show that this follows from a special property of the ray class fields. Our observations are complementary to but consistent with the claim that the overlap units can be calculated directly from the Shintani--Faddeev modular cocycle.

</details>


### [101] [Structure and information measures of few-electron systems under a spherically symmetric Gaussian potential within a density functional approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23538)
*Raveena Arya,Santanu Mondal,Amlan K. Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过密度泛函理论，利用高斯势和不同交换相关泛函，系统研究了H、类He（Z=2-18）离子、Li和Be的量子点能量特性及信息论测度，发现势阱宽度减小和深度增加会增强电子局域化并削弱相对电子关联效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究球对称高斯势约束下多电子体系的能量调控机制，分析不同交换相关泛函（Wigner与LYP）对电子关联效应的描述能力，并引入信息论测度（Shannon熵、Fisher信息）表征电子密度分布特性，为量子点等受限体系的能级调控提供理论依据。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论框架，基于功函数交换势求解径向Kohn-Sham方程；使用广义伪谱方法（GPS）进行非均匀最优空间离散化以满足Dirichlet边界条件；对比局域参数化Wigner泛函和非线性梯度-拉普拉斯依赖的LYP泛函两种电子关联处理方法；计算Shannon熵（位置空间S_r和动量空间S_p）及Fisher信息（I_r）等信息论测度。

Result: 能量随势阱宽度减小而增大；S_r出现极小值，而S_p和I_r呈现极大值；势阱深度增加会放大这些效应；Fisher-Shannon平面显示电子密度逐渐局域化和压缩，表明相对电子关联效应减弱；Collins猜想呈现非线性环状特征。

Conclusion: 高斯势参数可有效调控量子点体系能量与电子密度分布特性；电子关联效应的相对贡献随势阱约束增强而减弱；信息论测度为表征受限体系电子结构提供了有效工具；大部分结果属首次报道，为相关领域研究提供了新见解。

Abstract: Energies of H, He-like ($Z=2-18$) ions, Li, and Be are investigated under a spherically symmetric Gaussian potential through a density functional formalism. The radial Kohn-Sham equation has been solved by invoking a work function-based exchange potential. The effect of electron correlation is analyzed by incorporating two functionals: a local parameterized Wigner functional and a non-linear gradient- and Laplacian-dependent Lee-Yang-Parr (LYP) functional. The generalized pseudospectral method is employed to provide accurate numerical eigenfunctions and eigenvalues. This allows nonuniform, optimal spatial discretization fulfilling the Dirichlet boundary conditions. This work demonstrates a possible manipulation of energy by controlling dot parameters. Apart from ground states, exploratory results are also reported for low-lying excited state $1s2s$ ($^{1,3}S$) of He atom. Companion calculations are also performed for various information-theoretic measures, such as Shannon entropy in position ($S_{r}$), momentum ($S_{p}$) spaces, and Fisher information in position space ($I_{r}$). The behavior of correlation functionals in presence of Gaussian potential is examined critically. We find that energy increases, $S_{r}$ exhibits minima, while $S_{p}$, $I_{r}$ attain maxima for a decrease in the width of potential, whereas an increase in potential depth further amplifies these effects across all properties. The Fisher-Shannon plane reveals a progressive localization as well as the compression of electronic density, and thereby indicates a weakening of relative electron-correlation effects. In the Collin's conjecture, it gives rise to a non-linear loop-like feature. Much of the results are presented here for the first time.

</details>


### [102] [Structure-Aware Variance Reduction for Unbiased Randomized Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23544)
*Joshua W. Dai,Fredrik Hasselgren,Chusei Kiumi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了经典方差缩减技术如何应用于随机化哈密顿量模拟方法，在不引入额外偏差的情况下降低采样开销。通过提出连续时间演化概率角度插值（continuous TE-PAI）协议，消除了Trotter离散化误差，并在张量网络模拟中避免了键维度的指数增长。进一步发现，基于随机乘积公式的估计器方差可分解为经典计数分量和量子排序分量，其中非对易部分主导模拟开销。通过利用计数分量，在小系统中实现了约70%的误差降低，在n=30自旋链的张量网络模拟中实现了约80%的误差降低，分别对应约91%和约96%的采样成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机化哈密顿量模拟方法通常面临系统偏差与采样开销之间的权衡，现有方法难以同时消除偏差和降低方差。本文旨在通过经典方差缩减技术，在不改变平均信道（即不引入额外偏差）的前提下，减少随机化模拟的统计误差和采样成本。

Method: 提出连续TE-PAI协议，一种拟概率随机电路方法，用有限深度随机电路消除Trotter离散化误差（确定性Trotter化需无限深度才能做到）。将方差分解为经典计数分量和量子排序分量，分别对应可对易部分和非对易部分的贡献。针对计数分量设计粗粒度统计策略，使其适配特定可观测量和估计器。

Result: 连续TE-PAI在有限深度下消除离散化误差，避免了Trotter化中因离散化误差导致的键维度指数增长。方差分解表明非对易部分主导开销。在小系统中利用计数分量实现约70%误差降低；在n=30自旋链中利用粗粒度统计实现约80%误差降低，偏差可忽略，采样成本分别降低约91%和约96%。

Conclusion: 经典方差缩减技术可有效应用于随机化哈密顿量模拟，在不引入额外偏差的前提下大幅降低采样开销。连续TE-PAI协议为无偏随机化模拟提供了实用方案，方差分解为优化随机化模拟策略提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Randomized Hamiltonian simulation methods are often governed by a trade-off between systematic bias and sampling overhead. We study how classical variance-reduction techniques can be applied to such methods without changing their mean channel, and therefore without introducing additional bias. As a motivating unbiased estimator, we formulate continuous time-evolution probabilistic angle interpolation (continuous TE-PAI), a quasiprobabilistic random-circuit protocol whose remaining Monte Carlo error is purely statistical. Continuous TE-PAI removes Trotter discretization error with finite-depth random circuits, whereas deterministic Trotterization does so only in the infinite-depth limit. Further, in tensor-network simulations, we demonstrate that discretization error can cause an unphysical exponential growth in the bond dimension required for Trotterized simulations, whereas comparable-depth continuous TE-PAI circuits avoid this growth. We then show that the variance of randomized product-formula-based estimators admits a canonical decomposition into a classical counting component and a quantum ordering component such that the dominant simulation overhead results from the non-commutative parts of the Hamiltonian dynamics. Motivated by this decomposition, we achieve an $\approx70\%$ error-reduction using the counting-component for small systems whereas our tensor-network simulations of $n=30$ spin-chain dynamics use coarser statistics tailored to the observable and estimator attaining a negligible bias and a reduction of $\approx 80\%$ leading to $\approx91\%$ and $\approx96\%$ sampling-cost reductions, respectively.

</details>


### [103] [Genuine Global Kochen-Specker Contextuality as Classical Coordination Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23577)
*Ming Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出协调代价框架，研究第三类量子关联——**真正全局Kochen-Specker语境性**：局部子系统非语境化且测试的多体块广义贝尔局域，但全局经验模型无法用非语境隐变量解释。引入协调比特、全局语境覆盖数、标度律和提升定理，通过Hardy阻塞和KCBS型任务实例分析经典模拟下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注量子关联经典模拟失败的两个原因：低通信局域隐变量模型不存在，或单一非语境隐状态无法解释所有相容测量语境。本文旨在探索第三种机制——**真正全局KS语境性**，并建立统一框架量化其经典模拟所需的资源代价。

Method: 提出协调代价框架，将通信、记忆和局域计算视为经典模拟器维持全局经典解释的不同方式。引入**协调比特**衡量所需协调量，定义**全局语境覆盖数**，建立**标度律**描述任务族渐近行为，证明**提升定理**将KS语境种子族的经典模拟下界迁移到真正全局KS模型。

Result: 通过极化路径Hardy阻塞和后选择KCBS型任务的工作实例，展示了框架的适用性。提升定理实现了从种子族到全局KS模型的下界传递，协调比特等概念提供了量化语境性复杂度的新工具。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了第三类量子关联——真正全局KS语境性，建立了协调代价框架及其理论工具，为理解量子语境性复杂度、设计经典模拟下界提供了新视角和数学基础。

Abstract: Classical simulations of quantum correlations can fail because no low-communication local hidden-variable model exists, or because no single noncontextual hidden state can explain all compatible measurement contexts. This manuscript studies a third regime: genuine global Kochen-Specker contextuality, where local subsystems are noncontextual and the tested multipartite blocks are generalized-Bell-local, but the whole empirical model admits no global noncontextual hidden-variable explanation. We propose a coordination-cost framework in which communication, memory, and local computation are treated as different ways for a classical simulator to maintain a global classical explanation from locally available information. We introduce coordination bits, global contextual covering numbers, scaling laws for task families, and an abstract lifting theorem showing how classical simulation lower bounds for KS-contextual seed families can be transferred to genuinely global-KS models. As worked examples, we analyze a polarization-path Hardy obstruction and postselected KCBS-type tasks.

</details>


### [104] [Random dimension reduction and learning symmetric properties of quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23592)
*Angus Lowe,Xinyu Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“随机维度缩减”的程序，能够同时降低多个量子态的维度，同时保持等距张量幂作用下的不变性质。该方法可将学习对称性质（包括依赖多个输入态的性质）的样本复杂度中的维度替换为最大秩。研究表明，维度缩减后结合全状态层析成像，能够改进状态对之间距离、保真度和相对熵的估计上界。通过Choi-Jamiolkowski同构，该程序与Tang、Wright和Zhandry最近提出的随机纯化信道存在密切联系，并完成了无需引用Schur变换或Schur多项式的样本最优层析成像的端到端分析。最后，证明不存在能同时纯化多个不同输入态的随机纯化信道，表明随机维度缩减与随机纯化相关但不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低学习量子态对称性质时的样本复杂度，将维度依赖替换为更宽松的最大秩依赖，并建立与随机纯化信道的理论联系。

Method: 提出随机维度缩减程序，利用Schur变换实现高效量子电路，并通过Choi-Jamiolkowski同构建立与随机纯化信道的联系。

Result: 改进了状态对间距离、保真度和相对熵估计的上界；完成样本最优层析成像的端到端分析；证明不存在能同时纯化多个不同输入态的随机纯化信道。

Conclusion: 随机维度缩减是一种与随机纯化相关但不同的新工具，能有效降低量子态学习问题的样本复杂度。

Abstract: We introduce a procedure called random dimension reduction that simultaneously reduces the dimensions of many, potentially distinct quantum states while preserving properties invariant under the tensor power action of an isometry. This provides a black-box method to replace the dimension with the maximum rank in the sample complexity of learning symmetric properties, even those depending on multiple input states. We show that dimension reduction followed by full state tomography yields improved upper bounds for estimating distances, fidelities, and relative entropies between pairs of states. We also give an efficient quantum circuit implementation of the procedure using the Schur transform.
  Expressing the action of our procedure through the Choi-Jamiolkowski isomorphism reveals an intimate connection with the recently introduced random purification channel by Tang, Wright, and Zhandry. This perspective also completes an end-to-end analysis of sample-optimal tomography without requiring a reference to the Schur transform or Schur polynomials. Finally, we prove that there does not exist a random purification channel that simultaneously purifies copies of multiple, potentially different input states. Hence, random dimension reduction is related to, but distinct from, random purification.

</details>


### [105] [A quantum algorithm for one-shot signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23612)
*Gopikrishnan Muraleedharan,Minh Thuy Truc Pham,Vir Pathak,Thomas Gardner,Chuanqi Zhang,QPerfect,Gavin K. Brennen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种预混淆电路级实现的单次签名方案，具有可委托签名、安全代币转移和公开可验证随机性等应用。方案包括密钥生成（经典公钥/量子密钥对）和签名（量子密钥处理消息生成经典签名）两个阶段，无算法错误，经典验证者可高效验证。方案基于可穿刺伪随机函数输出的随机仿射陪集叠加态及陪集成员测试电路实现。逻辑量子比特数为 Θ(κ log(r) + n + l)，门复杂度为 Θ(n³ + nl)，其中 r 为公钥大小，n+l 为签名大小，l 为消息长度，κ=Ω(n) 为安全参数。文章给出了不同 n 下的量子比特和门计数，并指出了抵御经典和量子多项式时间攻击所需的混淆电路组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造一个高效的、可预混淆电路级实现的单次签名方案，该方案具有可委托签名、安全代币转移和公开可验证随机性等应用价值，并且能够抵御经典和量子多项式时间攻击。

Method: 方案分为两个阶段：密钥生成阶段产生经典公钥/量子密钥对；签名阶段用量子密钥处理消息字符串生成经典签名。核心方法是通过可穿刺伪随机函数的输出确定随机仿射陪集，制备该陪集元素的叠加态，并结合陪集成员测试电路实现签名。

Result: 逻辑量子比特数 Θ(κ log(r) + n + l)，门复杂度 Θ(n³ + nl)，其中 r 为公钥大小，n+l 为签名大小，l 为消息长度，κ=Ω(n) 为安全参数。给出了不同 n 下的显式量子比特和门计数，并确定了需要混淆的电路组件。

Conclusion: 该单次签名方案无算法错误，支持经典验证者高效验证，且通过预混淆电路可实现安全性。提供了具体的资源开销分析，为实际实现和安全性分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: We provide a pre-obfuscation circuit-level implementation of an efficient one shot signature scheme, which has known applications to delegated signatures, secured token transfer, and publicly verifiable randomness. The algorithm consists of two stages: a key generation stage where a classical public key/quantum secret key pair is produced, and a signing stage where the quantum secret key is processed with a message string to produce a classical signature. There is no algorithmic error in the construction and the signed message can be efficiently checked by a classical verifier. Our scheme works by preparing a superposition over elements of a random affine coset determined by the output of a puncturable pseudorandom function, together with a circuit that tests coset membership. The logical qubit number scales like $Θ( κ\log(r) + n + l)$ and the gate complexity scales like $Θ(n^3 + nl)$, where $r$ is the public key size, $n+l$ is the signature size, $l$ is the message size, and $κ= Ω(n)$ is the cryptographic security parameter. We provide explicit qubit and gate counts for varying $n$ and identify the circuit components where obfuscation would be required for security against classical and quantum polynomial time attacks.

</details>


### [106] [Log-concavity and tunneling: adiabatic quantum optimization for convex functions (with a spike)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23614)
*Arthur Braida,Elie Bermot,Simon Apers*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了离散薛定谔算子的基态对数凹性框架，将其从凸势扩展到含局部极小值的势函数，并利用该性质将HWS问题的隧穿分析从线性势推广至二次势，表明隧穿效应在凸势加尖峰问题中的广泛适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子隧穿被认为能为量子计算提供加速，绝热量子优化旨在利用这一效应。尽管已有"汉明重量加尖峰"等学术概念验证研究，但隧穿效应的算法优势仍未得到充分探索。本文旨在将HWS问题的分析推广到更一般的势函数，以揭示隧穿更广泛的应用前景。

Method: 首先建立离散一维薛定谔算子基态的对数凹性框架，覆盖凸势及含局部极小值的势函数（对凸势情形，给出了Brascamp-Lieb连续结果的离散版本）；利用该对数凹性结果，将Reichardt的HWS扰动分析从线性势（精确可解）扩展到更广泛的具有对数凹基态的势函数族，具体以二次势为例验证。

Result: 建立了离散对数凹性框架，获得了超越Jarret-Jordan凸势谱隙界的新界限；将HWS的隧穿分析从线性势推广到二次势（非精确可解），强有力地表明隧穿效应对凸势加尖峰问题的广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 对数凹性是理解隧穿加速的关键结构性质；所建立的框架可用于更广泛势函数的隧穿分析，暗示凸势加尖峰问题中隧穿效应具有普适性。

Abstract: Quantum tunneling is expected to provide a computational speedup in quantum computing, a phenomenon that Adiabatic Quantum Optimization (AQO) aims to leverage. While some academic proofs of concept have been studied, such as the "Hamming weight with a spike" (HWS) problem, the algorithmic gains of this effect remain underexplored. In this work we extend the analysis underlying HWS to more general potentials.
  In the first half of the work, we establish (discrete) log-concavity of the ground state as a key structural property in this context. We devise a framework for establishing log-concavity of the ground state for a large family of discrete, 1-dimensional Schrödinger operators. The family includes convex potentials, but also certain potentials with local minima. In the convex case, this provides a discrete version of a continuous result by Brascamp and Lieb ('76). We demonstrate the utility of our result by establishing new spectral gap bounds, going beyond related results by Jarret and Jordan ('14) for convex potentials.
  In the second half of the work, we use our results on log-concavity to extend the perturbative analysis of HWS by Reichardt ('04) to the larger family of potentials with log-concave ground state. As a concrete instantiation, we use our result to extend the HWS analysis from a linear potential (which is exactly solvable) to a quadratic potential (which is no longer solvable). Our result strongly suggests the broader applicability of tunneling to convex potentials with spikes

</details>


### [107] [Genuine certification of incompatible quantum instruments through sequential communication tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23647)
*Arindam Mitra,Satyaki Manna,Debashis Saha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子仪器不相容性的操作意义，通过引入三方通信任务实现了不兼容量子仪器的半设备无关认证，并证明该认证是量子优势在通信中的体现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子仪器是量子动力学的一般描述，其不相容性是量子理论中非经典性的基本表现。然而，这一概念的操作意义尚未明确建立，需要探索如何在实际任务中认证和利用量子仪器的不相容性。

Method: 引入了一类三方通信任务（发送者-转发者-接收者），推导了所有兼容仪器所能达到的优值紧上界，该上界与经典通信中同等维度约束下的最优性能一致。通过违反该上界来认证转发者实施的量子仪器对的不相容性。

Result: 该认证协议是真正的（genuine），能够认证一对仪器的不可容性，即使这些仪器诱导的测量和信道成对兼容；识别了能够认证不相容量子仪器的最简通信实例。

Conclusion: 量子仪器不相容性的认证是量子优势在通信中的一种体现，该协议不依赖于仪器诱导的测量和信道的相容性，具有独立的认证能力。

Abstract: Quantum instruments constitute the general description of quantum dynamics, encompassing both quantum measurements and quantum channels as special cases. Consequently, the incompatibility of quantum instruments represents a fundamental manifestation of nonclassicality in quantum theory. Here, we establish the operational significance of this notion by demonstrating communication tasks with classical inputs and outputs that enable the semi-device-independent certification of incompatible quantum instruments. We introduce a class of three-party communication tasks involving a sender, a relayer, and a receiver, and derive the tight upper bound of the figure of merits of these tasks achievable by all compatible instruments implemented by the relayer and this bound coincides with the optimal performance attainable in a classical communication subject to the same dimensional constraints. Violation of this bound certifies the incompatibility of the pair of quantum instruments implemented by the relayer. This identifies certification of incompatible instruments as a manifestation of quantum advantage in communication. This certification protocol is genuine as it is able to certify the incompatibility of a pair of instruments where the measurements and channels induced by the instruments are pairwise compatible and, therefore it does not depend on the incompability of measurements and channels induced by the instruments. Finally, we identify the simplest instances of our communication scenario that enable the certification of incompatible quantum instruments.

</details>


### [108] [Robust Structure Learning of $k$-local Lindbladians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23652)
*Tim Möbus,Thiago Bergamaschi,Daniel Stilck França,Cambyse Rouzé*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效协议，仅使用乘积态制备、短时间演化和单量子比特泡利测量，即可学习n量子比特上未知的k-局域林德布拉德生成元，无需先验知识。对于固定k和有界加权相互作用强度，该协议以约Õ_k(ε⁻²n^{2k}log(1/δ))样本量实现汉密尔顿项和耗散泡利-GKSL系数的逐项ε精度估计，并可通过半定投影转化为有效的k-局域林德布拉德生成元。在满足稳定稀疏条件时，样本复杂度可从多项式依赖降为对数依赖。本文还提供了鲁棒结构学习过程、模型误设保证及下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子动力学学习协议大多局限于酉动力学（哈密顿量学习），对开放量子系统（林德布拉德主方程）的学习缺乏高效保证，尤其缺乏在仅有乘积态制备、短时间演化和单量子比特测量等有限实验控制下的通用k-局域耗散动力学学习算法。

Method: 协议首先通过乘积态制备、短时间演化和单量子比特泡利测量来估计所有k-局域泡利-GKSL系数（包括哈密顿和耗散部分），使用统计样本平均实现逐项ε精度。随后通过半定规划（SDP）投影将这些估计值转化为满足完全正性和保迹性的有效k-局域林德布拉德生成元，保证金刚石范数误差有界。对于稀疏结构，利用稳定稀疏条件实现对数依赖。

Result: 1) 无先验结构时：样本复杂度Õ_k(ε⁻²n^{2k}log(1/δ))；2) 经SDP投影后获得有效林德布拉德生成元需Õ_k(ε⁻²n^{4k}log(1/δ))样本；3) 满足稳定稀疏条件时，样本复杂度可降至Õ_k(ε⁻²log(n/δ))（有界交度支持）；4) 对长程相互作用有类似降级；5) 提供了鲁棒结构学习和模型误设下的保证；6) 证明了样本复杂度的互补下界。

Conclusion: 本文提供了首个在有限实验控制下（仅乘积态、短时间演化、单量子比特测量）学习一般k-局域耗散量子动力学的可证明高效算法，并建立了完备的样本复杂度上下界。该工作为开放量子系统的量子设备认证和表征奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present an efficient protocol for learning an unknown $k$-local Lindblad generator on $n$ qubits using only product-state preparations, short-time evolution, and single-qubit Pauli measurements, without prior knowledge of the interaction structure. For fixed $k$ and bounded weighted interaction strength, the protocol estimates all Hamiltonian and dissipative Pauli--GKSL coefficients to entrywise accuracy $\varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$ using $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_k(\varepsilon^{-2}n^{2k}\log(1/δ))$ samples and polylogarithmically many evolution times. A semidefinite projection converts these estimates into a valid $k$-local Lindblad generator with diamond-norm error at most $\varepsilon$ using $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_k(\varepsilon^{-2}n^{4k}\log(1/δ))$ samples and polynomial-time classical postprocessing. If a suitable set of influential coefficients is supplied and satisfies a stable sparsity condition, the dependence on $n$ can improve from polynomial to logarithmic; in particular, exact supports of bounded intersection degree require only $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_k(\varepsilon^{-2}\log(n/δ))$ samples, with analogous reductions in system-size dependence for sufficiently decaying long-range interactions. We also provide a robust structure-learning procedure, extend the guarantees to model misspecification, and prove complementary sample-complexity lower bounds. To our knowledge, these are the first efficient learning guarantees for general $k$-local dissipative quantum dynamics under such limited experimental control.

</details>

### [109] [Scalar-scalar-tensor interaction in DHOST theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20730)

TL;DR: 本文在二次型DHOST理论子类中，推导了宇宙学背景扰动的标量-标量-张量三阶作用量的普遍形式，采用协变方法并在微扰层面固定酉规范，为估算暗能量模型中引力波衰变为两个标量激发的衰减率提供了关键工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在修正引力理论（特别是DHOST理论）中，理解引力波在宇宙学背景下的传播行为及其与标量场的相互作用，对于检验暗能量模型和区分不同引力理论至关重要。现有研究缺少对标量-标量-张量混合耦合的完整三阶作用量，而这正是计算引力波衰变到标量激发过程的前提。

Method: 采用完全协变的形式体系，在二次型DHOST理论的子类中围绕宇宙学背景展开微扰，固定酉规范以消除冗余自由度，系统推导标量-标量-张量三阶耦合作用量。

Result: 得到了描述两个标量激发与一个张量激发（引力波）之间混合相互作用的普遍三阶作用量，该结果适用于二次型DHOST理论中的暗能量模型。

Conclusion: 该作用量是计算晚期宇宙中引力波衰变为两个标量模式的衰减率的关键成分，为在DHOST暗能量模型中检验引力波传播性质及区分不同修正引力理论提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We derive the general cubic action for perturbations about a cosmological background in the quadratic subclass of degenerate higher-order scalar-tensor (DHOST) theories, focusing on scalar-scalar-tensor interactions. We adopt a fully covariant formulation and implement unitary gauge at the level of perturbations. This mixed sector provides the key ingredient for estimating the decay rate of a gravitational wave into two scalar excitations in the quadratic DHOST models of dark energy in the late-time Universe.

</details>


### [110] [Study of Cosmic Acceleration of the Universe in the Presence of Bulk Viscous Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20759)
*Dheeraj Singh Rana*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本论文在非黎曼几何框架下研究具有体粘滞性的f(Q)引力理论，通过精确宇宙学解、观测数据约束及动力学系统分析，证明该模型能够成功解释宇宙晚期加速膨胀，并再现从物质主导到加速膨胀的不同宇宙演化阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论虽成功描述引力现象，但宇宙加速膨胀的观测证据以及精密宇宙学面临的挑战，促使研究者寻找广义相对论的扩展或替代理论。

Method: 在非黎曼几何框架下构建具有体粘滞性的f(Q)引力模型，首先寻找粘滞流体的精确宇宙学解，利用观测数据集约束模型自由参数；随后对多类非线性粘滞f(Q)模型进行动力学系统分析与观测分析。

Result: 所提出的粘滞f(Q)引力模型能够成功解释观测到的晚期宇宙加速膨胀；约束后的模型可用于研究宇宙膨胀的演化历史；该模型能够再现从物质主导到加速膨胀的不同宇宙演化阶段。

Conclusion: 基于非黎曼几何的粘滞f(Q)引力模型是广义相对论可行的扩展方案，能够有效解释宇宙晚期加速膨胀并再现宇宙演化的不同历史时期。

Abstract: Over the past century, both theoretical advancements and experimental observations have established General Relativity (GR) as the most successful framework for describing gravitational phenomena. However, observations from multiple cosmological probes over the last two decades have provided compelling evidence for the accelerated expansion of the Universe. The rapid progress in observational astronomy and precision cosmology has highlighted several challenges that motivate the search for extensions or alternatives to General Relativity. In this thesis, we investigate alternative formulations of gravity based on non-Riemannian geometry, with particular emphasis on (f(Q)) gravity in the presence of bulk viscosity. We first explore the existence of exact cosmological solutions for viscous fluid models within the framework of (f(Q)) gravity and constrain the free parameters of these solutions using observational datasets. The resulting constrained models are then employed to study the evolutionary history of cosmic expansion. Our analysis demonstrates that the proposed viscous (f(Q)) gravity models can successfully account for the observed late-time acceleration of the Universe. Furthermore, we examine several classes of nonlinear viscous (f(Q)) gravity models through both dynamical systems and observational analyses. Particular attention is given to the cosmological viability of these models and their ability to reproduce the different evolutionary epochs of the Universe, ranging from matter-dominated eras to accelerated expansion phases.

</details>


### [111] [Selective enhancement of quantum decay channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20766)
*Pritam Nanda,Kinjalk Lochan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在腔体边界条件下，量子粒子衰变率的增强机制。与自由空间不同，腔体几何结构能够使产物场的模式函数在红外波段获得显著支持，从而提升低能过程的衰变概率和速率。通过选择谐振几何结构，可在低能下大幅增强衰变率，替代高能实验研究。这对QED过程和奇异新物理研究具有直接意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自由空间中，量子粒子衰变到红外波段时，模式密度严重不足，导致低能衰变效率低下。为了获得足够大的衰变率，通常需要过渡到高能区域，但这增加了实验难度和成本。因此，寻找在低能下也能获得高衰变率的机制成为研究动机。

Method: 采用场论方法，在受控边界条件（如腔体）环境下，分析产物场模式函数的红外波段行为。通过调节腔体几何结构，寻找谐振几何的"甜点"位置，研究在该条件下主场与产物场相互作用的增强效应。

Result: 腔体边界条件能够显著增强产物场在红外波段的模式函数支持，从而提升低能过程的衰变概率和速率。特定谐振几何结构下，主场与产物场的相互作用获得数量级上的增强，使低能衰变率显著提高。

Conclusion: 通过合理选择腔体几何结构，可以在低能条件下实现与高能过程相当的衰变率，为研究高能物理过程提供了一种低成本的替代方案。该结果对QED过程及新物理探索具有重要意义。

Abstract: In the decay of quantum particles under field theoretic consideration, the decay rate is typically a convolution of the density of modes the primary field is allowed to decay into and the allowed probability density for the field to decay into such modes. In free space, though many such processes show high amplitude of such transitions towards the infrared sector, the depletion of allowed mode density in that regime arrests the efficacy of such decays at low energies. Therefore in free space, in order to enhance the decay rate, one needs the transition probability density to be rich enough towards the high energy sector where mode density support is also high enough to make the rate sufficiently large. In this work we argue that in the controlled boundary condition environment e.g. in a cavity, the mode functions of product field receive significant support towards their infrared sector, boosting the probability (and hence rates) of low energy processes. The cavity geometry offers sweet spots in terms of resonant geometry around which the interaction of a primary field with product fields receives dramatic enhancement, significantly enlarging its decay rates. Therefore, a judicious selection of cavity geometry serves as a potential substitute to studying interesting processes at high energy. The results have direct relevance for the study of QED processes and implications for the study of exotic new physics are also discussed.

</details>


### [112] [The Cosmological Constant Problem: An Accessible Introduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20778)
*Ali Kaya,Adam Lahey*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对宇宙学常数问题进行了教学性介绍，通过实标量场展示了量子真空能量密度和压力的计算方法，涵盖了维度正则化、截断正则化和绝热正则化三种方案。文章指出，虽然通常引用的120个数量级差异未考虑正则化和重整化，但量子场论的基本原理仍导致理论与观测的巨大不匹配。此外，宇宙膨胀还带来了共动尺度与物理尺度的选择、真空非唯一性等概念性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为仅具备量子场论和广义相对论基础知识的读者提供一份关于宇宙学常数问题的教学性介绍，澄清通常引用的120个数量级差异的统计基础，并指出除数值差异外还存在概念性挑战。

Method: 使用大质量实标量场作为示例，在平直时空和膨胀宇宙中计算量子真空能量密度和压力；分别采用维度正则化、截断正则化和绝热正则化三种方案进行详细计算；对膨胀宇宙背景采用经典处理，不涉及量子引力效应。

Result: 指出通常引用的120个数量级差异的估计未考虑正则化和重整化过程；但即使考虑正则化和重整化，量子场论基本原理仍导致理论与观测之间的巨大不匹配；除数值差异外，宇宙膨胀还带来共动尺度与物理尺度选择、真空非唯一性等概念性挑战。

Conclusion: 宇宙学常数问题不仅包含巨大的数值差异（即使考虑正则化和重整化），还涉及与宇宙膨胀相关的根本性概念挑战，需要更深入的理论思考。

Abstract: We present a pedagogical introduction to the cosmological constant problem that requires only basic knowledge of quantum field theory and general relativity. A massive real scalar field is used to illustrate how the quantum vacuum energy density and pressure can be calculated both in flat spacetime and in an expanding universe. Detailed computations are provided for dimensional, cutoff, and adiabatic regularizations. No attempt is made to address quantum gravitational effects, and the expanding-universe background is treated classically. We point out that although the commonly cited discrepancy of 120 orders of magnitude between theory and observation is based on an estimate that does not account for regularization and renormalization, fundamental principles of quantum field theory nevertheless lead to a huge mismatch. In addition to this large discrepancy, we emphasize that there are also conceptual challenges related to cosmic expansion, such as the choice between comoving and physical scales in certain contexts and the non-uniqueness of vacuum.

</details>


### [113] [$\mathrm{ISIM}(2)$ gravitational gauge model via Soft Algebra formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20806)
*J. M. B. Matzenbacher,J. M. Hoff da Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于软代数形式化方法，通过规范ISIM(2)洛伦兹子群参数，构建了一个完整的无挠引力模型，并得到了不变作用量。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于通过规范ISIM(2)洛伦兹子群参数，利用软代数形式化框架，构建一个完整的引力模型，以探索更广泛的引力理论可能性。

Method: 采用软代数形式化方法，首先给出sim(2)及其非齐次扩展的封闭形式，然后在此基础上发展无挠引力模型，最终推导出不变作用量。

Result: 成功构建了一个基于ISIM(2)规范的无挠引力模型，并得到了具有不变性的作用量。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了通过软代数形式化方法规范ISIM(2)洛伦兹子群可以构建出完整的引力模型，为引力理论的推广提供了新途径。

Abstract: This work presents a comprehensive account of a gravitational model based on the gauging of the $\mathrm{ISIM}(2)$ Lorentz subgroup parameters through the so-called soft algebra formalism. After presenting a closed form for $\mathfrak{sim}(2)$ and its inhomogeneous extension, we proceed to develop a complete torsionless gravitational model, culminating in an invariant action.

</details>


### [114] [Exact solutions using power law scalar potential in the Saez-Ballester-K-essence like theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20894)
*J. Socorro,A. Gil-Ocaranza,Ximena López-Mujica,Cesar Aarón Pacheco-Vázquez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过场重新定义将负幂律Sáez--Ballester势能的K-essence宇宙学模型映射到已知可解FLRW模型，在哈密顿形式下获得了尺度因子和标量场的精确经典解，并推导出Wheeler-DeWitt方程的精确量子解。该模型展现出以q→-1为特征的晚期加速膨胀（de Sitter相），标量场作为宇宙演化的背景存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一类由负幂律Sáez--Ballester势构造的K-essence宇宙学模型，探索其在经典和量子层面的可解性及宇宙学 implications。

Method: 通过场重新定义（φ→φ）将模型映射到已知可解的FLRW宇宙学模型，在哈密顿形式下求解经典场方程，并推导对应的Wheeler-DeWitt方程以获得量子精确解。

Result: 获得了尺度因子和标量场的精确经典解；宇宙学动力学呈现晚期加速膨胀，减速参数渐近于q→-1（de Sitter相）；获得了WDW方程的精确量子解。

Conclusion: 该K-essence模型在经典和量子层面均具有一致性，标量场作为宇宙演化的背景，为理解此类模型的宇宙学演化提供了完整描述。

Abstract: We investigate a K-essence like cosmological model whose scalar-field potential is constructed from a negative power-law Sáez--Ballester potential. By means of a suitable field redefinition from $φ$ to $\varphi$, we show that the resulting field equations acquire a mathematical structure analogous to that of a previously solved Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) cosmological model. This correspondence allows us to obtain exact classical solutions for both the scale factor and the scalar field within the Hamiltonian formalism. The resulting cosmological dynamics exhibits a late-time accelerated expansion, with the deceleration parameter approaching the asymptotic value $q\rightarrow -1$, characteristic of a de Sitter phase. At the quantum level, the corresponding Wheeler-DeWitt (WDW) equation is derived and exact quantum solutions are obtained. These results provide a consistent classical and quantum description of the cosmological evolution generated by this class of K-essence models. In this formalism, the scalar field remains as a cosmic background where the universe unfolds, which is glimpsed from the quantum solution perspective.

</details>


### [115] [Scattering, Hawking Radiation and Neutrino Energy Deposition in Euler-Heisenberg Black Holes Surrounded by Perfect Fluid Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20931)
*Ramon Becar,P. A. Gonzalez,Ali Ovgun,Joel Saavedra,Yerko Vasquez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有完美流体暗物质（PFDM）的Euler-Heisenberg黑洞的动力学与散射性质，分析了EH非线性电动力学修正与PFDM暗物质晕对标量、电磁和轴向自旋-2扰动的影响，发现PFDM参数收缩光学结构、增加振荡频率和阻尼率并抑制透射，而EH修正仅在电荷足够大时产生显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非线性电动力学（Euler-Heisenberg修正）与暗物质环境（完美流体暗物质）共同作用下黑洞的动力学和散射特性，为比较暗物质环境与非线性电动力学对黑洞观测效应的影响提供统一框架。

Method: 采用十三阶WKB方法结合Padé重求和计算拟正规模频谱，与光子球角频率和Lyapunov指数导出的eikonal预言进行比较；通过径向波动方程直接数值积分计算精确的灰体因子并对比解析下界；分析吸收截面和Hawking辐射谱；计算中微子-反中微子湮灭通道的相对论增强。

Result: PFDM参数收缩光学结构、增加振荡频率、增强阻尼率并抑制透射；Euler-Heisenberg修正产生较弱的近地平线形变，仅在大电荷时显著；近极端构型下导出纯虚部拟正规频率分支，阻尼率随PFDM参数增加且几乎与自旋无关。

Conclusion: 该工作为比较暗物质环境与非线性电动力学对黑洞观测效应（如引力波信号、吸收和辐射谱）的影响提供了统一的散射框架。

Abstract: We study the dynamical and scattering properties for the Euler-Heisenberg BH surrounded by perfect fluid dark matter. The geometry contains a compact non-linear electrodynamic correction governed by the EH coupling and a logarithmic dark-matter contribution governed by the surrounding PFDM halo. We study the scalar, electromagnetic and a particular effective axial spin-2 channel constructed on the fixed EH plus PFDM background, acting as a proxy for the gravitational-like perturbation problem and not for the fully coupled gravitational perturbation problem. We compute the quasinormal-mode spectrum employing a thirteenth-order WKB method supplemented with Padé resummation and compare it with the eikonal prediction calculated in terms of the angular frequency of the photon sphere and the Lyapunov exponent. Moreover, we study the near-extremal configurations and derive a purely imaginary branch of quasinormal frequencies in the near-horizon region, whose damping rate increases with the PFDM parameter and is nearly spin-independent. We then compute exact greybody factors by direct numerical integration of the radial wave equation and compare them to analytical lower bounds. We also analyze the absorption cross sections and the Hawking emission spectra. We also calculate the relativistic enhancement of the neutrino-antineutrino annihilation channel outside the EHPFDM black hole. We find that the PFDM parameter contracts the optical structure, increases the oscillation frequency, enhances the damping rate and suppresses transmission. On the other hand, the Euler-Heisenberg correction leads to a weaker near-horizon deformation whose effect becomes relevant for sufficiently large charge. These results provide a common scattering framework for comparing the impact of dark-matter environments and nonlinear electrodynamics

</details>


### [116] [Quantum Dust from the Curse of Dimensionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20943)
*Kenan Oggad*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出，多种量子引力方法在最短尺度上发现时空二维化，可能并非源于物理本质，而是源于态空间几何的测度集中现象：高维Fubini-Study空间中随机纯态退化为等距尘埃，扩散探针在单一弛豫尺度上必然读出二维，这一读数可通过Laplacian谱近零处的特征值密度检验来区分是测量假象还是真实维度。该结论经Lean 4形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何互不相关的量子引力方法都在最短尺度上发现时空呈二维，探究这一共同结果是物理本质还是测量假象，并给出可检验的判据。

Method: （1）利用Fubini-Study几何上测度集中现象，证明高维纯态空间中随机样本退化为等距尘埃；（2）扩散探针在此尘埃上读出谱维数2；（3）通过Laplacian特征值密度近零处的功率律分布，区分真实维度（幂律尾）与测量假象（单尺度或带隙）。所有结论在Lean 4中形式化验证。

Result: 高维态空间中随机尘埃的扩散探针必然读出谱维数2；该读数是否为结构本身的维度由Laplacian谱近零处的特征值密度决定：幂律尾对应真实维度，单弛豫尺度对应假象二维，带隙双尺度则读出普适线。

Conclusion: 量子引力中普遍出现的二维化可能只是测量假象，而非时空的真实维度，给出了基于谱检验的区分标准。

Abstract: Why do unrelated approaches to quantum gravity nearly all find spacetime two-dimensional at the shortest scales? Each theory answers only within its own dynamics; we highlight a single kinematic route to the same value, one assuming no field equation and living in the geometry of the space of states alone. That route is concentration of measure on the Fubini-Study geometry of pure states, which forces the pairwise distances of a random sample to equalize as the dimension grows, so any finite sample collapses to an equidistant dust whose thresholded metric graph is the complete graph. Handed this dust, a diffusion probe reads it as two-dimensional in the large-sample limit, the value the running spectral dimension takes at the dust's single relaxation scale, a property of the measurement rather than the structure; this convergence on two is not, by itself, evidence that spacetime is two-dimensional. Whether a given two is such an artifact is governed by the Laplacian spectrum near zero, and whether that reading carries across an emergence map is the condition we call spectral faithfulness; a single relaxation scale encodes no spectral dimension that tells one structure from another. The collapse, the probe value, and the eigenvalue-density criterion are machine-checked in Lean 4 against Mathlib, resting on the standard Beta law of overlaps; a power-law tail of small eigenvalues reads a genuine dimension, a single scale above a gap reads two at its own clock, and a gapped two-scale band reads off the universal line. These classes are run on graph-Laplacian proxies, and whether a link-graph reading carries to the physical nonlocal operator is left open. The spectral test reads the eigenvalue density near zero and separates, on a given structure, a measurement artifact from a dimension the structure genuinely expresses.

</details>


### [117] [Out-of-Equilibrium Effects in Non-Radial Relativistic Stellar Perturbations: A Model-Agnostic Formulation and Mode Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20957)
*Takuya Katagiri,Victor Guedes,Kent Yagi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个通用框架，在相对论性恒星的非径向振荡中系统纳入非平衡效应（粘性与热导率），并在偶宇称与奇宇称通道中统一描述，无需依赖特定本构关系，揭示了这些效应如何影响扰动方程、几何形变和流体涨落，并以Bemfica-Disconzi-Noronha-Kovtun流体为例分析了频率与阻尼时间的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有Lindblom-Detweiler形式仅考虑理想流体，缺乏对非平衡效应（如粘性和热导率）在非径向恒星振荡中系统、模型无关的处理，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 扩展Lindblom-Detweiler形式，基于张量结构和热力学分解构建线性、非径向相对论性恒星扰动的通用框架，偶宇称与奇宇称通道均纳入非平衡修正，不依赖特定本构关系；并以Bemfica-Disconzi-Noronha-Kovtun流体进行微扰应用。

Result: 成功构建了首个通用形式框架，揭示了非平衡效应在扰动方程中的结构贡献；在Bemfica-Disconzi-Noronha-Kovtun流体中获得了频率和阻尼时间的微扰偏移；发现封闭本征值问题的结构特征可能产生额外模式族。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析不同相对论性流体理论如何修改非径向恒星扰动结构提供了统一途径，有助于理解粘性和热导率在致密星振荡中的具体作用。

Abstract: We present a systematic, model-agnostic analysis of out-of-equilibrium effects, including viscosity and thermal conductivity, in non-radial oscillations of relativistic stars. Extending the Lindblom-Detweiler formalism, we construct, to our knowledge, the first general framework for linear, non-radial relativistic stellar perturbations that incorporates generic nonequilibrium corrections to the perfect-fluid sector in both the even- and odd-parity channels. Our framework is formulated in terms of the tensorial structure and thermodynamic decomposition of generic corrections without relying on any specific constitutive relations, thereby allowing us to elucidate, at a structural level, how these effects enter the perturbation equations and contribute to geometric deformations and fluid fluctuations. As an application, we consider the Bemfica-Disconzi-Noronha-Kovtun fluid and perturbatively investigate shifts in the frequencies and damping times of modes connected to their perfect-fluid counterparts in the limit of vanishing transport coefficients. We also identify structural features of the closed eigenvalue problem that can give rise to additional mode families. Our formalism provides a unified framework for analyzing how different relativistic fluid theories modify the structure of non-radial stellar perturbations.

</details>


### [118] [Solving Einstein Field Equations on a Digital Quantum Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20975)
*Clelia Altomonte,Malcolm Fairbairn*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子算法求解爱因斯坦场方程的初步方案，在WEBB数值相对论形式体系下，对史瓦西黑洞时空进行演化和扰动，并在IBM量子计算机上进行了实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典数值相对论中的偏微分方程组求解问题映射到量子算法上，探索利用量子计算加速黑洞时空演化和引力波模拟的可行性。

Method: 基于WEBB tetrad数值相对论形式体系，开发了一个原理验证级的量子算法来求解爱因斯坦场方程；使用Qiskit软件在门控数字量子计算机上编程实现该算法；在经典模拟器和IBM物理量子计算机上运行，并量化计算资源和运行时间。

Result: 在经典模拟器和IBM量子硬件上成功运行了该算法，对史瓦西黑洞时空进行了演化和扰动，得到了引力准正则模；并量化了所需的计算资源和运行时开销。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了量子算法在数值相对论领域具有潜在应用价值，为未来利用量子计算加速引力物理模拟提供了初步的原理验证基础。

Abstract: In this work, we show how simulations performed on classical computers such as those of Numerical Relativity can be tackled by quantum algorithms for solving systems of partial differential equations. We develop a proof-of-principle quantum algorithm for solving Einstein Field Equations in the Wahlquist-Estabrook-Buchman-Bardeen(WEBB) tetrad Numerical Relativity formalism [1], and test it by evolving the Schwarzschild Black Hole spacetime in the WEBB Numerical Relativity formalism [2], perturbing it to obtain gravitational Quasinormal Modes [3]. We program the algorithm components for a gate-based, digital quantum computer using the Qiskit software [4] and run it on classical simulators and physical IBM quantum computers through the UKRI National Quantum Computing Centre (NQCC) Quantum Access program and quantify the computational resources and runtime.

</details>


### [119] [Probing the Circular Unruh Effect with Cavity-Controlled Lamb Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21019)
*Yan Peng,Jiawei Hu,Hongwei Yu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出通过测量高速旋转原子在高质量谐振腔中的兰姆位移，可在极低加速度（低至0.5 m/s²）下探测圆型Unruh效应，比传统方案所需的10²⁰ m/s²低二十多个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Unruh效应的直接观测极富挑战性，因为产生1 K的Unruh温度需要约10²⁰ m/s²的巨大加速度，远超当前实验能力。现有方案难以在实验室条件下实现直接探测。

Method: 将加速原子置于高Q腔中，利用腔体重塑电磁态密度，将原本微小的非惯性修正转化为可调的能级移动。通过分析原子角速度与腔失谐对兰姆位移的调制（增强、淬灭或屏蔽），实现光谱学探测。

Result: 在实验可行参数下，仅需0.5 m/s²的加速度即可产生约10 Hz的旋转诱导兰姆位移，比传统Unruh探测所需加速度低二十多个数量级。

Conclusion: 腔控兰姆位移光谱学为在极低加速度条件下实现圆型Unruh效应的实验室检验提供了可行路线。

Abstract: The Unruh effect predicts that accelerated observers perceive the inertial vacuum as populated by particles, providing a flat-spacetime analogue of Hawking radiation. Its direct observation, however, remains experimentally challenging, since an Unruh temperature of $1\,\mathrm{K}$ requires accelerations of order $10^{20}\,\mathrm{m/s^2}$. Here, we show that the Lamb shift of a centripetally accelerated atom inside a high-$Q$ cavity provides a sensitive spectroscopic probe of the Unruh effect at dramatically lower accelerations. The cavity reshapes the electromagnetic density of states and converts otherwise tiny noninertial corrections into tunable level shifts. Depending on the atomic angular velocity and cavity detuning, the Lamb shift can be enhanced, strongly quenched, or completely screened. Remarkably, for experimentally realistic parameters, a rotation-induced shift of order $10\;\mathrm{Hz}$ can arise already at accelerations as low as $0.5\,\mathrm{m/s^2}$, more than twenty orders of magnitude below the acceleration scale conventionally associated with direct Unruh detection. These results identify cavity-controlled Lamb-shift spectroscopy as a viable route toward laboratory tests of the circular Unruh effect in the ultralow-acceleration regime.

</details>


### [120] [An open-source numerical tool for rational orbits and gravitational radiation in static spherically symmetric spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21029)
*Dan Li,Shiyang Hu,Chen Deng,Shijie Tan,Guansheng He*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于Mathematica和OpenMP并行架构开发了一套通用计算框架，用于模拟静态球对称时空中类时粒子的有理轨道及其引力辐射，仅需用户定义协变度规即可高效计算轨道、引力波极化状态和特征应变，为弯曲时空性质研究提供一站式解决方案。在史瓦西时空的测试验证了代码的有效性，并展示了中等质量黑洞与银心超大质量黑洞组成的极端质量比旋近系统发射的引力波有望被未来空间探测器识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类时轨道是探索弯曲时空内在性质的重要探针，其携带的引力辐射信号为强场引力提供了直接窗口。目前缺乏一个高效、通用且综合的计算工具，能够仅基于用户定义的度规自动模拟各类轨道及对应的引力波信号。

Method: 基于Mathematica和OpenMP并行架构开发数值计算框架，用户只需定义协变度规，程序即可自动计算有理轨道、引力波极化状态和特征应变。

Result: 在史瓦西时空上的测试验证了代码在有效势、稳定轨道区域、有理/无理轨道及引力波信号等多个关键方面的可靠性；中等质量黑洞与银心超大质量黑洞组成的EMRI系统发射的引力波有望被未来空间探测器识别。

Conclusion: 该计算框架为弯曲时空性质及其潜在观测特征的研究提供了高效、全面的一站式解决方案，具有重要的理论价值和观测应用前景。

Abstract: Timelike orbits constitute a crucial probe for exploring the intrinsic properties of curved spacetimes, and the carried gravitational radiation signals provide a direct window into strong field gravity. In this paper, we develop a versatile computational framework based on Mathematica and the OpenMP parallel architecture to simulate the rational orbits of timelike particles and their gravitational radiation in static spherically symmetric spacetimes. Specifically, requiring only the user defined covariant metric, this numerical tool can efficiently calculate rational orbits across various configurations, as well as the corresponding gravitational wave polarization states and characteristic strains. The package presented here offers a highly efficient and comprehensive one-stop solution for investigating the properties of curved spacetimes and their potential observational signatures. To demonstrate the reliability and capability of our code, we apply it to the Schwarzschild spacetime as a test case, illustrating the functionality of the code across several key aspects, including the effective potential, stable orbital regions, rational and irrational orbits, and gravitational wave signals. Furthermore, we show that the gravitational waves emitted by an extreme mass ratio inspiral system composed of an intermediate mass black hole and the Galactic Center supermassive black hole have the potential to be identified by future space detectors.

</details>


### [121] [Population-level correlations in Bayesian statistics: an illustrative model for gravitational-wave astronomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21076)
*Caroline B. Owen,Alexandre Toubiana,Davide Gerosa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个理想化的高斯分析模型，研究单事件相关性、系统偏差与总体相关性之间的相互作用，发现单事件相关性会增大总体相关性的不确定性，而跨目录相关的波形系统偏差可能被错误地解释为总体相关性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着引力波事件数量的增加，总体推断已从边缘分布预测扩展到研究致密双星参数（质量、自旋、红移）之间的相关性。这些相关性对天体物理学和广义相对论检验具有重要约束力，但需要理解单事件相关性及系统偏差如何影响总体层面相关性的推断，以避免虚假检测。

Method: 构建了一个基于高斯分布的理想化分析模型，通过数学框架同时处理单事件参数相关性、系统偏差以及总体层面的相关性，研究三者之间的相互作用机制。

Result: 1) 单事件层面参数间的相关性会增大该参数对在总体层面相关性的不确定性，可能掩盖真实的总体相关性。2) 波形建模的系统偏差如果跨越目录具有相关性（在参数空间中某些区域更难建模的情况下很常见），会被总体相关性分析有效吸收并被误判为真正的总体相关性。

Conclusion: 该简化高斯模型可作为未来更详细研究的宏观指南，帮助识别和避免引力波总体推断中虚假的总体相关性测量。

Abstract: With increasingly large numbers of gravitational-wave events, population inference is now beginning to move beyond predictions of marginal distributions and to probe correlations between compact-binary parameters such as masses, spins, and redshifts. These correlations have strong constraining power for both astrophysics and tests of general relativity. In this paper, we present an idealized analytical model to study the interplay between single-event correlations, systematic biases, and population-level correlations. With this, we investigate the potential emergence of false-positive measurements of population-level correlations. We quantify how the presence of correlations at the single-event level between a pair of parameters increases the uncertainty of population-level correlations for those parameters, potentially obscuring the true underlying population correlation if present. We also find that if waveform systematics lead to biases that are correlated across the catalog (which is likely, because certain regions of the parameter space are more difficult to model), this can be effectively absorbed by a population analysis that targets correlations and can be misinterpreted as such. This simple Gaussian-based model may serve as a broad compass for future, more detailed explorations.

</details>


### [122] [Reconstructing the slope of a nearly flat quintessence potential from cosmography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21127)
*Saikat Chakraborty,Peter K. S. Dunsby,Robert J. Scherrer*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了具有近乎平坦标量场势的thawing quintessence模型，采用宇宙学框架进行分析。研究表明，在慢滚条件下，仅需知道减速参数q和密度参数Ω_φ即可重建近乎平坦势场的斜率λ。然而，DESI DR2后的宇宙学数据与近乎平坦假设之间存在潜在张力。模型在w-Ω_φ和w-w'相平面中表现出吸引子行为，对应早期w≈-1的普遍thawing演化。推导了q-j平面中的对应关系，表明不同膨胀历史可产生相同thawing演化，但所有可行轨迹都接近ΛCDM极限j=1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究表明，一般情形下quintessence势斜率λ的宇宙学重构需要知道直至jerk参数j的高阶宇宙学参数。本文旨在探索在慢滚近似条件下，是否能用更少的参数（低至减速参数q）实现近乎平坦势的重构，并检验该假设与最新DESI DR2数据的兼容性。

Method: 采用宇宙学（cosmographic）框架，对thawing quintessence模型进行分析。引入慢滚条件[(dV/dφ)/V]²≪1和|(d²V/dφ²)/V|≪1简化势场重构。利用DESI DR2数据检验近乎平坦假设，并通过相平面分析（w-Ω_φ和w-w'）研究模型的吸引子行为，导出q-j平面的解析关系。

Result: 发现慢滚条件下仅需q和Ω_φ即可重构λ；DESI DR2数据与近乎平坦假设存在潜在张力；模型在相平面中展现出吸引子行为，早期w≈-1；q-j平面中不同膨胀历史可产生相同thawing演化，但所有可行轨迹均靠近j=1的ΛCDM极限。

Conclusion: 慢滚近似能简化thawing quintessence势场的宇宙学重构，但最新数据对近乎平坦假设提出了挑战。thawing演化具有普适性，且与ΛCDM接近，这为区分暗能量模型提供了观测约束。

Abstract: We revisit thawing quintessence models with nearly flat scalar-field potentials using a cosmographic framework. Earlier work indicates that the cosmographic reconstruction of the slope $λ=-(dV/dφ)/V$ of the quintessence potential in the general case requires the knowledge of the cosmographic paremeters up to the jerk parameter $j$. In this work we show that the slow-roll conditions $[(dV/dφ)/V]^2 \ll 1$ and $|(d^2V/dφ^2)/V| \ll 1$ allow the reconstruction of the slope of a nearly flat potential with knowledge of only the deceleration parameter $q$ (and the density parameter $Ω_φ$). Confronting the assumption of near-flatness with the cosmographic data after DESI DR2, however, reveals possible tension between the two. We further show that these models exhibit attractor behaviour in the $w$--$Ω_φ$ and $w$--$w'$ phase planes, corresponding to a universal thawing evolution with $w \approx -1$ at early times. We also derive the corresponding relation in the cosmographic $q$--$j$ plane and show that different cosmological expansion histories can produce the same thawing evolution. Nevertheless, all viable trajectories remain close to the $Λ$CDM limit $j=1$.

</details>


### [123] [Entanglement and firewalls in quantum circuit model of black hole evaporation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21181)
*Yi-Chen Lu,Tien Hsieh,Da-Shin Lee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了黑洞蒸发的量子电路模型，引入带参数θ的扰乱幺正矩阵生成更一般的黑洞初始态，研究量子单态性在防火墙形成中的作用。发现θ=π/2时防火墙比θ=0时更早出现；在一定θ范围内，所有Mω值下黑洞与辐射间都会出现防火墙结构，且信息通过辐射携带出去。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子单态性在黑洞与辐射之间防火墙形成中的作用，并研究更一般的黑洞初始态对黑洞蒸发过程的影响。

Method: 采用带有单一参数θ的扰乱幺正矩阵作用于落入黑洞的量子比特基态，生成更一般的黑洞初始态；基于三部分系统（黑洞、刚辐射、早辐射）的量子电路模型，分析纠缠和防火墙形成对黑洞质量M和霍金辐射频率ω的依赖关系。

Result: θ=π/2（最大扰乱）时防火墙比θ=0（无扰乱）时更早出现；在一定θ范围内，所有Mω取值下黑洞与刚辐射之间都会产生防火墙结构；信息可通过辐射携带出来，初始黑洞量子比特态可从最终辐射态的印记中恢复。

Conclusion: 不同初始态对黑洞蒸发量子电路模型中的多部分纠缠性质有重要影响，为理解黑洞信息悖论和防火墙问题提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We reexamine the quantum circuit model of black hole evaporation proposed in Class. Quantum Grav. 35, 235013 (2018). This model incorporates the tripartite systems: black hole, just radiation and early radiation. We apply the scrambling unitary matrix with a single parameter $θ$ to the ground state of the qubits in infalling matter towards a black hole in order to generate initial qubit states of the black hole that are more general than those in Class. Quantum Grav. 35, 235013 (2018). Specifically, the scrambling unitary matrix reduces to no scrambling and maximum scrambling when $θ=0$ and $θ=π/2$, respectively. Our aim is to explore the role of quantum monogamy in the firewall formation between the black hole and radiation. In this model, entanglement and firewall formation depend on the black hole mass $M$ and the frequency of Hawking radiation $ω$. For the initial state with $θ=π/2$, a firewall emerges at an earlier stage of the evolution than with $θ=0$. We also find that a firewall structure emerges between BH and JR, and that the information is carried away by radiation for all values of $Mω$, provided that $θ$ lies within a certain analytically determined range. Following the unitary gate dynamics, the initial black hole qubit state can be retrieved from its imprint on the final radiation state, which was originally hidden behind the black hole's horizon. These results may provide insight into the properties of multipartite entanglement due to the different initial states in the evolution of a quantum circuit model for black hole evaporation.

</details>


### [124] [Temperature of free gravitational field: A geometrical perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21183)
*Samarjit Chakraborty,Gareth Amery,Sunil D Maharaj,Rituparno Goswami*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过新颖的几何方法，将自由引力场的热力学温度与Newman-Penrose标架中零测地线的非仿射性联系起来，在局域旋转对称性下统一了多种温度函数（Clifton-Ellis-Tavakol温度、Hawking温度、Unruh温度等），并推导出温度函数的几何与因果输运方程（带强迫项的双曲波动方程），最后将几何温度解释为沿零测地线传播的光线的引力红/蓝移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立自由引力场热力学温度的几何基础，统一不同引力温度概念（如Clifton-Ellis-Tavakol温度、Hawking温度、Unruh温度），并揭示其内在几何联系。

Method: 采用新颖的几何方法，在Newman-Penrose标架下建立零测地线非仿射性与热力学温度的关系；在局域旋转对称性背景下分析，并推广至Petrov D型时空（如Kerr时空）；推导温度函数的几何输运方程。

Result: 成功将多种引力温度函数在各自极限下统一；证明结果可从局域旋转对称时空推广至Kerr等Petrov D型几何；得到带Weyl曲率和物质源项的双曲波动方程形式的温度输运方程；建立了几何温度与引力红/蓝移的对应关系。

Conclusion: 零测地线的非仿射性为引力温度提供了统一的几何诠释，该温度可理解为沿零测地线传播的光子经历的引力红/蓝移的几何表现。

Abstract: In this paper, using a novel geometrical approach, we relate the concept of the thermodynamic temperature of the free gravitational field, to the non-affinity of real null geodesics in a Newman Penrose tetrad. This naturally links various temperature functions like Clifton, Ellis and Tavakol temperature, Hawking temperature, Unruh temperature etc., in their respective proper limits. Although our analysis is done within the realm of local rotational symmetry, we show that the result can be extended to other Petrov type D geometries, like the Kerr spacetime. We also obtain the geometrical and causal transport equations for this temperature function, in the form of a hyperbolic wave equation with a forcing term, sourced by Weyl curvature and matter. Finally, as a possible physical interpretation of the non-affinity, we relate the geometrical temperature with the gravitational red/blue shift of light rays travelling along null geodesics.

</details>


### [125] [Thermal Stability and QNMs of a Hairy Black Hole in the Presence of a Monopole Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21332)
*George Koutsoumbas,Andri Machattou,Eleftherios Papantonopoulos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了含标量场和单极子场的广义GHS-GM黑洞的稳定性，发现系统的热稳定性随标量电荷减小而增强，且随着黑洞电荷增加，准正则频率的实部和虚部绝对值均减小，系统稳定（无非正虚部）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究广义GHS-GM黑洞在存在标量场和单极子场时的稳定性，分析系统参数（如标量电荷、黑洞电荷）对热力学行为和扰动衰减特性的影响。

Method: 通过研究黑洞的热力学稳定性（热容等）分析热行为；通过计算准正则频率（实部与虚部）分析扰动下的动力学稳定性。

Result: 1）系统热稳定性随标量电荷减小而增强；2）黑洞电荷增加时，准正则频率的实部和虚部绝对值均减小；3）泛音模式衰减快于基模；4）未出现正虚部，表明系统稳定。

Conclusion: 该广义GHS-GM黑洞系统在标量场和单极子场作用下是稳定的，热力学和动力学稳定性受标量电荷和黑洞电荷的调节。

Abstract: We study the stability of a generalization of the GHS-GM black hole in the presence of a dilaton and a monopole field. We find that the thermal behaviour of system depends on the scalar charge of the dilaton field and as this parameter is decreasing the system becomes more thermally stable. We also find that, as the charge of the black hole is increasing, both the real and the imaginary parts of the quasi-normal frequencies decrease in absolute value. The overtone modes die out faster than the fundamental modes and no positive imaginary parts appear, indicating the stabilty of the system

</details>


### [126] [Relativistic effects in extreme-mass-ratio inspirals within scalar clouds: Eccentric and inclined orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21439)
*Qi-Xuan Xu,Richard Brito,Riccardo Della Monica,Rodrigo Vicente,Chen Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用 fully relativistic perturbative framework 研究了在标量云环境中演化的极端质量比旋近（EMRIs），该环境可通过超辐射不稳定性形成。与以往仅限于圆形赤道运动的研究不同，本文聚焦于 Schwarzschild 黑洞背景，引入偏心率和轨道倾角。结果发现：轨道偏心率可在最近稳定轨道附近诱发密集的共振谱；轨道倾角显著改变能量和角动量损失，存在临界倾角使得在足够小的轨道半径下标量云向轨道传递能量；对于大偏心率，相对论近星点进动相关的共振在全倾角范围内持续存在。该工作为未来围绕自旋黑洞的完全一般轨道上的 EMRI 研究奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于标量云中 EMRI 的相对论研究仅局限于圆形、赤道运动（无偏心率和倾角），无法反映真实天体物理环境中轨道的一般性特征。因此，需要发展能够同时处理偏心率和轨道倾角的 fully relativistic 框架，以更准确地刻画 EMRI 在标量云环境中的演化动力学。

Method: 采用 fully relativistic perturbative framework，以 Schwarzschild 黑洞为背景（暂忽略自旋以单独考察偏心率和倾角效应），计算被散射至空间无穷远和被黑洞事件 horizon 吸收的标量能量与角动量，系统分析轨道参数（偏心率 e、倾角 ι）对标量场能量损失的影响。

Result: (1) 轨道偏心率可在最近稳定轨道附近诱发密集的共振谱，该共振与强相对论近星点进动相关；(2) 轨道倾角显著改变能量和角动量损失速率；(3) 存在临界倾角，当轨道半径足够小时标量云向轨道净传递能量（云能量回流至轨道）；(4) 大偏心率下，近星点进动共振在全倾角范围内持续存在，但顺行与逆行轨道的共振结构显著不同。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套能够处理偏心率和倾角的 fully relativistic 框架，揭示了标量云环境中 EMRI 动力学因轨道几何效应而呈现丰富的新现象（如共振谱、能量回流、倾角依赖的临界行为），为后续围绕 Kerr 黑洞的全一般轨道（同时包括偏心率、倾角及自旋）研究提供了重要的理论基础和方法论支撑。

Abstract: We study extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) evolving in a scalar cloud environment that may form through superradiant instabilities, using a fully relativistic perturbative framework that allows for eccentric and inclined orbits. EMRIs, consisting of a stellar-mass compact object inspiraling into a supermassive black hole, are key sources for space-based gravitational-wave detectors such as LISA. Previous relativistic studies of EMRIs in scalar clouds have been restricted to circular, equatorial motion. Here, instead, we focus on a Schwarzschild black hole background to incorporate eccentricity and orbital inclination. By computing the scalar energy and angular momentum scattered off to spatial infinity and absorbed at the event horizon, we show that orbital eccentricity can induce a dense spectrum of resonances near the last stable orbit, associated with strong relativistic apsidal precession. We further find that orbital inclination can significantly modify the orbital energy and angular momentum losses. In particular, we identify a critical inclination angle below which, at sufficiently small orbital radii, there is a net transfer of energy from the scalar cloud to the orbit. Moreover, for sufficiently large eccentricities, resonances associated with relativistic apsidal precession persist across the full range of inclinations, although their structure changes significantly between prograde and retrograde orbits. These results provide a foundation for future studies of EMRIs in scalar cloud environments on fully generic orbits around spinning black holes.

</details>


### [127] [The WDM Time-Frequency Transform in Gravitational-Wave Data Analysis I: Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21473)
*Aaron Johnson,Katerina Chatziioannou,Jake Summers*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文为引力波分析人员提供了关于时频方法（特别是Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer基）的自包含论述，涵盖了正交性、时频局域化、边界效应、变换实现及似然函数等关键概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管时频方法在缓变噪声处理中兼具频域效率和时域通用性的优势，且在引力波领域逐渐被采用，但其文献记录不够完善，分析人员对其熟悉程度远低于频域方法。因此需要一篇系统性的论述来填补这一空白。

Method: 采用自包含的论述方式，聚焦于Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer（WDM）基，详细推导其数学形式，并分享实践经验与直觉。

Result: 系统整理了WDM基的正交性和良好时频局域化特性，阐明了DC和Nyquist频率处的边界效应，给出了正逆变换的实际实现方案，以及时频域中的噪声协方差矩阵和似然函数。

Conclusion: 本文旨在使引力波分析人员能够更熟悉和有效地使用时频方法，特别是WDM基，为这一领域提供一份实用的参考文档。

Abstract: For slowly-varying noise, time-frequency methods offer a natural middle ground between the efficiency of the frequency domain and the generality of the more expensive time domain. Despite growing adoption, such methods remain less well documented and less familiar in the gravitational-wave literature, compared to the ubiquitous frequency domain. Aimed at gravitational-wave analysts, in this paper we present a self-contained account of time-frequency methods, detail derivations for a specific basis, namely the Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer (WDM) basis, and share intuition and lessons learned. We document key concepts: the orthogonality and good time-frequency localization of the basis, the edge effects at the DC and Nyquist frequencies, the forward and inverse transforms and their practical implementation, and the noise covariance matrix and likelihood in the time-frequency domain.

</details>


### [128] [Binary Black Hole Coalescence and the Dynamics of Scalar Hair in Einstein-Maxwell-Scalar Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21549)
*Jean Pierre Díaz,P. A. González,Eleftherios Papantonopoulos,Yerko Vásquez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过数值相对论模拟，研究了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量（EMS）理论中带电黑洞对头并合过程。结果表明，非最小电磁-标量耦合可以动态触发标量化，而最终黑洞能否保持标量毛取决于耦合强度与残余黑洞的电荷量：强耦合且残余荷电时形成标量化黑洞，弱耦合或电荷中和后系统发生去标量化。标量辐射波形与引力波主导模态在非线性阶段存在定性关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索EMS理论中带电黑洞并合过程能否通过非最小电磁-标量耦合动态激发标量毛，以及标量毛的最终命运（标量化vs去标量化）由何种物理因素决定。

Method: 采用带电 puncture 初始数据构建两个 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞，嵌入纯动力学标量微扰（初始标量场为零，共轭动量提供小种子）。耦合引力、电磁和标量场进行数值演化，监测视界、辐射和视界上的标量场。

Result: 弱耦合或并合后电荷抵消导致电磁源被抑制时，标量场辐射或吸收，系统去标量化；强耦合且残余黑洞荷电时，标量场保持有限，最终黑洞趋近标量化构型。并合还激发标量辐射，其时域特征与引力波主导模在非线性阶段定性相关。

Conclusion: 实现了EMS理论中双黑洞并合过程中的标量化/去标量化相变，证明标量毛的命运由标量耦合强度与残余黑洞电荷含量的共同作用控制。

Abstract: We investigate the head-on coalescence of charged binary black holes in Einstein-Maxwell-Scalar (EMS) theory using numerical relativity. The binaries are built from charged puncture initial data representing two Reissner-Nordström black holes immersed in a purely kinetic scalar perturbation: the scalar field initially vanishes, while its conjugate momentum provides a small seed for the instability. We evolve the coupled gravitational, electromagnetic, and scalar sectors and monitor the apparent horizons, the emitted radiation, and the scalar field on the horizons. Our simulations show that the nonminimal electromagnetic-scalar coupling can dynamically trigger the growth of scalar hair even when the individual black holes are initially scalar-free. The subsequent evolution depends on the coupling strength and on the charge retained by the remnant. For weak coupling, or when charge cancellation suppresses the electromagnetic source after merger, the scalar field is radiated away or absorbed by the final horizon and the system dynamically descalarizes. For sufficiently strong coupling and nonzero remnant charge, the scalar field remains finite and the final black hole approaches a scalarized configuration. The coalescence also excites scalar radiation whose time profile is qualitatively correlated with the dominant gravitational-wave mode during the nonlinear stage of the collision. These results provide a binary realization of scalarization/descalarization transitions in EMS theory and show that the fate of scalar hair is controlled by the interplay between the scalar coupling and the charge content of the remnant.

</details>


### [129] [Consistency Conditions and the Derivation of Harmonic Structure in Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21603)
*Bardia H. Fahim,Joshua G. Fenwick*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过考虑任意维度的爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-胀子理论，在没有预先假设度规函数形式的情况下，证明了场方程强制胀子耦合相等、空间基几何为Ricci平坦，并使度规函数在该基上调和，从而统一推导了调和行为与共形结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦以及爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-胀子理论的许多精确解共享一个共同的结构模式：度规函数由给定空间基（通常为平坦基）上的调和函数构建。作者希望探究这一模式的根源，而不预先假设基几何或度规函数的调和形式。

Method: 作者在任意维度下考虑具有非平凡耦合的胀子场和Liouville型势（与宇宙学参数成比例）的爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-胀子理论。不预先假设基几何或度规函数的调和形式，直接分析场方程，推导出胀子耦合常数必须相等、空间基几何必须为Ricci平坦、度规函数在该基上为调和函数，并进一步得到共形势的表示。此外还考虑了纯空间分支，得到不同的耦合常数约束。

Result: 对于一般分支，场方程强制：(1)胀子耦合常数相等；(2)空间基几何为Ricci平坦；(3)度规函数在该基上为调和函数。由此得到的时空可用共形势表示。对于纯空间分支，则得到对耦合常数的不同约束。

Conclusion: 这些结果为包括多中心几何、宇宙学解和动力学黑洞在内的几类解中出现的调和行为与共形结构提供了统一的场方程推导，揭示了这类解公共结构模式的深层理论根源。

Abstract: Many exact solutions of Einstein-Maxwell and Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory share a common structural pattern in which the metric functions are built from harmonic functions on a specified spatial base, often taken to be flat. We investigate this pattern by considering the Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory in arbitrary dimensions, where the dilaton field is non-trivially coupled to the Maxwell field and to a Liouville-type potential proportional to the cosmological parameter. Without imposing either the base geometry or the harmonic form of the metric function in advance, we show that for the generic branch, the field equations force the dilaton couplings to be equal, restrict the spatial base geometry to be Ricci flat, and make the metric function harmonic on this base. The resulting spacetime can then be written in terms of a conformal potential. We also consider a purely spatial branch, which instead leads to a distinct constraint on the coupling constants. These results provide a unified field-equation derivation of the harmonic behavior and conformal structure that appear in several classes of solutions, including multi-center geometries, cosmological solutions, and dynamical black holes.

</details>


### [130] [Mirror Symmetry and Double Signature Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21805)
*Tevian Dray,Charles Hellaby*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了黑色镜面时空（Black Mirror Spacetime），确认其在视界处不存在分布曲率奇点或脉冲引力波，澄清了此前关于曲率分量解析性的争议，并讨论了该时空的整体结构及穿过视界的世界线行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新检验黑色镜面时空是否在视界处存在表面层（即分布曲率奇点对应的脉冲引力波），澄清此前关于曲率分量解析性的争议。

Method: 借鉴先前关于号型变化（signature change）的技术，将黑色镜面时空视为双号型变化模型，分析其视界处的几何性质。

Result: 确认黑色镜面时空不包含任何分布曲率奇点或脉冲引力波，与先前声称曲率分量解析的结论进行了比较。

Conclusion: 黑色镜面时空在视界处无奇点，其结构允许两个外部区域直接连通而无内部区域，为理解黑洞时空拓扑提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The black mirror spacetime proposed by Tzanavaris, Boyle, and Turok [1] connects the two exterior regions of the extended Schwarzschild black hole directly to each other, with no intervening interior region. Using techniques adapted from previous work on signature change, we reexamine the black mirror spacetime as a model of double signature change, and investigate whether there is a surface layer at the horizon, that is, a distributional curvature singularity corresponding to an impulsive gravitational wave. We confirm that the black mirror spacetime does not contain any such singularity, and compare our result with previous claims that the curvature components are analytic. We also discuss the global structure of the black mirror spacetime, and examine what happens to worldlines and curves passing through.

</details>


### [131] [Quantum Memory in Scalar-Induced Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21835)
*Waqas Ahmed*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明，尽管标量诱导引力波通常被视为经典随机背景，但其中仍残留量子相干信息。通过退相干双模高斯标量态，推导出标量反常相干性与张量协方差矩阵之间的转移关系，并发现局域标量功率谱下，张量功率由标量功率收缩产生，而反向模张量相干性则源于标量反常相干收缩。这种相干性即使在标量纠缠消失后仍能产生非零张量失调和连通张量功率协方差。研究提出连通协方差和相位敏感应变关联可作为探测二次引力波背景中原始量子相干的探针，并讨论了其对未来空间干涉仪和脉冲星计时阵列的可能意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统标量诱导引力波通常被作为经典随机背景处理，忽略了其中可能残留的量子信息。作者旨在揭示这种描述的不完备性，并探索如何利用残余量子相干性作为探测原始量子引力效应的新探针。

Method: 从退相干的双模高斯标量态出发，推导标量反常相干性与诱导张量模式协方差矩阵之间的显式转移关系，分析局域标量功率谱下张量功率和反向模张量相干性的不同来源，并计算量子失调和连通功率协方差。

Result: 张量功率由标量功率收缩主导，反向模张量相干性由标量反常相干收缩产生。即使标量纠缠完全消失，这种相干性仍能产生非零张量失调和连通张量功率协方差。

Conclusion: 连通协方差和相位敏感应变关联可作为探测二次引力波背景中原始量子相干的观测探针，可能对未来空间干涉仪和脉冲星计时阵列实验具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Scalar-induced gravitational waves are usually treated as a classical stochastic background sourced by phase-random curvature perturbations. We show that this description can miss residual quantum information. Starting from a decohered two-mode Gaussian scalar state, we derive explicit transfer relations between the scalar anomalous coherence and the covariance matrix of induced tensor modes. For a localized scalar power spectrum, the ordinary tensor power is sourced by scalar power contractions, whereas the opposite-mode tensor coherence is sourced by scalar anomalous-coherence contractions. This coherence can generate nonzero tensor discord and a connected tensor-power covariance even after scalar entanglement has vanished. We identify the connected covariance and phase-sensitive strain correlations as probes of primordial quantum coherence in secondary gravitational-wave backgrounds, and discuss their possible relevance for future space-based interferometers and pulsar timing arrays.

</details>


### [132] [Observational Constraints on $f(Q,T)$ Gravity in the Presence of DBI-Essence Scalar Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21839)
*Mayur Mune,Praveen Kumar Dhankar,Goutam Manna,Safiqul Islam,Bidisha Samanta,Behnam Pourhassan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在f(Q,T)+DBI引力框架下研究晚期宇宙学，通过MCMC分析联合H(z)、DESI BAO和Pantheon+SHOES数据约束模型参数，发现该模型虽不能完美复现所有观测特征，但作为ΛCDM的统计可行替代方案，可为缓解H₀张力等问题提供有用框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索扩展对称遥平行引力中耦合DBI标量场的晚期宇宙学行为，检验f(Q,T)引力结合DBI场是否能为标准宇宙学模型提供可行的替代方案，并探讨缓解现有宇宙学张力（如H₀分歧）的可能性。

Method: 在空间平坦FLRW背景下，将宇宙介质视为有效完美流体，推导f(Q,T)+DBI引力的背景场方程；针对线性形式f(Q,T)=αQ+βT解析求解；利用H(z)、DESI BAO (DR2)和Pantheon+SHOES超新星数据，通过MCMC分析约束模型参数。

Result: 联合后验分布（表II和III）与当前晚期宇宙学约束大体一致，可直接与ΛCDM比较，量化了βT耦合和DBI扇区导致的偏离。模型虽未精确复现所有观测特征，但提供了统计上可行的替代方案。

Conclusion: f(Q,T)+DBI引力是ΛCDM的一个统计可行替代途径，为探索缓解现有宇宙学张力（包括H₀分歧）提供了有用框架，但未声称解决了这一问题。

Abstract: We investigate late-time cosmology in extended symmetric teleparallel gravity coupled to a Dirac-Born-Infeld (DBI) scalar field within $f(Q,T)$ gravity, where $Q$ is the non-metricity scalar and $T$ is the trace of the matter energy-momentum tensor. Working on a spatially flat Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker background and treating the cosmic medium as an effective perfect fluid, we derive the background field equations for $f(Q,T)+\mathrm{DBI}$ gravity and obtain analytic solutions for the linear choice $f(Q,T)=αQ+βT$. We then constrain the model parameters with a Markov Chain Monte Carlo analysis using Hubble-rate data, DESI BAO (DR2) measurements, and the Pantheon+SHOES Type~Ia supernova sample. The joint posteriors (Tables II and III) are broadly consistent with current late-time constraints and allow a direct comparison with $Λ$CDM, quantifying the departures driven by the $βT$ coupling and the DBI sector. Although the model does not reproduce every observational feature exactly, it provides a statistically viable alternative avenue to the standard paradigm and a useful framework for exploring potential remedies to existing tensions, including the $H_0$ discrepancy, without claiming a definitive resolution.

</details>


### [133] [Entanglement, Discord, and Residual Coherence in Scalar-Induced Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21901)
*Waqas Ahmed*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究标量诱导引力波中残留的量子信息特征，发现异常标量相干性可以传递到张量模态，产生非平凡的协方差和相位结构，为探测原始量子关联提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究标量诱导引力波是否保留原始曲率扰动的量子信息特征（如量子关联），探索超越纠缠的量子相关性在引力波背景中的可观测印记。

Method: 采用协方差矩阵形式，将原始曲率扰动描述为退相干的双模压缩高斯态；推导标量到张量的主导转移关系；分析张量相干性如何产生高斯失谐及相位敏感可观测量。

Result: 发现张量功率由标量功率收缩产生，而对动张量相干性由异常标量相干收缩产生；该相干性控制高斯失谐并产生连接功率协方差 κ(k)∝|γ_k|²/α_k²；稳健特征不是引力波谱的普遍移动，而是具有非平凡协方差和相位结构的相关张量背景。

Conclusion: 标量诱导引力波可作为探测超越纠缠的原始量子相关性的新探针，为未来引力波观测提供现象学模板和Fisher估计参考。

Abstract: Scalar-induced gravitational waves are usually modeled as a classical stochastic background sourced by primordial curvature perturbations. We investigate whether residual quantum-information properties of the scalar sector can survive decoherence and leave imprints in the induced tensor background.
