Using the covariance-matrix formalism, we describe primordial curvature perturbations as decohered two-mode squeezed Gaussian states and identify the anomalous scalar coherence that may remain after scalar entanglement has vanished. We then derive the leading scalar-to-tensor transfer relations for opposite-momentum induced tensor modes. The ordinary tensor power is sourced by scalar power contractions, whereas the opposite-mode tensor coherence is sourced by anomalous scalar-coherence contractions. This tensor coherence controls the induced Gaussian discord and generates connected and phase-sensitive observables, including a connected power covariance $κ(k)\propto |γ_k|^2/α_k^2$. Thus the robust signature is not a universal shift of the gravitational-wave spectrum, but a correlated tensor background with nontrivial covariance and phase structure. We discuss phenomenological templates and provide an illustrative Fisher estimate for future gravitational-wave observations. Our results suggest that scalar-induced gravitational waves may offer a new probe of primordial quantum correlations beyond entanglement.

</details>


### [134] [Determining Kerr black hole spin and inclination from a segment of the critical curve in black hole images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21921)
*Kenta Hioki,Umpei Miyamoto,Tomohiro Harada*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种方法，通过黑洞图像中临界曲线（critical curve）的片段来确定非极端克尔黑洞的自旋参数 $a/M$ 和倾角 $i$。即使只观测到临界曲线的局部片段（通过高阶光子环的亮度增强），定义的三类几何观测量也能唯一确定 $(a/M,i)$ 及辅助参数 $r_{nl}\in[0,1]$，无需重构完整临界曲线。该方法适用于实际观测，并可推广到更一般的旋转黑洞时空。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际黑洞成像观测中，临界曲线本身不可直接观测，但高阶光子环在其附近累积产生亮度增强，使得获取临界曲线的局部片段成为可能。现有方法通常需要完整重构临界曲线来约束自旋和倾角，而现实观测往往只能获得片段信息，因此需要一种仅利用局部片段就能确定黑洞参数的方法。

Method: 引入临界曲线的标准化片段，定义三类几何观测量来表征片段特征；证明这些观测量在给定参数域内能唯一确定 $(a/M,i)$ 以及标识片段位置的辅助参数 $r_{nl}\in[0,1]$。

Result: 临界曲线的局部片段包含足够的几何信息，足以唯一约束黑洞自旋和倾角，无需完整重构临界曲线。

Conclusion: 该方法自然适配实际观测条件（仅获取局部片段），且可扩展至更一般的旋转黑洞时空，为未来EHT等观测数据约束黑洞参数提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We present a method for determining the spin parameter $a/M$ and inclination angle $i$ of a non-extremal Kerr black hole from segments of the critical curve identified in black hole images. Although the critical curve itself is not directly observable, higher-order photon rings accumulate near it, and in realistic observations localized portions of the resulting brightness enhancement may be available for identifying segments of the critical curve. We introduce standardized segments of the critical curve and define three observables that characterize their geometry. We show that these observables uniquely determine $(a/M,i)$, together with an auxiliary parameter $r_{nl}\in[0,1]$ specifying the location of the identified segment along the critical curve, within the domain considered. Thus, even a segment of the critical curve contains sufficient geometric information to constrain the black hole spin and inclination without reconstructing the full critical curve. The framework is naturally suited to realistic observations and may be extended to more general rotating black hole spacetimes.

</details>


### [135] [Gravitational Radiation and Charges on de Sitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22060)
*Emine Şeyma Kutluk*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文总结了与Geoffrey Compère和Sk.Jahanur Hoque合作的[3]和[4]工作，讨论了Λ-BMS解空间及其线性化方法，通过广义调和规范求解线性化爱因斯坦方程，并利用源多极矩描述度量扰动，分析未来无穷远处的记忆效应，以及使用全息应力张量定义荷流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Λ-BMS解空间中定义Bondi质量损失的模拟并非平凡，因此需要研究线性化解来解决这一问题。

Method: 在广义调和规范下求解线性化爱因斯坦方程；采用绝热近似和远源距离假设，将度量扰动用源多极矩展开；一致截断多极展开；使用全息应力张量定义荷流，并在二阶扰动下用多极矩表示。

Result: 推导了未来无穷远处由于有限时间区间内时变源引起的度量扰动净变化，并将其与记忆效应联系起来；得到了二阶扰动下荷流的多极矩形式。

Conclusion: 在Λ-BMS框架下，通过线性化方法和全息应力张量，可以明确定义Bondi质量损失的模拟，并建立记忆效应与荷流之间的联系。

Abstract: We give a summary of our work [3] and [4] with Geoffrey Compère and Sk.Jahanur Hoque. After quickly reviewing the $Λ$-BMS solution space and why it is not trivial to define an analogue of Bondi mass loss, we discuss how this can be resolved by studying the linearized solutions. Then we describe how to solve the linearized Einstein s equations in the generalized harmonic gauge and the set of assumptions such as adiabaticity and being at a large distance from the source that allows us to write the metric perturbations in terms of the source multipole moments. We point out how to consistently truncate the multipole expansion. We then discuss the net change of the metric perturbation at future infinity due to a source that is time-varying in a finite time interval that will be connected to the memory effect. We explain how to define charge flows using the holographic stress tensor and what form they take in terms of multipole moments at second order in perturbations. We conclude with a some remarks on the results.

</details>


### [136] [Extended spherically symmetric solutions in revised Deser--Woodard nonlocal gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22086)
*Haida Li,Xiangdong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在修正的Deser-Woodard非局部引力框架下，扩展了静态球对称黑洞解。利用场方程的线性特性，证明了围绕Schwarzschild解的一阶展开在时间度规分量和径向度规倒数上呈线性，从而可定义不同修正项的模式叠加。进一步研究了两种渐近平坦扩展：对数修正（缓慢衰减，产生更大范围的非局部效应）和指数修正（局域于视界附近，正振幅时内移视界）。这两种修正分别与量子修正效应和有限范围/屏蔽引力效应相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在修正的D-W非局部引力理论中，寻找更丰富的静态球对称黑洞解，探索超越标准Schwarzschild解的非局部引力效应。

Method: 利用场方程线性特性，对Schwarzschild解进行一阶线性展开，通过模式叠加构造不同修正项；分别研究对数型修正和指数型修正的渐近行为与视界结构。

Result: 对数修正导致偏离Schwarzschild的缓慢衰减，表现为扩展的非局部效应；指数修正局域于视界附近，正振幅使视界向内移动。两种修正均保持渐近平坦性。

Conclusion: 在修正D-W非局部引力中，线性叠加原理允许系统性地构造多种黑洞解，对数与指数修正分别与量子引力效应和屏蔽引力机制有物理关联。

Abstract: In this work, we extend the static spherically symmetric black hole solutions of revised Deser--Woodard (D-W) nonlocal gravity. Due to the linearity of the field equations, we show that the first-order expansion around the Schwarzschild solution is linear in both the temporal metric component and the reciprocal of the radial metric component. Therefore, a mode-by-mode superposition can be properly defined for different types of correction terms. We then further study two additional asymptotically flat extensions, a logarithmically dressed correction and an exponentially suppressed correction. The logarithmically dressed correction gives a slowly decaying deviation from Schwarzschild as the radius becomes large, making it a more extended nonlocal effect, whereas the exponentially suppressed correction is localized near the horizon and shifts the horizon inward for positive correction amplitude. These correction terms are physically motivated by common mechanisms in modified gravity: Logarithmic terms are possibly related to effects of quantum corrections, and exponential terms can arise from finite-range or screened gravitational effects.

</details>


### [137] [Horizon Singularities in the Schwarzschild Geometry of the Teleparallel Equivalent of General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22121)
*D. F. López,A. A. Coley,R. J. van den Hoogen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Teleparallel Equivalent of General Relativity (TEGR)中Schwarzschild黑洞的挠率标量不变量在视界处的发散问题源于Lorentz扇区的选择，并确定了使得视界正则的完整Lorentz扇区函数类，从而支持一致的黑洞解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在TEGR中，Schwarzschild解在视界处存在挠率标量不变量发散，阻碍了其作为黑洞时空的解释。本文旨在澄清这些发散的本质，区分其是几何本征的还是由Lorentz扇区选择引起的伪发散。

Method: 采用三种独立方法：(1) 惯性系条件 (inertial frame condition)；(2) 穿透视界坐标 (horizon-penetrating coordinates)；(3) 视界正则性判据 (horizon regularity criterion)。通过这些方法确定与正则视界相容的完整Lorentz扇区函数类。

Result: (1) Schwarzschild TEGR几何可分为两个不同子类：正则子类中发散消失，视界属于流形；奇异子类中发散是真实的，视界被排除在流形之外。(2) 惯性系本身不能消除惯性效应，视界奇异性与框架的惯性结构无关。(3) 确定了使得所有挠率标量不变量在视界处有限且允许解析延拓的完整Lorentz扇区函数类。

Conclusion: Schwarzschild TEGR解中视界发散问题的根源在于Lorentz扇区的选择，而非几何本身的问题。通过选择适当的Lorentz扇区，可获得正则的视界并支持一致的黑洞解释，这澄清了teleparallel引力中惯性贡献的角色。

Abstract: Certain torsion scalar invariants are known to diverge at the horizon of the Schwarzschild solution in the Teleparallel Equivalent of General Relativity (TEGR), obstructing its interpretation as a black hole spacetime. We show that Schwarzschild TEGR geometries split into two distinct subclasses determined by the Lorentz sector of the geometry -- the elements of the tetrad and spin connection not encoded in the metric but appearing in the torsion. In the regular subclass, the divergences are absent and the horizon belongs to the manifold, supporting a consistent black hole interpretation. In the singular subclass, the divergences are genuine and the horizon is excluded from the manifold. As part of this analysis, we clarify the role of inertial contributions in teleparallel gravity, showing that the proper frame does not, by itself, eliminate inertial effects and that the horizon singularities are independent of the inertial structure of the frame. Using three independent approaches -- the inertial frame condition, horizon-penetrating coordinates, and the horizon regularity criterion -- we determine the complete class of Lorentz sector functions compatible with a regular horizon in Schwarzschild TEGR geometries. For this class, all torsion scalar invariants remain finite at the horizon and analytic extensions across it are admitted.

</details>


### [138] [Covariant virtual work and the d'Alembert-Lagrange formulation of general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22196)
*A. Cuevas,C. Ortiz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了时空几何的协变虚功结构与相应的d'Alembert-Lagrange原理。广义相对论作为该原理的一个特例出现，其Einstein场方程源于允许度规变分上总协变虚功为零，而非作用量极值。约束产生反应项，其中等周约束产生作为理想反应的宇宙学项，由时空平均值固定，宇宙学常数因此成为由可容性决定的全局参数，具有内在非局域几何起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为时空几何建立一种协变的虚功结构和d'Alembert-Lagrange原理，从而以虚功原理而非作用量极值的方式推导Einstein场方程，并统一处理各类约束条件及其产生的反应项。

Method: 发展协变虚功结构，引入d'Alembert-Lagrange原理；将广义相对论嵌入该框架；对约束进行分类（理想反应与非理想贡献）；扩展至单侧可容性条件以得到协变不等式结构；特别分析等周约束产生宇宙学项。

Result: 广义相对论作为该原理的特例实现；Einstein场方程由总协变虚功为零导出；等周约束产生宇宙学常数作为理想反应，由时空平均值固定，具有非局域几何起源。

Conclusion: 协变虚功结构为广义相对论提供了另一种推导路径，将宇宙学常数解释为约束诱导的全局几何参数，而非自由参数或暗能量。

Abstract: We develop a covariant virtual work structure and a corresponding d'Alembert-Lagrange principle for spacetime geometry. Within this framework, General Relativity arises as a particular realization of the principle, leading to a covariant d'Alembert-Lagrange formulation in which the Einstein field equations arise from the vanishing of total covariant virtual work on admissible metric variations, rather than from action extremality. The covariant virtual work structure provides a covariant classification of constraint-induced contributions, distinguishing ideal reactions, which perform no virtual work, from non-ideal sectors contribute explicitly to it. The structure extends naturally to one-sided admissibility conditions, yielding a covariant inequality structure. Constraints generate reaction terms. In particular, an isoperimetric constraint produces a cosmological term as an ideal reaction fixed by spacetime averages, so that the cosmological constant emerges as a global parameter determined by admissibility, reflecting an intrinsically nonlocal geometric origin.

</details>


### [139] [Non-Metricity Corrections Approach to Alleviate $H _0$ Tension: The Logarithmic and Nonlinear $f(Q)$ Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22262)
*Rahul Bhagat,Kesava Chodavarapu,B. Mishra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在对称平行引力（$f(Q)$引力）框架下，探讨了对数模型和非线性饱和模型两种修正引力模型能否缓解哈勃常数张力。通过贝叶斯MCMC分析结合多种观测数据，发现对数模型在统计上与ΛCDM相当，并能推断出介于Planck和SH0ES之间的$H_0$值，且AIC和BIC惩罚更低，是缓解$H_0$张力的更有希望的候选模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期宇宙与晚期宇宙测量的哈勃常数之间存在持续差异（即$H_0$张力），这是现代宇宙学中亟待解决的重要问题之一。本文旨在探索修正引力（具体为对称平行引力框架下的$f(Q)$引力）能否为缓解这一张力提供可行路径。

Method: 考虑两种$f(Q)$引力函数形式：对数模型和非线性饱和模型，它们在不引入宇宙学常数的情况下对标准膨胀历史引入几何修正。通过贝叶斯MCMC分析，利用宇宙计时器、Ia型超新星数据集（Pantheon、Pantheon+SH0ES、DES SN5YR）以及SDSS和DESI的BAO测量数据对模型进行约束。

Result: 两种模型在统计上与ΛCDM具有竞争力。对数模型在所有数据集组合中一致推断出介于Planck和SH0ES基准值之间的$H_0$值，且具有更低的AIC和BIC惩罚，是部分缓解$H_0$张力的更有前景的候选模型。

Conclusion: 在修正引力框架下，对数$f(Q)$引力模型是部分缓解哈勃常数张力的有前途的候选模型。

Abstract: The persistent discrepancy between early-time and late-Universe measurements of the Hubble constant commonly known as the $H_0$ tension remains one of the most pressing open questions in modern cosmology. In this work, we explore whether modifications to the gravitational sector, specifically within the framework of symmetric teleparallel gravity, can offer a viable pathway toward alleviating this tension. We consider two functional forms of $f(Q)$ gravity: a logarithmic model and a nonlinear saturation model, both of which introduce geometric corrections to the standard expansion history without invoking a cosmological constant. Constraining these models through a Bayesian MCMC analysis against a comprehensive suite of observational data, including cosmic chronometers, Type Ia supernova compilations (Pantheon, Pantheon$+$SH0ES, and DES SN5YR), and BAO measurements from SDSS and DESI, we find that both models remain statistically competitive with $Λ$CDM. The logarithmic model, in particular, consistently infers intermediate values of $H_0$ between the \textit{Planck} and SH0ES benchmarks across all dataset combinations, and carries lower AIC and BIC penalties, establishing it as the more promising candidate for partially easing the $H_0$ tension within a modified gravity framework.

</details>


### [140] [Null geodesic defocusing in dynamical black-hole-to-white-hole transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22404)
*Johanna Borissova,Stefano Liberati,Matt Visser*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究动态非奇异黑洞-白洞转变中零测地线的散焦现象。在不假设特定引力场方程的情况下，基于时空运动学证明：捕获区的收缩消失与反捕获区的形成膨胀必然要求零收敛条件被破坏。通过构建广义Painlevé-Gullstrand坐标系下的显式反弹几何，展示了该破坏局域于中间动力学阶段。极限情形下的瞬时转变需要无界破坏零收敛条件，暗示有效连续度规描述的失效，需诉诸全量子引力描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在动态、非奇异的黑洞-白洞转变过程中，零测地线散焦的机制与必然性，尤其是零收敛条件（NCC）在其中扮演的关键角色，同时考察瞬时转变极限下的理论边界。

Method: 采用时空运动学分析，不依赖具体引力场方程，通过零扩张在捕获与反捕获视界上的行为推导一般结论。随后构建广义Painlevé-Gullstrand坐标系下的显式反弹几何模型（基于Bardeen型质量函数），将静态规则黑洞（含de Sitter核）推广为动态转变模型，数值验证NCC破坏的局域化特征。

Result: 证明捕获区的收缩消失与反捕获区的形成膨胀必然要求零收敛条件（NCC）被破坏，且该结论独立于具体的奇点消解机制。基于Bardeen型质量函数的显式模型显示，NCC破坏局域于中间动力学阶段（捕获区蒸发与反捕获区形成期）。极限瞬时转变情形需NCC无界破坏。

Conclusion: 零收敛条件的破坏是动态黑洞-白洞转变的必然特征，而非具体引力理论的偶然后果。瞬时转变极限导致有效度规描述崩溃，必须依赖全量子引力理论。这为量子引力候选理论检验提供了清晰的判据。

Abstract: We investigate the defocusing of null geodesics in dynamical, non-singular black-hole-to-white-hole transitions. Working at the level of spacetime kinematics, and without assuming any specific gravitational field equations, we show that the contraction and disappearance of a trapped region, as well as the subsequent formation and expansion of an anti-trapped region, necessarily require a violation of the null convergence condition. This conclusion follows directly from the behaviour of the null expansions across the trapping and anti-trapping horizons, and is therefore independent of the microscopic mechanism responsible for singularity resolution. We then illustrate this general argument by constructing a class of explicit bouncing geometries in generalised Painlevé-Gullstrand coordinates, obtained by promoting static regular black holes with de Sitter cores to time-dependent black-hole-to-white-hole transition models. For a Bardeen-type mass function, we show that the required violation of the null convergence condition is localised within the intermediate dynamical phase in which the trapped region evaporates and the anti-trapped region forms. Finally, we argue that the limiting case of an instantaneous black-hole-to-white-hole transition would require an unbounded violation of the null convergence condition, signalling a breakdown of the effective continuum metric description, and the need to appeal to a full quantum-gravitational description.

</details>


### [141] [Starobinsky-inflation in asymptotically safe shift-symmetric scalar-tensor theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22408)
*Benjamin Koch,Cristobal Laporte,Frank Saueressig*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量子驱动的暴胀对渐近安全标量-张量理论的约束能力。从Horndeski型理论出发，应用重整化群改进程序生成高阶导数耦合，发现非最小引力-物质耦合的自由参数不仅影响引力-物质扇区，还进入有效高阶导数耦合。通过多场暴胀模型分析，得出来自宇宙学观测的约束边界，结果排除了三个非高斯不动点中的两个，仅保留了一个现象学上可行的重整化群轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究渐近安全标量-张量理论中，标量子暴胀对理论参数的约束能力，特别是非最小引力-物质耦合的自由参数如何受到宇宙学观测的限制，并评估不同非高斯不动点的现象学可行性。

Method: 从Horndeski型理论出发，应用重整化群改进程序生成由微观参数固定的高阶导数耦合；采用多场暴胀模型框架分析暴胀动力学；结合宇宙学观测数据导出对非最小引力-物质耦合的显式约束边界。

Result: 非最小引力-物质耦合的自由参数不仅影响引力-物质扇区，还进入引力扇区的有效高阶导数耦合中；宇宙学观测排除了三个非高斯不动点中的两个，只保留了第三个不动点对应的现象学可行的重整化群轨迹。

Conclusion: 渐近安全标量-张量理论中，标量子暴胀提供了一个强有力的约束工具，能够区分不同的非高斯不动点，从而筛选出现象学上可行的紫外完备化方案。

Abstract: We investigate the constraining power of scalaron-driven inflation on asymptotically safe scalar-tensor theories. Starting from a Horndeski-type theory and applying a renormalization group improvement procedure generates higher-derivative couplings which are fixed in terms of the microscopic parameters - a structure that is expected to occur also within first principle computations based on the asymptotic safety mechanism. The latter are taken to be the free parameters appearing at the Gaussian fixed point. We find that the free parameter initially associated with the non-minimal gravity-matter coupling is not confined to the gravity-matter sector of the theory and also enters the effective higher-derivative couplings in the gravitational sector. We review the setting of multi-field inflationary models which is appropriate to analyze the inflationary dynamics in this context and illustrate their applicability by working out the explicit bounds on the non-minimal gravity-matter coupling resulting from cosmological observations. Given the fixed point structure of asymptotically safe scalar-tensor theories, the results indicate that UV-completions by two of the three available non-Gaussian fixed points can be ruled out while pinpointing phenomenologically viable RG trajectories emanating from the third fixed point.

</details>


### [142] [An Interplay Between Fractional Calculus and Holographic Dark Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22431)
*Ayush Bidlan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统构建了分数阶全息暗能量（FHDE）框架，通过在黑洞热力学中引入分数阶（Riesz）导数，得到分数阶Bekenstein-Hawking熵，并据此定义新的暗能量密度。研究探讨了FHDE在Hubble截断下的宇宙学演化，将其重构到有效场描述中，并扩展到BD、DGP、EPN及Horndeski等修正引力理论，最后在Granda-Oliveros截断下研究了宇宙晚期奇点（大撕裂、小撕裂、伪撕裂）的命运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全息暗能量（HDE）模型存在Hubble截断相关挑战，且缺乏与非局域性或量子引力记忆效应的关联。本文旨在通过分数阶微积分扩展HDE，建立非局域性量子引力与晚期宇宙加速膨胀之间的理论桥梁。

Method: 在量子几何动力学中将Riesz分数阶空间导数引入Schwarzschild黑洞的Hamilton约束，得到分数阶Wheeler-DeWitt方程；求解获得受Lévy指数α（1<α≤2）修正的分数阶Bekenstein-Hawking熵；利用该熵结合Cohen不等式构造新的暗能量密度（FHDE）；在Hubble截断下研究其宇宙学演化，并扩展到BD、DGP、EPN、Horndeski等引力理论；在Granda-Oliveros截断下分析晚期奇点。

Result: 分数阶修正的熵及暗能量密度依赖于Lévy指数α；FHDE在Hubble截断下表现出不同于标准HDE的演化行为；在多种修正引力理论中获得了状态方程参数和减速参数关于红移z和α的解析表达式；在Granda-Oliveros截断下描述了三种晚期奇点（大撕裂、小撕裂、伪撕裂）的发生条件。

Conclusion: FHDE作为HDE的理论扩展，成功将量子引力中的非局域性与晚期宇宙动力学联系起来，为超越ΛCDM模型理解宇宙加速提供了新的理论途径。

Abstract: This dissertation aims to put forth a systematic construction of a fractional-calculus extension of holographic dark energy (HDE). We show that linking late-time cosmic acceleration to non-local or memory effects encoded in a fractional (Riesz) derivative within black hole thermodynamics produces deviations from standard HDE and can address some challenges of the Hubble cutoff. In particular, a Riesz fractional spatial derivative is introduced into the Hamiltonian constraint of a Schwarzschild black hole in quantum geometrodynamics, leading to a Fractional Wheeler--DeWitt equation whose solutions yield fractionally corrected thermodynamic quantities, notably fractional Bekenstein--Hawking entropy governed by the Lévy index \(α\), with \(1<α\leq2\). Using this entropy with Cohen's inequality, a new dark energy density is constructed, defining the Fractional Holographic Dark Energy (FHDE) framework.
  The cosmological implications of FHDE are then investigated. Within the Hubble cutoff, its late-time evolution is analysed through cosmological observables, and the model is reconstructed using effective field descriptions with spin-\(0\) and spin-\(1\) candidates, allowing kinetic and potential terms to be extracted as functions of redshift \(z\) and \(α\). The framework is then extended to BD, DGP, EPN, and Horndeski theories to derive the equation-of-state and deceleration parameters in terms of \(z\) and \(α\). In addition, the fate of the Universe is studied through late-time singularities, namely the big, little, and pseudo-rip, within the Granda--Oliveros FHDE setting. In short, this dissertation proposes FHDE as a theoretically motivated extension of HDE, bridging non-locality in quantum gravity with the late-time dynamics of the Universe, and offering a route toward understanding cosmic acceleration beyond \(Λ\)CDM.

</details>


### [143] [Steering a warp drive without exotic matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22531)
*An T. Le*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了任何渐进平坦的翘曲驱动必须通过辐射才能改变速度，并构建了一个满足正能量的加速翘曲驱动模型——利用Kinnersley光子火箭的外场与内部平坦空腔的匹配，实现了无需奇异物质的转向机制，转向代价由Bondi质量损失承担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决翘曲驱动无法自然改变速度的根本性问题：现有的正能量翘曲驱动都是静态或匀速的，而加速驱动则需要奇异物质。Bobrick和Martire指出翘曲驱动"没有任何自然方式改变其速度"。

Method: 通过守恒律证明转向必须辐射；采用精确Kinnersley光子火箭作为外场，与内部平坦乘客时空通过类时壳层匹配；壳层使用微扰和数值方法验证；能量条件简化为n²≥0；转向律为−ṁ≥3m|a|。

Result: 壳层满足表面主导能量条件（2m/R<24/25时）；存在可容许的加速壳层；Buchdahl型边界a_max R≤g(2m/R)约束加速度；最优机动为Damour偶极子；壳层边缘稳定，严格稳定无需额外能量条件代价；驱动因果、亚光速、正能量。

Conclusion: 翘曲驱动的转向问题本质上是能量预算问题，而非奇异物质问题；所构造的驱动满足正能量条件，转向通过辐射（光子火箭反冲）实现，代价是Bondi质量损失。

Abstract: A useful warp drive must change its velocity, yet known positive-energy constructions are static or constant-velocity, while accelerating ones require exotic matter; Bobrick and Martire noted that warp drives ``do not have any natural way of changing their velocities.'' First, a conservation law settles the principle: for any asymptotically flat drive with a confined dominant-energy source and standard peeling, the Bondi--Sachs four-momentum changes only through radiation to null infinity, so the drive cannot steer without radiating. Second, we construct a positive-energy spacetime that saturates this bound. Prescribing the passenger worldtube, we take the exact Kinnersley photon rocket as exterior and solve for the matching timelike shell; the exterior and steering law are exact, while the shell is certified perturbatively and numerically. The mechanism is photon-rocket recoil on a tidally protected, exactly flat cavity; the warp drive is the decoupled flat interior, not a warp field. The exterior energy conditions reduce to $n^2\ge0$, and steering obeys $-\dot m\ge 3m|a|$, paid for by Bondi mass loss. The shell satisfies the surface dominant energy condition for $2m/R<24/25$; admissible accelerating shells exist near the Schwarzschild--Minkowski anchor and, for slow burns, as time-evolved spacetimes. A Buchdahl-type frontier $a_{\max}R\le g(2m/R)$ caps the acceleration between a numerical lower bound $\sim\!0.2$ and a closed-form ceiling ${1\over2}(1-2m/R)$. On the gravitational-wave--silent class, the optimal maneuver is the Damour dipole. The wall is marginally stable, with strict stability available at no energy-condition cost; fully dynamical flux-coupled stability remains open. The drive is causal, subluminal, and energetically costly but positive-energy: steering is a problem of energy budget, not exotic matter.

</details>


### [144] [Extended parameterized spin expansion formalism for ringdown analysis with GW250114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22580)
*Jia-Ning Chen,Liang-Bi Wu,Zong-Kuan Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文扩展了ParSpec框架，通过同时采样特征长度尺度ℓ和标度指数p（在后续分析中分别替换为$\tilde{p}$和$\tilde{\ell}$），对Kerr环down谱进行理论无关的描述。将框架应用于GW250114，发现$\tilde{p}$的后验主要由先验主导，$\tilde{\ell}$的约束主要受参数空间几何影响，γ<1时得到$\tilde{\ell}_{90}\simeq 83$ km的弱上限。数据驱动信息增益很小，220-only模型比220+221模型提供更有效的约束，联合GW231123分析结果由GW250114主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有环down测试通常将标度指数p固定为理论动机的整数值，缺乏对修正结构标度行为的理论无关描述。本文旨在扩展ParSpec框架，同时采样ℓ和p，提供一种受有效场论启发的、对Kerr环down谱的理论无关描述，以更全面地检验黑洞环down信号中的修正结构。

Method: 扩展ParSpec框架，将特征长度尺度ℓ和标度指数p替换为$\tilde{\ell}$和$\tilde{p}$进行联合采样；利用pyRing对GW250114进行贝叶斯分析，采用$M_{\rm f}$和$D_{\rm L}$的信息性先验；对比220-only和220+221两种模式模型，在不同γ条件下分析；使用Kullback-Leibler散度量化数据驱动信息增益；并纳入GW231123进行联合分析。

Result: $\tilde{p}$的后验主要由先验主导，当前环down数据无法区分修正的不同标度行为；$\tilde{\ell}$的约束主要受γ条件导致的允许参数空间几何控制；γ<1是最自然的设定，给出$\tilde{\ell}_{90}\simeq 83$ km的弱上限；KL散度显示扣除有效先验后数据对$\tilde{\ell}$和$\logγ$的信息增益极小；220-only模型比220+221模型提供更信息性的约束；联合分析由GW250114主导。

Conclusion: 当前信噪比水平下，环down数据无法对修正的标度指数p提供有效约束，对特征尺度ℓ的约束也很弱，主要受参数空间几何而非数据驱动。建议使用γ<1作为自然先验设定，220-only模型优于220+221模型。未来更高信噪比的观测数据才能对ParSpec框架中的参数提供有意义的约束。

Abstract: We extend the Parameterized Spin Expansion Coefficients (ParSpec) framework for ringdown tests of black holes by simultaneously sampling the characteristic length scale $\ell$ and the scaling index $p$, where the two parameters are replaced by $\tilde{p}$ and $\tilde{\ell}$ in subsequent analysis, respectively. Unlike previous analyses that fix $\tilde{p}$ to theory-motivated integer values, this extension provides a theory-agnostic description inspired by effective-field theory to the Kerr ringdown spectrum. Using \texttt{pyRing}, we apply the framework to GW250114 with informative priors on $M_{\rm f}$ and $D_{\rm L}$, comparing the $220$-only and $220+221$ mode models under different conditions on $γ$. The posterior distributions of $\tilde{\ell}, \tilde{p}$ and $γ$ are presented with different ringdown start times. We show that the posterior of $\tilde{p}$ remains largely prior dominated, indicating that current ringdown data cannot distinguish different scaling behaviors of the correction. The inferred constraint on $\tilde{\ell}$ is instead mainly controlled by the geometry of the allowed parameter space induced by the $γ$ condition. In particular, $γ<1$ provides the most natural prescription in the $(\tilde{\ell},\tilde{p})$ plane and yields stable but weak upper bounds of $\tilde{\ell}_{90}\simeq 83\, {\rm km}$. An analysis based on the Kullback-Leibler divergence further shows that, after accounting for the effective prior associated with $γ<1$, the data-driven information gain on $\tilde{\ell}$ and $\logγ$ is very small. At the current SNR level, the $220$-only model provides a more informative constraint than the $220+221$ model. We also perform a joint analysis including GW231123 and find that the combined constraint is dominated by GW250114.

</details>


### [145] [Bianchi IX dynamics with a phantom field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22675)
*Adel Awad,Dmitry Chirkov,Alexey Golovnev,Alexey Toporensky*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了充满无质量幻影场的Bianchi IX宇宙动力学，分析了这种奇异物质源导致的双负Kasner指数和大绝对值负指数现象，并解释了数值方法发现的体积振荡本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无质量幻影场作为奇异物质源对Bianchi IX宇宙动力学的影响，特别是其引发的真空或标准标量场条件下不可能出现的宇宙演化模式。

Method: 基于Bianchi IX度规框架，引入无质量幻影场作为物质源，通过分析BKL振荡中的Kasner指数行为，结合数值模拟方法研究动力学特征。

Result: 发现了幻影场存在下两个Kasner指数可同时为负且负指数绝对值可很大的新型振荡模式，揭示了体积振荡现象的物理机制。

Conclusion: 幻影场从根本上改变了Bianchi IX宇宙的BKL振荡行为，产生了真空或标准标量场中不可能实现的动力学特征，并解释了体积振荡的起源。

Abstract: We consider Bianchi IX dynamics of a Universe filled with a massless phantom field. Such an exotic matter source enables regimes impossible in vacuum or with a standard scalar field. In particular, two Kasner indices of BKL oscillations can be simultaneously negative, and the absolute value of a negative index can be large. We describe the consequences of these features and explain the nature of volume oscillations recently discovered in such a system by numerical methods.

</details>


### [146] [Orientation matters: Consequences for gravitational-wave background detectability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22985)
*Nelson Christensen,Joseph D. Romano,Mairi Sakellariadou,Jishnu Suresh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了地球L形激光干涉仪对中探测器的几何配置（物理距离和相对方向）对引力波背景探测能力的影响，发现相对方向会显著影响各向同性和各向异性引力波背景的可探测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解探测器几何配置如何影响引力波背景的交叉相关搜索，特别是相对方向对探测能力的影响机制。

Method: 对简单例子应用标准交叉相关搜索技术，分析地球L形激光干涉仪网络中探测器对的几何配置影响。

Result: 相对方向会显著影响各向同性和各向异性引力波背景的可探测性。

Conclusion: 探测器对的相对方向是影响引力波背景探测能力的关键因素，在设计探测网络时需要重点考虑。

Abstract: Cross-correlation searches for gravitational-wave backgrounds depend on the geometrical configuration (physical separation and relative orientation) of the detectors comprising the network. Applying standard techniques to a few simple examples, we illustrate how the relative orientation of a pair of Earth-based L-shaped laser interferometers can drastically impact the detectability of both isotropic and anisotropic gravitational-wave backgrounds.

</details>


### [147] [Periodic Timelike Motion and Gravitational Wave Signatures around a Magnetically Charged Black Hole Surrounded by Quintessence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23021)
*R. H. Ali*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性电动力学产生的静态磁荷黑洞在精质背景下的类时测地线运动及引力波信号。通过分析有效势、边界稳定轨道和最内稳定圆轨道，利用zoom-whirl分类法对束缚轨迹进行分类，发现精质参数c_q系统性改变轨道半径、守恒量和转向点结构。随后在数值kludge近似下模拟极端质量比旋进的引力辐射，波形呈现zoom-whirl运动的爆裂特征，精质耦合参数变化调制相位演化、爆裂时序和频谱含量，特征应变集中在LISA等空间探测器敏感的毫赫兹频段，表明精质背景可在周期轨道动力学及引力波可观测信号中留下系统性印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非线性电动力学产生的磁荷黑洞在精质背景下的轨道动力学及引力波特征，探索精质暗能量对极端质量比旋进系统的可观测影响。

Method: 分析有效势确定边界稳定轨道(MSCO)和最内稳定圆轨道(ISCO)；采用zoom-whirl (z,w,v)分类法对束缚周期轨道进行分类；利用数值kludge近似模拟极端质量比旋进的引力波形。

Result: 精质参数c_q系统性改变轨道半径、守恒量和转向点结构；波形呈现zoom-whirl运动特征爆裂结构；精质耦合参数调制相位演化、爆裂时序和频谱谐波成分；傅里叶谱呈离散梳状结构，特征应变集中在毫赫兹频段。

Conclusion: 精质背景可在周期轨道动力学及引力波时域和频域可观测信号中留下系统性印记，对LISA等空间探测器具有潜在的可观测性。

Abstract: We investigate timelike geodesics and gravitational wave signatures of periodic motion around a static magnetically charged black hole arising from nonlinear electrodynamics and immersed in a quintessence background. We analyze the effective potential for massive particles, determine the marginally bound and innermost stable circular orbits, and classify the resulting bound trajectories using the zoom-whirl taxonomy $(\mathit{z},\mathit{w},\mathit{v})$. We show that the quintessence parameter $c_q$ systematically shifts the orbital radii, conserved quantities, and turning-point structure associated with representative periodic families.
  We then model the gravitational radiation emitted by periodic extreme-mass-ratio inspirals within the numerical kludge approximation. The resulting waveforms exhibit the characteristic burst-like structure of zoom-whirl motion, while variations in the quintessence coupling parameter modify the phase evolution, burst timing, and harmonic content of the signal. The corresponding Fourier spectra display a discrete comb-like structure, and the characteristic strain is concentrated in the millihertz band relevant for space-based detectors such as LISA. These results indicate that a quintessence background can leave systematic imprints on periodic orbit dynamics and on the associated time and frequency-domain gravitational wave observables.

</details>


### [148] [MaxWave Signal: Rapid, coherent maximum likelihood wavelet reconstruction of transient signals in gravitational wave data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23035)
*Sudhi Mathur,Neil J. Cornish*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文扩展了MaxWave算法，实现多探测器相干信号重建与瞬态噪声剔除，通过构建合成探测器增强弱信号灵敏度并抑制巧合性噪声干扰，提供实时、低延迟、模型无关的引力波信号重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波探测器灵敏度的提高导致瞬态信号探测率增加，需要更快速的自动化分析方法来应对日益增长的数据流。

Method: 将MaxWave最大似然小波重建算法扩展为多探测器相干重建；利用z统计量进行时间/相位偏移和振幅缩放对齐；采用几何平均噪声谱的自适应噪声加权；构建合成探测器放大非高斯特征、抑制噪声探测器并保持高斯噪声统计量。

Result: 算法能够实时、低延迟、模型无关地重建信号；安全地去噪而不移除瞬态信号；对弱事件灵敏度有所提高；能拒斥缺乏一致相位和振幅演化的巧合性噪声；可补充现有爆发搜索和重建框架。

Conclusion: 该算法通过根本上不同的方法增强了探测置信度，提高了对不同信号形态的灵敏度，可作为现有引力波爆发搜索框架的重要补充。

Abstract: Advances in gravitational-wave detector sensitivity have increased the rate of transient signal detections, demanding faster automated analysis. We extend MaxWave, a fast maximum likelihood wavelet reconstruction algorithm, to perform coherent multi-detector signal reconstruction and glitch rejection. We coherently search for a common set of wavelets modeling the signal in all detectors. Multi-detector data are aligned using z-statistic time and phase offsets and amplitude scalings relative to the dominant reconstruction, as well as adaptive noise weightings derived from a geometrically averaged noise spectrum. By aligning and weighting individual detectors, we form a synthetic detector that amplifies non-Gaussian features, down-weights noisy detectors, and preserves Gaussian noise statistics. We extract the coherent signal using this synthetic detector, improving sensitivity to weak events while rejecting coincident glitches that lack consistent phase and amplitude evolution. Our algorithm provides real-time, low-latency, model-independent signal reconstructions, safely denoises gravitational wave data without removing transient signals, and can complement existing burst search and reconstruction frameworks through a fundamentally distinct approach, strengthening detection confidence and improving sensitivity to diverse signal morphologies.

</details>


### [149] [Polarization States and Effective Stress Energy Tensor of Gravitational Waves in Metric $f(R)$ Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23056)
*Sakshi Srivastava,Utkal Keshari Dash,Murli Manohar Verma*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在线性化近似下研究了度规f(R)引力中引力波的极化特性与有效应力-能量张量，揭示了标量场激发的呼吸模和纵向模极化态，并利用Isaacson高频平均形式推导了包含张量和标量贡献的有效应力-能量张量，发现大质量标量模的亚光速群速度导致能量通量的频率依赖性抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究度规f(R)引力中引力波超出广义相对论两种张量模的额外极化态及其能量输运特性，建立极化与能量输运的统一联系，为修正引力理论提供可观测检验信号。

Method: 采用度规f(R)引力在闵可夫斯基时空背景下的线性化近似，利用线性化Riemann张量的电分量导出极化振幅表达式，并应用Isaacson高频平均形式推导有效应力-能量张量。

Result: 无质量标量场激发横向呼吸模，有质量标量场通过单个传播标量激发同时产生呼吸模和纵向模响应；张量和标量扰动均贡献总引力波能量密度，大质量标量模的亚光速群速度导致其能量通量出现频率依赖性抑制。

Conclusion: 建立了度规f(R)引力中引力波极化与能量输运的统一框架，为利用当前和未来引力波探测器检验修正引力理论提供了潜在的观测特征。

Abstract: We investigate the polarization properties and effective stress--energy tensor of gravitational waves in metric $f(R)$ gravity within the linearized approximation around Minkowski spacetime. Owing to the additional scalar degree of freedom inherent in the theory, gravitational waves exhibit polarization states beyond the two tensor modes predicted by general relativity. Using the electric components of the linearized Riemann tensor, we derive explicit expressions for the polarization amplitudes and show that a massless scalar field excites a transverse breathing mode, whereas a massive scalar field generates both breathing and longitudinal responses through a single propagating scalar excitation. Employing the Isaacson high-frequency averaging formalism, we further derive the effective stress--energy tensor and demonstrate that both tensor and scalar perturbations contribute to the total gravitational-wave energy density. The energy transport associated with the massive scalar mode is reduced by its subluminal group velocity, leading to a frequency-dependent suppression of the scalar energy flux. These results establish a unified connection between gravitational-wave polarization and energy transport in metric $f(R)$ gravity and provide potential observational signatures for testing modified gravity with current and future gravitational-wave detectors.

</details>


### [150] [Dark matter from the quadratic spinor Lagrangian II: A spin-3/2 no-go and the uniqueness of the spin-1/2 candidate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23273)
*Roh-Suan Tung*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明复合二次旋量拉格朗日量(QSL)中，将旋量1-形式提升为独立狄拉克矢量场并不能产生自旋3/2暗物质候选者。通过三项结果组成的no-go定理，最终确定复合自旋1/2狄拉克费米子是QSL中唯一的传播物质激发和暗物质候选者，其通过超SL(2,C)结构可解读为被度量凝聚体破缺的局部超对称性的Goldstino。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究将复合二次旋量拉格朗日量(QSL)中的旋量1-形式$Ψ_μ$提升为独立的狄拉克矢量场是否能产生真正的自旋3/2暗物质候选者。

Method: 通过克利福德约化精确计算挠率项；分析二阶形式$2 DΨγ_5 DΨ$的动力学性质；研究曲率恒等式另一侧$S=-\int\barψψR\sqrt{-g}$的二阶变分分解。

Result: 三项结论构成no-go定理：(1) 挠率项是限定在时间分量扇区上的框架对齐质量，而非均匀的自旋3/2质量；(2) 二阶形式的动能项和交叉项恒为零，仅为边界项；(3) 真正的动力学存在于曲率侧，其二阶变分分解通过线性化度规和标量传播，所有极点均在度规光锥$k^2=0$上——只有引力子和一个标量，不存在有质量自旋3/2模式。

Conclusion: 复合自旋1/2狄拉克费米子是QSL中唯一的传播物质激发和唯一暗物质候选者，通过超SL(2,C)结构可解读为Pagels-Primack轻引力子暗物质的复合引力后裔——即被度量凝聚体破缺的局部超对称性的Goldstino。

Abstract: The composite Quadratic Spinor Lagrangian (QSL) propagates a spin-1/2 Dirac fermion whose mass is generated geometrically by cosmological trace torsion. It is natural to ask whether promoting the spinor 1-form $Ψ_μ$ to an \emph{independent} Dirac-vector field yields a genuine spin-3/2 dark-matter candidate. We prove that it does not. Three results combine into a no-go theorem. First, the torsional term, computed exactly by Clifford reduction, is a frame-aligned mass confined to the time-component sector -- not a uniform spin-3/2 mass. Second, the second-order form $2 DΨγ_5 DΨ$ has identically vanishing kinetic and cross terms for the independent field: as a component expression it is the boundary part of the spinor-curvature identity and carries no bulk dynamics. Third, the genuine dynamics therefore reside in the curvature side of that identity, $S=-\int\barψψR\sqrt{-g}$, in which the metric $g=Ψ\otimes_SΨ$ and the scalar $\barψψ$ are \emph{both} composites of $Ψ$; the second variation consequently factors, $δ^2S=\mathcal Q(h_{μν}[δΨ],δΦ[δΨ])$, through the linearized metric and a scalar, both massless. Every propagating pole therefore lies on the metric light cone $k^2=0$ -- the graviton and a scalar -- and no massive spin-3/2 mode exists, on any background. This is the dynamical completion of the kinematic fact that the composite spinor 1-form has no spin-3/2 part, and it establishes the composite spin-1/2 Dirac fermion as the unique propagating matter excitation, and the unique dark-matter candidate, of the QSL. Through the super-SL(2,C) structure this surviving mode is naturally read as the Goldstino of the local supersymmetry broken by the metric condensate -- a composite, gravitational descendant of the light-gravitino dark matter of Pagels and Primack.

</details>


### [151] [Primordial Black Holes from Vector-Induced Curvature Perturbations Sourced by Primordial Magnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23307)
*Chang Han,Zu-Cheng Chen,Hongwei Yu,Puxun Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的暴胀后机制，在刚硬（kinaton）时期通过原初磁场的电磁应力张量的矢量分量激发 metric 矢量扰动，进而作为二阶标量扰动的非线性源产生增强的原初曲率扰动，从而有效生成原初黑洞。该机制在 kinaton 带红外截止端产生 $\mathcal P_{\mathcal R}(k)\propto k^{-5}$ 标度律，基于 Ratra 型磁发生场景可实现足够的 PBH 丰度构成暗物质的主要部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初黑洞的形成需要小尺度原初曲率扰动显著增强，现有机制多依赖暴胀阶段特殊动力学过程，本文旨在提出一种暴胀后的替代机制。

Method: 利用暴胀后的刚硬（kinaton）时期，原初磁场的电磁应力张量矢量分量源产生 metric 矢量扰动；由于这些一阶矢量模式在 kinaton 时期近似恒定，它们作为持续的非线性源驱动二阶标量扰动，从而产生曲率扰动增强。

Result: 得到的矢量诱导曲率扰动在 kinaton 带红外截止端被放大，呈现 $\mathcal P_{\mathcal R}(k)\propto k^{-5}$ 标度律。基于 Ratra 型磁发生场景的参数计算表明，诱导的曲率扰动可产生足够丰度的原初黑洞，构成暗物质的主要部分。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需暴胀阶段特殊动力学的暴胀后原初黑洞形成新机制，通过 kinaton 时期的原初磁场矢量扰动诱导出足够强的曲率扰动，为原初黑洞作为暗物质候选者提供新的理论途径。

Abstract: Generating an appreciable abundance of primordial black holes (PBHs) requires a substantial enhancement of primordial curvature perturbations on small scales. In this work, we propose a new post-inflationary mechanism in which such an enhancement arises during a stiff, or kination, epoch. The mechanism is driven by metric vector perturbations sourced by the vector component of the electromagnetic stress-energy tensor associated with primordial magnetic fields (PMFs). Since these first-order vector modes remain approximately constant during kination, they act as persistent nonlinear sources for second-order scalar perturbations. We show that the resulting vector-induced curvature perturbations are amplified toward the infrared cutoff of the kination band and exhibit the characteristic scaling $\mathcal P_{\mathcal R}(k)\propto k^{-5}$. As a concrete realization, we consider PMFs generated in a Ratra-type magnetogenesis scenario and find that the induced curvature perturbations can produce PBHs with an abundance large enough to constitute a substantial fraction of the dark matter.

</details>


### [152] [Scattered wave functions and worldline instantons for particle production in curved spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23389)
*Philip Semrén,Greger Torgrimsson*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种互补方法（散射波函数法和开世界线瞬子法）来研究弯曲时空中自旋1/2粒子-反粒子对的产生，两种方法在二维度规上结果一致。SWF方法提供数值精确结果，而瞬子方法在高维背景和极端参数区域更具扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以处理具有多坐标非平凡依赖性的弯曲时空中的粒子对产生问题，需要开发新的理论工具来研究这一领域。

Method: 1) 将原本用于电磁背景的散射波函数(SWF)方法扩展到弯曲时空背景；2) 通过推导对产生概率的指数前因子，完善了开世界线瞬子方法。

Result: 两种方法在多个二维度规上得到了一致的对产生概率结果。SWF方法在例子中提供了数值精确的高效计算，瞬子方法则展示了向高维背景和更极端参数区域扩展的优势。

Conclusion: 这两种方法为研究多维引力背景下的对产生提供了超越现有方法的新工具，其中SWF适用于精确数值计算，瞬子法适用于高维和极端参数情形。

Abstract: We study the production of spin-$1/2$ particle-antiparticle pairs in curved spacetimes with nontrivial dependence on more than one coordinate. To this end, we develop two complementary approaches. First, we extend the scattered-wave-function (SWF) method, originally introduced for pair production in electromagnetic backgrounds, to curved spacetime backgrounds. Second, we complete the development of an open-worldline-instanton method by deriving the pre-exponential factor of the pair-production probability. We apply both methods to several two-dimensional metrics and find good agreement between the resulting probabilities. While the SWF approach provides numerically exact results and is particularly efficient for the examples considered here, the instanton approach offers favorable scaling to higher-dimensional backgrounds and more extreme parameter regimes. These methods provide new tools for studying pair production in multidimensional gravitational backgrounds beyond the reach of many existing approaches.

</details>


### [153] [High-Stability Deformable Mirrors for Correcting Non-Axisymmetric Residual Aberrations in Thermal Compensation of Future Gravitational Wave Interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23415)
*Luciano Antonio Corubolo,Matteo Lorenzini,Lorenzo Aiello,Elisabetta Cesarini,Maria Cifaldi,Matteo Ianni,Diana Lumaca,Yury Minenkov,Ilaria Nardecchia,Alessio Rocchi,Claudia Taranto,Viviana Fafone*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出利用可变形反射镜对CO2激光束进行相位整形，以校正引力波探测器中非轴对称波前畸变，并通过改进的Gerchberg-Saxton算法计算目标相位图，仿真与实验验证了该策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波探测器中的光学像差主要由涂层吸收激光和光学元件制造缺陷引起，导致干涉仪偏离最佳工作点，降低稳定性和灵敏度。现有校正方法（热致动器、CO2激光投影仪）仅能处理轴对称畸变，非轴对称波前畸变尚未得到缓解，亟需一种灵活的解决方案。

Method: 采用可变形反射镜作为解决方案，通过反射时对CO2激光束相位进行整形，在透镜光学元件上 imprint 所需的不对称强度图案，且不引入频率相关噪声。目标相位图通过改进的Gerchberg-Saxton算法计算得到。

Result: 通过仿真和实验验证，证明该投影策略能够一致地再现所需的强度图案，表明可变形反射镜可有效校正非轴对称波前畸变。

Conclusion: 可变形反射镜为未来高功率引力波探测器（如爱因斯坦望远镜高频探测器）中非轴对称波前畸变的校正提供了一种灵活、无频率相关噪声的有效方案。

Abstract: In gravitational wave detectors, optical aberrations arise mainly from laser absorption in coatings and production process defects in the optics along the laser path. If left uncorrected, these optical path distortions drive the interferometer away from its optimal working point, degrading both stability and sensitivity. Future instruments such as the Einstein Telescope high-frequency detector will operate with unprecedented circulating power, further amplifying the aberration budget. In the current detectors Advanced Virgo and Advanced LIGO, the axisymmetric distortions are corrected using thermal actuators and CO2 laser projectors, however, non-axisymmetric wavefront distortions remain unmitigated. Deformable mirrors are investigated as a flexible solution for mitigating such defects: by shaping the CO2 beam phase upon reflection, they can imprint the required asymmetric intensity pattern on the lensing optics without introducing frequency dependent noise. The target phase map is computed via a modified Gerchberg-Saxton algorithm. We present simulations of this projection strategy and experimental validation demonstrating consistent reproduction of the desired intensity patterns.

</details>


### [154] [Spacetime torsion fixes the mass and spin of gravitationally produced dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23418)
*Roh-Suan Tung*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出在爱因斯坦-嘉当理论框架下，时空挠率可为随机引力波产生的暗物质费米子提供质量，无需希格斯机制或自由质量参数。该质量源于宇宙学旋量凝聚体产生的矢量迹挠率，通过克利福德约化得到纯狄拉克质量，且该自旋1/2狄拉克费米子是唯一的暗物质候选者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型中随机引力波产生的暗物质费米子需要由未指定的晚期物理获得质量，这一缺失促使作者寻找更自然的机制。作者旨在证明时空挠率本身即可提供质量，从而消除对希格斯扇区或自由质量参数的依赖。

Method: 采用二次旋量拉格朗日形式下的广义相对论，将其扩展至爱因斯坦-嘉当理论。通过引入宇宙学旋量凝聚体生成矢量迹挠率K∝χ̇/χ，并进行显式克利福德约化，推导出产生的自旋1/2费米子获得纯狄拉克质量，不含赝标量或交叉项。

Result: 有效狄拉克质量为M_eff=(1/√6)|χ̇/χ|，与产生时的哈勃率锁定：M_eff≃(c_χ/√6)H_*。剩余丰度呈现单参数预言Ωh^2∝H_*^{5/2}，且该框架不允许传播的自旋3/2模式，因此自旋1/2狄拉克费米子是唯一的暗物质候选者。

Conclusion: 时空挠率可为随机引力波产生的暗物质提供质量，无需额外物理机制，且预言了暗物质的自旋和丰度标度关系，为暗物质起源提供了简洁且可检验的理论框架。

Abstract: The gravitational production of dark matter from stochastic gravitational waves requires the produced fermion to acquire a mass by unspecified late-time physics. We show that this mass is supplied by spacetime torsion alone -- no Higgs sector and no free mass parameter. In the Quadratic Spinor Lagrangian formulation of general relativity, extended to Einstein--Cartan, a cosmological spinor condensate generates a vectorial trace torsion $K\propto\dotχ/χ$; an explicit Clifford reduction confers on the produced spin-1/2 fermion a pure Dirac mass $M_{\rm eff}=(1/\sqrt6)\,|\dotχ/χ|$, with no pseudoscalar or cross terms, locked to the Hubble rate at production, $M_{\rm eff}\simeq(c_χ/\sqrt6)H_*$. The relic abundance is then a one-parameter prediction, $Ωh^2\propto H_*^{5/2}$, and the spin is fixed: the same framework admits no propagating spin-3/2 mode, so the composite spin-1/2 Dirac fermion is its unique dark-matter candidate.

</details>


### [155] [Closed and broken electromagnetic orbits in Kerr--Newman spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23446)
*Erasmo Caponio,Giulio Sanzeni,Stefan Suhr*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了次极端Kerr-Newman时空中的类时洛伦兹力方程解，重点关注时间机器区域𝔗。构建了光滑的闭合电磁轨道、球面电磁轨道和飞越轨道，并引入了断裂电磁轨道概念描述带电粒子衰变过程，展示了能量提取和因果性破坏过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Kerr-Newman时空时间机器区域中带电粒子的动力学行为，特别是探索因果关系破坏的可能性以及能量提取机制。

Method: 在次极端Kerr-Newman时空中，通过求解洛伦兹力方程，构建不同类型的电磁轨道（闭合轨道、球面轨道、飞越轨道），并引入断裂电磁轨道概念描述衰变过程，通过连接不同荷质比的轨道片段实现能量提取和因果性破坏。

Result: 成功构建了时间机器区域正赤道面上的光滑闭合电磁轨道；证明了赤道时间机器区域中存在球面电磁轨道并导出了半径与荷质比、能量、角动量的显式关系；给出了飞越轨道存在的充分条件；通过断裂电磁轨道展示了能量提取过程和因果性破坏过程。

Conclusion: 次极端Kerr-Newman时空的时间机器区域中存在丰富的电磁轨道结构，带电粒子的衰变过程通过断裂电磁轨道可实现能量提取和因果性违反。

Abstract: We study future-pointing timelike solutions of the Lorentz force equation in the sub-extremal Kerr--Newman spacetime, with special attention to the time-machine region $\mathfrak T$, where the axial Killing field $\partial_φ$ is timelike. We first construct smooth closed electromagnetic orbits tangent to $\partial_φ$ in the positive equatorial part of $\mathfrak T$: the radius of such a circle determines, and is determined by, the charge-to-mass ratio of the particle which must have opposite sign to that of the black hole charge. We then prove the existence of spherical electromagnetic orbits contained in the equatorial time-machine region and derive explicit relations between their radius, charge-to-mass ratio, energy and angular momentum. Next we give sufficient conditions ensuring that an equatorial electromagnetic orbit is a flyby orbit with radial turning point in $\mathfrak T$. Finally, to describe charged-particle decay processes whose fragments have different charge-to-mass ratios, we introduce the notion of a broken electromagnetic orbit: a continuous, piecewise smooth, future-pointing worldline whose smooth pieces solve the Lorentz force equation. Imposing conservation of kinetic four-momentum and electric charge at the decay vertices, we exhibit an energy extraction process followed by a causality-violating one. The latter is realized by a closed broken electromagnetic orbit, which we construct in both the $r$-positive and the $r$-negative regions inside the inner horizon, by concatenating two flyby branches sharing a common radial turning value $\bar r$, and having charge-to-mass ratios with opposite sign to that of the black hole charge, with a spherical electromagnetic orbit of radius $\bar r$.

</details>


### [156] [Dark matter from the quadratic spinor Lagrangian I: Geometric mass for a gravitationally produced spin-1/2 fermion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23447)
*Roh-Suan Tung*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出二次旋量拉格朗日量（QSL）形式，将引力波诱导的冷暗物质产生机制与爱因斯坦-嘉当理论中的几何质量起源统一起来。QSL中单狄拉克场构造的旋量1-形式纯为自旋1/2，宇宙学旋量凝聚体产生的矢量挠率为费米子提供纯狄拉克质量，该质量锁定于产生时的哈勃率，从而将暗物质丰度简化为单一尺度H*的函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Maleknejad和Kopp (2026)的引力波诱导freeze-in机制产生了暗费米子，但这些费米子需要另外的机制来获得质量。本文旨在发展一种几何框架，为这些暗费米子提供质量而不需要引入自由参数。

Method: 发展了广义相对论的二次旋量拉格朗日量（QSL）形式，并扩展至爱因斯坦-嘉当理论。通过克莱福德约化分析，证明宇宙学旋量凝聚体产生的矢量挠率给出纯狄拉克质量，并推导出有效质量与哈勃率的关系。

Result: QSL中单狄拉克场构造的旋量1-形式纯为自旋1/2（自旋3/2部分恒为零），传播激发为狄拉克费米子。矢量挠率给出纯狄拉克质量$M_{eff}=(1/\sqrt6)\,|\dotχ/χ|$，该质量锁定于产生时的哈勃率$M_{eff}\simeq(c_χ/\sqrt6)H_*$，暗物质丰度$\Omega h^2\propto H_*^{5/2}$简化为单一尺度H*的函数。

Conclusion: QSL形式通过几何方式为引力波诱导产生的暗费米子提供了质量，无需引入自由参数。复合自旋1/2狄拉克费米子是QSL唯一的暗物质候选者。该结果与渐近自由标量场宇宙学相关联，为暗物质现象学提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The gravitational-wave induced freeze-in of Maleknejad and Kopp (2026) produces dark fermions from a stochastic gravitational-wave background, but requires them to acquire mass by separate means. We develop the Quadratic Spinor Lagrangian (QSL) formulation of general relativity, extended to Einstein--Cartan, as a framework that supplies this mass geometrically. The spinor 1-form built from a single Dirac field is purely spin-1/2 -- its gamma-traceless (spin-3/2) part vanishes identically -- so the propagating excitation is a Dirac fermion, the same content as the produced Weyl fermion. A cosmological spinor condensate sources a vectorial trace torsion $K\propto\dotχ/χ$, and an explicit Clifford reduction shows that this torsion gives the fermion a pure Dirac mass $M_{eff}=(1/\sqrt6)\,|\dotχ/χ|$, with no pseudoscalar or cross terms. The mass is not a free parameter but is locked to the Hubble rate at production, $M_{eff}\simeq(c_χ/\sqrt6)H_*$, making the relic abundance a function of essentially the single scale $H_*$ ($Ωh^2\propto H_*^{5/2}$) and supplying the mass the parent mechanism must postulate. Whether promoting the spinor 1-form to an independent field yields a propagating spin-3/2 candidate is a distinct dynamical question; Paper II shows that it does not -- the QSL channels all propagation into the gravitational sector -- so the composite spin-1/2 Dirac fermion is the unique QSL dark-matter candidate. We discuss the resulting dark-matter phenomenology and its link to asymptotically free scalar-field cosmology.

</details>


### [157] [Affine quantization of the dynamical Reissner--Nordström region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23552)
*Morteza Bajand,Babak Vakili*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用微超空间约化和仿射量子化方法研究了Reissner-Nordström几何动力学区域的量子动力学，得到可分离的Wheeler-DeWitt方程，分析了电荷在量子动力学中的作用，将仿射量子化研究从Schwarzschild情况推广到了带电情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将仿射量子化方法从Schwarzschild黑洞扩展到带电的Reissner-Nordström几何，研究电荷对量子动力学的影响，并探索仿射量子化引入的短距离修正如何改变波函数的小半径行为。

Method: 采用微超空间约化（minisuperspace reduction）和仿射量子化（affine quantization）方法处理正定几何变量，导出可分离的Wheeler-DeWitt方程，并构建可归一化的半经典波包分析概率分布。

Result: Wheeler-DeWitt方程可分离，得到Hermite多项式模和高斯型径向解；仿射量子化引入的短距离贡献改变了波函数的小半径行为；电荷在量子动力学中起重要作用。

Conclusion: 成功将仿射量子化研究从Schwarzschild推广到Reissner-Nordström带电情形，揭示了电荷对量子黑洞动力学的影响以及仿射量子化带来的小尺度修正。

Abstract: We study the quantum dynamics of the dynamical region of the Reissner--Nordström geometry using a minisuperspace reduction and affine quantization, which is naturally suited for positive-definite geometrical variables. The resulting Wheeler--DeWitt equation becomes separable, yielding Hermite-polynomial modes in one sector and Gaussian-like radial solutions in the other. Affine quantization introduces additional short-distance contributions that modify the small-radius behaviour of the wave function. By constructing normalizable semiclassical wave packets, we analyze the resulting probability distributions in minisuperspace and the role played by the electric charge in the quantum dynamics. Our results extend previous affine-quantization studies of the Schwarzschild case to the charged Reissner--Nordström geometry.

</details>


### [158] [Equatorial Periodic Orbits and Gravitational Wave Phenomenology around Spherically-symmetric vacuum solution in Freund-Nambu scalar-tensor gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23635)
*Dhruba Jyoti Gogoi,Jyatsnasree Bora,Himanshu Chaudhary,M. Yousaf,G. Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Freund-Nambu标量-张量引力框架下，研究了球对称真空解中的测试粒子动力学和引力波现象。通过几何非线性耦合参数q和标量-粒子直接耦合参数gs，系统分析了ISCO和MBO的修正，识别了极端zoom-whirl轨道，并利用数值Kludge方法揭示了EMRI中高频引力波暴的宏观时间失谐现象，为LISA等空间探测器检验标量-张量引力扩展提供了诊断信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Freund-Nambu标量-张量引力中球对称真空解（推广的JNW裸奇点）的测试粒子动力学和引力波特征，为未来空间引力波探测器（如LISA）提供检验标量-张量引力扩展的强场诊断工具。

Method: 采用几何非线性耦合q和标量-粒子直接耦合gs构建广义JNW解；计算ISCO和MBO的修正；分类有界周期轨道以隔离极端zoom-whirl轨道；应用数值Kludge方法模拟EMRI引力波信号。

Result: 标量-张量修正导致ISCO向内移动（gs>0时）和MBO改变；极端zoom-whirl轨道表现出强近心点进动；EMRI的高频引力波暴出现宏观时间失谐，即使轨道空间拓扑保持不变。

Conclusion: 标量-张量修正产生的独特相位偏移为LISA等空间探测器提供了强场区域探测和广义相对论标量-张量扩展约束的稳健诊断特征。

Abstract: We investigate test particle dynamics and gravitational wave (GW) phenomenology in an exact spherically symmetric vacuum solution of Freund - Nambu scalar - tensor gravity. This framework generalizes the Janis - Newman - Winicour (JNW) naked singularity via a geometric non - linear coupling $q$ and a direct scalar - particle coupling $g_s$. We demonstrate that these parameters systematically modify the Innermost Stable Circular Orbit (ISCO) - which shifts inward for $g_s > 0$ - and the Marginally Bound Orbit (MBO). Furthermore, we classify bound periodic trajectories to isolate extreme zoom - whirl orbits exhibiting intense periapsis precession. By applying the Numerical Kludge method to Extreme Mass - Ratio Inspirals (EMRIs), we reveal that scalar - tensor corrections induce a macroscopic temporal dephasing in high - frequency GW bursts, even when the orbit's spatial topology is preserved. These unique phase shifts offer a robust diagnostic signature for future space-based observatories like LISA to probe the strong - field regime and constrain scalar - tensor extensions of general relativity.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [Towards CSI-Native Foundation Models: A Channel-Adaptive Roadmap for 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20670)
*Chenyu Zhang,Xinchen Lyu,Chenshan Ren,Shuhan Liu,Qimei Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向CSI原生基础模型的信道自适应框架，通过统一预训练、位置建模和注意力控制与三个信道需求（尺度感知异构暴露、物理时频空坐标、相关性约束的token交互），在零样本泛化（NMSE降低超4 dB）、尺度外推（8倍未见天线缩放增益达5.4 dB）和推理效率（移动感知处理加速达18.8%）上表现优异，系统级评估表明仅需7.01%的密集导频开销即可实现-18.64 dB平均NMSE，净频谱效率相比密集LMMSE提升36.6%、相比WiFo提升15.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线基础模型将CSI视为任务张量而非传播条件信道响应，未能捕获无线环境固有的时频空几何结构，缺乏面向信道原生特性的统一建模框架。

Method: 提出信道自适应的CSI原生基础模型统一框架，通过尺度感知异构暴露、物理时频天线坐标位置建模、相关性约束的注意力控制三个关键设计，对齐预训练、位置建模和注意力机制与信道物理特性。

Result: 零样本泛化NMSE降低超4 dB；8倍未见天线缩放增益达5.4 dB；移动感知处理加速18.8%；系统级评估仅用7.01%密集导频开销，平均NMSE达-18.64 dB，净频谱效率提升36.6%（vs密集LMMSE）和15.5%（vs WiFo）。

Conclusion: CSI原生表示学习能够支持导频高效的无线接入，为6G无线基础模型提供了信道自适应的发展路径。

Abstract: Wireless foundation models offer a path toward reusable channel state information (CSI) intelligence for sixth-generation (6G) systems. However, existing generic-backbone adaptation and CSI pretraining methods often treat CSI as task tensors rather than propagation-conditioned channel responses, thereby failing to capture the intrinsic time-frequency-spatial geometry of wireless environments. This paper presents a channel-adaptive roadmap toward CSI-native foundation models, proposing a unified framework that aligns pretraining, positional modeling, and attention control with three channel requirements: scale-aware heterogeneous exposure, physical time-frequency-antenna coordinates, and correlation-bounded token interaction. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of the proposed framework across three dimensions: zero-shot generalization, reducing NMSE by more than 4 dB across spatial-temporal-frequency tasks; scale extrapolation, yielding up to a 5.4 dB gain under 8 times unseen antenna scaling; and inference efficiency, accelerating mobility-aware processing by up to 18.8%. A system-level evaluation with Sionna SYS further shows that the proposed framework uses only 7.01% of dense-pilot overhead, reaches -18.64 dB average NMSE, and improves average net spectral efficiency by 36.6% over dense LMMSE and 15.5% over WiFo, indicating that CSI-native representation learning can support pilot-efficient radio access.

</details>


### [160] [NeuroShield: A Device-Agnostic Foundation Model for EEG Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20673)
*Matin Fallahi,Patricia Arias-Cabarcos,Thorsten Strufe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroShield是一个基于双阶段Transformer架构的EEG身份认证基础模型，能在可变通道和可变长度的EEG记录中学习身份判别性嵌入。在三个公开数据集（15,762名受试者，28,116个session）上预训练，在两个未见过的下游数据集上评估。微调后等错误率比当前最优方法降低0.44-8.06个百分点，并能泛化到训练时未见过的更长片段和通道布局。已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG身份认证模型通常依赖于固定的采集设置（头显硬件、通道布局、信号时长），不同数据集间的异质性导致每个新设备或数据集都需要从头开发模型，造成了碎片化问题，限制了多数据集学习、知识迁移和模型复用。

Method: 提出NeuroShield，采用双阶段Transformer架构，能够处理可变通道和可变长度的EEG记录，通过预训练-微调范式在三个大型公开EEG数据集上学习身份判别性嵌入。

Result: 微调后NeuroShield在等错误率上比当前最优方法降低0.44-8.06个百分点。能泛化到训练时未见过的更长EEG片段和不同通道布局。

Conclusion: NeuroShield被证明是一个可复用且自适应的EEG身份编码器，能够在异构记录设置下工作，并已开源以支持可复现性和社区采用。

Abstract: A central challenge in EEG authentication is that models are typically tied to the acquisition settings in which they are trained. In particular, variations in headset hardware, channel layout, and signal duration create heterogeneous recordings that existing models are not designed to handle, causing each new headset or dataset to be treated as a separate model-development problem. This fragmentation limits multi-dataset learning, hinders knowledge transfer, and reduces model reusability. To address this limitation, we present NeuroShield, a reusable foundation model for EEG authentication that learns identity-discriminative embeddings from variable-channel and variable-length EEG recordings through a dual-stage transformer architecture. We pretrain NeuroShield on three public EEG datasets comprising 15{,}762 subjects and 28{,}116 sessions, and evaluate transfer on two unseen downstream datasets. Our evaluations show that, after fine-tuning, NeuroShield reduces equal error rate by 0.44--8.06 percentage points relative to the state of the art. NeuroShield further generalizes to segments longer than those seen during training and operates across channel layouts not encountered during pretraining. These results establish NeuroShield as a reusable and adaptable EEG identity encoder across heterogeneous recording settings. We release NeuroShield as open source to support reproducibility and community adoption.

</details>


### [161] [Massive Activations Are Architecturally Robust: A Controlled Scratch/Commitment Residual Stream Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20743)
*Maruthi Vemula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过提出"Ledger Residuals"架构（将残差流分为可变暂存流和受保护解码流）检验大规模激活是否是残差流读写角色冲突的伪影。实验表明，在160M和290M参数的语言模型中，即使提供了专用的答案通道，模型仍会在受保护通道中重建固定维度的起始标记异常值，且更强的稀疏性惩罚反而使其更集中，支持大规模激活是功能性而非偶然性的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对Transformer中大规模激活（massive activations）现象存在争议：一种观点认为这是残差流同时承担读写角色的架构伪影（artifact），另一种认为这是模型功能需求所致。本文旨在通过架构干预直接检验"伪影假说"。

Method: 设计Ledger Residuals架构，将残差流拆分为两个子流：Deliberation（可变暂存流，供中间计算自由覆写）和Commitment（受保护解码专用流，仅用于最终读取）。在匹配损失条件下训练160M和290M参数的语言模型，观察大规模激活是否消失。

Result: 即使在专用解码通道中，模型仍重建了与传统Transformer中相同的固定维度起始标记异常值。虽然幅度有所减小，但在起始标记上的集中度更高；施加更强的稀疏性惩罚无法移除该特征，反而使其更持久、更集中。

Conclusion: 大规模激活具有架构鲁棒性，会在模型用于解码的表示中重新出现，支持其功能性而非偶然性伪影的解释。

Abstract: Trained transformers reliably develop massive activations, a small number of hidden dimensions whose magnitude is far above the median and which concentrate on the sequence-start token. Whether these outliers are a removable artifact of the residual stream's overloaded read and write role, or instead a functional necessity, is actively debated. We test the artifact hypothesis directly, with an architectural intervention. Our architecture, Ledger Residuals, splits the residual stream into a mutable scratch stream (Deliberation) that intermediate computation may freely overwrite and a protected, decode-only accumulator (Commitment) that holds the representation the model reads out. If massive activations exist only because one stream is forced to be both scratchpad and answer, then a dedicated answer channel should remove the need for them. We find that it does not. In matched-loss language models at the 160M and 290M scales, the model rebuilds the canonical fixed-dimension, start-token outlier inside the protected channel. The rebuilt feature is smaller in magnitude than in a standard transformer but more sharply concentrated on the start token, and a stronger sparsity penalty makes it more persistent and more concentrated still, rather than removing it. Massive activations therefore look architecturally robust: they re-emerge in whichever representation the model decodes from, which is what we would expect if they are functional rather than incidental. We release our architecture and measurement code.

</details>


### [162] [CIExplainer++: Generating Causal and Interpretable Explanations for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20747)
*Francisco Caldas,Sahil Satish Kumar,Ruben Belo,Cláudia Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CIExplainer和G2TeXplainer两个方法：CIExplainer基于因果推断和扰动分析，使用潜在结果框架识别对GNN预测最具因果影响的子图；G2TeXplainer则将因果子图转换为包含特征和关系信息的自然语言解释，从而提升GNN的可解释性和人类可理解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释人工智能旨在通过呈现模型中真正影响输出的因果要素来增强黑盒模型的可信度，而现有GNN解释方法在因果识别和人类可理解性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出CIExplainer，基于潜在结果框架的扰动因果推断方法，识别对GNN预测因果效应最大的子图；进一步提出G2TeXplainer，将因果子图转换为融合特征级和关系级信息的自然语言解释。

Result: 在多种GNN架构（GCN、GraphSAGE、GAT、GIN）和不同数据集上进行了评估和比较。

Conclusion: CIExplainer能够有效识别具有因果影响力的子图结构，G2TeXplainer能将因果子图转化为人类可理解的自然语言，两者结合提升了GNN解释的因果可靠性和可读性。

Abstract: Explainable Artificial Intelligence aims to make black-box models more trustworthy by presenting, in a human-understandable manner, the elements that lead to the model's output. This involves both (i) identifying components and connections with genuine causal influence on outputs and (ii) translating such structures into an interpretable representation. For the former, we introduce CIExplainer, a novel perturbation-based method grounded in causal inference for explaining Graph Neural Networks (GNNs). CIExplainer identifies the subgraph with the highest causal effects on GNN predictions using the Potential Outcome Framework. We evaluate and compare CIExplainer on various GNN architectures (GCN, GraphSAGE, GAT, GIN) and datasets.
  To bridge subgraph explanations with human interpretability, we further propose G2TeXplainer, a method that transforms causal subgraphs into natural language explanations that capture both feature-level and relational information.

</details>


### [163] [Evidential Fusion Network for Multimodal Survival Prediction under Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20757)
*Yucheng Xing,Hailan Mo,Zi Wang,Ling Huang,Mengling Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EMMS模型，利用Dempster-Shafer理论和高斯随机模糊数进行多模态决策融合，在不需生成缺失数据的情况下处理缺失模态场景下的生存预测，同时提供校准的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态生存预测模型假设数据完整，对临床中常见的模态缺失场景鲁棒性不足。

Method: 采用Dempster-Shafer理论和高斯随机模糊数进行多模态决策融合，将缺失模态视为空证据，同时考虑偶然不确定性和认知不确定性以及模态可靠性。

Result: 在四个癌症数据集上达到最先进性能，在缺失模态下提供校准且可解释的不确定性估计，且无额外计算开销。

Conclusion: EMMS提供了一种无需生成式阶段、计算高效且鲁棒的多模态生存预测方案，有效处理临床中常见的模态缺失问题。

Abstract: Recent multimodal survival prediction models have demonstrated strong predictive performance by leveraging complementary information across modalities. However, such models generally assume data completeness and exhibit limited robustness toward missing modalities, which are frequently encountered in real-world clinical settings. We propose the Evidential Missing Modality Survival Fusion (EMMS) model for multimodal survival prediction under missing modalities. EMMS offers a straightforward, computationally effective approach to survival analysis without requiring a generative phase for missing data. By employing Dempster-Shafer theory and Gaussian Random Fuzzy Numbers for multimodal decision fusion, it considers both aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty alongside modality reliability for fusion. Moreover, the model treats missing modalities as vacuous evidence, preventing interference with available inputs and naturally reflecting increased uncertainty and calibrated predictions. Extensive experiments on four cancer datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance while providing calibrated and interpretable uncertainty estimates under incomplete multimodal observations, without introducing additional computational overhead.

</details>


### [164] [ELADO: Elliptic PDE Assessment Datasets for Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20771)
*Frank Ehebrecht,Toni Scharle,Martin Atzmueller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ELADO是一个系统性的基准测试套件，专门用于揭示和量化神经算子架构在学习椭圆PDE解算子时的失败模式。通过围绕Poisson方程和Helmholtz方程构建多个非均匀系数数据集，并设计可控数据生成过程来隔离特定困难源（如重尾解分布、谱分布偏移、输入敏感性等），实验表明多种神经算子架构在这些挑战下预测精度显著下降，而这些下降在标准数据集和指标（如平均相对L²误差）中可能被掩盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准数据集主要关注平均性能，忽视了椭圆PDE问题中自然出现的挑战性特征。需要构建能够系统性地揭示神经算子架构失败模式的数据集，以更全面地评估和推动算子学习领域的发展。

Method: 1) 围绕Poisson方程和Helmholtz方程构建多个非均匀系数数据集；2) 设计可控数据生成过程，隔离五种困难源：重尾解分布、谱分布偏移、频域重尾分布、输入敏感性（通过经验局部Lipschitz分析量化）、输入信号复杂度影响（控制振幅归一化）；3) 在全部数据集上评估多种神经算子架构。

Result: 重尾目标、谱分布偏移和输入敏感性均会导致多种神经算子架构的预测精度显著退化，而这些退化在标准数据集和平均相对L²误差等指标下可能被掩盖。

Conclusion: ELADO基准揭示了现有神经算子在椭圆PDE问题中的关键脆弱性，强调了需要使用更具挑战性的基准来全面评估算子学习方法的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: We introduce ELADO (Elliptic PDE Assessment Datasets for Operator Learning), a systematic benchmark suite constructed to show and quantify failure modes of neural operator architectures when learning solution operators of elliptic PDEs. While the benchmarks of existing datasets focus on average case performance, the ELADO datasets are constructed to highlight challenges that arise naturally in elliptic PDE problems. In particular, we construct several datasets built around Poisson's equation and the Helmholtz equation, each with non-constant coefficients. We define a controllable data-generating process to create datasets, that are designed to isolate a distinct source of difficulty. Specifically, these are (1) heavy-tailed solution distributions arising from light-tailed coefficient field distributions, (2) spectral distribution shift of the input data, (3) heavy-tailed distributions in the frequency domain of solutions, arising from light-tailed coefficient field distributions, (4) input sensitivity of learned operators, quantified by an empirical local Lipschitz analysis, and (5) the effect of input signal complexity on prediction accuracy under controlled amplitude normalization. We evaluate several neural operator architectures across all datasets and show that heavy-tailed targets, spectral shift, and input sensitivity each cause substantial degradation of the prediction accuracy that standard datasets and metrics (e.g., the mean relative $L^2$ error) may obscure.

</details>


### [165] [B[FM]$^2$: Brain Foundation Model via Flow Matching with SplitUNet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20812)
*Jaedong Hwang,Kathleen Zhang,Wei Dai,Konstantinos Kontras,Maarten Vanmarcke,Maarten De Vos,Ila Fiete,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出B[FM]²，一种基于连续流匹配（Flow Matching）的脑电图基础模型。与现有将EEG离散化为片段或编码本token的方法不同，B[FM]²直接在原始信号上进行连续时间流匹配预训练，无需分块、标记化或掩码。针对多通道EEG中时间轴密集采样而电极轴较短的特性，设计了SplitUNet速度网络，在每个模块中分解为独立的1D时间卷积和1D电极卷积，仅沿时间轴下采样以保持电极拓扑结构。该模型仅用36,895个片段（约307小时）的预训练数据，在9个标准下游任务中7个达到SOTA，预训练数据量比现有EEG基础模型少1-2个数量级（约30倍），并且生成的合成EEG连神经科医生都无法区分（Cohen's κ=-0.096）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型通常将连续的多通道EEG波形离散化为片段或编码本token，并用掩码自监督训练Transformer。但这种离散化会割裂连续的脑节律，掩盖细粒度的时序动态。作者希望设计一种归纳偏置与EEG数据特性更契合的预训练方法，直接在原始信号上学习，避免信息损失。

Method: 采用连续时间流匹配（Flow Matching）在原始EEG信号上直接预训练，无需分块、token化或掩码。针对多通道EEG时间轴（数千时间点）与电极轴（数十通道）的不对称性，设计SplitUNet：每个模块分解为独立的1D时间卷积和1D电极卷积，仅沿时间轴下采样，保持电极拓扑结构。

Result: 在9个标准EEG分类下游任务中，7个达到SOTA。预训练仅需36,895个片段（约307小时），数据量比现有EEG基础模型少1-2个数量级（约30倍）。生成的合成EEG经两位认证神经科医生盲评，无法与真实脑电区分（Cohen's κ=-0.096）。

Conclusion: B[FM]²证明了连续流匹配预训练范式在EEG基础模型中的有效性，通过契合EEG信号连续性的归纳偏置设计，在显著降低数据需求的同时取得更优性能，并具备生成高质量合成EEG的能力，为脑电基础模型提供了新的方向。

Abstract: EEG foundation models can learn generalizable representations from large-scale EEG corpora to enable single-backbone transfer across diverse clinical and brain-computer interface tasks. Existing models typically discretize the continuous multi-channel EEG waveform into patches or codebook tokens and train a transformer with masked self-supervision. Recognizing that this discretization fragments continuous brain rhythms and obscures fine-grained temporal dynamics, we present B[FM]$^2$(Brain Foundation Model via Flow Matching), whose inductive bias aligns with the data by pretraining directly on the raw signal using continuous-time flow matching without patches, tokenization, or masking. However, multi-channel EEG signals pose an architectural challenge for flow matching: time is densely sampled and highly autocorrelated (thousands of timepoints), while the electrode axis is short (tens of channels) at distinct scalp positions. To address this time-electrode asymmetry, we introduce SplitUNet, a velocity network that factorizes each block into separate 1D temporal and 1D electrode convolutions and downsamples only along time, preserving electrode topology throughout the hierarchy. B[FM]$^2$ sets a new state of the art on 7 of 9 standard downstream EEG classification tasks, using a pretraining budget of only 36,895 segments ($\approx$ 307h), 1-2 orders of magnitude ($\approx$ 30x) less than required by existing EEG foundation models. Further, it generates synthetic EEGs that two board-certified neurologists cannot distinguish from brain data (Cohen's $κ=$ -0.096). https://jd730.github.io/projects/BFM2

</details>


### [166] [CELEUS: Certifiable and Efficient LLM Evaluation via E-Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20820)
*Zhijian Zhou,Zesheng Ye,Zhaorun Chen,Bo Li,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Celeus框架，通过E-process构建任意有效置信区间，结合不确定性引导采样和代理辅助近似，在保证统计严格性的同时，将评估所需样本量减少54-62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估方法在迭代更新置信区间并依此决定停止时，无法保证声称的覆盖概率（如95%）始终有效，存在理论严格性与实践之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出Celeus框架，利用E-process构建任意有效置信区间；提出结合(1)不确定性引导采样选择信息量大的样本，和(2)代理辅助近似估计未评估样本的信号；证明该信号在给定历史条件下对评估分数无偏，使得CI能以近参数率收缩。

Result: Celeus在保持任意有效覆盖的前提下，相比基线方法减少54-62%的评估样本即可达到目标精度。

Conclusion: 通过E-process和两种方差减少策略，实现了既统计严格又高效的LLM可认证评估，弥合了理论与实践的差距。

Abstract: Can we trust evaluation scores to capture an LLM's true real-world performance? Certifiable evaluation answers this question by providing guarantee for LLM evaluation. In particular, existing methods sequentially curate evaluation samples and keep updating confidence intervals (CIs) that cover the true performance with high probability (e.g., 95%) until some conditions are satisfied, e.g., the CI width reaches a target precision. However, existing methods are not generally anytime-valid: the claimed coverage (e.g., 95%) may fail when CIs are repeatedly updated and used to decide when to stop, leaving a gap between theoretical rigor and practice. This paper bridges this gap by proposing Celeus, a Certifiable framework for Efficient LLM evaluation, which leverages E-processes to build anytime-valid CIs. Concretely, we propose signals that combine two ingredients: (i) Uncertainty-guided sampling to select informative samples for evaluation, and (ii) Surrogate-assisted approximations for unevaluated samples. We prove that such signals remain unbiased for the evaluation score conditional on the past, enabling statistically-grounded and anytime-valid $e$-process CIs. More importantly, the two ingredients reduce estimation variance and help reach the target precision with fewer evaluated samples. We also prove that CIs obtained by Celeus can shrink at a near-parametric rate up to logarithmic factors and analyze the oracle variance-optimal sampling rule that motivates the empirical uncertainty-guided one. Experiments show that Celeus reaches the target precision using 54-62% fewer evaluated samples than baselines, while preserving anytime-valid coverage.

</details>


### [167] [Evolutionary Discovery of Developmental Reward Schedules in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20858)
*Alan Nadelsticher Ruvalcaba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种进化框架，用于自动发现深度强化学习中的发展性奖励调度，该框架动态组合三种生物启发式动机成分（能动性、新颖性、反应性），并在MiniGrid稀疏奖励任务上验证了其有效性。实验表明，进化方法在DoorKey-6x6任务上全面超越非进化基线（L-SHADE提升约11.4%），在KeyCorridorS3R1任务上CMA-ES表现最佳。有趣的是，发现的最优调度中新颖性始终作为早期主导信号，这与预设的发展顺序不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中奖励组成的时序结构通常由人工设计并在训练中固定不变，动机优先级如何随训练进程演化这一问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出了一种进化框架，将能动性、新颖性和反应性三种生物启发式动机成分通过时变权重动态组合，并采用四种进化算法（CMA-ES、xNES、DE、L-SHADE）自动发现发展性奖励调度。

Result: 在DoorKey-6x6任务上，所有进化方法均优于非进化基线，L-SHADE取得最佳性能（相对均值提升约11.4%）；在KeyCorridorS3R1任务上，仅CMA-ES表现最佳，其余进化方法泛化能力较弱。发现的最优调度中，新颖性始终作为早期主导信号。

Conclusion: 进化优化是发现深度强化学习中发展性奖励调度的有前景方法；进化在计算设置中找到的最优解可能与生物学中的最优解存在差异。

Abstract: The temporal structure of reward composition in reinforcement learning (RL) is typically hand-designed and held fixed throughout training, leaving the progression of motivational priorities largely unexplored. In this work, we propose an evolutionary framework for discovering developmental reward schedules, in which three distinct biologically inspired motivational components -- agency, novelty, and reactivity -- are combined through time-varying weights that dynamically shift over the course of training. Evaluated on two sparse-reward MiniGrid tasks: DoorKey-6x6 and KeyCorridorS3R1, our framework compares the generalizability of four evolutionary algorithms: CMA-ES, xNES, DE, and L-SHADE against an extrinsically motivated baseline (our main comparison point), and three additional hand-designed methods. On DoorKey-6x6, all evolved methods outperform the non-evolved baselines, with L-SHADE achieving the best performance -- an approximate relative mean improvement of 11.4% over the extrinsic only baseline. On KeyCorridorS3R1, CMA-ES achieves the best overall performance, with the remaining evolved methods showing weaker and less reliable generalization capability compared to the extrinsic only baseline. Interestingly, the discovered schedules diverge from our defined developmental ordering, with novelty consistently emerging as the dominant early signal during training, across both tasks. Collectively, our results position evolutionary optimization as a promising approach for developmental reward schedule discovery in deep reinforcement learning, and suggest that what evolution finds to be optimal in computational settings may differ from what it finds to be optimal in biology. The code for this project can be found at: https://github.com/alannadels/Evolutionary_RL.git.

</details>


### [168] [One-Step Flow Matching for Generative Modeling of Path-Dependent Physical Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22752)
*Yijing Zhou,Jasmin Jelovica*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer骨干网络的流匹配（Flow Matching）模型，用于高分辨率路径依赖应力场生成。该模型在变分自编码器（VAE）的潜空间中运行，将塑性场模拟视为视频合成任务，直接生成所有时间步的应力场。通过设计非高斯源分布减少训练中条件传输路径的交叉，实现一步生成满意样本。引入token级加载嵌入和两个辅助网络增强性能。结果表明，即使训练数据有限，该模型也能准确生成高分辨率路径依赖场，在CPU上比有限元分析快6-7倍，在消费级GPU上快约两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂几何与路径依赖本构模型的物理模拟计算成本极高。虽然基于U-Net的去噪扩散概率模型已被用于弹性应力场生成，但通常需要数百个采样步骤，且生成模型在塑性等路径依赖应力场中的应用仍然非常有限。

Method: 提出基于Transformer骨干的流匹配模型；在VAE潜空间中操作；将塑性场模拟建模为视频合成任务；设计非高斯源分布以一步生成；引入token级加载嵌入和两个辅助网络。

Result: 即使训练数据有限，模型也能准确生成高分辨率路径依赖场；CPU上比有限元分析快6-7倍，消费级GPU上快约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 所提出的流匹配模型能够高效、准确地生成路径依赖应力场，显著提升了计算效率，为复杂物理模拟提供了有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Physical simulations for intricate geometries with path-dependent constitutive models face difficulties due to the enormous computational cost they require. Recently, the emergence of generative AI models, which succeed in image and video synthesis tasks, has provided a promise to further improve simulations. Although U-Net-based denoising diffusion probabilistic models (DDPMs) have been adopted for elastic stress field generation, they typically require hundreds of sampling steps, and applications of generative models to path-dependent, e.g. plastic, stress fields remain very limited. In this work, we propose a novel flow matching (FM) model based on a transformer backbone for high-resolution path-dependent stress field generation with stochastic loading-unloading paths and geometry. The proposed model operates within the latent space of a variational autoencoder (VAE) and formulates the simulation of plastic fields as a video synthesis task, directly generating the stress fields across all time steps. Meanwhile, we design a non-Gaussian source distribution for flow matching, such that crossings among conditional transport paths are reduced during training. This enables our model to generate satisfactory samples in one step without relying on distillation. In addition, we introduce token-level loading embeddings and two auxiliary networks to further enhance the model performance in path-dependent simulation. The results demonstrate that, even with a limited training dataset, our model can accurately generate high-resolution path-dependent fields. It is much more computationally efficient than finite element analysis, providing a speedup of 6 to 7 times over FEM on CPUs and approximately two orders of magnitude speedup on consumer-grade GPUs.

</details>


### [169] [Machine Learning Classification of Cryopathy Syndromes: A Comprehensive Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20874)
*Nataliya Shakhovska,Valentyna Chopyak,Ivan Izonin,Vira Haievska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了12种机器学习策略，用于从实验室数据自动分类14种冷球蛋白血症综合征。由于类别不平衡和临床重叠，任务具有挑战性。最优的多分类性能由随机森林与梯度提升树的软投票集成实现，基于冷球蛋白的交互特征是最具信息量的预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷球蛋白血症综合征的分类困难，因为实验室模式在不同诊断类别间重叠，部分诊断罕见，常规检测结果解读具有挑战性，高度依赖专家经验。

Method: 分析了2,686名患者的实验室记录（14个诊断类别），包括人口统计学变量、冷球蛋白测量、沉淀测试、血凝素和溶血素滴度。数据预处理包括清洗、编码、插补、归一化及构建临床交互特征。评估了12种建模策略：随机森林、梯度提升树、多层感知器、软投票集成、SMOTE过采样、层次分类、周期感知模型、目标二分类器和概率校准。性能评估采用分层训练-测试和5折交叉验证，指标包括宏平均F1、准确率、Top-3准确率和预期校准误差。

Result: 整体任务因严重的类别不平衡和临床诊断重叠而困难。最优多分类性能由随机森林与梯度提升树的软投票集成实现。交叉验证证实平衡随机森林模型性能稳定。树模型一致优于神经网络。特征工程提升了判别能力，最有效的预测因子是衍生自冷球蛋白的交互特征。

Conclusion: 树集成方法（特别是软投票集成和平衡随机森林）是冷球蛋白血症综合征自动分类的实用策略，可为临床决策支持提供可靠方案。

Abstract: Cryopathy syndromes are difficult to classify because laboratory patterns often overlap across diagnostic categories, while some diagnoses are rare. This makes routine interpretation of cryoglobulin-related tests challenging and increases dependence on expert judgment. The aim of this study was to develop and compare machine learning approaches for automated classification of cryopathy syndromes from laboratory data and to identify a practical strategy for clinical decision support. Methods: We analysed laboratory records from 2,686 patients assigned to 14 diagnostic categories. The dataset included demographic variables, cryoglobulin measurements, precipitation tests, and hemagglutinin and hemolysin titers. Data preprocessing included cleaning, encoding, imputation, normalization, and construction of clinically informed interaction features. We evaluated 12 modelling strategies, including Random Forest, Gradient Boosted Trees, Multi-Layer Perceptron, soft-voting ensembles, class balancing with Synthetic Minority Over-sampling Technique, hierarchical classification, period-aware models, targeted binary classifiers, and probability calibration. Performance was assessed using stratified train-test evaluation and stratified 5-fold cross-validation. The main metrics were macro-averaged F1 score, accuracy, Top-3 accuracy, and expected calibration error. The overall task proved difficult because of marked class imbalance and clinical overlap between diagnoses. The best multiclass performance was achieved by a soft-voting ensemble of Random Forest and Gradient Boosted Trees. Cross-validation confirmed stable performance for the balanced Random Forest model. Tree-based methods consistently outperformed the neural network model.
