Feature engineering improved discrimination, and the most informative predictors were derived cryoglobulin-based interaction features.

</details>


### [170] [Understanding Latent Flow Models for Tabular Data Synthesis: Targets, Paths, and Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20878)
*Bahrul Ilmi Nasution*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过七个数据集系统研究了表格数据潜空间流模型在学习目标、概率路径和采样动态方面的经验性能，发现学习目标决定了效用-风险权衡，速度匹配和后验匹配倾向更高效用，分数匹配和噪声匹配倾向更低披露风险；最优传输路径相比方差保持路径更早收敛，可节省计算预算；并提炼出实用配置建议以支持受披露风险和资源约束的模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管领域共享合成表格数据需要在分析效用、披露风险和计算成本之间取得平衡，而潜空间连续时间生成模型在这些维度上的理论等价性在有限步积分和有限计算预算下可能表现出不同的实际行为，目前缺乏系统的经验比较研究。

Method: 在七个数据集上对潜空间流模型进行实证研究，比较速度匹配、分数匹配、噪声匹配和后验匹配四种学习目标，在最优传输（OT）和方差保持（VP）两种概率路径下，结合ODE和SDE采样策略，在不同积分预算下评估效用、风险和计算效率。

Result: (1)学习目标决定效用-风险权衡，速度匹配和后验匹配倾向于更高效用，分数匹配和噪声匹配倾向于更低披露风险；(2)中点配置可提升分布保真度，OT路径比VP路径更早收敛，可在固定预算或风险阈值下节省计算；(3)提炼出可操作的默认配置和实用建议。

Conclusion: 为受披露风险和计算资源约束的合成表格数据发布提供经验驱动的模型选择指导，帮助实践者在效用、风险和计算三者之间做出权衡决策。

Abstract: Synthetic tabular data enables microdata sharing in regulated domains, yet deploying continuous-time generative models requires balancing analytical utility, disclosure risk, and computational cost. Latent-space flow models are flexible, but theoretical equivalences across learning targets, probability paths, and sampling dynamics can translate into different behaviour under finite-step integration and explicit compute budgets. We present an empirical study of tabular latent flow models across seven datasets, evaluating velocity, score, noise, and posterior matching objectives under optimal transport (OT) and variance-preserving (VP) paths, ODE and SDE sampling, and varying integration budgets. Our contributions are threefold: (1) we show that the learning target largely determines the utility-risk operating regime, with velocity and posterior matching tending to yield higher utility, while score and noise matching tend to achieve lower disclosure risk; (2) we demonstrate that configuration and sampling choices shift performance, with midpoint often improving distributional fidelity and OT paths often tolerating earlier stopping than VP, enabling compute savings under fixed budgets or risk thresholds; and (3) we distil these findings into actionable defaults and practical configuration guidance to support pre-release model selection under disclosure risk and resource constraints. The code implementation and supplementary materials can be accessed in https://github.com/rulnasution/tabular-latent-flow/.

</details>


### [171] [Temporal Causal Prior-Data Fitted Networks for Panel Data with Learned Reliability Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20889)
*Shravan Talupula,Saurabh Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TCPFN，一种用于零样本时序因果发现的基础模型，能同时输出因果效应估计和可靠性信号，在19个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能，并在工业规模数据集上显著优于PCMCI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果基础模型仅处理静态横截面数据，神经时序方法需逐数据集训练，且没有方法能同时输出CATE估计和逐对可靠性信号。工业时序数据的因果推断需要处理时间动态、时变处理和未观测混杂，现有方法无法满足工业规模的需求。

Method: (1) 因果判断头：联合预测零效应概率、混杂强度、可识别性、中介分数和因果机制；(2) 混合训练先验：覆盖六种因果机制加前门和工具变量先验；(3) 离散token面板数据架构：交叉注意力掩码防止跨时间步信息泄漏；(4) 零样本工业规模推理：FAISS上下文选择加单步后验校正。

Result: 19个基准数据集上：TEP AUROC 0.96，SWaT 0.93，Causal Rivers 0.98，CAUSRCA 0.97。零效应检测器NullF1 0.94，AUROC 0.99。工业数据集上V=1275仅6小时（单GPU），而PCMCI在V=666子面板需81.5小时，外推至V=1275约12.5天。

Conclusion: TCPFN是首个在工业规模上实现零样本时序因果发现并输出可靠性信号的基础模型，在精度和效率上显著超越现有方法，特别是PCMCI的可扩展性对比案例显示了TCPFN的工业实用价值。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects in industrial time series requires handling temporal dynamics, time-varying treatments, and unobserved confounders. Existing causal foundation models (CausalPFN, CausalFM) operate only on static cross-sectional data; neural temporal methods (CRN, G-Net) require per-dataset training; and concurrent temporal-PFN proposals have not been demonstrated at industrial scale. None output explicit per-pair reliability signals alongside their CATE estimates.
  We introduce Temporal Causal Prior-Data Fitted Networks (TCPFN), a foundation model for zero-shot temporal causal discovery with learned reliability signals. TCPFN makes four contributions: (1) a Causal Judgment Head that jointly predicts null-effect probability, confounding strength, identifiability, mediation fraction, and causal regime; (2) a mixed training prior covering six causal regimes (independent, direct, confounded, mediated, time-varying confounded, feedback) plus CausalFM-style front-door and instrumental-variable priors; (3) a discrete-token panel-data architecture with cross-attention masking that prevents inter-horizon leakage; (4) zero-shot inference at industrial scale via FAISS-based context selection and one-step posterior correction.
  On 19 benchmark datasets across five domains, TCPFN achieves competitive zero-shot causal discovery: AUROC 0.96 on Tennessee Eastman, 0.93 on SWaT, 0.98 on Causal Rivers, 0.97 on CAUSRCA. The null detector reaches NullF1 0.94, AUROC 0.99. TCPFN scales to V=1,275 on a proprietary Kraft pulp-and-paper dataset in 6 hours on a single GPU; PCMCI, a CPU-only library, on a V=666 sub-panel of the same data took 81.5 hours, extrapolating by O(V^2) to ~12.5 days at V=1,275. TCPFN's top edges identify cross-subsystem causal relationships while PCMCI's surface within-instrument controller-measurement coupling -- a scalability case study.

</details>


### [172] [MMGNN: Multi-level, multi-color graph neural networks for molecular property prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20906)
*Trung Nguyen,Duc Duy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MMGNN，通过将分子图分解为重叠的原子类型对子图，分别处理化学键（2D）和几何空间（3D）信息，在多个分子性质预测基准上取得竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统消息传递神经网络在单一图上混合不同化学交互信号，且需要深层传播才能捕获长程效应，限制了分子性质预测的表现。

Method: 构建多层级多颜色图神经网络（MMGNN）：MMGNN-2D基于共价键构建化学着色子图，MMGNN-3D基于空间邻近构建几何着色子图并增强距离、角度和二面角描述符，每个子图共享通信消息传递骨干，再通过原子级聚合和分子读出组合表示。

Result: 在5个分类和3个回归基准上，MMGNN-2D在分类任务上平均AUC-ROC达0.838，ESOL上RMSE最低(0.803)；MMGNN-3D在BBBP上AUC-ROC最高(0.956)，FreeSolv上RMSE最低(1.793)。

Conclusion: 重叠的交互特异性子图分解是分子性质预测的有效策略，拓扑和几何表征具有互补优势。

Abstract: Molecular message-passing neural networks commonly propagate chemically diverse interactions through a single graph, which may mix interaction-specific signals and require deep propagation to capture long-range effects. We introduce the Multi-level, Multi-color Graph Neural Network (MMGNN), a hierarchical framework that decomposes a molecular graph into overlapping atom-type-pair-specific subgraphs while preserving atom-level resolution. MMGNN-2D constructs chemical-colored subgraphs from covalent connectivity, whereas MMGNN-3D constructs geometric-colored subgraphs from spatial proximity and augments their edges with distance, angular, and torsional descriptors. Both variants apply a shared communicative message-passing backbone to each subgraph and combine the resulting representations through atom-wise aggregation and molecular readout. We evaluated MMGNN on five classification and three regression benchmarks from MoleculeNet using common scaffold splits and five independent runs. MMGNN-2D achieved the highest macro-average AUC-ROC of 0.838 across the classification datasets and the lowest RMSE on ESOL (0.803). MMGNN-3D obtained the highest mean AUC-ROC on BBBP (0.956) and the lowest RMSE on FreeSolv (1.793), indicating complementary strengths of topological and geometric representations. Structural and leave-one-out analyses further illustrate how the subgraph decomposition affects learned representations and atom-type-pair sensitivities. These results support overlapping interaction-specific graph decomposition as a competitive strategy for molecular property prediction.

</details>


### [173] [Physics-Guided Dual-Stream Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network for Predicting Full-Field Structural Response of Stiffened Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20916)
*Yuecheng Cai,Jasmin Jelovica*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双流异构图神经网络（DS-HGNN），用于薄壁结构（如加筋板盒梁）的全场应力和位移预测。该方法在面板级异构图表示上运行，通过物理引导的边状态初始化、双流消息传递（分离纵向和横向信息并允许跨流交换）、FiLM条件化1-D谱卷积和谱旁路低秩读取实现高效预测。在多种几何、边界、载荷和材料非线性条件下的测试中，DS-HGNN相比六种基准模型取得最低应力与位移RMSE，且仅需19%-38%的训练样本即可达到可比精度，并能捕捉屈服及后屈服应力特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型复杂结构的迭代设计与优化需要快速准确的场预测（应力、位移等），但有限元分析计算成本高，现有神经网络代理在处理变化拓扑和复杂边界条件时存在困难。

Method: 提出DS-HGNN，包含：（1）面板级异构图表示；（2）物理引导的边状态初始化（融合边类型、空间信息和边界运动学）；（3）双流消息传递分离纵向/横向信息并允许跨流交换；（4）FiLM条件化1-D谱卷积融合几何与载荷效应；（5）谱旁路低秩读取重构物理场。

Result: 在加筋板数据集上，DS-HGNN的应力和位移RMSE低于六种基准异构图神经网络模型；仅用19%-38%的训练样本即可达到与最强基准模型相当的精度；能够捕捉屈服及后屈服应力特征。

Conclusion: DS-HGNN有效解决了变拓扑和复杂边界条件下薄壁结构全场预测的挑战，在精度和数据效率上均优于现有方法，且具备捕捉非线性材料行为的能力。

Abstract: Iterative design and optimization of large, complex structures require fast and accurate prediction of stress, displacement, and other fields. Finite element analysis (FEA) is computationally expensive for this task. Existing neural network surrogates often struggle with varying topologies and complex boundary conditions. This study proposes the novel Dual-Stream Heterogeneous Graph Neural Network (DS-HGNN) for full-field stress and displacement prediction in thin-walled structures, demonstrated on box beams made of stiffened panels. DS-HGNN operates on panel-level heterogeneous graph representations and introduces physics-guided edge states initialized from edge types, spatial information, and boundary kinematics. These states are updated through dual-stream message passing that separates longitudinal and transverse structural information while allowing cross-stream exchange. Geometry and loading effects are incorporated through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM)-conditioned 1-D spectral convolutions, and physical fields are reconstructed using a spectral-bypass low-rank readout. The model is evaluated on stiffened panel datasets with different geometries, boundary kinematics, loading conditions, and material nonlinear responses. DS-HGNN achieves the lowest stress and displacement RMSE compared with six benchmark heterogeneous graph neural network models. It also reaches comparable accuracy to the strongest benchmark models using 19%-38% fewer training samples. A targeted evaluation further shows that DS-HGNN captures yield and post-yield stress features.

</details>


### [174] [Short-Term Electricity Demand Forecasting for New England Using a Hybrid Transformer-XGBoost Framework with Weather, Calendar, and COVID-19 Indicators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20918)
*Reza Ghanavati,Behrooz Mosallaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合Transformer编码器与XGBoost的混合框架用于新英格兰地区短期电力需求预测，融合气象、日历、自回归滞后及COVID-19流行病学变量。混合模型测试RMSE为8,876 MWh，MAPE为2.05%，R-squared为0.906，略优于纯XGBoost基线。但Diebold-Mariano检验表明两者差异不显著。消融实验与SHAP分析揭示COVID-19特征存在**时间有效性衰减**问题：疫情期间学习到的行为模式在疫情后阶段已退化为噪声，导致过拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期电力需求预测对电力系统可靠运行、能源市场规划和基础设施优化至关重要，现有方法在处理复杂时序特征与外部变量（如疫情这类突发干扰）的融合方面存在不足。

Method: 采用混合架构：Transformer编码器提取时序特征后输入XGBoost进行预测。输入特征包括6城市气象观测、日历与节假日效应、自回归需求滞后项及COVID-19流行病学变量。超参数优化使用Optuna结合多变量Tree-structured Parzen Estimator，500次试验，采用无泄漏的70/15/15时序划分。

Result: 混合模型测试RMSE为8,876 MWh，MAPE为2.05%，R-squared为0.906；纯XGBoost基线RMSE为9,304 MWh，MAPE为2.21%，R-squared为0.896。Diebold-Mariano检验（DM=-1.126, p=0.262）显示两者差异不显著。消融实验：移除COVID特征使混合模型RMSE恶化3.2%，反而使XGBoost提升1.2%。SHAP分析显示5/8个COVID特征在疫情后测试集上的重要性排序高于疫情训练期。

Conclusion: 时间有效性衰减是关键机制：行为扰动在2020-2021年驱动了强COVID-需求信号，但到2022年中适应已完全完成，流行病学特征退化为噪声，放大了对陈旧疫情模式的过拟合。混合模型未能从根本上缓解这一泛化问题。

Abstract: Accurate short-term electricity demand forecasting is critical for reliable power system operation, energy market planning, and infrastructure optimization. This paper presents a hybrid framework combining a Transformer encoder for temporal feature extraction with gradient-boosted decision trees (XGBoost) for daily electricity demand forecasting across New England. The framework integrates meteorological observations from six cities spanning all six New England states, calendar and holiday effects, autoregressive demand lags, and COVID-19 epidemiological variables. Hyperparameter optimization uses Optuna with a multivariate Tree-structured Parzen Estimator over 500 trials, with a leakage-free 70/15/15 chronological train-validation-test split. The hybrid model achieves a test RMSE of 8,876 MWh, MAPE of 2.05%, and R-squared of 0.906. A tabular-only XGBoost baseline achieves RMSE of 9,304 MWh, MAPE of 2.21%, and R-squared of 0.896. A Diebold-Mariano test (Harvey-Leybourne-Newbold correction) confirms the 427.7 MWh difference is statistically indistinguishable from noise (DM = -1.126, p = 0.262). An ablation study reveals COVID-19 features improved training accuracy but had asymmetric test effects: removal degraded hybrid RMSE by 3.2% while marginally improving XGBoost-only by 1.2%. A SHAP temporal analysis shows 5 of 8 COVID features rank higher on the post-acute test set than during pandemic-active training, indicating the model over-applies learned pandemic patterns. These findings establish temporal validity decay as a central mechanism: behavioral disruptions drove a strong COVID-demand signal during 2020-2021, but adaptation was complete by mid-2022, leaving epidemiological features as noise amplifying overfitting to stale pandemic patterns.

</details>


### [175] [$Ω$: Operator-based Mixture Ensemble for Generative Assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20920)
*Pouria Behnoudfar,Nan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Ω框架结合条件高斯代理模型、无监督分数学习与生成采样，为高维非线性部分观测系统提供非高斯后验推断的可扩展方案，在湍流模型中验证了其优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集合卡尔曼滤波的高斯近似在强非高斯后验下不准确，粒子滤波存在严重可扩展性限制，而现有基于分数的生成方法通常需要真实后验样本进行监督训练，这在大多数实际应用中是不可行的。

Method: Ω框架包含四个核心步骤：(1) 利用条件高斯代理模型解析恢复高维未观测分量，降低计算成本；(2) 通过去噪分数匹配仅学习超出解析基线的残差差异，使用集合轨迹而非真实后验样本；(3) 通过高斯混合表示重构观测和未观测变量的完整非高斯后验分布；(4) 采用退火朗之万采样将集合成员从基线迭代精炼至目标后验。

Result: 在多个具有间歇性和极端事件的湍流模型上验证，Ω一致地提高了后验精度。

Conclusion: Ω为高维非线性部分观测系统提供了一种无需真实后验样本、可扩展的非高斯后验推断框架，有效结合了解析条件分布与无监督残差学习，克服了维数灾难和计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Characterizing non-Gaussian posterior distributions in partially observed high-dimensional nonlinear systems remains a fundamental challenge in data assimilation. Ensemble Kalman filters rely on Gaussian approximations that can be inaccurate for strongly non-Gaussian posteriors, whereas particle filters suffer from severe scalability limitations. Recent score-based generative approaches improve posterior characterization but typically require supervised training with ground-truth posterior samples, which are unavailable in most practical applications. We introduce $Ω$ (Operator-based Mixture Ensemble for Generative Assimilation), a scalable framework that integrates conditional Gaussian surrogate modeling, unsupervised score learning, and generative sampling. The conditional Gaussian surrogate provides a nonlinear non-Gaussian baseline approximation while admitting closed-form conditional posterior distributions for the unresolved variables. First, $Ω$ exploits these closed-form conditional distributions to analytically recover the high-dimensional unobserved component, reducing computational cost and mitigating the curse of dimensionality. Second, $Ω$ learns only the residual discrepancy beyond an analytical baseline through denoising score matching using ensemble trajectories alone, eliminating the need for ground-truth posterior samples and substantially reducing the learning burden. Third, $Ω$ reconstructs the full non-Gaussian posterior distribution of both observed and unobserved variables via a Gaussian mixture representation, capturing multimodal, skewed, and heavy-tailed statistics. Finally, $Ω$ employs annealed Langevin sampling to iteratively refine ensemble members from the baseline toward the target posterior. $Ω$ is validated on several turbulent models with intermittency and extreme events, consistently improving posterior accuracy.

</details>


### [176] [Hierarchical Pooling for Sheaf Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20932)
*Dionisia Naddeo,Carlo Abate,Pietro Liò,Nicola Toschi,Filippo Maria Bianchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiSP是一种基于局部谱粗化的层次化Sheaf池化框架，通过投影到簇内Sheaf Laplacian的低频特征模式构建粗粒度的茎空间，并利用Galerkin算子保持能量表示，同时分析了截断损失和实现损失两种近似误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Sheaf神经网络仅在固定图分辨率上操作，缺乏构建层次化表示的、有原则的池化机制。

Method: HiSP：基于局部谱粗化的Sheaf感知池化框架。给定图划分后，通过将细粒度茎值特征投影到簇内Sheaf Laplacian的低频特征模式上来构建粗粒度茎，这些局部模式定义了上链层面的延拓映射，通过Galerkin算子在粗空间上表示细粒度Sheaf能量。分离了截断损失（丢弃局部模式）和实现损失（投影算子的粗Sheaf表示）。

Result: 实现了HiSP作为与SNN兼容的GNN池化层，提供了支持批处理、提升Sheaf Laplacian和层次化架构的PyG实现。

Conclusion: HiSP为Sheaf神经网络提供了首个原则性的层次化池化机制，通过谱粗化保持Sheaf结构信息，并理论上分析了粗化过程中的近似误差来源。

Abstract: Sheaf Neural Networks (SNNs) generalize Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) by replacing scalar node signals with stalk-valued signals and by using restriction maps to measure compatibility across edges. Unlike standard graph diffusion, which encourages neighboring node features to become similar, sheaf diffusion promotes consistency through the restriction maps and can therefore model more general relationships between neighboring nodes. However, existing sheaf neural architectures mainly operate at a fixed graph resolution and do not provide a principled pooling mechanism for building hierarchical representations. In this paper, we introduce Hierarchical Sheaf Pool (HiSP), a sheaf-aware pooling framework based on local spectral coarsening. Given a partition of the graph, HiSP constructs each coarse stalk by projecting fine stalk-valued features onto the low-frequency eigenmodes of the cluster-internal sheaf Laplacian. These local modes define a cochain-level prolongation map, which allows the fine sheaf energy to be represented on the coarse space through a Galerkin operator. We further analyze the approximation induced by coarsening by separating truncation loss, due to discarded local modes, from realization loss, due to representing the projected operator as a coarse sheaf. Finally, we implement HiSP as a GNN pooling layer compatible with SNNs and provide a PyG implementation supporting batching, lifted sheaf Laplacians, and hierarchical architectures.

</details>


### [177] [Towards Robust Training in NNGPT AutoML Pipeline: A Loss-Optimizer Pairing Selection Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20933)
*Anton Abramochkin,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了6种优化器与3种损失函数（共18种组合）在33种异构架构上的表现，发现不存在通用的最优配对。交叉熵搭配Adam/AdamW是最稳健的选择，NGL损失仅在自适应优化器下表现良好，而Adagrad/Adadelta在固定超参数下普遍表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化机器学习（AutoML）中损失函数与优化器的配对选择对模型训练影响显著，但现有研究缺乏对异构架构池中该配对选择的系统性分析，尤其是单一配方是否适用于不同结构的模型尚不明确。

Method: 对6种优化器（SGD+Momentum、Adam、AdamW、RMSprop、Adagrad、Adadelta）与3种损失函数（交叉熵、负对数似然、NGL）进行全组合（共18种），应用于LEMUR架构池中33种兼容的基架构，通过源码级注入流水线自动生成594个变体，在6个图像分类数据集上以固定超参数进行评估。

Result: ① 无单一配对普遍最优；② 交叉熵+Adam/AdamW是跨架构和数据集最稳健的选择；③ NGL仅在搭配自适应优化器时是交叉熵的有竞争力替代，与SGD或累积方法组合时性能显著下降；④ Adagrad和Adadelta在固定超参数下始终表现不佳，对学习率调优敏感。

Conclusion: 研究为NNGPT框架中的损失-优化器选择提供了可操作的指导，强调异构架构池中配对选择的重要性，揭示了不同配对的适用条件和局限性。

Abstract: The choice of loss function and optimizer is an important decision, that shapes further model training. Yet automated architecture search pipelines (AutoML) benefits significantly more from the optimal pairing selection and vice versa. This paper investigates whether a single recipe is sufficient for heterogeneous architecture pools, or whether the optimal pairing varies across structurally diverse models. We conduct a systematic empirical study of all $3 \times 6 = 18$ combinations of six optimizers (SGD+Momentum, Adam, AdamW, RMSprop, Adagrad, Adadelta), paired with three loss functions: Cross-Entropy (CEL), Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL), and the recently introduced genetically evolved NGL loss across the base models presented in LEMUR heterogeneous architecture pool on six image classification datasets (CelebA-Gender, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, ImageNette, MNIST, SVHN). The 18 loss-optimizer configurations are applied to each of the 33 compatible base architectures taken from the LEMUR pool, resulting in 594 variants that were generated fully automatically by a source-level injection pipeline and evaluated under fixed hyperparameters, ensuring that observed accuracy differences are attributable solely to the loss-optimizer pairing. Our results confirm that no single pairing is universally optimal. Cross-Entropy with Adam or AdamW is the most robust choice across architecture families and datasets. NGL is a competitive alternative to CEL on standard convolutional classifiers, but only when paired with adaptive optimizers; it degrades substantially with SGD or accumulation-based methods. Adagrad and Adadelta consistently underperform under fixed hyperparameters regardless of loss function, highlighting their sensitivity to learning rate tuning. These findings provide actionable guidance for loss-optimizer selection within NNGPT Framework.

</details>


### [178] [Learning through Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20937)
*Nikolaos Tsilivis,Nirmit Joshi,Marko Medvedev,Julia Kempe,Nati Srebro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了神经网络系统通过将显式计算过程内化到自身权重中的机制（内部化过程），重点关注Transformer如何将思维链（CoT）令牌的内部模拟内化，分析不同半自动机类别的内化难度，并揭示内化的负面影响——即分布外性能的逐步退化。首次提供了成功内化的可证明分析：在奇偶性学习任务中，简化单层Transformer先在显式CoT监督下学习目标，然后随着CoT令牌逐步移除而内化自回归生成过程，学会直接计算奇偶性。最后讨论了内化学习与Positive Distribution Shift现象的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络如何将外部计算过程吸收进自身权重（内部化），以及这一过程如何促进学习，特别是探索内部化机制在Transformer架构中的运作原理和局限性。

Method: 通过理论分析和可证明性研究，使用简化的单层Transformer模型在奇偶性学习任务上进行实验，分析从显式CoT监督到CoT令牌逐步移除过程中模型行为的变化。

Result: 成功证明简化单层Transformer能够先学习显式CoT监督下的目标，然后通过内部化过程将CoT生成能力吸收到权重中，最终直接计算奇偶性。同时发现不同半自动机类别的内化难度存在差异，且内化过程伴随着分布外性能的退化。

Conclusion: 内部化是神经网络学习的重要机制，连接了显式推理（CoT）与隐式权重学习，但存在分布外泛化能力下降的代价；该过程与Positive Distribution Shift现象存在关联。

Abstract: We study internalization processes, by which neural-network-based systems absorb an explicit computational procedure into their own weights, and how they facilitate learning. We investigate how transformers internalize the simulation of semiautomata by internalizing chain-of-thought (CoT) tokens, which classes of semiautomata are harder to internalize, and expose the flip side of internalization, that is, a progressive degradation of out-of-distribution performance. We then provide the first provable analysis of successful internalization: for the task of learning parities, we show that a simplified one-layer transformer provably first learns the target with explicit CoT supervision and then internalizes the autoregressive generation as CoT tokens are progressively removed, learning to directly compute the parity. This task is computationally hard to learn from data without CoT supervision. Finally, we discuss how learning through internalization relates to the \textit{Positive Distribution Shift} phenomenon recently introduced by~\citet{Med+26}.

</details>


### [179] [Grouped Query Experts: Mixture-of-Experts on GQA Self-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20945)
*Vishesh Tripathi,Abhay Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GQE在分组查询注意力（GQA）基础上引入混合专家层，每token仅激活一半查询头，在250M参数规模、30B token预算下达到与全激活GQA基线相当的下游精度，同时减少查询头计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准密集注意力对所有token使用相同数量的注意力头，计算量随序列长度二次增长，造成计算浪费。作者希望在保持GQA的KV缓存优势的同时，进一步减少查询头的计算开销。

Method: 在GQA的每个组中引入路由器（router），为每个token选择k个查询头专家（query-head experts），而所有键值头保持密集且不变。这样仅减少活跃的查询头计算量，维持GQA的KV缓存特性。

Result: 在250M参数规模、30B token预算的固定设置下，GQE激活一半的查询头，在下游精度上与全激活的GQA基线持平。

Conclusion: GQE通过混合专家策略在查询头上实现稀疏激活，在不损失精度的情况下有效降低注意力计算成本，尤其适合长序列场景。

Abstract: Self-attention is central to Transformer performance and is often the most expensive part of the Transformer at long context lengths because its pairwise token interactions scale quadratically with sequence length. Standard dense attention also applies the same set of attention heads to every token regardless of token difficulty or information content. This uniform activation can waste compute, especially as sequences grow longer and attention cost increases rapidly. We propose Grouped Query Experts (GQE), a mixture-of-experts layer on top of grouped-query attention (GQA). Within each GQA group, a router selects k query-head experts per token while all key-value (KV) heads remain dense and unchanged. Thus, GQE keeps the KV cache benefits of GQA and reduces only the active query-head computation. On a fixed 30B token budget at the 250M parameter scale, GQE matches the all-active GQA baseline in downstream accuracy while activating half the query heads per token.

</details>


### [180] [A Gated Graph Neural Network Approach to Fast-Convergent Dynamic Average Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20955)
*Antonio Marino,Claudio Pacchierotti,Paolo Robuffo Giordano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于门控图神经网络（GGNN）的分布式动态平均估计算法，通过将估计过程建模为分布式自回归模型，并引入正则化项保证收敛性和编码-解码机制降低通信开销，在收敛速度和精度上显著优于传统基于模型的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的动态平均估计方法存在收敛速度慢和对网络拓扑变化敏感的问题，亟需一种能够快速收敛且鲁棒的分布式解决方案。

Method: 利用GGNN的结构特性将动态平均估计建模为分布式自回归过程；在训练中引入正则化项以保证收敛性；设计编码-解码机制以降低通信开销。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法在收敛速度和估计精度上均显著优于传统基于模型的估计器。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于GGNN的分布式动态平均估计方法是多智能体系统中动态平均估计任务的一个有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Dynamic average estimation is a critical problem in multi-agent systems, enabling agents to collaboratively estimate time-varying signals using only local information exchange. Traditional model-based approaches often face challenges related to convergence speed and sensitivity to network topology changes. This paper introduces a novel learning-based solution leveraging Gated Graph Neural Networks (GGNNs) for fast-convergent dynamic average estimation in a fully distributed manner. Taking advantage of the inherent structure of GGNNs, the proposed method models the estimation process as a distributed autoregressor, ensuring rapid convergence while maintaining stability. We incorporate a regularization term during training to enforce convergence guarantees and introduce an encoding-decoding mechanism to reduce communication overhead without sacrificing accuracy compared to standard GGNNs. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms conventional model-based estimators in terms of both convergence speed and precision, making it a promising alternative for multi-agent applications that require dynamic average estimation.

</details>


### [181] [Right Knowledge, Wrong Answer: Test-Time Steering for Temporal Fact Conflicts in Open-Weight Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20959)
*Elias Hossain,Sourav Saha,Umesh Chandra Biswas,Sanjeda Sara Jennifer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出参数化时间冲突（PTC）问题，并设计了一种无需重训练或外部检索的三阶段测试时干预方法——时间吸引子引导（TAS），通过在关键层引导隐状态向新事实表示偏移，在多个开源模型上实现了29-57%的PTC解决率，同时保持非冲突查询85-99%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数中同时存储了过时事实和更新的替代事实，但标准提示仍可能输出过时答案，现有方法缺乏在推理时选择性覆盖过时参数化知识的有效手段。

Method: 提出三阶段测试时干预方法TAS：1）检测潜在冲突；2）识别冲突关键层；3）通过单层激活修补将隐状态向新事实表示吸引，无需重训练或外部检索。

Result: 在5个Wikidata关系、8746条记录基准上，Qwen-2.5-1.5B/7B、Mistral-7B-v0.3和Llama-3.1-8B上单层激活修补实现0.72-0.85的答案翻转率；端到端TAS解决29-57%的PTC案例，非冲突查询保持85-99%准确率，在4个模型中的3个上优于ITI基线。

Conclusion: 过时的参数化知识可以在推理时被选择性覆盖，为无需重训练的语言模型知识更新提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Large language models can store both outdated facts and newer superseding facts in their parameters, but standard prompting may still elicit the outdated answer. We formalize this problem as Parametric Temporal Conflict (PTC) and introduce Temporal Attractor Steering (TAS), a three-stage test-time intervention that detects likely conflicts, identifies a conflict-critical layer, and steers hidden states toward newer-fact representations without retraining or external retrieval. We construct an 8,746-record verified benchmark across five Wikidata relations and evaluate four open-weight language models from three families: Qwen-2.5-1.5B/7B, Mistral-7B-v0.3, and Llama-3.1-8B. Single-layer activation patching achieves answer-flip rates of 0.72-0.85 across all models. End-to-end TAS resolves 29-57% of PTC cases while preserving 85-99% accuracy on non-conflict queries, outperforming a matched ITI baseline on three of four models. These results show that outdated parametric knowledge can be selectively overridden at inference time.

</details>


### [182] [Is Our Benchmark Enough? An Analysis of Continual Learning for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20961)
*Van-Tuan Tran,Shruthi Gowda,Merim Dzaferagic,Marco Ruffini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑MR-LoRA中基于MLLM的路由器必要性，提出无需训练、无重放的简单原型路由方法（RePRo）在性能匹配的同时计算成本更低；同时发现共享专家并未提升持续学习效果。这些发现源于MLLM-CL基准的两个结构缺陷：任务高度可分且固定任务顺序导致结论对单一课程敏感。为此提出新的持续MLLM学习基准设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视持续适应领域中对MLLM路由器的依赖假设，质疑MR-LoRA方法的核心设计是否必要，揭示现有基准的结构缺陷，推动更合理的评估体系设计。

Method: 提出RePRo（Repetition-free Prototypical Routing），一种无训练、无重放的原型路由方法，利用冻结的预训练特征和任务原型进行分类；通过实验对比分析MR-LoRA与RePRO的性能和计算成本，并系统分析MLLM-CL基准的两个结构缺陷。

Result: RePRo以远低于MR-LoRA的计算成本达到了与其基于MLLM的路由器相匹配的性能；共享专家在持续学习中并未带来实质性提升；MLLM-CL基准的任务在表示空间中高度可分，且固定任务顺序使结论对单一课程敏感，导致基准主要奖励隔离学习而非真正的持续迁移。

Conclusion: 现有MLLM持续学习基准存在结构性问题，需要设计具有重叠任务流形、多种任务顺序、细粒度领域迁移和评估前向迁移与保留能力的新型基准。

Abstract: Continual adaptation is essential for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) deployed across evolving domains, but the state-of-the-art MR-LoRA method highly relies on the assumption that a MLLM-based router is necessary to process complex multimodal inputs. This paper revisits this claim on the MLLM-CL benchmark and argues for two claims. \textbf{First}, routing does not require an MLLM: a simple training-free, replay-free ptotypical routing method (\textsc{RePRo}), uses frozen pretrained features and task prototypes to match the MLLM-based router of MR-LoRA at far lower computational cost. \textbf{Second}, shared experts do not improve continual learning for MLLMs, despite their theoretical appeal. We show that these findings arise from two structural limitations of MLLM-CL: (1) its tasks are \textbf{highly separable} in representation space, and (2) its fixed task order makes conclusions \textbf{sensitive to a single curriculum} rather than robust across diverse continual-learning trajectories. As a result, the benchmark primarily rewards learning in isolation rather than genuine continual transfer. This motivates a new design for future benchmarks of continual MLLM learning, with overlapping task manifolds, multiple task orders, fine-grained domain shifts, and evaluation protocols that reward forward transfer as well as retention.

</details>


### [183] [Formalizing Task-Space Complexity for Zero-Shot Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20967)
*Jung-Hoon Cho,Heling Zhang,Siqi Du,Roy Dong,Cathy Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于性能的有向任务差异度量——符号散度（signed divergence），用于刻画上下文动态系统中零样本泛化的泛化差距上界。通过ε-容忍集和任务空间复杂度概念，结合贪婪源选择策略，在LQR和CartPole系统中验证了该方法能以更少策略实现同等覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一策略在面对多样化条件时往往过于保守，而完全自适应的方案又过于复杂。现有方法缺乏一种以性能为中心的任务相似性度量来量化策略在不同上下文间的泛化能力，从而指导如何选择源策略以实现高效零样本泛化。

Method: 1) 提出符号散度（signed divergence），一种有向的任务不相似度量，上界了从源上下文到目标上下文的泛化差距。2) 基于符号散度定义ε-容忍集，当源策略类处于该集合内时可保证泛化。3) 引入任务空间复杂度概念——保证所有目标上下文泛化差距不超过ε所需的最少源上下文数量。4) 在局部平滑假设下，推导容忍集的认证内/外球和基于实例的体积界。5) 将有限oracle下的源选择问题转化为集合覆盖问题，采用贪心策略，具有标准H(n)近似保证。6) 在Mass-Spring-Damper系统（LQR控制器）和非线性CartPole系统（深度强化学习控制器）上验证。

Result: 在两类系统中，贪心源选择策略相比均匀采样或随机基线，能够用更少的策略实现相同的ε-覆盖，验证了符号散度作为任务相似性度量的有效性和泛化保证的实际价值。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种以性能为导向的任务相似性度量（符号散度）以及实用的泛化保证框架，使得在复杂动态系统中可以用简单的策略构建具有泛化能力的控制器，同时降低了策略部署的复杂度。

Abstract: Policies must operate across diverse conditions, yet a single policy is often conservative while fully adaptive schemes can be complex. We study zero-shot generalization in contextual dynamical systems and introduce a performance-centric, directional task dissimilarity--the signed divergence--that upper bounds the generalization gap from a source context to a target context. The signed divergence induces $\varepsilon$-tolerance sets that certify when a source policy class generalizes, and it yields a concrete notion of task-space complexity: the minimum number of source contexts needed so that every target context incurs at most $\varepsilon$ generalization gap. Under a mild local smoothness assumption on performance, the induced tolerance sets admit certified inner/outer balls and instance-dependent volume bounds on task-space complexity. In the finite-oracle setting, source selection reduces to set cover; a greedy strategy inherits the standard $H(n)$ approximation guarantee. Using a Mass-Spring-Damper system with linear-quadratic regulator (LQR) controllers and a nonlinear CartPole system with deep reinforcement learning controllers, we show that greedy selection achieves the same $\varepsilon$-coverage with fewer policies than uniform or random baselines. Our approach delivers a performance-based task similarity measure and practical certificates for building generalizable control with simple policies.

</details>


### [184] [Detecting Satellites in Radio-Frequency Data via Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20976)
*Cade W. Trotter,Maksim E. Eren,Justin C. Holmes,J. Brent Parham,David Ewing,Boian S. Alexandrov,Gian Luca Delzanno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种半监督射频监测与分类工作流，结合非负矩阵分解（NMFk）、专家引导的聚类解释和分类器预测，用于卫星监测中的RF数据检测与分类，减少对大规模标注数据的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频监测在空间态势感知中至关重要，但RF数据集通常规模大、变化多、稀疏且缺乏标注，依赖监督深度学习方法需要大量标注样本且泛化性不足，因此需要一种适用于有限标注场景的半监督方法。

Method: 首先将RF观测数据表示为非负特征矩阵，使用NMFk自动确定最佳聚类数；然后由领域专家对聚类结果赋予物理意义（如卫星检测、电离层环境等）；最后基于解释后的聚类训练分类器，用于测试集评估和未来数据分类。

Result: 该方法在测试集上验证了分类性能，减少了预标注数据需求，实现了可解释、可迁移的RF数据检测与分类流程。

Conclusion: 通过无监督分解与专家知识结合，该半监督工作流为RF数据中的卫星监测提供了一种低标注成本、可解释且可迁移的实用方案。

Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) monitoring is essential for space domain awareness, but it often generates large, variable, and sparsely populated datasets with few labels. These observations can capture satellites, space debris, and the ionospheric background, yet interpreting them typically requires specialized subject-matter expertise. Supervised deep learning methods can perform well on labeled RF data, but they require many annotated examples and may need careful retraining as RF conditions change. Semi-supervised approaches offer a practical alternative for limited-data settings by using unlabeled observations to reveal latent patterns that experts can interpret. In this paper, we present a semi-supervised RF detection and classification workflow for satellite monitoring that combines Non-negative Matrix Factorization with automatic model determination (NMFk), expert-guided cluster interpretation, and classifier-based prediction. We first represent RF observations as a non-negative feature matrix and apply NMFk to estimate the number of clusters that best captures patterns in the unlabeled data. Subject-matter experts then assign physical meaning to the resulting clusters, including satellite detections, ionospheric environmental conditions, and other RF event categories. Finally, we train a classifier on these interpreted clusters to evaluate performance on a test set and categorize future observations. This pipeline reduces reliance on large pre-labeled datasets by pairing unsupervised factorization with expert interpretation, enabling an interpretable and transferable methodology for detecting, observing, and classifying behavior in RF data.

</details>


### [185] [Physics-Guided Fully Convolutional Spatiotemporal Learning Toward Digital-Twin-Enabled Microstructure Evolution Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20983)
*Michael Trimboli,Wenxi Liu,Xianqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理引导的全卷积时空学习框架用于微观结构演化预测，通过将控制物理方程显式纳入训练目标，相比纯数据驱动方法显著提升了预测精度、长期稳定性和鲁棒性，在旋节线分解实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的时空学习模型常缺乏物理一致性，导致长期预测精度下降，亟需一种既能保持数据驱动可扩展性又具备物理一致性的微观结构演化预测方法。

Method: 提出物理引导的全卷积时空学习框架，将热力学和动力学控制方程作为物理引导残差正则项显式融入训练目标中，约束学习到的动力学行为与已知物理定律一致。

Result: 在旋节线分解实验中，该方法相比纯数据驱动基线更忠实地再现了微观结构形貌、统计特征和演化趋势，在不同空间分辨率和时间预测设置下均表现出更好的预测精度、长期稳定性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架弥合了高保真物理仿真与数据驱动代理模型之间的差距，在保持全卷积架构的可扩展性和计算效率的同时，为数字孪生赋能的微观结构演化预测提供了可靠的代理建模方法。

Abstract: Understanding and predicting microstructure evolution is central to materials design, yet purely data-driven spatiotemporal learning models often suffer from limited physical consistency and degraded long-term prediction accuracy. In this work, we introduce a physics-guided fully convolutional spatiotemporal learning framework for microstructure evolution prediction. Unlike prior self-supervised approaches, the proposed method explicitly incorporates governing physical equations into the training objective, thereby encouraging the learned dynamics to remain consistent with known thermodynamic and kinetic laws. This physics-guided formulation improves predictive accuracy, long-horizon stability, and robustness across spatial resolutions and temporal prediction settings. Extensive experiments for spinodal decomposition demonstrate that incorporating physics-guided residual regularization leads to more faithful reproduction of microstructural morphology, statistics, and evolution trends compared with purely data-driven baselines. The proposed framework preserves the scalability and computational efficiency of fully convolutional architectures while bridging the gap between high-fidelity physics-based simulations and data-driven surrogate modeling, offering a reliable and efficient surrogate-modeling step toward digital-twin-enabled microstructure evolution prediction.

</details>


### [186] [Continuous-Time Probabilistic Correctors for Uncertainty-Aware Physics-Based Spacecraft Trajectory Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21021)
*Muhammad Bilal Shahid,Zhanhong Jiang,Soumik Sarkar,Cody Fleming*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种预测-校正框架，将基于物理的确定性轨道传播器与基于潜变量神经控制微分方程（Latent NCDE）的概率校正器相结合，用于长时域航天器轨迹预测。校正器可包裹现有确定性传播器，在提升预测精度的同时生成校准良好且锐利的不确定性估计。在NASA CDDIS真实数据上的实验表明，该方法在2-4天无观测的预测区间内，相比确定性基线和基于潜变量ODE的校正器，在精度和不确定性校准方面均取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域航天器轨迹预测因缺乏纠偏观测导致误差累积，而现有高保真物理传播器虽能提供精确确定性预测，但缺乏经过校准的长时域不确定性估计，这对空间态势感知和交会评估等安全关键决策至关重要。

Method: 提出Predictor-Corrector框架：使用基于物理的连续时间确定性传播器（如NASA的GMAT）作为预测器，利用基于Latent Neural Controlled Differential Equations（Latent NCDEs）的概率校正器对预测误差进行连续时间建模，校正器可自然处理不规则采样和缺失特征，并引入促进校准性和锐利性的损失函数。

Result: 在NASA CDDIS真实数据上，包裹GMAT传播器后的方法在2-4天无观测的滚动测试窗口上，一致提升了预测精度和不确定性校准效果，优于确定性基线和基于Latent ODE的校正器。

Conclusion: 连续时间概率校正器能够有效增强确定性传播器的长时域轨迹预测性能，提供校准良好的不确定性估计，为空间态势感知等安全关键决策提供可靠支撑。

Abstract: Long-horizon spacecraft trajectory forecasting suffers from error accumulation due to the absence of corrective observations in the forecast regime, making reliable uncertainty estimation crucial for safety-critical decision-making such as space domain awareness and conjunction assessment. While high-fidelity physics-based orbit propagators provide accurate deterministic forecasts, they typically lack calibrated uncertainty estimates over long horizons. We introduce a Predictor--Corrector framework in which a physics-based continuous-time $\textit{deterministic}$ forecaster is augmented with a learned continuous-time $\textit{probabilistic}$ Corrector that models forecast errors. The proposed Corrector can be wrapped around an existing deterministic propagator to improve forecast accuracy while producing sharp and calibrated full-covariance uncertainty estimates. The Corrector is based on Latent Neural Controlled Differential Equations (Latent NCDEs) and models the probabilistic temporal evolution of forecast errors in continuous time, naturally supporting irregular sampling and missing features. We further introduce a loss function that promotes calibration and sharpness in long-horizon uncertainty propagation. We evaluate the proposed framework on long-horizon spacecraft trajectory forecasting using real-world data from NASA's Crustal Dynamics Data Information System (CDDIS), wrapping the Corrector around NASA's General Mission Analysis Tool (GMAT). Across forecast horizons of 2--4 days without observations and six rolling test windows, the proposed approach consistently improves accuracy and uncertainty calibration compared to deterministic baselines and Latent ODE-based correctors, demonstrating the effectiveness of the continuous-time probabilistic Corrector for trajectory forecasting.

</details>


### [187] [Structure-Aware Graph Multi-Task Learning for Dynamic Sparse OD Demand Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21022)
*Ming Xu,Jiawei Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAGMTL框架，将OD预测分解为结构状态建模和流量强度估计两部分，通过多任务学习联合建模区域活动状态、OD连接活动和边级流量强度，在三个真实城市数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实OD流具有动态稀疏性、长尾分布和异构零流模式，现有方法将OD预测视为单一流量回归任务，难以有效建模低频、间歇性和长尾的OD交互。

Method: 提出SAGMTL框架：1) 节点-边协同表示模块，通过交互式节点-边更新捕获区域语义、时空动态和空间先验；2) 将OD预测分解为结构状态建模和流量强度估计；3) 联合建模稳定需求模式和短期波动；4) 多约束目标函数提升稀疏感知和结构一致性。

Result: 在北京、成都、南京三个真实城市交通数据集上，SAGMTL在所有评估指标上优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 显式建模区域活动、连接状态和流量强度能有效提升动态稀疏OD需求预测的鲁棒性，验证了结构感知多任务学习框架的有效性。

Abstract: Origin-Destination (OD) demand prediction is fundamental to intelligent transportation systems, yet real-world OD flows are often dynamically sparse, long-tailed, and characterized by heterogeneous zero-flow patterns. These properties make it difficult to distinguish whether an OD connection is active from how much demand it generates once activated. Many existing methods primarily treat OD prediction as a single flow regression task, which limits their ability to model low-frequency, intermittent, and long-tailed OD interactions. To address these challenges, we propose SAGMTL, a Structure-Aware Graph Multi-Task Learning framework for dynamic sparse OD demand prediction. SAGMTL decomposes OD prediction into structural state modeling and flow intensity estimation, jointly learning regional activity states, OD connection activity, and edge-level flow intensity within a unified framework. Specifically, a node-edge collaborative representation module captures regional semantics, temporal dynamics, and spatial priors through interactive node-edge updates, producing structure-aware representations for dynamic OD interactions. Based on these representations, SAGMTL estimates OD flows by jointly modeling stable demand patterns and short-term fluctuations. A multi-constraint objective further improves sparsity awareness and structural consistency. Experiments on three real-world urban mobility datasets from Beijing, Chengdu, and Nanjing show that SAGMTL achieves superior overall performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines. Further analysis demonstrates that explicitly modeling regional activity, connection states, and flow intensity improves the robustness of dynamic sparse OD demand prediction.

</details>


### [188] [Demystifying Numerical Instability in LLM Inference: Achieving Reproducible Inference for Mission-Critical Tasks with HEAL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21023)
*Zhenting Zhu,Lucas Thai,Shan Yu,Yicheng Liu,Yifan Qiao,Chenxi Wang,Harry Xu,Junyi Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HEAL通过INT16量化和代数误差补偿策略，在保持FP32级别可重复性的同时，将性能开销降低最高7.1倍，解决LLM在不同GPU上的输出不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在关键领域部署时，即使使用贪婪解码和16位精度，在不同GPU上仍会出现灾难性的输出发散。全局FP32管线虽能解决但带来不可接受的计算和内存开销。

Method: 1) INT16量化：利用Q、K、V张量未充分利用浮点格式位宽的特性，在不扩大KV缓存的前提下保持数值稳定性；2) 代数误差补偿策略：在16位Tensor Core上合成高精度矩阵乘法。

Result: 在MCR-Bench基准上，HEAL在下游任务中达到与FP32基线同等的可重复性，性能开销降低最高7.1倍。

Conclusion: HEAL是一种在保证LLM输出可重复性的同时，兼顾计算效率的混合精度干预方案，通过硬件友好的设计解决了由kernel边界截断误差导致的数值不一致问题。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) deploy into mission-critical domains (e.g., finance, medicine, and law), output reproducibility has become a strict system requirement. While practitioners use greedy decoding to eliminate algorithmic stochasticity, empirical deployments with 16-bit precisions still exhibit catastrophic output divergence across heterogeneous GPUs. Through SASS-level profiling, we reveal that this inconsistency is fundamentally driven by truncation errors introduced during downcasting at kernel boundaries. However, achieving reproducibility via a global FP32 pipeline incurs prohibitive system penalties: bypassing 16-bit hardware accelerators hurts compute efficiency, while upcasting the KV cache doubles memory overhead. To bridge this gap, we propose Hybrid Error ALleviation (HEAL), a targeted intervention that approximates FP32 precision while resolving hardware constraints through two targeted mechanisms. First, recognizing that floating-point formats underutilize their bit-width for Q, K, V tensors, HEAL applies INT16 quantization that preserves numerical stability without expanding the KV cache footprint. Second, HEAL synthesizes high-precision matrix multiplications via an algebraic error compensation strategy, executing entirely on high-throughput 16-bit Tensor Cores. To evaluate our approach practically, we introduce MCR-Bench, a benchmark targeting reproducibility in mission-critical tasks. HEAL achieves the same level of reproducibility on downstream tasks as the FP32 baseline while reducing the performance overhead by up to 7.1x.

</details>


### [189] [An Efficient and Effective Architecture for Large-Scale Traffic Prediction via Geometry-Adaptive Square Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21072)
*Yongfeng Su,Hongwen Li,Zijian Zhang,Ziquan Fang,Lu Chen,Christian S. Jensen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SqLinear，一种用于大规模交通预测的高效架构。其核心包括：1) Square Partition——一种几何自适应分区算法，能够将海量传感器划分为平衡、无重叠且近似正方形的空间区域，并提供理论保证；2) 层次化线性交互模块(HLI)——以线性复杂度替代Transformer中的注意力机制，高效捕获局部与全局时空依赖。实验表明，SqLinear在标准设置下平均MAE降低2.30%，极端可扩展场景下降低5.81%，同时训练时间减少13.27%-30.84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经网络的大规模交通预测方法计算可扩展性差，虽引入数据库分区技术（如Grid、Quadtree、K-D Tree）尝试改进，但这些方法依赖手工几何启发式，产生不规则或失衡的数据分区，导致边界碎片化、过度填充开销以及模型精度下降。

Method: 1) Square Partition: 几何自适应分区算法，将大规模传感器划分为平衡、无重叠、近正方形的空间区域，具有理论上的纵横比、平衡性和分区利用率保证。2) Hierarchical Linear Interaction (HLI): 摒弃Transformer中的注意力机制，通过轻量级线性交互方案捕获局部区域内动态和全局区域间依赖，计算复杂度为线性。

Result: 在四个大规模交通数据集和10个基线模型上的实验表明：标准设置下MAE平均降低2.30%，极端可扩展设置下降低5.81%；在空间扩展和时间跨度扩展场景下，训练时间减少13.27%-30.84%。

Conclusion: SqLinear通过理论驱动的均衡分区和线性复杂度时空建模，有效解决了大规模交通预测中的可扩展性与精度权衡问题，为现实世界海量传感器部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Traffic prediction is a core task in intelligent transportation systems and urban-scale decision making. Despite the effectiveness of mainstream neural-network based methods, their deployment in real-world settings with thousands of traffic sensors is jeopardized severely by their poor computational scalability. To address this, the community has attempted to incorporate spatial database partitioning techniques (e.g., Grid, Quadtree, and K-D Tree) to improve model scalability. However, these approaches rely on handcrafted geometric heuristics and often produce irregular or imbalanced data partitions, leading to boundary fragmentation, excessive padding overheads, and degraded model accuracy. In this paper, we propose SqLinear, an efficient and effective architecture for large-scale traffic prediction. First, we design Square Partition, a geometry-adaptive algorithm that partitions massive traffic sensors into balanced, non-overlapping, and near-square spatial regions. Unlike existing heuristic-based designs, Square Partition is theoretically grounded and provides provable guarantees on aspect ratio, balance, and partition utilization, establishing a high-quality foundation for downstream spatiotemporal modeling. Next, we propose a Hierarchical Linear Interaction (HLI) module that abandons the costly attention mechanisms commonly used in Transformer-based spatio-temporal models. HLI efficiently captures both local intra-region dynamics and global inter-region dependencies through a lightweight linear interaction scheme, enabling effective spatiotemporal modeling with linear computational complexity. Extensive experiments on four large-scale traffic datasets and 10 baselines show that SqLinear reduces MAE by 2.30% on average under standard setting and by 5.81% under extreme scalability settings, while reducing training runtime by 13.27%--30.84% in spatial- and horizon-scaling scenarios.

</details>


### [190] [Sim2O: Efficient Offline-to-Online MARL via Joint Action Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21085)
*Bingchang Song,Yiqin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sim2O是一个极简的多智能体离线到在线强化学习框架，通过组合式方法动态混合各智能体的离线与在线动作提议，并利用集中式价值函数评估混合联合动作，无需额外训练目标或结构开销，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线到在线适应在单智能体场景已广泛研究，但在多智能体强化学习（MARL）中尚未充分探索，而复杂协调决策场景对此有迫切需求。

Method: 将适应过程视为组合式过程，动态混合各智能体的离线与在线动作提议生成候选联合动作，利用集中式价值函数评估这些混合组合，无需辅助训练目标或结构开销。

Result: 在多个基准测试中显著优于现有基线方法，证明极简设计在多智能体离线到在线适应中既可行又高效。

Conclusion: 极简主义设计在多智能体离线到在线适应中不仅可行，而且高度有效；Sim2O框架通过组合式动作混合与集中式评估实现了优异的协调策略。

Abstract: Offline-to-online adaptation serves as a pivotal paradigm for mitigating the prohibitive cost of online exploration by bootstrapping reinforcement learning from offline datasets. While this paradigm has been extensively studied in single-agent settings, its extension to Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) remains largely unexplored, despite its critical relevance to complex coordinated decision-making. To bridge this gap, we introduce Sim2O, an elegant and minimalist framework for offline-to-online MARL. Rather than treating adaptation as a monolithic joint decision, Sim2O conceptualizes it as a compositional process. Specifically, candidate joint actions are synthesized by dynamically blending offline and online action proposals across agents. By leveraging a centralized value function to evaluate these hybrid combinations, Sim2O identifies high-value coordination strategies without requiring auxiliary training objectives or structural overhead. Empirical evaluations across diverse benchmarks demonstrate that Sim2O significantly outperforms existing baselines, underscoring that a minimalist design is not only viable but highly effective for multi-agent offline-to-online adaptation.

</details>


### [191] [BASIL: Bayesian Application for Scientific Iteration and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21092)
*Kelvin P. Idanwekhai,Valeriia Kaneva,Stefano Menegatti,Alexander Tropsha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BASIL是一款用户友好的桌面应用程序，采用贝叶斯方法结合特殊采集函数，支持单目标和多目标过程优化，通过图形界面输入实验参数、优化目标和历史数据，构建代理模型并引导优化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实验和过程优化提供一个用户友好、无需编程的桌面工具，降低贝叶斯优化的使用门槛。

Method: 采用贝叶斯方法，结合特殊采集函数，通过图形界面输入参数和目标，构建代理模型并引导优化。

Result: 提供了一个图形化桌面应用BASIL，支持单目标和多目标优化，内置多种预定义代理模型模板。

Conclusion: BASIL可用于优化任意具有已知用户定义输入变量、优化目标和定义输出的实验或过程。

Abstract: We introduce BASIL, a user-friendly desktop application for process optimization. BASIL employs a Bayesian approach, incorporating special acquisition functions that can be used to solve both single and multi-objective optimization problems. It provides a graphical interface that enables users to input their experimental parameters, optimization objectives, and legacy data. This is then used to build surrogate models, which are coupled with acquisition functions to guide and optimize a process towards a desired objective. To facilitate model building, BASIL provides a variety of predefined surrogate model templates. BASIL can be used to optimize any arbitrary experiment or process with known, user-defined input variables, optimization objectives, and defined output.

</details>


### [192] [SLeDGe: Semi-Supervised Learning on Data Streams with Graph Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21096)
*Heechan Moon,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SLeDGe，一种面向数据流的半监督学习方法，通过联合学习预测模型和自适应图结构，在严格内存和标签约束下利用样本间演化关系，显著提升分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据流半监督学习方法依赖固定相似度度量或静态图结构，无法捕捉样本关系随时间的演化，且在高容量数据流和标签稀缺场景下表现受限。

Method: SLeDGe 联合学习预测模型与自适应图结构；维护带标签和无标签的紧凑记忆库并采用不同更新策略以平衡快速适应与历史一致性；通过鼓励关系图稀疏性过滤虚假连接，实现标签监督的有效传播。

Result: 在12个数据集上，SLeDGe 在0.1%标签率下平均相对准确率提升31.7%，在1%标签率下提升14.8%，优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: SLeDGe 通过自适应图结构与记忆管理策略，有效解决了数据流中关系动态演化与标签稀缺的双重挑战，验证了联合学习框架的优越性。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning (SSL) on data streams is challenging due to the continuous evolution of high-volume data and the scarcity of labels. Existing methods are limited in leveraging the intrinsic relationships among samples because they typically rely on fixed similarity measures or static graph structures, which cannot capture how relationships evolve over time. We propose SLeDGe, an SSL method for data streams that jointly learns a predictive model and an adaptive graph structure under strict memory and label constraints. SLeDGe maintains compact labeled and unlabeled memories using distinct update strategies, balancing rapid adaptation to novel features with the retention of historical consistency. In addition, by encouraging sparsity in the relational graph, SLeDGe filters out spurious connections and enables effective propagation of label supervision. Across 12 datasets, SLeDGe outperforms state-of-the-art competitors, achieving average relative accuracy gains of 31.7% with 0.1% labels and 14.8% with 1% labels.

</details>


### [193] [Enhancing Differentially Private Mechanisms via Empirical Bayes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21107)
*Minwoo Kim,Junyong Park,Sungkyu Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于经验贝叶斯估计的去噪方法，仅以简单高斯机制的输出作为输入即可降低均方误差，在直方图发布、主成分分析和线性回归等统计问题中优于现有私有算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私算法虽然能提升效用，但往往过于复杂或计算效率低下，亟需一种简单高效的方法来提升隐私算法效用。

Method: 采用经验贝叶斯估计对简单加性高斯机制的输出进行去噪处理，仅依赖高斯机制的输出作为输入来降低均方误差。

Result: 在直方图发布、主成分分析和线性回归等多个统计问题中，该方法均优于现有私有算法。

Conclusion: 这种简单而强大的经验贝叶斯去噪方法可以有效提升差分隐私算法的效用，且无需复杂计算。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) has become the gold standard for ensuring the privacy protection of machine learning and statistical algorithms in recent decades. A plethora of algorithms and methods have been developed to enhance the utility of DP algorithms while maintaining the same level of DP. However, these are often overly complex or computationally ineffective. We propose a novel approach focusing on denoising the output of the simple additive Gaussian mechanism by adopting the idea of \textit{empirical Bayes estimation}. We highlight that the empirical Bayes approach can reduce the mean-squared error solely by taking the output of the Gaussian mechanism as input. Our numerical studies show that this simple yet powerful approach can be applied to improve upon various statistical problems, including histogram release, principal component analysis, and linear regression, often outperforming existing private algorithms.

</details>


### [194] [Chem2Gen-Bench: Benchmarking Chemical-to-Genetic Translation in Perturbation Response Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21109)
*Yuxiang Lin,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Chem2Gen-Bench基准，用于评估化学扰动和基因扰动在共享靶点上的对齐程度，发现翻译保真度可测量但存在异质性，且在K562审计中基础模型嵌入并未一致优于基因-德尔塔基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常分别评估化学响应预测或基因扰动预测，缺乏对靶点匹配的化学-基因翻译的测试，化学和基因扰动并非自动可互换。

Method: 构建包含260,084个化学扰动和1,099,045个基因扰动谱的Chem2Gen-Bench基准，按细胞-靶点上下文组织，评估成对对齐、检索、协变量关联、特征空间和基础模型嵌入。

Result: 翻译保真度可测量但存在异质性；背景调整增加了成对相似性与检索成功之间的关联，但在评估设置下配对检验的平均检索成功率降低；在K562审计中，基础模型嵌入未一致优于基因-德尔塔基线。

Conclusion: Chem2Gen-Bench提供了一个可审计的框架，用于测试化学和基因扰动何时围绕共享靶点对齐，以及表示增益是否得到匹配扰动证据的支持。

Abstract: Virtual-cell and perturbation models are increasingly used to predict cellular responses for biomedical discovery, but chemical and genetic perturbations are not automatically interchangeable. Existing evaluations often study chemical response prediction or genetic perturbation prediction separately, leaving target-matched chemical-to-genetic translation under-tested. We introduce Chem2Gen-Bench, a benchmark comprising 260,084 chemical and 1,099,045 genetic perturbation profiles organized into cell-target contexts, and evaluate pairwise alignment, retrieval, protocol covariate associations, feature spaces, and foundation-model embeddings. Across matched contexts, translation fidelity is measurable but heterogeneous; background adjustment increases the association between pairwise similarity and retrieval success, while paired tests show lower mean retrieval success after adjustment under the evaluated settings. In a target-matched K562 audit, the evaluated foundation-model embeddings did not consistently improve over gene-delta baselines. Chem2Gen-Bench provides an auditable framework for testing when chemical and genetic perturbations align around shared targets and when representation gains are supported by matched perturbation evidence.

</details>


### [195] [What Accuracy and Gradient Cosine Miss: Evaluating Feedback Alignment via Scale Stability, Reference Validity, and Depth Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21126)
*Yuren Hao,Xiang Wan,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现反馈对齐（FA）方法的标准评估指标（任务准确率和余弦相似度）存在两个隐蔽失效模式：（1）测量退化：在末端LayerNorm残差架构中，BP参考梯度崩溃到数值下限导致余弦相似度不可解释；（2）聚合坍塌：聚合余弦掩盖了逐层异质性，导致信用分配集中于网络一端。作者提出包含尺度稳定性、参考有效性和深度效用的三检查诊断协议，并建议使用逐层而非聚合余弦报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前反馈对齐（FA）方法的评估依赖于任务准确率和余弦相似度这两个指标，但研究表明这对指标存在两个独立的失效模式，且这些失效在现行报告标准下是"沉默的"（不产生任何预警信号）。作者旨在揭示这些失效模式并提出更完善的诊断评估协议。

Method: 作者通过理论分析和实验验证了两种失效模式：测量退化（在末端LayerNorm残差架构中，BP梯度数值崩溃导致余弦不可解释）和聚合坍塌（逐层余弦异质性被聚合统计掩盖）。提出了基于三个检查（尺度稳定性、参考有效性和深度效用）的诊断协议，以及逐层余弦报告方案。设计了每块尺度惩罚来缓解残差尺度爆炸问题。

Result: 标准报告对（任务准确率+聚合余弦）在审计的所有案例中均未给出任何失效信号，而作者提出的协议能以宽校准裕度识别所有失效。两种失效模式因果独立：每块尺度惩罚可缓解模式1（残差尺度爆炸驱动参考崩溃）但不影响模式2（余弦排名与每个功能性指标相矛盾）。

Conclusion: 现有FA方法评估标准存在严重缺陷，可能导致研究者建立在非功能性信用分配的基础上。本文提出的诊断协议能有效识别隐蔽失效，为开发真正能训练深层网络的FA方法提供可操作指导。

Abstract: Despite the success of deep learning, training deep networks in biologically plausible and hardware-efficient ways remains an open challenge. Feedback alignment (FA) methods address this by replacing backpropagation's symmetric backward weights with fixed random matrices, but their effectiveness depends critically on whether they can be accurately evaluated. The standard evaluation relies on two quantities: task accuracy and cosine similarity between the method's credit signal and the backpropagation gradient. We show that this reporting pair is insufficient by identifying two independent failure modes, both silent under current reporting: (1) measurement degeneracy, where the BP reference gradient collapses to the numerical floor in terminal-LayerNorm residual architectures, rendering cosine uninterpretable; and (2) aggregation collapse, where the aggregate cosine masks layerwise heterogeneity that concentrates credit at one end of the network. To address these limitations, we propose a diagnostic evaluation protocol based on three checks -- scale stability, reference validity, and depth utility -- together with per-layer rather than aggregate cosine reporting. Across multiple architectures and methods, the standard reporting pair gives no signal of failure in any audited case, while our protocol identifies all failures with wide calibration margins. The two failure modes are causally independent: a per-block scale penalty alleviates Mode 1 (residual scale explosion driving reference collapse) without affecting Mode 2 (cosine ranking that contradicts every functional metric we measured). Identifying these silent failures prevents researchers from building on non-functional credit assignment and provides actionable guidance for developing FA methods that genuinely train deep layers.

</details>


### [196] [Horizon Adaptive Offline Policy Learning via Value Stitching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21136)
*Kexin Zheng,Xianyuan Zhan,Xintao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VAST方法，通过动态、自适应地组合可变长度回报来学习精确的值函数，替代传统固定步长值备份，在50个OGBench任务中特别是在长视界、高复杂度的离线强化学习任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的时序差分学习存在随视界累积的值估计偏差，而n步TD备份和Q-chunking等扩展视界方法采用固定的、刚性的视界建模方式，难以灵活捕捉长视界、多阶段任务中复杂的价值结构。

Method: VAST的核心是：（1）引入一个以未来状态和视界长度为条件的辅助值函数，通过直接数据监督学习；（2）设计一个拼接策略，能选择奖励最大化的视界长度和未来子目标，实现视界自适应的值拼接。通过递归的、视界自适应的值组合替代固定视界备份。

Result: 在OGBench的50个任务上，VAST优于固定步长的扩展视界方法和生成式值函数的离线强化学习基线，在高复杂度、长视界决策任务中表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 动态视界组合的值更新方式能够产生强大的离线策略学习方案，直接估计并组合基于可操作子目标的可变长度回报，为离线策略优化提供准确且可贪婪利用的值监督信号。

Abstract: Learning accurate value functions plays a decisive role for reinforcement learning (RL) agents to solve long-horizon, complex tasks. Conventional temporal-difference (TD) learning objectives suffer from value-estimation bias that accumulates over the horizon, while extended-horizon modeling methods, such as n-step TD backups and Q-chunking, adopt a rigid, fixed-horizon value-modeling recipe that is often not flexible enough to capture complex value structures in long-horizon, multi-stage tasks. In this paper, we show that enabling value updates with dynamic horizon composition can yield a strong offline policy learning scheme. Our method, Horizon Adaptive Offline Policy Learning via VAlue STitching (VAST), replaces fixed-horizon backups with recursive, horizon-adaptive value composition. Its key ingredient is to couple value optimization with a future state- and horizon-length-conditioned auxiliary value function that is learned through direct data supervision, and a stitching policy that optimally selects the reward-maximizing horizon length and future sub-goal to achieve horizon-adaptive value stitching. This design enables direct estimation and compositional "stitching" of variable-length returns grounded in actionable sub-goal states, providing an accurate and greedily exploitable value-supervision signal for offline policy optimization. Across 50 tasks on OGBench, VAST outperforms fixed-step, extended-horizon methods, and generative-value offline RL baselines, achieving strong performance particularly in high-complexity, long-horizon decision-making tasks.

</details>


### [197] [Dead-Direction Signatures: A Cheap Spectral Reading of Singular Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21158)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar,P. J. Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出死方向签名（DDS），一种通过激活矩阵或Fisher-Gram谱的闭式谱分析替代昂贵SGLD的奇异结构读取方法，能在无贝叶斯后验链的情况下，以极低成本追踪网络的秩亏缺和局部学习系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLC估计需要通过SGLD进行贝叶斯后验采样，需要大量前向-反向传播（10⁴-10⁶次）且需要逐任务校准，计算成本极高。本文旨在设计一种廉价的闭式谱分析方法，直接读取网络的奇异几何结构信息。

Method: 提出死方向签名（DDS）框架，核心包括：(1) 激活侧与Fisher侧谱之间的结构性关联预测；(2) 秩乘体积恒等式，即log det⁺(G)的斜率可计数死方向数r∈{1,2,3,4}，且斜率比2.0,3.1,4.0接近理论预测的2,3,4；(3) 在降秩回归和非线性模加变换器上进行验证。

Result: 在降秩回归中，校准后的LLC恢复λ的均值达99%，DDS可观测变量按照框架预测的符号追踪秩；在非线性模加变换器上，DDS能在18个数量级范围内区分d_model，而同预算下的校准LLC呈秩平坦。

Conclusion: DDS作为LLC的补充工具，提供了方向性、层级局部化的死方向读取能力，通过闭式谱分析实现，替代了昂贵的SGLD后验链，极大降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Singular learning theory characterises the complexity of a deep network through the geometry of its loss singularities. The local learning coefficient (LLC), the standard estimator of Watanabe's real log canonical threshold (RLCT, $λ$), reads this geometry as an integrated Bayesian scalar through SGLD, which needs per-task calibration and $10^4$-$10^6$ forward-backward passes per checkpoint. We introduce Dead-Direction Signatures (DDS), a family of cheap closed-form spectral readings of singular structure: each reads a network's activation matrix or per-sample-gradient Fisher-Gram at a chosen layer, replacing the SGLD posterior chain with spectral linear algebra. The readings rest on a dead-direction framework that predicts a structural correlation between activation- and Fisher-side spectra at any singular minimum, and a rank-multiplicative volume identity that single-eigenvalue monitors cannot produce: the active-volume $\log\det^{+}(G)$ slope counts the dead directions, tracking the rank-deficit $r$ across $r \in \{1,2,3,4\}$ (slope ratios $2.0, 3.1, 4.0$ at $r{=}2,3,4$ against the predicted $2,3,4$), where the smallest eigenvalue is rank-blind. On reduced-rank regression with closed-form $λ$, calibrated LLC recovers $λ$ at $99\%$ mean and the DDS observables rank-track it at the framework-predicted sign; on a non-linear modular-addition transformer DDS separates $d_{\mathrm{model}}$ across eighteen orders of magnitude where calibrated LLC at the protocol budget is rank-flat. Complementary to LLC's integrated posterior reading, DDS gives a directional, layer-local handle on a network's dead directions, read in closed form from its activation and gradient spectra.

</details>


### [198] [Inverting the Bellman Equation: From $Q$-Values to World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21173)
*Alistair Letcher,Mattie Fellows,Alexander D. Goldie,Jonathan Richens,Jakob N. Foerster,Oliver Richardson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明基于价值的强化学习智能体在足够丰富的奖励函数（如目标条件RL）训练下，会隐式编码一个唯一且准确的世界模型。作者提出P-learning算法从Q值中解码该模型，并给出编码真实转移核的充分条件，实验表明即使条件不满足，智能体也能学习准确动力学，且隐式世界模型具有跨分布泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统上基于模型与无模型强化学习视为截然不同范式的观点，证明无模型价值基智能体在足够丰富的奖励函数训练下实际上隐式编码了世界模型，从而弥合两种范式之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出P-learning算法，作为Q-learning的逆模拟，通过从智能体的Q值、策略和奖励中采样来解码其内部环境模型。提供在随机和确定性MDP上编码真实转移核P的充分条件（关于目标的类型和数量），并在Reacher、MountainCar和随机FourRooms环境中进行实验验证。

Result: 实验表明即使在理论条件被违反的情况下，智能体仍能编码准确的动力学。更令人惊讶的是，仅由位置训练的Reacher智能体的隐式世界模型，在速度相关分布外目标上训练的策略能达到准最优性能，揭示了智能体存在隐藏的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 价值基智能体隐式编码世界模型，这为理解基于模型、无模型和目标条件RL之间的联系提供了新视角，并揭示了智能体具有超出预期的泛化能力。

Abstract: Model-based and model-free reinforcement learning are traditionally viewed as separate paradigms: instead of learning a model of the transition kernel $P$, model-free agents typically estimate value functions tied to a specific policy and reward. In this paper, we challenge this dichotomy by proving that value-based agents trained on a sufficiently rich set of reward functions, e.g. using goal-conditioned RL, implicitly encode a unique and accurate world model. To extract this model in practice, we introduce \textit{$P$-learning}, an inverse analogue to $Q$-learning that samples from an agent's $Q$-values, policies and rewards to decode its internal model of the environment. We then provide sufficient conditions on the type and number of goals for which agents encode the true kernel $P$, covering both stochastic and deterministic MDPs over finite or continuous state spaces. Even when our assumptions are violated, we empirically demonstrate that agents trained on a handful of reward functions encode accurate dynamics in $\texttt{Reacher}$, $\texttt{MountainCar}$ and stochastic variants of $\texttt{FourRooms}$. Surprisingly, we find that policies trained exclusively on a \texttt{Reacher} agent's implicit world model are quasi-optimal on out-of-distribution, velocity-based goals despite position-only training -- suggesting that agents contain hidden generalisation capabilities and providing a new lens into the connection between model-based, model-free, and goal-conditioned RL.

</details>


### [199] [Rejections Based on Predictive Uncertainty Enable Reliable Routine Soil Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21179)
*Jonas Schmidinger,Robin Gebbers,Marc-Olivier Gasser,Viacheslav Barkov,G. Mick Wu,Viacheslav I. Adamchuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"拒绝-重测"框架，将光谱学与机器学习结合，通过不确定性引导的拒绝机制筛选预测不可靠的样本进行传统实验室重测，在满足精度要求的同时降低测量成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统土壤属性测量方法准确但昂贵耗时，光谱学结合机器学习虽快速经济但预测可靠性不足，需要一种平衡成本与可靠性的方案。

Method: 提出reject-to-remeasure框架：先用光谱学分析土壤样本，再用概率模型（TabPFNv2.5和TabICLv2）进行预测，当预测不确定性超过预设质量阈值时拒绝该预测，被拒绝的样本改用传统实验室方法重测。

Result: 在魁北克区域可见-近红外光谱土壤库上验证，该框架能在满足用户定义精度要求的同时降低测量成本。

Conclusion: reject-to-remeasure框架可以有效将光学光谱学整合到常规实验室工作流程中，在保证可靠性的前提下实现成本效益。

Abstract: Soil properties relevant to agricultural and environmental applications are conventionally measured using elaborate laboratory methods involving physical and chemical processing. While highly accurate, these conventional methods are costly and time-consuming. In contrast, optical spectroscopy paired with machine learning enables rapid and cost-effective predictions of multiple soil properties. However, spectroscopic modelling is often considered unreliable, as the predictive accuracy varies between soil properties and individual samples. To balance this trade-off between cost and reliability, we introduce reject-to-remeasure: an AI-based measurement framework that combines probabilistic modelling with uncertainty-guided rejection. In this framework, soil samples are first analysed using spectroscopy, after which predictions are rejected if their predictive uncertainty exceeds predefined quality constraints. Rejected samples are subsequently remeasured using conventional laboratory procedures. On a regional visible-near-infrared spectral soil library from Québec, we demonstrate that reject-to-remeasure with modern foundation models (TabPFNv2.5 and TabICLv2) can facilitate the integration of optical spectroscopy into routine laboratory workflows while meeting user-defined accuracy requirements and reducing measurement costs.

</details>


### [200] [TF-SNO: Time-Frequency Gated Spectral Neural Operators for Learning Non-Stationary Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21189)
*Yitian Zhou,Chaoning Zhang,Zhenzhen Huang,Haoxuan Yu,Jiaquan Zhang,Yiran Li,Fan Mo,Kuien Liu,Jie Zou,Caiyan Qin,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出时间-频率门控谱神经算子(TF-SNO)，通过状态自适应的可学习时间-频率门控机制，使谱响应随非平稳PDE系统的演化状态动态调整，在不引入显式时间维度的情况下提升长时程预测精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳偏微分方程在科学计算中普遍存在，其主频内容和能量分布随时间漂移。现有谱神经算子在整个 rollout 阶段使用共享的谱响应，导致与非平稳系统中时变谱的不匹配问题。

Method: 提出TF-SNO框架，核心是在谱块内部引入可学习的时间-频率门控机制：从当前状态中提取紧凑的频域和物理空间统计量，生成调制系数，使谱响应随动力学演化而自适应调整。无需显式时间维度或时间嵌入，隐式地从演化状态中学习时间变化，并嵌入自适应算子块以捕获多尺度特征。

Result: 在6个非平稳PDE基准测试（1D和2D）上，TF-SNO显著降低了预测误差，提升了鲁棒性，尤其在长时程 rollout 中增益明显，优于多个强基线方法。

Conclusion: 状态依赖的谱自适应机制在建模非平稳物理系统中是有效的，能够解决传统谱神经算子在时变系统中的谱响应失配问题。

Abstract: Non-stationary partial differential equations (PDEs) arise throughout scientific computing, where the dominant frequency content and energy distribution can drift over time. While efficient in PDE solving, many spectral neural operators apply a shared spectral response across rollout stages, leading to mismatch with time-varying spectra in non-stationary systems. To address this issue, we propose Time-Frequency Gated Spectral Neural Operator (TF-SNO), a state-adaptive framework with learnable time-frequency gating inside spectral blocks. TF-SNO extracts compact frequency-domain and physical-space statistics from the current state to generate modulation coefficients, enabling the spectral response to evolve with the dynamics. TF-SNO learns temporal variation implicitly from the evolving state without introducing an explicit time dimension or time embedding, keeping the modeling complexity low. We further embed the adaptive operator blocks to accurately capture the multi-scale features, thereby improving long-horizon stability. Experiments on six non-stationary PDE benchmarks in 1D and 2D demonstrate that TF-SNO significantly reduces prediction errors and improves robustness compared to strong baselines, with particularly clear gains in long rollout, suggesting the effectiveness of state-dependent spectral adaptation in modeling non-stationary physical systems.

</details>


### [201] [DCD-PFN: A Decoupling-Aware Foundation Model for Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21212)
*Zhengkang Guan,Yikang Chen,Yi He,Yunze Tong,Zijing Hu,Haoyuan Qian,Fei Wu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DCD-PFN，一种基于解耦感知的因果发现基础模型，通过预训练在多样化合成SCM上学习样本级解耦权重，实现马尔可夫边界识别和全局因果图重构，在零样本泛化中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果发现算法难以处理高度非线性和噪声系统，且存在计算瓶颈；现有基于PFN的基础模型在零样本推理上表现出色，但其在显式结构因果发现方面的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出DCD-PFN，采用解耦感知范式：通过预训练在多样化合成SCM上学习样本级解耦权重，实现马尔可夫边界识别；利用并行化局部发现高效重构全局因果图。

Result: 实验表明，该基础模型在零样本泛化任务中达到了鲁棒的性能。

Conclusion: DCD-PFN通过解耦感知的基础模型范式，有效弥合了PFN在显式结构因果发现中的空白，实现了鲁棒的零样本因果结构学习。

Abstract: Causal discovery is critical for understanding complex data-generating mechanisms, yet traditional algorithms often struggle with highly non-linear and noisy systems, or suffer from severe computational bottlenecks. Recent tabular foundation models based on Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs) have demonstrated remarkable zero-shot inference capabilities, but their potential for explicit structural causal discovery remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose DCD-PFN, a decoupling-aware foundation model for causal discovery. Instead of directly amortizing global graph reconstruction, DCD-PFN focuses on local causal discovery through a decoupling-based paradigm. Through pre-training on diverse synthetic Structural Causal Models (SCMs), the model learns sample-wise decoupling weights that enable Markov boundary (MB) identification. Furthermore, by leveraging parallelized local discovery, DCD-PFN efficiently reconstructs global causal graphs while remaining grounded in the theoretical foundations of decoupling-based causal discovery. Experiments demonstrate that our foundation model achieves robust zero-shot generalization.

</details>


### [202] [Sakana Fugu Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21228)
*Yujin Tang,Edoardo Cetin,Jinglue Xu,Qi Sun,Stefan Nielsen,Vincent Richard,Haruto Goda,Iaroslav Tymchenko,Nhan Nguyen,Hyunin Lee,Mari Ashiga,Shashank Kotyan,So Kuroki,Tarin Clanuwat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Sakana Fugu系列模型，这是一种通过动态编排多个LLM代理协同工作来超越单一模型能力的集体智能系统。Fugu模型本身是经训练的语言模型，能够理解用户查询并动态构建代理化脚手架来解决问题，在SWE-Bench Pro、Terminal Bench等多项基准测试中取得了领先性能。研究团队发布了平衡性能与延迟的Fugu模型和优先答案质量的Fugu-Ultra模型，并描述了结合大规模微调、进化算法和强化学习的训练范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同前沿大语言模型在各自领域逐渐形成专业分化，如何将多个LLM的个体专长整合为集体智能系统成为一个自然的研究目标。本文旨在开发能够动态编排多LLM代理团队以发挥集体智能的协调模型。

Method: 训练Fugu系列语言模型作为协调器，使其能够理解用户查询并动态构建代理化脚手架；采用大规模微调、进化算法和强化学习相结合的混合训练范式。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Pro、Terminal Bench、LiveCodeBench、GPQA-Diamond、Humanity's Last Exam和CharXiv Reasoning等多项挑战性基准测试中取得领先性能（state-of-the-art）。

Conclusion: 多代理系统和动态查询自适应代理化脚手架是通向AI能力下一个前沿的有前景路径。通过集体智能可以超越单个LLM的性能上限。

Abstract: The capabilities of frontier Large Language Models (LLMs) continue to advance, with different providers increasingly specializing in distinct domains. This raises a natural next objective: how to combine the individual specializations of various LLMs into a collectively intelligent system. To this end, we report the development of Sakana Fugu, a family of orchestrator models that harness and amplify the capabilities of an LLM agent team. Fugu models are themselves language models trained to understand user queries and dynamically devise agentic scaffolds to solve them. Through these adaptive scaffolds, Fugu accesses performance beyond any individual LLM agent, achieving state-of-the-art results compared to other publicly accessible models across a range of challenging tasks, including SWE-Bench Pro, Terminal Bench, LiveCodeBench, GPQA-Diamond, Humanity's Last Exam, and CharXiv Reasoning. We release two models: Fugu, which balances performance with latency for everyday use, and Fugu-Ultra, which prioritizes answer quality on the hardest problems. We describe our training paradigm, which encompasses large-scale fine-tuning, evolutionary algorithms, and reinforcement learning approaches, along with the infrastructure and core design principles that turn these methods into a production system. We hope this report encourages further research into multi-agent systems and dynamic, query-adaptive agentic scaffolds as a path toward the next frontier of AI capabilities, accessed through collective intelligence.

</details>


### [203] [Comparative Evaluation of Machine Learning and Deep Learning Models for Wound-Rotor Synchronous Motor Performance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21230)
*Kıvanç Doğan,Ahmet Orhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了8种机器学习和深度学习模型在预测电励磁同步电机转矩和效率方面的性能，发现基于神经网络的模型（尤其是FT-Transformer，R²=0.9928）优于树模型，且推理速度比有限元分析快数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电励磁同步电机（WRSM）作为稀土永磁电机的替代方案具有重要价值，但其设计参数空间巨大，传统有限元分析计算成本过高，而现有研究中机器学习代理模型缺乏对深度学习架构的全面比较和针对WRSM的专门基准测试。

Method: 采用拉丁超立方采样在Motor-CAD仿真环境中生成3351个样本，系统比较了来自4个算法家族的8种机器学习与深度学习模型（含FT-Transformer等最新架构），每个模型使用10个不同随机种子训练并通过Optuna超参数优化，进行了计算成本-精度帕累托分析。

Result: 基于神经网络的模型系统性地优于树模型，FT-Transformer达到最高精度（R²=0.9928），单次预测仅需0.33毫秒，相比有限元分析实现数个数量级的加速。使用R²、MAE、RMSE和MAPE四个指标进行多维度评估。

Conclusion: 深度学习代理模型可有效替代高成本有限元分析，加速WRSM设计优化，FT-Transformer在精度和速度之间实现了最佳平衡。

Abstract: Wound rotor synchronous motors have emerged as a strong alternative that eliminates dependence on REEs. However, WRSM design requires the simultaneous optimization of numerous geometric and electromagnetic parameters, and the high computational cost of conventional finite element analysis severely limits the rapid exploration of the large parameter space. Although there are machine-learning-based surrogate modeling studies in the literature, they generally compare only a limited number of models, exclude deep learning architectures, and do not provide a comprehensive benchmark specific to WRSM. In this study, the performance of a total of eight machine learning and deep learning models from four different algorithmic families was systematically compared for the prediction of WRSM torque and motor efficiency. On a dataset of 3351 samples generated using Latin Hypercube Sampling in the Motor-CAD simulation environment, each model was trained with 10 different random seed values and tuned via Optuna hyperparameter optimization. Different from the existing literature, this study jointly offers a broad model spectrum including recent deep learning architectures such as FT Transformer, a multi-seed reproducibility protocol, and a Pareto analysis of the computational cost-accuracy trade-off. The results revealed that neural-network-based models systematically outperform tree-based models. The FT-Transformer model achieved the highest single-model accuracy with R^2 = 0.9928, producing predictions in 0.33 milliseconds and thus obtaining several orders of magnitude speedup compared to FEA. Model performances were evaluated in a multidimensional manner using R^2, MAE, RMSE, and MAPE metrics.

</details>


### [204] [Does RoPE Prevent or Degrade Retrieval Heads? A Mechanistic Analysis Across Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21249)
*Cengizhan Bayram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了旋转位置编码（RoPE）中的低频维度对检索头（retrieval heads）功能的关键作用。发现检索头是长上下文回忆的因果必要组件，RoPE的高频/低频维度划分比theta基超参数更能解释检索头功能，零化低频维度会剂量依赖性地破坏召回性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证两个假设：(1) RoPE是否会阻止检索头的形成或损害其功能；(2) 高theta基超参数是否减少检索头数量。旨在揭示RoPE与检索头之间的因果机制。

Method: 在四种开源7-8B模型（覆盖多头注意力和分组查询注意力，theta范围100倍）上，使用配对种子的needle-in-a-haystack测试、层聚类置换和因果头掩码方法。对检索头进行低频RoPE维度零化补丁实验。

Result: (1) 检索头是因果必要的：掩码87个检索头使OLMo-2召回率从1.00降至0.00，随机头无影响；(2) 高theta不减少检索头数量（LLaMA-3.1 theta=500K有47个头，LLaMA-2 theta=10K有42个）；(3) 范数-效用关系存在家族特异性且方向相反；(4) 零化检索头的最低频RoPE维度会剂量依赖性地降低召回率（1.00至0.18），而随机维度无影响。

Conclusion: 因果变量是RoPE频率而非范数-效用；低频RoPE维度对检索头的功能至关重要；该方向在5个模型（涉及4个模型家族和2个规模）中得到复现。

Abstract: Retrieval heads, attention heads that copy information from earlier context to the current position, have been proposed as the mechanistic substrate for long-context recall. Rotary position embeddings (RoPE) rotate queries and keys by frequencies decaying with a base hyperparameter theta, and a natural hypothesis is that this rotation either prevents retrieval heads from forming or degrades their function. We test both across four open-weight 7-8B models spanning multi-head and grouped-query attention and a 100x range of theta, using paired-seed needle-in-a-haystack tests, layer-clustered permutation, and causal head-masking. (i) Retrieval heads are causally necessary: masking the 87 detected heads in OLMo-2 collapses recall from 1.00 to 0.00, while masking matched random heads has no effect; this replicates in Qwen. (ii) Higher theta does not reduce retrieval-head count (LLaMA-3.1 at theta=500K has 47 heads vs LLaMA-2 at theta=10K with 42), refuting the prevention hypothesis. (iii) The norm-utility relation is family-specific and significant in opposite directions (Qwen d=-0.49, OLMo d=+0.50, both significant; LLaMA null); since OLMo and LLaMA-3.1 share theta=500K yet differ, the effect is not theta-driven. (iv) Building on Chiang and Yogatama (2025), a controlled patch shows that zeroing the lowest-frequency RoPE dimensions of retrieval heads degrades recall dose-dependently (1.00 to 0.18 when 32 of 128 dimensions are zeroed, vs 0.98 for random dimensions); the effect is head-specific and task-specific. The causal variable is RoPE frequency, not norm-utility. The direction holds in all five models patched (OLMo-2, Qwen2.5-7B/14B, Gemma-2, Mistral) across four lineages and two scales. We do not claim cross-model magnitude. Code and a paired-seed harness are released.

</details>
