  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer内部计算不透明，现有解释性方法在模块边界粒度上的归因分析不足，需要一种无需模型特定重训练或逐操作解释器设计的诊断工具。

Method: 将每一层视为动态图，节点为token表示和注意力输出，以ATT和MLP为模块边界；使用LIG在非线性模块边界应用集合到集合的积分梯度，通过L2标量化和层级组合实现完整归因。

Result: 在BERT-base和PTB上，最佳配置为：ATT基线使用目标token嵌入，MLP基线使用ATT输出（a=0）或零向量；LIG能有效评估模块级组合与层级归因的一致性，并分离追踪ATT和MLP的层内信息流。

Conclusion: LIG是一种有效的模块边界粒度的XAI诊断工具，无需模型重训练，可揭示Transformer层内信息流动机制。

Abstract: Transformers achieve strong performance, but their internal computations remain opaque. We view each Transformer layer as a dynamic graph whose nodes are token representations and per-head attention outputs, with Multi-Head Attention (ATT) and MLP as module boundaries. On this graph we use LIG (Layer-wise Integrated Gradients), which applies set-to-set Integrated Gradients (IG) at nonlinear module boundaries. Set-to-set IG applies IG to a map from a set of input token representations to a set of output representations, evaluating token-to-token contributions, which is not standard in prior IG applications. This extends IG from the usual scalar-objective setting to set-to-set maps via an L2 scalarization, and composes within-layer contributions in the spirit of Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP), with IG completeness playing the role of LRP-style conservation at each boundary. We use LIG to analyze (i) the agreement between module-wise composition and layer-whole attribution under an L2 criterion, and (ii) within-layer information flow by tracing separated ATT and MLP contributions. On BERT-base and PTB, configurations that best preserved within-layer consistency used the target token's embedding as the ATT baseline and either the ATT output at a=0 or Zero as the MLP baseline. We therefore present LIG as a diagnostic XAI tool at module-boundary granularity, without model-specific retraining or per-operation interpreter design. Code is available at https://github.com/eightsuzuki/layer-wise-integrated-gradients.

</details>


### [242] [The Cost Geometry of Belief: finite-resource inference under noisy observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21585)
*Laurent Caraffa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在信念空间中引入基于最优传输和Fisher信息的代价几何，证明了三个关键不变性结果：推断的"墙"（确定性不可达）、"诚实性"（eikonal代价与Fisher信息几何等价）和"刚性"（双曲几何与Gaussian分布的最优性），揭示了代价单位的相对性和精度的几何下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为信念空间（信念即贝叶斯后验概率密度）赋予一个合理的代价度量，该度量应同时反映改变信念所需的成本（Wasserstein最优传输）和精度本身的代价（Fisher信息），从而统一观察不确定性和物理不确定性对信念系统的影响。

Method: 将信念空间装备为Wasserstein最优传输代价，并用Fisher信息进行共形加权（conformal reweighting），得到区别于Fisher-Rao度量的新几何结构。在该共形类（本质上是位置-尺度族）上分析代价几何的不变量。

Result: 得出三个不变性结果：(1) 墙——一旦代价主导Fisher信息，达到确定性（完美信念）需要无限距离；(2) 诚实性——诚实的（eikonal）代价，即每纳特长度相同，导致几何与Fisher信息成正比；(3) 刚性——该几何为双曲几何，且Stam界表明Gaussian分布是最双曲的位置-尺度信念，即最优分布。代价单位的改变仅缩放几何但保留上述不变性，表明只有相对代价有意义。

Conclusion: 信念空间中达到给定精度的代价存在几何下限，且在确定性处发散。热力学固定了代价单位并提供了框架的动机；所有结果以纳特（nats）为单位，本质上是几何的。

Abstract: We equip the space of beliefs with a cost geometry (what it costs to pass from one belief to another): optimal transport in Wasserstein space, reweighted conformally by Fisher information (the price of the precision at stake), distinct from the Fisher-Rao metric. In the setting we consider, a finite machine maintains a digital twin of a system; observing the territory through finite, noisy sensors, we model its coherent output as a belief: a probability density over states, the Bayes posterior. Certainty (the perfect twin) is denied twice, by observation and by physics, both read off the Fisher information. On the conformal class, essentially location-scale, three results emerge, all invariants of one change of cost unit. A wall: a well-posed inference rejects certainty to infinite distance as soon as the cost dominates the Fisher information (necessity conjectured beyond power laws). An honesty: an honest (eikonal) cost, each nat the same length everywhere, selects the geometries proportional to the Fisher information. A rigidity: these geometries are hyperbolic, and the Stam bound crowns the Gaussian, the most hyperbolic location-scale belief. Changing the unit dilates the geometry yet preserves the wall, the curvature ordering, and the extremality of the Gaussian: an absolute cost says nothing, only relative cost carries meaning, the value -1/4 being one of its images. The cost of reaching a given precision then has a geometric floor diverging at certainty. Thermodynamics fixes the cost unit and motivates this framework; the results are geometric, in nats.

</details>


### [243] [FAST: A Framework for Aligned Sampling and Training in Parallel Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21587)
*Bonan Wang,Letian Tao,Bin Shuai,Jiaxin Gao,Wenxin Zhao,Wei Xiong,Kehua Sheng,Bo Zhang,Yang Guan,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FAST框架，通过动态并行采样对齐（DPSA）和缩放掩码填充优化（SMPO）解决同步并行强化学习中的掉队者效应，实现闭环自动驾驶模拟中至少1.78倍的加速且保持统计无偏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在闭环自动驾驶中面临采样效率瓶颈，标准并行采样存在掉队者效应（单个环境提前终止导致整个批次同步重置），造成样本利用率低下和重置延迟过高。

Method: 提出FAST同步并行框架：1) DPSA通过虚拟延续扩展已终止的轨迹来保持向量化同步，基于并行片段终止率动态触发全局截断；2) SMPO利用有效性掩码和自适应损失归一化消除辅助填充数据带来的偏差。

Result: 相比单片段基线，FAST实现至少1.78倍的墙上时钟加速，同时保持统计无偏性。

Conclusion: FAST有效消除了同步并行采样中过早重置的瓶颈，在不牺牲数据多样性的前提下显著提升采样效率，且理论一致性严格保持。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning is pivotal for closed-loop autonomous driving yet remains constrained by severe bottlenecks in sampling efficiency. Standard parallel sampling mitigates this but suffers from the straggler effect, where the premature termination of a single environment necessitates a synchronized batch re-initialization, leading to suboptimal sample utilization and prohibitive re-initialization latency. To address this, we propose FAST, a synchronous parallel framework tailored for closed-loop simulation. Specifically, FAST employs Dynamic Parallel Sampling Alignment (DPSA) to maintain vectorization synchronization by extending terminated episodes via virtual continuation, thereby decoupling the sampling loop from individual terminations. By dynamically triggering global truncation based on the termination rate of parallel clips, FAST effectively eliminates the bottleneck of premature resets without sacrificing data diversity. Furthermore, to strictly preserve theoretical consistency, we incorporate a Scaled Mask-Padding Optimization (SMPO) that leverages validity masking and adaptive loss normalization to nullify the bias from auxiliary padding data. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that FAST achieves at least a 1.78 times wall-clock speedup over the single-clip baseline while preserving statistical unbiasedness.

</details>


### [244] [Geometric and Information Compression of Representations in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21593)
*Linara Adilova,Henning Petzka,Asja Fischer,Bernhard C. Geiger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度神经网络中互信息（MI）与几何压缩之间的关系，发现低MI并不必然对应几何压缩，两者关系复杂且非线性，可能受泛化能力混淆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的潜在表示可从信息论和几何维度刻画，但两者之间的关系尚不明确。核心问题是：低互信息是否必然意味着几何压缩，反之亦然。

Method: 使用条件熵瓶颈（CEB）网络和连续dropout网络，以类内聚类作为几何压缩的度量，结合理论可靠的互信息估计，在分类任务中通过受控噪声注入方案研究MI、几何压缩与泛化之间的相互作用。

Result: 低MI并不可靠地对应几何压缩，两者呈负相关且非线性关系，在不同的训练设置下这种关系甚至可能反转。

Conclusion: 泛化能力可能是MI与几何压缩之间关系的混淆因素，而非直接后果。

Abstract: Deep neural networks transform input data into latent representations that support a wide range of downstream tasks. These representations can be characterized along information-theoretic and geometric dimensions, but their relationship remains poorly understood. A central open question is whether low mutual information (MI) between inputs and representations necessarily implies geometrically compressed latent spaces and vice versa. We investigate this question using class-wise clustering as a measure of geometric compression and theoretically sound MI estimation in conditional entropy bottleneck (CEB) networks and continuous dropout networks. We evaluate the interplay between MI, geometric compression, and generalization on classification tasks under controlled noise injection schemes. Our findings show that low MI does not reliably correspond to geometric compression, and that the connection between the two is more nuanced than often assumed. Indeed, our experiments reveal a negative and nonlinear relationship that can reverse when varying training setup. Our results put forward a hypothesis that generalization acts as a potential confounder in this connection rather than being their direct consequence.

</details>


### [245] [Learning to Place Guards by Reinforcement: A Geo-Free Neural Policy for the Vertex-Guard Art Gallery Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21604)
*Domagoj Ševerdija,Jurica Maltar,Nathan Chappel,Domagoj Matijević*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经组合优化（NCO）在顶点守卫艺术画廊问题上的表现，发现强化学习训练的策略在推理时存在覆盖率不足的尾部分布。通过冻结编码器并用小型分类器读取其嵌入，可以大幅缩小可行性差距，证明强化学习训练的表征已内化了几何知识，残余失败源于解码器校准而非知识缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经组合优化策略实际学习到了什么，而非仅仅关注其解码器表达了什么。具体而言，探究强化学习训练的策略是否真正内化了求解NP难问题所需的几何知识。

Method: 在顶点守卫艺术画廊问题上，训练一个基于注意力指针网络的策略，使用覆盖率感知的奖励进行强化学习。训练后冻结编码器，用一个小型单样本分类器（仍保持几何无关推理）读取编码器嵌入，对比原始策略与分类器辅助方法在分布内外的可行性表现。

Result: 冻结编码器+小型分类器的方法几乎消除了可行性差距，在分布内外以及高达约五倍训练范围的场景下，将未覆盖多边形数量减少约一个数量级，代价是守卫数量略有增加。

Conclusion: 强化学习训练的表征已经编码了解决问题所需的几何知识，残余的不可行失败源于解码器的校准问题而非知识缺失。冻结编码器进行探针分析是一种实用的方法，可以检验神经组合优化器内部化了什么知识。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) has shown that policies trained by reinforcement can construct strong solutions to NP-hard problems directly from raw instances. What such a policy actually learns, as opposed to what its decoder expresses, remains much less clear. We study this distinction on the vertex-guard Art Gallery Problem, the NP-hard task of choosing polygon vertices from which to observe an entire region. A pointer-network policy is trained from a coverage-aware reward over its own rollouts under the constraint we call geo-free inference: at test time it sees only vertex coordinates, with no visibility computation and no geometric oracle. The policy places guards economically but leaves a tail of under-covered polygons that widens far beyond the training range. To locate the cause, we freeze the trained encoder and read its embeddings with a small single-shot classifier, still geo-free at inference. The classifier closes most of the feasibility gap, in and out of distribution and at up to roughly five times the training range, cutting under-covered polygons by about an order of magnitude at an explicitly reported cost in guard count. We read this as evidence that the reinforcement-trained representation already encodes the geometry required for feasibility, and that residual failures reflect decoder calibration rather than missing knowledge. Probing a frozen encoder thus offers a practical way to ask what a neural combinatorial solver has internalized.

</details>


### [246] [The Two-Hump Problem: Bridging the Difficulty Gap in Mathematical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21611)
*Lucas Fagan,Michele Tarquini,Ali Shehper,Maksymilian Manko,Angus Gruen,Coco Huang,Giorgi Butbaia,Davide Passaro,Sergei Gukov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Andrews-Curtis（AC）猜想这一数学搜索问题，识别出"双峰分布"这一结构障碍（问题要么极易解决，要么几乎不可解，缺乏中间难度的实例），通过新颖的数据生成技术填补难度缺口，并引入supermove和Transformer架构等算法改进，显著提升了性能。同时发布了AC-19和AC-1M两个大规模公开基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学搜索问题因搜索空间巨大且奖励稀疏，对强化学习构成独特挑战。作者以AC猜想为例，发现其问题实例呈现"双峰分布"——要么极易解决，要么几乎不可能，中间难度的"困难但可解"实例极度稀缺，这成为有效学习的结构性障碍。

Method: 1) 新颖的数据生成技术，用于填补难度缺口，生成更多中等难度的实例；2) 算法增强，包括引入supermove（超移动操作）和基于Transformer的架构。

Result: 相较于之前的基线方法取得了显著的性能提升，并发布了两个大规模公开数据集：AC-19（125,192个难度各异、长度不超过19的AC平凡表示）和AC-1M（1,136,154个困难、长度不超过30的AC平凡表示）。

Conclusion: 通过解决双峰分布问题，结合数据生成与算法改进，有效提升了RL在AC猜想上的性能，并提供了首个大规模公开基准数据集，为后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Mathematical search problems present a unique challenge for Reinforcement Learning (RL) due to vast search spaces and sparse rewards. In previous works, the Andrews-Curtis (AC) conjecture was established as an illustrative example of such problems. In this work, we identify a critical structural barrier in the AC landscape: a "Two-Hump" distribution, where problem instances are either trivially solvable or effectively impossible, with a scarcity of intermediate "hard-but-solvable" instances required for effective learning. We tackle this challenge through two primary avenues: novel data generation techniques to populate the difficulty gap, and significant algorithmic enhancements including the introduction of supermoves and Transformer-based architectures. We demonstrate substantial performance improvements over previous baselines, and release new comprehensive benchmark datasets including AC-19 (125,192 AC-trivial presentations of varying difficulty with length at most 19) and AC-1M (1,136,154 hard AC-trivial presentations of length at most 30), the first large-scale, publicly available datasets of this kind.

</details>


### [247] [HERALD: High-Throughput Block Diffusion LLM Serving via CPU-GPU Cooperative KV Cache Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21633)
*Omin Kwon,Doyeon Kim,Jongseok Park,Seung Yul Lee,Ion Stoica,Jae W. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERALD是一个针对块级扩散LLM的KV缓存卸载系统，通过利用块内去噪步骤中KV条目一致性，仅需选择一次top-k条目即可复用整个块，将选择计算量降低块大小倍，并与去噪过程重叠。在5-10% KV预算下实现近乎无损精度，相比纯GPU推理，每块延迟降低1.59倍，吞吐量提升2.47倍，且加速比随上下文长度增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散LLM虽然通过一次前向传播生成多个token提高了GPU利用率，但其KV缓存随上下文线性增长，限制了长上下文场景的吞吐量。将KV缓存卸载到主机DRAM虽能缓解内存压力，但PCIe带宽有限，只能召回稀疏的KV条目子集。

Method: HERALD利用块级扩散LLM中同一块内所有去噪步骤的KV条目一致性，通过识别top-k条目并在整个块的所有去噪步骤中复用，将选择开销摊销到整个块上。同时，通过将选择计算量降低块大小倍，并使选择过程与去噪过程重叠，解决了卸载下精确注意力计算成本过高的问题。

Result: 在三个块级扩散LLM和五个长上下文任务上，HERALD在5-10% KV预算下实现近乎无损的精度；每块延迟降低1.59倍，吞吐量提升2.47倍，且加速比随上下文长度增长而增加。

Conclusion: HERALD有效解决了块级扩散LLM在长上下文场景下的KV缓存卸载问题，通过块内KV一致性和选择-去噪重叠策略，在显著降低延迟和提升吞吐量的同时保持了近乎无损的精度。

Abstract: Diffusion LLMs (dLLMs) improve GPU utilization over autoregressive decoding by generating multiple tokens per forward pass, but their KV cache still grows linearly with context, limiting throughput at long contexts. KV cache offloading to host DRAM alleviates this memory pressure, but the limited PCIe bandwidth necessitates recalling only a sparse subset of KV entries. In block dLLMs, the relevant KV entries remain consistent across denoising steps within a block, enabling high-accuracy selection by identifying the top-k entries once and reusing them throughout all denoising steps. This property appears attractive for offloading as it amortizes the selection overhead across the entire block, but it requires exact attention over the full KV cache, which is too expensive under offloading. We present HERALD, a KV offloading system for block dLLMs that resolves this through two opportunities that reduce the required selection compute by a factor of the block size and enable selection to be overlapped with denoising. Across three block dLLMs and five long context tasks, HERALD achieves near-lossless accuracy at 5-10% KV budget and up to 1.59x lower per block latency and 2.47x higher throughput over GPU-only inference, with speedups growing with context length.

</details>


### [248] [A new classification method based on Minimum Spanning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21639)
*Julio González-Díaz,Beatriz Pateiro-López,Iria Rodríguez-Acevedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最小生成树的监督学习分类算法，并引入了鲁棒性更强、计算效率更高的改进版本，通过模拟研究和飞机轨迹真实数据案例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小生成树在无监督学习（特别是聚类任务）中已被广泛应用，但尚未被充分探索用于监督学习。本文旨在研究最小生成树在监督学习中的使用，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 提出了一种基于最小生成树的分类算法，并进一步开发了一个鲁棒性更强且计算效率更高的改进版本。

Result: 通过广泛的模拟研究验证了方法的有效性，并在飞机轨迹的真实案例中进行了应用验证。

Conclusion: 基于最小生成树的分类方法可以有效地应用于监督学习任务，且鲁棒性改进版本在性能和计算效率上更具优势。

Abstract: Minimum Spanning Trees have been used in unsupervised learning, particularly in clustering tasks, due to their ability to recognize clusters by removing edges that are considered inconsistent in defining those clusters. This paper aims to study the use of Minimum Spanning Trees in supervised learning. Specifically, we propose a classification algorithm based on Minimum Spanning Trees. To improve its performance, we introduce a robust version of the method that is also computationally more efficient. We evaluate the effectiveness of our proposed method through an extensive simulation study. We also apply the proposed methodology to a real-world case study involving aircraft trajectories.

</details>


### [249] [When Is an LLM Worth It for Hyperparameter Optimization? A Budget-Matched Study on Tabular Data Finds the Warm-Start Is a Default Configuration, Not the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21641)
*Carson Rodrigues,Oysturn Vas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在严格的预算匹配、多种子协议下，测试了LLM作为超参数优化顾问的能力，发现其优势完全来自一个固定的默认配置（非LLM输出），LLM自身提议几乎不提升性能。一旦给经典搜索同样的种子，LLM在少数评估内就被追平并超越。建议：在表格型HPO中，用合理的默认配置初始化经典搜索即可，LLM顾问没有可测量的泛化收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证LLM能否作为超参数优化（HPO）顾问，利用先验知识在极少量评估中提出强配置，从而"热启动"搜索。

Method: 在8个PMLB表格基准上，采用预算匹配、多种子（8×5=40个任务-种子单元）协议，将LLM顾问（LLM-OptFlow）与随机搜索、Optuna-TPE、高斯过程贝叶斯优化、连续减半四种基线对比，使用配对检验和bootstrap 95% CI进行统计评估。

Result: LLM顾问的强初始点并非LLM输出，而是固定的默认配置（占88.7%平均最佳CV）。LLM自身提议仅增加+0.40 pp交叉验证准确率，测试集上无提升（-0.01 pp, p=0.92）。给经典搜索相同种子后，LLM在2次评估后领先+0.20 pp，5次评估被追平，12次评估落后-0.37 pp。不给种子时，经典搜索在12次评估追平，40次评估显著超越（+0.6~+0.8 pp, p≤1e-4）。LLM特有行为仅剩单任务探索失败和规则置信过滤（移除33%无效计算而不改变准确率）。

Conclusion: 在表格型HPO任务中，LLM顾问没有可测量的泛化收益；建议直接用合理的默认配置初始化经典搜索，LLM在少数评估内就会被超越。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been proposed as hyperparameter-optimization (HPO) advisors that "warm-start" search from prior knowledge, proposing strong configurations in very few evaluations. We test that claim under a budget-matched, multi-seed protocol on eight PMLB tabular benchmarks, comparing an LLM advisor (LLM-OptFlow) against four classical baselines (random search, Optuna-TPE, Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization, and successive halving) over one shared search space, with paired tests and bootstrap 95% CIs across 8 x 5 = 40 (task, seed) units. The finding is cautionary. The advisor's strong first point is not an LLM output at all: like prior LLM-HPO systems the loop is seeded with a fixed default configuration, evaluated before any model call, which alone reaches 88.7% mean best-CV, identical to within 0.01 pp across all seven advisor models tested. The LLM's own proposals add only +0.40 pp of cross-validation accuracy over that seed and nothing on held-out test (LLM-Default = -0.01 pp, p = 0.92). When the same seed is granted to classical search, the apparent lead collapses: against seeded random search it leads by +0.20 pp at 2 evaluations, is tied by 5, and is behind by 12 (-0.37 pp). Without the seed, classical search ties the advisor by 12 evaluations and beats it by 40 (+0.6 to +0.8 pp, p <= 1e-4). Two LLM-specific behaviors survive: a single-task exploration failure (vehicle), and a rule-based confidence filter that removes ~33% of wasted compute without changing accuracy. The recommendation is deflationary: on tabular HPO, seed classical search with a sensible default; an LLM advisor adds no measurable generalization benefit and is overtaken within a handful of evaluations. We release the harness and a script that reproduces every statistic.

</details>


### [250] [A Framework for Directed Acyclic Hypergraph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21668)
*Zhiyuan Dong,Carlos Mundo-Levano,Wei Qian,Daniel Lau,Gonzalo R. Arce*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个从观测数据学习有向超图（DAHG）的框架，通过张量t-积的幂零性刻画无环性，并利用可微的无环约束实现连续优化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续优化方法仅能学习带权邻接矩阵表示的有向无环图（DAG），只能建模两两因果关系，无法捕捉多个父节点对子节点的联合影响。

Method: (i) 使用含乘性交互项的广义线性结构方程模型，非零权重与有向超边一一对应；(ii) 构建带权邻接张量，基于张量t-积的幂零性刻画无环性；(iii) 通过t-积的傅里叶分解导出可微无环约束，将张量幂零性简化为切片级矩阵幂零性，并通过增广拉格朗日方法进行最小二乘学习。

Result: 成功将可微无环约束从图扩展到超图，实现利用连续优化学习有向无环超图结构。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够有效建模联合父节点影响，突破了传统DAG学习只能处理成对因果关系的局限。

Abstract: Continuous optimization methods for learning Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs) operate on weighted adjacency matrices and are therefore limited to pairwise causal relationships. We propose a framework for learning Directed Acyclic Hypergraphs (DAHGs) from observational data, capturing joint parental influences that pairwise models cannot represent. Our approach rests on three components: (i) a generalized linear structural equation model (SEM) with multiplicative interaction terms whose non-zero weights correspond one-to-one with directed hyperedges; (ii) a weighted adjacency tensor representation whose acyclicity is characterized via nilpotency under the tensor t-product; and (iii) a differentiable acyclicity constraint derived through the Fourier decomposition of the t-product, which reduces tensor nilpotency to slice-wise matrix nilpotency and enables least-squares learning via the augmented Lagrangian method.

</details>


### [251] [Decodable but Not Faithful: Coupling Natural-Language Rationales to Programmatic Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21678)
*Vatsal Ananthula,Adarsh Kumarappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一致性训练可以使验证器信息从推理表示中解码，但可解码性并不保证忠实生成——模型在LeanCheck和KataGo中表现良好，但在代码场景中达到98.6%耦合度却生成不忠实解释（流畅但描述无关算法），表明一致性损失是有效的诊断和表征塑造工具，而非忠实推理的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型生成的解释可能无法忠实反映其内部推理过程，现有方法缺乏有效手段来评估和确保解释的忠实性。

Method: 提出验证器耦合推理框架，在推理轨迹中插入内联声明，训练辅助一致性头从理性跨度隐藏状态预测程序化验证器输出。通过可解码性（decodability）与忠实性（faithfulness）的对比分析，在LeanCheck（定理证明）、KataGo（围棋引擎）和代码生成三个场景下进行实验，并采用合成激活补丁和FEVER等方法进行因果分析。

Result: LeanCheck和KataGo中实现完美或高精度分离；代码场景中达到98.6%耦合度但解释不忠实（流畅描述无关算法）；合成激活补丁显示73-89%因果影响（基线31%）；FEVER表明证据池化牺牲原始准确率但获得证据敏感性；细粒度声明比二元声明更受益于一致性损失。

Conclusion: 一致性损失是有效的诊断和表征塑造工具，但不是忠实推理的充分条件。可解码性与忠实性之间存在根本性差距。

Abstract: Language models can generate plausible rationales for their predictions, but these explanations may not faithfully represent the model's internal reasoning. We propose verifier-coupled reasoning, a framework that inserts inline claims into reasoning traces and trains an auxiliary consistency head to predict programmatic verifier outputs from rationale-span hidden states. The central finding is a gap between decodability and faithfulness: consistency training reliably makes verifier information decodable from rationale representations, but decodability does not guarantee faithful generation. In LeanCheck (formal theorem proving), rationale-only and proof-only pooling achieve perfect directional separation under counterfactual conflict. In KataGo (Go engine), commentary spans encode 10-way win-rate buckets at 81% accuracy. Yet in a code setting, the model achieves 98.6% coupling while its generated explanations remain unfaithful: fluent prose with correct structured claims, but describing unrelated algorithms; a controlled pretrained-vs-from-scratch comparison shows the gap is not capacity-driven. Synthetic activation patching confirms causal influence (73-89% vs. 31% baseline), FEVER reveals that evidence-only pooling isolates genuine evidence sensitivity at the cost of raw accuracy, and per-claim analysis shows that consistency loss disproportionately benefits fine-grained claims over binary ones. These results establish that consistency losses are effective diagnostics and representation-shaping tools, but not sufficient conditions for faithful reasoning.

</details>


### [252] [Expressivity Saturation: Reduced Affine Region Usage Under Increasing Task Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21687)
*Xuan Qi,Yi Wei,Fanqi Yu,Manuel Lecha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论界限和实证观察两个互补视角，研究了分段仿射神经网络（如ReLU网络）中仿射区域的理论容量与实际训练后实现的区域数量之间的差距，提出了"表达饱和度"现象，并揭示了训练过程中的区域细化动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分段仿射神经网络的表达容量通常用仿射区域数量来度量，但理论上的最坏情况容量与实际训练后实现的区域数量之间存在显著差距，这一差距尚未被充分理解。

Method: 1) 理论推导了沿仿射线段的仿射片段数上界定理（与架构宽度和激活函数断点相关），给出了神经元阈值下界；2) 在受控任务复杂度下，精确枚举了2D及更高维有界域内训练后实际实现的仿射区域数量。

Result: 1) 证明了沿仿射线段的仿射片段数由层宽度项的显式乘积上界约束；2) 在固定架构和训练协议下，增加输入-标签复杂度导致评估域内实际实现的区域显著减少，称为"表达饱和度"；3) 高复杂度区域中，区域使用的坍缩常伴随决策边界退化；4) 训练过程中区域划分和决策边界呈现一致的细化过程。

Conclusion: 理论容量与实际表达能力之间存在根本性差距，表达饱和度现象揭示了训练过程对网络容量的限制作用，这对理解神经网络的泛化行为和表达能力具有重要启示。

Abstract: Piecewise-affine neural networks (e.g., with ReLU or LeakyReLU activations) implement continuous piecewise-affine maps, and the number of affine regions provides a natural proxy for expressive capacity. However, the gap between theoretical region capacity and the affine regions realized after training remains insufficiently understood. We study this gap from two complementary perspectives. First, we give a rigorous, architecture-dependent theorem for affine line-segment probes: for multilayer perceptrons with piecewise-affine activations, the number of affine pieces realized along an affine line-segment probe is upper bounded by an explicit product of layer-wise width terms (and activation breakpoint factors). This yields a neuron-threshold lower bound for representing target functions with prescribed one-dimensional piece complexity, formalizing the minimal region budget required for complex signals. Second, we exactly enumerate affine regions realized within bounded 2D and higher-dimensional domains under controlled task complexity. Under fixed architectures and training protocols, increasing input--label complexity yields trained solutions with markedly fewer realized regions in the evaluation domain, even though worst-case architectural capacity is unchanged; we call this reduced region usage expressivity saturation. Moreover, in the most challenging regimes, 2D visualizations show that region-usage collapse often coincides with degraded decision boundaries. Finally, we visualize the training dynamics of affine-region partitions and decision boundaries, revealing a consistent refinement process during optimization.

</details>


### [253] [Embedding Linear Equality Constraints in Probabilistic Neural Networks for Dynamic Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21728)
*Matthew Marsh,Benoit Chachuat,Antonio del Rio Chanona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种概率神经网络框架，能够在给定容差内保证满足线性等式约束并捕获偶然不确定性，在减少数据时提高了预测精度、不确定性校准和约束遵守，在大数据时训练更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在化学过程系统中缺乏原则性的不确定性量化和物理约束强制执行机制。

Method: 提出一种概率神经网络框架，在给定容差内保证满足线性等式约束，同时捕获偶然不确定性。

Result: 在减少数据时，相比现有方法提高了预测精度、不确定性校准和约束遵守；在大数据场景下，性能相当但训练速度显著更快。

Conclusion: 所提框架在化学过程系统中能有效平衡物理约束、不确定性量化和计算效率。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly used to model chemical process systems, yet they often lack principled uncertainty quantification and mechanisms to enforce physical constraints. We propose a probabilistic neural network framework that guarantees satisfaction of linear equality constraints within a given tolerance, while capturing aleatoric uncertainty. Compared to state-of-the-art methods, our formulation demonstrates improved predictive accuracy, uncertainty calibration, and adherence to constraints on reduced data. It also demonstrates competitive performance, but with significantly faster training times when evaluated on large data regimes. We evaluated this on two batch reactor case studies, enforcing mass balances.

</details>


### [254] [AdaPrivate-TS: Private Thompson Sampling for Contextual Bandits with Privacy Amplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21757)
*Mohammadreza Riyazat,Eranga Ukwatta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AdaPrivate-TS，一种结合Thompson Sampling与批量zCDP组合的差分隐私上下文Bandit算法，通过将隐私噪声解释为不确定性而非污染，在事件级隐私下实现对数级别的隐私成本，实验表明在ε∈[0.5,5]时可达到非隐私性能的93-99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私Bandit算法通常将噪声视为对模型的污染，导致性能显著下降。作者希望将隐私噪声重新解释为不确定性，利用Thompson Sampling天然处理不确定性的优势，在保护隐私的同时保持较高的算法性能。

Method: 1) 将高斯噪声N(0,σ²I)注入到b中，使采样协方差变为v²A⁻¹+σ²A⁻²，Thompson Sampling将其解释为增加的 uncertainty；2) 采用批量zCDP组合，利用并行组合在批次间分摊噪声；3) 探索通过Poisson子采样实现隐私放大，降低有效噪声。

Result: 1) ε∈[0.5,5]时达到非隐私性能93-99%，比UCB高0.5-3.7%，极端ε下通过自适应探索提升18%；2) 隐私放大在低ε下额外提升2-5%；3) MovieLens和Jester数据集上，AdaPrivate-TS在事件级基线中最佳，ε≥2时主导；4) DP-SVD隐私特征下，TS比UCB优势增至11%。

Conclusion: 将隐私噪声重新解释为不确定性是有效的，AdaPrivate-TS在隐私预算较宽范围内几乎达到非隐私性能，证明了"噪声即不确定性"这一视角在奖励隐私之外也具有普适性。

Abstract: We present AdaPrivate-TS, a differentially private contextual bandit algorithm that combines Thompson Sampling with batched zCDP composition. Our key insight is that differential privacy noise inflates the posterior covariance in a structured way: adding Gaussian noise $N(0,σ^2 I)$ to $b$ yields sampling covariance $v^2 A^{-1} + σ^2 A^{-2}$, which Thompson Sampling interprets as increased uncertainty rather than pure corruption. Under event-level privacy (protecting individual interactions) with stochastic contexts, we prove that the privacy cost is only $O(\sqrt{d}\,\log T/\sqrtρ)$, logarithmic in $T$, because parallel composition amortizes noise across batches. Additionally, we explore privacy amplification via Poisson subsampling, which can reduce effective noise at stringent privacy budgets. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate: (1) AdaPrivate-TS achieves 93-99% of non-private performance at $\varepsilon \in [0.5, 5]$, outperforming UCB by 0.5-3.7% and up to 18% with tuned adaptive exploration at extreme $\varepsilon$; (2) privacy amplification provides additional 2-5% gains at low $\varepsilon$; (3) on MovieLens and Jester, AdaPrivate-TS achieves the best overall performance among event-level baselines, dominating at $\varepsilon \geq 2$; (4) under DP-SVD private features, TS's advantage over UCB grows to +11%, confirming noise-as-uncertainty is not limited to reward privacy. We provide rigorous proofs for privacy guarantees under interactive zCDP composition and comprehensive evaluation including convergence curves, 12-seed CIs, and DP-SVD feature ablation.

</details>


### [255] [Decision-Focused Learning: When and Why Traditional Prediction Models Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21773)
*Mo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了决策驱动学习（DFL）在“预测-优化”范式中的最新进展，重点关注以随机线性规划为下游决策问题的情况，指出了传统统计学习工具（如基于预测不确定性的数据收集策略和Wasserstein距离等分布度量）在决策驱动场景中的不适用性，并总结了DFL区别于传统预测建模的关键特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统“预测-优化”范式中，提高预测精度并不总能转化为更好的决策质量，这一脱节问题促使运筹学领域对决策驱动学习（DFL）产生日益增长的兴趣。

Method: 本文以综述形式梳理DFL的最新发展，聚焦随机线性规划作为下游决策问题，讨论了传统统计学习工具（如基于预测不确定性的数据收集策略和Wasserstein距离）在决策驱动场景中的局限性，并总结DFL的核心方法论特征。

Result: 指出传统统计学习中的常用工具（如纯基于预测不确定性的数据收集策略和Wasserstein距离等分布距离度量）在DFL场景下需要重新审视，并总结了DFL区别于传统预测建模的若干关键特性。

Conclusion: 为开发新的决策驱动工具提供了方法论见解，强调了在决策驱动场景中需要重新思考传统统计学习工具的必要性。

Abstract: Plugging predictions of unknown parameters into downstream optimization problems, often referred to as the ``predict-then-optimize'' paradigm, has long been a standard approach in decision-making under uncertainty. However, improved predictive accuracy does not, in general, translate into improved decision quality. This disconnect has motivated growing interest in decision-focused learning (DFL) within the operations research community. This tutorial reviews recent developments in DFL and highlights key methodological insights, with a particular focus on stochastic linear programming as the downstream decision-making problem. We discuss why several widely used tools in traditional statistical learning are not directly suited to decision-focused settings and must be rethought, including (i) data collection strategies driven purely by predictive uncertainty and (ii) distributional distance measures such as the Wasserstein distance. We summarize properties of DFL that distinguish it from conventional predictive modeling and provide insights into the development of new decision-focused tools.

</details>


### [256] [Beyond the Next Step: Variable-Length Latent World Models for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21775)
*Tianqi Du,Qi Zhang,Yifei Wang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出变长潜在世界模型（VLWMs），通过学习变长动作序列下的未来潜状态预测，替代传统单步递归预测方式，有效缓解累积误差问题。结合课程训练策略和适配规划方法，在长时域控制任务上平均提升13%，特别在需要长程规划的任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA风格的潜在世界模型通常只进行单步预测，长时域规划需递归展开，导致误差累积且训练目标与下游规划任务不匹配。

Method: 1) 提出VLWMs框架，直接建模变长动作序列下的时间扩展动力学；2) 引入课程训练策略，渐进扩展动作预测时长；3) 设计适配VLWMs的测试时规划方法。

Result: 在长时域控制任务上，VLWMs相比SOTA方法LeWM平均提升13%，长程规划任务上增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: VLWM提供了一种简单而有效的范式，显著改进了潜在世界模型的长时域预测与规划能力。

Abstract: Recently, world models have emerged as a promising paradigm for building intelligent agents by learning predictive models that estimate future environment states conditioned on observations and actions. In particular, JEPA-style latent world models provide an efficient alternative to pixel space prediction by learning action-conditioned dynamics in compact representation spaces. However, existing latent world models typically rely on one-step prediction and must be recursively rolled out for long-horizon planning, which leads to compounding errors and a mismatch between training objectives and downstream planning tasks. To address this limitation, we propose Variable-length Latent World Models (VLWMs), a framework that learns to predict future latent states conditioned on action sequences of variable lengths. Instead of training only on one-step transitions, VLWMs directly model temporally extended dynamics, allowing the same predictor to evaluate action plans over different horizons. We further introduce a curriculum training strategy that progressively expands the action horizon, stabilizing optimization from short-range dynamics to long-range prediction. At test time, we design planning methods tailored to VLWMs to better exploit their variable-length predictive capabilities. Experiments on long-horizon control tasks show that VLWMs significantly improve latent space world models, achieving 13\% average improvement over the state-of-the-art LeWM across different datasets, with especially large gains on tasks requiring extended planning. These results suggest that VLWM provides a simple yet effective paradigm for improving long-horizon prediction and planning in latent world models.

</details>


### [257] [A Causal DAG Prior for Synthetic Time-Series Classification Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21776)
*Franco Martino O'Rourke,Ana Trisovic,Dimitris Bertsimas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种因果先验生成器，用于生成具有跨模态时间结构的多变量时间序列分类数据集，并微调TabPFN v2.5在75个UCR/UEA数据集上显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将先验数据拟合网络（Prior-data Fitted Networks）范式推广到多变量时间序列分类（TSC）任务，需要能够生成完整带标签的、具有时间结构的数据集的合成生成器，而现有合成先验无法处理跨通道、时间步和标签的跨模态因果结构。

Method: (1) 提出一种因果先验，从随机采样的DAG（有向无环图）出发，在两类模态（表格属性与时间序列）的带类型节点上合成完整的多变量多类别TSC数据集，天然具备跨模态因果结构；(2) 对TabPFN v2.5进行最小适配微调，在75个UCR/UEA数据集上评估。

Result: 微调模型显著优于未修改的上游模型和纯表格先验消融版本，ROC-AUC的Wilcoxon符号秩检验p=3.0×10⁻⁸，验证了跨模态时间结构的贡献。

Conclusion: 所提出的因果先验生成器能够有效生成具有跨模态时间结构的TSC数据，微调后的TabPFN在该设置下表现优异，证明了跨模态时序信息对TSC任务的关键作用。

Abstract: A Prior-data fitted Network learns the posterior predictive induced by its training prior; bringing this paradigm to multivariate time-series classification therefore calls for a synthetic generator that produces complete labelled datasets with temporal structure. We introduce a causal prior that synthesizes each dataset from a randomly sampled DAG over typed nodes across two modalities (tabular attributes and time series), natively producing multivariate, multi-class TSC datasets with cross-modal causal structure across channels, timesteps and labels, a regime not addressed by existing synthetic priors. To validate the prior, we finetune TabPFN v2.5 with minimal adaptations and evaluate on 75 UCR/UEA datasets within TabPFN's operating regime. Finetuning on our generator significantly outperforms both the unmodified upstream model and a tabular-only ablation of the same prior (Wilcoxon signed-rank $p=3.0\times 10^{-8}$ on ROC-AUC), isolating the contribution of the cross-modal temporal structure.

</details>


### [258] [RocketPFN: Accurate Time Series Classification via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21786)
*Franco Martino O'Rourke,Ana Trisovic,Dimitris Bertsimas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RocketPFN是一种无需训练的时序分类流程，将随机卷积特征提取（Rocket）与上下文分类（TabPFN v2.5）结合，在92个UCR数据集上达到与最强集成方法HC2相同的平均准确率（0.900），且无需目标数据训练，显著优于单个分类器及多个时序基础模型（MOMENT、Mantis等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出一种无需训练、零样本的时序分类基线方法，作为评估时序基础模型（TSC foundation models）零样本性能的参考点，挑战当前依赖大规模预训练的主流范式。

Method: 两阶段流水线：第一阶段使用Rocket进行随机卷积特征提取（无参数学习），第二阶段将提取的特征输入预训练的表格基础模型TabPFN v2.5进行上下文分类（in-context classification），整个过程无需在目标数据上训练。

Result: 在92个UCR数据集上，RocketPFN平均准确率0.900，与最强集成方法HC2持平（Wilcoxon p=0.50），中位推理时间30秒/fold；显著优于HC2中的每个单独分类器；在UEA 20个数据集上差异不显著；显著优于MOMENT、Mantis、MantisV2等时序基础模型（p<0.001），即使这些模型在包含UCR训练样本的语料上预训练。

Conclusion: RocketPFN作为一种无需训练、无需学习参数的流水线，在时序分类任务上达到了与最先进集成方法相当的性能，并显著优于多个领域专用基础模型，可作为评估零样本时序分类基础模型的有力参考基准。

Abstract: We introduce RocketPFN, a training-free pipeline for time series classification that combines random convolutional feature extraction (Rocket) with in-context classification via a pretrained tabular foundation model (TabPFN v2.5). On 92 UCR datasets (30-resample protocol), RocketPFN matches HC2, the strongest published method on the archive, in mean accuracy (both 0.900, Wilcoxon p=0.50), with no training on the target data and a median inference time of 30 seconds per fold. It also significantly outperforms every individual classifier in the HC2 ensemble. On UEA (20 datasets) the difference is likewise not statistically significant. A separate comparison concerns TSC foundation models: when paired with the same downstream classifier, MOMENT, Mantis, and MantisV2 are all significantly outperformed by RocketPFN using fewer extracted features and no learned parameters (p<0.001 in each case). This holds even when the encoders were pretrained on corpora that include the UCR training samples. We propose this two-stage pipeline as a reference point for evaluating zero-shot TSC foundation models.

</details>


### [259] [What Do Lorentz-Equivariant Jet Taggers Learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21790)
*Jay Agarwal,Siddharth Khare,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了L-GATr、L-GATr-slim、LLoCa-T等洛伦兹等变喷注标记器，通过等变性测试、线性探针和年级消融实验发现：等变模型能抑制依赖参考系的赝快度，同时编码喷注质量和N-subjettiness；在L-GATr中，双向量通道对顶夸克标记贡献可忽略，而矢量化通道占主导但依赖种子变量，表明网络利用多重表征路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究洛伦兹等变喷注标记器内部学习到了什么物理特征和代数结构，理解哪些信息被用于判别任务。

Method: 使用等变性测试、线性探针和年级消融（grade ablations）五种模型（L-GATr、L-GATr-slim、LLoCa-T等）。

Result: 等变模型抑制了赝快度（frame-dependent）；强调喷注质量和N-subjettiness；L-GATr中双向量通道不重要，矢量化通道主导但依赖种子变量。

Conclusion: 揭示了等变标记器中哪些物理特征和代数年级结构携带判别信息，为未来模型开发提供指导。

Abstract: We study what Lorentz-equivariant jet taggers learn internally, using equivariance tests, linear probes and grade ablations across five models including L-GATr, L-GATr-slim and LLoCa-T. Linear probes show that equivariant models suppress frame-dependent pseudorapidity to zero while encoding jet mass and N-subjettiness strongly. Grade ablations on L-GATr reveal that bivector channels are negligible for top-quark tagging while vector-like channels are dominant but seed variable, consistent with the network exploiting multiple representational pathways. These results characterize which physical features and algebraic grade structures carry discriminative information in equivariant taggers and may inform future development of such models.

</details>


### [260] [Discretizing Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21795)
*Vijay Viswanathan,Shiqi Wang,Devamanyu Hazarika,Chirag Nagpal,Tongshuang Wu,Graham Neubig,Yuning Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示奖励模型的过度敏感性问题：奖励模型对同等质量的回答会给出不同评分，导致强化学习策略变差。作者提出用"区分能力"和"特异性"来评估奖励模型，并设计了一种基于蒙特卡洛dropout的无训练离散化算法，在降低过度敏感性的同时保持区分能力，实验证明离散化奖励能减少奖励破解并获得更优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型在强化学习中应用广泛，但其具体作用机制尚不明确。现有研究多关注奖励模型的"准确性"，而忽视了其过度敏感性这一关键缺陷。

Method: 理论上证明了完美奖励模型可能具有高度过度敏感性；提出用"区分能力"和"特异性"替代传统准确性指标；设计了一种基于蒙特卡洛dropout的无训练离散化算法，将连续奖励转化为离散聚类。

Result: 理论证明存在既能降低过度敏感性又几乎不损失区分能力的离散化方案；在受控和自然RL场景中，离散化奖励相比原始连续奖励能减少奖励破解并得到更好的策略。

Conclusion: 奖励模型的连续评分特性看似优势实为缺陷，离散化处理是缓解过度敏感性的有效手段，应重新定义奖励模型的评估标准。

Abstract: Despite their widespread use, the role of reward models in shaping reinforcement learning is poorly understood. Reward models offer a tempting promise: they automatically estimate response quality in the absence of verifiers or human judges. Unlike "verifiable rewards" which typically produce binary scores, reward models typically produce continuous scores, allowing them to be sensitive to fine-grained differences in responses. However, we show this apparent strength is a serious weakness: many popular reward models are oversensitive, assigning different scores to equally good responses. Theoretically, we show that seemingly perfect reward models can be highly oversensitive; empirically, this oversensitivity can lead to bad policies. In place of existing notions of "reward model accuracy," we propose evaluating reward models using distinct measures of "discriminative ability" and "specificity" (the complement of oversensitivity). As a solution, we describe a training-free algorithm that uses Monte Carlo dropout on any neural reward model to produce discrete reward clusters. Theoretically, we prove there exist discretizations that reduce oversensitivity at minimal expense of discriminative ability; empirically we show, in both controlled and natural RL settings, that discretizing rewards leads to less reward hacking and better policies than training on the original rewards.

</details>


### [261] [Causal Variational Deep Embedding: A Family of Interventional Generators for Confounded Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21806)
*Jingyuan Chen,Kangrui Ruan,Junzhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CauVaDE框架，通过将未观测混杂因子坍缩为离散潜变量并引入熵正则化，在观测数据与因果图兼容的前提下，生成覆盖可行域的一系列候选因果效应分布，从而解决深度生成模型中的混杂偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度生成模型会继承训练数据中的虚假关联，其中未观测混杂因子同时影响用户想要控制的属性（如生成条件）和期望独立变化的属性，导致因果效应识别不唯一。现有方法依赖过强结构假设，在图像领域不适用。

Method: 提出CauVaDE（因果变分深度嵌入模型），基于规范增广SCM：将未观测混杂因子无损坍缩为离散潜变量（有界支撑簇），连续变异吸收为独立噪声。证明该规范类在观测和干预Wasserstein距离下稠密。实例化为混合VAE，其中簇变量充当规范混杂因子，簇后验上引入权重γ的熵正则化项，刻画出一系列拟合观测数据但跨越可行域的候选因果效应。

Result: 在图像基准上，CauVaDE生成多样化的干预样本，相比未混杂的参考模型提高了FID分数。

Conclusion: CauVaDE通过无需强结构假设的规范SCM框架，有效处理了图像生成中的未观测混杂问题，为因果生成模型提供了更灵活且理论上合理的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep generative models reproduce the observational distribution of their training data, inheriting any spurious associations it contains. A common source is an unobserved confounder that shapes both an attribute the user wants to control at sampling time and an attribute expected to vary in response. Existing causal generative approaches resolve the resulting ambiguity by imposing structural assumptions strong enough to single out one interventional distribution; in image domains, such assumptions are rarely warranted, and the data is generally consistent with a set of distinct causal mechanisms -- a feasible region of interventional distributions. We propose CauVaDE (Causal Variational Deep Embedding), built on a canonical augmented SCM in which the unobserved confounder collapses, without loss of generality, into a discrete latent cluster of bounded support while continuous variation is absorbed into independent noises. We prove that this canonical class is dense, in both observational and interventional Wasserstein distance, in the class of augmented SCMs compatible with a given causal diagram, and instantiate it as a mixture variational autoencoder whose cluster variable plays the role of the canonical confounder. An entropy regularizer with weight $γ$ on the cluster posterior then traces a family of candidate causal effects that fit the observational data to comparable likelihood while spanning the feasible region. Experiments on image data benchmarks show that CauVaDE produces diverse interventional samples and improves FID against an unconfounded reference.

</details>


### [262] [Causal Gaussian Processes for Robust Treatment Effect Evaluation with Unobserved Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21809)
*Junzhe Zhang,Jingyuan Chen,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对连续域上存在混杂偏差的策略评估问题，提出了一种仅需处理与结果间基本时序顺序的先验知识，即可从混杂观测数据中近似任意因果模型的观测和干预分布的通用方法。该方法通过外生域的全离散化，用有限隐状态逼近任意因果模型，并在此基础上开发了因果高斯过程（CGP）模型族，有效解决了连续域中混杂观测下的因果效应评估问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混杂鲁棒评估策略要么需要详细的先验环境知识，要么仅适用于离散处理变量和离散结果变量；而连续域上的因果效应评估因混杂偏差导致可识别性问题尚未得到充分解决。

Method: 提出外生域的通用离散化方法，用有限隐状态以任意精度近似任意因果模型的观测和干预分布；基于此万能近似性质，开发了因果高斯过程（CGP）模型族。

Result: 所提出的CGP模型族能够有效近似任意混杂观测因果模型的观测分布和干预分布，实现连续域上的因果效应评估。

Conclusion: 仅需处理与结果间的基本时序顺序，即可在连续域上实现混杂观测下的因果效应近似评估，为实际政策评估提供了更实用的工具。

Abstract: The presence of confounding bias poses a key challenge in policy evaluation, as the target causal effects of actions are not identifiable (i.e., underdetermined) from observational data. On the other hand, existing confounding-robust evaluation strategies require detailed prior knowledge about the environment or apply only to discrete treatments and outcomes. This paper investigates causal effect evaluation over the continuous domain from confounded observations, while requiring only basic temporal ordering between the treatment and the outcome. We introduce a universal discretization of the exogenous domains that approximates the observational and interventional distributions of any causal model with arbitrary accuracy using a finite number of latent states. Building on this newfound universal approximation property, we develop a novel family of Causal Gaussian process (CGP) models that effectively approximate the observational and interventional distributions of any causal model with confounded observations.

</details>


### [263] [Local Causal Attribution of Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21821)
*Dennis Wei,Yannis Belkhiter,Erik Miehling,Radu Marinescu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AttriCoT方法，通过构建结构因果模型对思维链中各单元进行因果归因，仅需O(U)次前向传播即可估计各单元对后续输出对数概率的重要性。在5个数据集和4个推理模型上的评估表明，该方法产生的归因比替代方法更忠实于模型行为，并揭示了不同模型和领域间的思维结构差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型思维过程的因果结构对于模型透明度和安全性至关重要，现有方法缺乏对思维链中因果关系的细粒度局部归因分析。

Method: 将思维链中的各个组件（单元）构建为结构因果模型，通过O(U)次前向传播估计各单元在结构因果模型中的重要性参数，实现对后续输出单元对数概率的黑盒归因。

Result: 在5个数据集和4个推理模型上的扰动曲线评估显示，AttriCoT产生的归因比现有替代方法更忠实于模型实际行为，且归因结果揭示了不同模型和领域间思维结构的显著差异。

Conclusion: AttriCoT是一种高效的、忠实度高的黑盒因果归因方法，可用于分析语言模型思维链的内部因果结构，为模型透明度和安全性研究提供了有价值的分析工具。

Abstract: Understanding the causal structure of a language model's thought process is a problem of significant importance for both transparency and safety. In this work, we take a local approach toward this goal by analyzing the causal relationships among individual components, termed units, of a given, specific chain-of-thought trace. We construct a structural causal model on these units and relate each unit to the log probability of generating (subsequent) output units. Our algorithm, termed AttriCoT, is a black-box method that performs attribution by estimating importance parameters in the structural causal model using $O(U)$ forward passes through the model, where $U$ is the number of units. Evaluation of perturbation curves across 5 datasets and 4 reasoning models shows that AttriCoT produces attributions that are more faithful to the model's behavior than alternative methods. The attribution results also reveal notable differences in thought structure between models and domains.

</details>


### [264] [Mat-Pref: Verifiable-Reward Training Improves Compositional Reasoning in Inorganic Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21830)
*Sarrah R. Mikhail Leung,Taehan Kim,Jeongbin Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Mat-Pref 基准，包含 10,837 道离子替换问题，覆盖 11 种无机结构家族，并设计了三类评估拆分以隔离分布内性能、结构家族泛化及跨属性迁移。实验表明，零样本前沿模型（70-671B）仅达 33-54% 准确率，而 SFT+GRPO 两阶段流水线使 Qwen3-8B 达到 65.2% 分布内准确率和 71.6% 结构外泛化准确率，超越零样本 Qwen3-235B 超过 20 个百分点。机制分析表明，GRPO 通过重塑分布使正确答案从"可达"变为"模态"，并在关键决策层实现约 20pp 的答案结晶优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于可验证奖励的强化学习在数学和代码推理中取得了进展，但扩展到科学领域时，现有基准无法分解泛化的来源：性能提升是源于结构迁移、属性迁移还是记忆？

Method: 构建 Mat-Pref 基准（10,837 道离子替换问题，11 种无机结构家族，基于 Materials Project 的 DFT 计算），设计三类评估拆分（分布内、结构家族外泛化、跨属性迁移）；采用 SFT + GRPO 两阶段训练流水线；使用自一致性采样、logit lens 分析和干扰物-排列一致性度量进行机制分析。

Result: 零样本前沿模型（70-671B）在所有拆分上仅达 33-54%；SFT+GRPO 使 Qwen3-8B 达 65.2% 分布内和 71.6% 结构外泛化，超越零样本 Qwen3-235B 超 20pp；GRPO 使正确答案从"可达"变为"模态"响应，在关键决策层实现约 20pp 答案结晶优势；GRPO 将干扰物排列的宽松评分与严格评分差距从 24.0pp 缩小至 14.3pp。

Conclusion: 规模本身不足以解决组合化学推理；GRPO 通过重塑输出分布使正确答案成为模态响应，而非仅可达到，从而提升推理可靠性；干扰物-排列一致性度量可作为评估模型推理稳健性的有效指标。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) has driven rapid progress in mathematical and code reasoning, but when extended to science, existing benchmarks do not decompose what generalizes: do gains reflect structural transfer, property transfer, or memorization? We introduce Mat-Pref, a benchmark of 10,837 ionic-substitution questions across 11 inorganic structure families, grounded in density functional theory calculations from the Materials Project, with three evaluation splits that isolate in-distribution performance, generalization to entirely held-out structure families, and cross-property transfer: applying band-gap reasoning to hosts seen during training only through formation-energy supervision. Four zero-shot frontier models (70-671B parameters) remain in the 33-54% range on every split, confirming that scale alone does not resolve the compositional chemical reasoning this task demands. A two-stage pipeline of supervised fine-tuning followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) lifts Qwen3-8B to 65.2% in-distribution and 71.6% on held-out families, exceeding zero-shot Qwen3-235B by over 20 percentage points on both structural-generalization splits. Self-consistency sampling shows that the SFT policy can already produce correct answers but cannot reliably surface them as the modal response; GRPO reshapes the distribution so that correct answers become modal rather than merely reachable, and this sharper commitment is visible mechanistically: logit lens analysis reveals a ${\sim}$20pp advantage in answer crystallization at the critical decision layer. We formalize this observation as a distractor-permutation consistency metric under which GRPO narrows the gap between lenient scoring (at least one permutation correct) and strict scoring (all permutations correct) from 24.0 to 14.3 percentage points.

</details>


### [265] [UniRank: Unified Rank Allocation for Low-Rank LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21847)
*Chao Han,Haozhe Hu,Fei Ma,Wei Zhang,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于局部奇异能量比和全局功能重要性双准则的低秩分解排序-截断方法，用于大语言模型压缩，并引入保秩微调策略避免信息损失，在无需微调的情况下困惑度降低高达50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩分解压缩方法中，秩分配存在手动规则缺乏泛化性和学习方法计算开销大的问题。

Method: 将全局低秩分配形式化为排序-截断流程，通过局部奇异能量比（度量参数矩阵内在重要性）和全局功能重要性（输入-输出余弦相似度，评估模块功能重要性）双准则对奇异分量评分；提出保秩微调（直接在分解权重上进行LoRA微调，避免传统合并流程中重截断导致的信息损失）。

Result: 与均匀分配和启发式分配基线相比，在一次性压缩（无需微调）下困惑度降低高达50%，在不同分解方案、模型规模和架构上均有一致性能提升。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了高输入-输出余弦相似度与低有效秩之间的强相关性，并通过几何解释和实验验证支撑，实现了高效且泛化的低秩分配。

Abstract: Low-rank decomposition serves as a promising compression paradigm for large language models, however, rank allocation remains challenging: manual rules lack generalizability, and learning-based approaches incur heavy computational overhead. To address these issues, we formulate global low-rank allocation as a sorting-and-truncation pipeline, and score each singular component via dual criteria: \textbf{Local} singular energy ratio that quantifies the intrinsic importance within the decomposed parameter matrix and \textbf{Global} functional importance (measured by input-output cosine similarity) that evaluates the functional significance of decomposed modules. We verify the strong correlation between high input-output cosine similarity and low effective rank through geometric interpretation and experimental validation. Furthermore, we propose rank-preserving fine-tuning, which performs direct LoRA tuning on decomposed weights and avoids extra information loss caused by re-truncation in conventional merging pipelines. Empirical results confirm that our method delivers sustained performance enhancements when combined with models featuring distinct decomposition schemes, model sizes and architectural designs, e.g. in one-shot compression without further fine-tuning, our method reduces perplexity by up to 50\% compared with uniform and heuristic allocation baselines. Code will be available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/LLM-Pruning.

</details>


### [266] [WiSP: A Working-Set View of Mixture-of-Experts Serving on Extremely Low-Resource Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21868)
*Jiamu Zhang,Liang Wu,Mayank Darbari,Liangjie Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WiSP是一种路由感知的专家分页系统，通过将MoE专家权重和KV缓存视为GPU VRAM中的竞争性工作集，实现了在有限显存下的高效MoE模型推理。结合MV-WSA分配策略，在decode吞吐量上最高提升1.95倍，在线控制器额外带来1.20x的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE模型在低资源GPU上通过层级CPU卸载来节省显存，但这种方式会流式传输所有专家权重，丧失了MoE的稀疏性优势。需要一种更智能的显存管理方案来处理路由专家权重与KV缓存之间的竞争。

Method: 将低资源MoE服务建模为GPU工作集管理问题，提出WiSP（工作集分页），一个路由感知的专家分页器，无需修改服务引擎即可实现字节级一致输出。同时提出MV-WSA（边际价值工作集分配），通过均衡每字节的边际延迟收益来分配专家和KV缓存之间的显存。

Result: 在相同显存预算下，WiSP的decode吞吐量比静态卸载高达1.95倍。发现专家预取在单流decode中收益有限，瓶颈在PCIe带宽而非预测准确率。MV-WSA离线配置器在预填充和decode阶段均表现良好，固定拆分方案约差20%；在线控制器额外提升1.20倍。

Conclusion: MoE低资源推理的核心问题从专家预取转向了专家与KV缓存之间的显存分配。WiSP和MV-WSA通过工作集管理和边际价值分配有效解决了这一问题。

Abstract: Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models place most of their parameters in expert layers, yet only a small fraction of those experts are used for any token. The unused weights must still be stored where the GPU can reach them. On commodity GPUs the common fix is layer-level CPU offloading, which keeps memory low but streams all of a layer's experts across PCIe on every forward pass, losing much of MoE's sparsity benefit. We cast low-resource MoE serving as a working-set management problem on the GPU: routed expert weights and the key-value (KV) cache are two streams of memory demand competing for limited VRAM. We realize this in WiSP (Working-Set Paging), a routing-aware expert pager that plugs into an unmodified serving engine with byte-identical outputs. Keeping resident only the experts a workload reuses, WiSP reaches up to 1.95x the decode throughput of static offload at the same memory budget when the model does not fit. We also find that prefetching experts from predicted routing helps little in single-stream decode: the bottleneck is PCIe bandwidth, not prediction accuracy. This shifts the question from prefetching to allocation: how should VRAM be split between experts and the KV cache? We answer with MV-WSA (Marginal-Value Working-Set Allocation), which equalizes marginal latency benefit per byte subject to a KV admission floor. MV-WSA runs either as an offline configurator or as an online controller that resizes both pools while serving. In real serving the offline configurator is the only policy we test that does well on both prefill and decode; in trace-driven simulation it stays within a few percent of a per-workflow oracle while fixed splits are about 20% worse. The online controller adds a further 1.20x without changing model outputs.

</details>


### [267] [Protein contacts are already in the attention: a single-forward-pass alternative to the Categorical Jacobian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21876)
*Rome Thorstenson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Categorical Jacobian方法从蛋白质语言模型中读取残基接触的信号实际上集中在少量注意力头上，通过挑选top-K接触相关头（仅需10个标记蛋白）即可在一次前向传播中恢复接触，在无数据泄漏的测试中优于CJ方法。无参数均值本身就是选择的最简形式，优势来源于头选择而非平均化。两种方法在消除预训练数据泄漏后性能均下降30-36个百分点，说明先前结果被高估。此外，两种方法在因果语言模型（ProGen2）上均失效，表明基于注意力的成对结构可能依赖双向预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现Zhang等人(2024)提出的Categorical Jacobian方法虽能从蛋白质语言模型中读取残基接触，但需要约19L次前向传播，计算成本高。本文旨在探索是否存在更高效的方法来从模型中提取接触信息，并验证CJ方法所恢复的信号是否集中在一小部分注意力头上。

Method: 通过分析注意力头对接触预测的贡献，选择top-K接触相关头（仅需10个标记蛋白进行选择），对这些头的注意力输出进行无权重平均，即可在一次前向传播中获得接触预测。在无数据泄漏的CAMEO数据集上进行测试，并与CJ方法进行比较。另外引入representation-CJ作为Jacobian的隐状态泛化版本，适用于无掩码语言模型头的架构。

Result: 在无泄漏的CAMEO测试中，注意力头读取方法在ESM-2-650M上比CJ方法高出9个百分点（N=29, p<0.001）。两种方法在消除预训练数据泄漏后均下降30-36个百分点。最优K值与模型扩散接触头的程度相关。两种方法在ProGen2因果语言模型上均失效。

Conclusion: CJ方法的接触信号实际上集中在一小部分注意力头上，可以通过头选择更高效地提取。先前的结果因预训练数据泄漏而被高估。基于注意力的成对结构可能依赖于双向预训练。无参数均值本身就是选择的最简形式，优势来源于头选择而非平均化操作。

Abstract: The Categorical Jacobian (CJ) of Zhang et al. (2024) reads protein contacts from a language model by perturbing every residue with every alternative amino acid, about 19L forward passes. We show the signal it reconstructs is already concentrated in a small subset of attention heads: averaging the top-K contact-relevant heads, selected on as few as 10 labeled proteins, recovers contacts in one forward pass and beats CJ on leakage-clean data for every bidirectional model where CJ is defined, and matches or beats it in-distribution (the exceptions being the smallest 8M model and a statistical tie on ESM Cambrian). Ablations localize the gain to labeled head selection, not averaging: at a matched label budget the unweighted mean ties a supervised L1 logistic regression on the same heads, so the parameter-free mean is selection's minimal form, not the source of the advantage. Our primary test is leakage-clean: on a CAMEO split where neither selection nor evaluation touches data the models have plausibly memorized, the head readout beats CJ on ESM-2-650M by +9 pp (N=29, p<0.001), with the within-model margin reproducing across architectures on a wider pretraining-aware set. Both methods fall 30-36 percentage points from their in-distribution Zhang numbers to the leakage-clean numbers, consistent with substantial pretraining overlap inflating prior numbers (a CAMEO-vs-Zhang difficulty shift contributes too, so we read it as an upper bound on the leakage component). We additionally introduce representation-CJ, a hidden-state generalization of the Jacobian for architectures without a masked-LM head; show that the optimal K tracks how diffusely a model spreads its contact heads; and find that both methods lose the contact signal on both causal LMs we test (ProGen2), suggesting attention-encoded pair structure may depend on bidirectional pretraining.

</details>


### [268] [A Verifiable Search Is Not a Learnable Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21884)
*Harsh Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于需要搜索（如密码算术）的推理任务，前向链式思维无法蒸馏，因为模型只能学会记忆和验证而非搜索，即使拥有足够能力也无法实现。唯一有效的解决方案是预先计算搜索核心并转化为记忆+验证模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战一个普遍假设：任何可由短程序解决的任务都可以通过链式思维蒸馏教给模型。作者想探究是否存在一类任务，其前向链式思维本质上不可教。

Method: 在9个确定性生成器产生的推理任务上，将生成器逆向工程为Python求解器并渲染为链式思维，通过LoRA（秩≤32）蒸馏到30B（3.5B活跃）Nemotron模型。测试了前向可计算任务、密码算术及其多种变体，以及跨不同骨干网络和方法的对比实验，最后通过揭示密码键进行受控干预。

Result: 前向可计算任务（查找/算术、8位布尔任务）成功转移（≥0.99和0.68）。密码算术蒸馏失败（0.01-0.07），尽管模型能完成97-100%的算术行且71%的情况下正确密码在前八名。跨3B到671B骨干网络和多种微调/提示方法均存在上限。揭示密码键（使推导变为前向）将准确率从0.03提升到0.57。最终通过预先计算搜索核心为目录，将任务转化为记忆+验证，获得0.92的Private LB分数。

Conclusion: 当过程的唯一解决方案是对无信息结构进行搜索时，不存在可模仿的忠实前向链式思维。蒸馏所能学习的是记忆和验证，而非搜索本身。要使任务可学习，必须消除搜索，将其组合核心预计算为目录，将轨迹简化为回忆加验证。

Abstract: It is tempting to assume any task solvable by a short program can be taught to a model as its chain-of-thought: write the steps out, fine-tune, and the model follows. This paper shows the assumption fails for an identifiable class of procedures. The testbed is nine reasoning tasks, each from a deterministic generator; public and hidden splits share generators, so held-out data proxies test accuracy. I reverse-engineer the generators into Python solvers, render them as chain-of-thought, and distill into a rank-<= 32 LoRA over a 30B (3.5B-active) Nemotron model. Forward-computable tasks install readily: lookup/arithmetic and an 8-bit boolean task transfer (>= 0.99 and 0.68). Cryptarithm does not: distilling its backtracking search holds at 0.01-0.07 across eleven chain-of-thought designs, RL from verifiable rewards, and self-training, even though a search solver answers 71% of instances. This is not a capability gap. The model does the arithmetic on 97-100% of lines and ranks the correct cipher in its top eight on 71%; it cannot carry the search forward as a left-to-right derivation. Fine-tuning learns the shape of a verifiable elimination step while its verdicts become unconditional templates, correct only 16-57% of the time ("verdict-as-token"). The ceiling holds across backbones from 3B to 671B and across fine-tuning and prompting; a controlled intervention isolates the cause: revealing the cipher key, which turns the derivation forward, lifts the same instances from 0.03 to 0.57. When a procedure's only solution is search over information-free structure, no faithful forward chain-of-thought exists to imitate. The task becomes learnable only by removing the search, precomputing its combinatorial core into a catalog and reducing the trace to recall plus verification; the 1st-place solution reaches Private LB 0.92 this way. What distills is memorization and verification, not search.

</details>


### [269] [Cohort-Anchored Foundation Models for Electronic Health Records: From Risk Scores to Auditable Peer Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21885)
*Kaiping Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAFM框架，将患者队列作为一等对象融入基础模型学习流程，通过偏差感知数据整理、队列条件预训练、多模态队列对齐和临床医生在环优化四个阶段，提升模型的可解释性、鲁棒性和临床可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型在医学问答、影像和EHR任务中表现优异，但由于可解释性不足、对分布偏移脆弱、与临床推理对齐弱，可靠临床部署仍具挑战。作者认为根本原因在于现有方法将患者比较视为表征学习的副产品而非主要临床证据来源。

Method: CAFM框架包含四个阶段：(1) 偏差感知数据整理；(2) 队列条件预训练；(3) 多模态队列对齐；(4) 临床医生在环优化。该框架具有组合性，可增强现有EHR基础模型而无需修改其底层编码器。

Result: 通过四个临床案例研究（急性肾损伤预测、心电图心血管风险分层、眼眶影像视神经病变分诊、视网膜电图报告生成）进行说明，并提出五个可检验假设及开放挑战。

Conclusion: 将基础模型显式锚定于患者队列是通往可信临床AI的原则性路径。

Abstract: Foundation models have achieved remarkable performance across medical question answering, imaging, and electronic health record (EHR) tasks, yet reliable clinical deployment remains challenging due to limited interpretability, vulnerability to distribution shift, and weak alignment with clinician reasoning. We argue that these limitations arise because existing approaches prioritize representation learning while treating patient comparison as an emergent property rather than a primary source of clinical evidence. To address this gap, we propose CAFM, a Cohort-Anchored Foundation Model framework that elevates patient cohorts to a first-class object throughout the learning pipeline. The framework consists of four stages: deviation-aware data curation, cohort-conditioned pretraining, multimodal cohort alignment, and clinician-in-the-loop refinement. Together, these stages improve data quality, organize representations around clinically meaningful cohort structure, preserve modality-specific relationships, and support auditable clinical decision-making. The framework is compositional and can augment existing EHR foundation models without modifying their underlying encoders. We illustrate CAFM through four clinical case studies spanning acute kidney injury prediction, cardiovascular risk stratification from electrocardiograms, optic neuropathy triage from orbital imaging, and electroretinogram-grounded report generation. We further present five empirically testable hypotheses and identify open challenges in data quality, irregular temporality, multimodal learning, distribution shift, and evaluation beyond predictive accuracy. We argue that explicitly anchoring foundation models to patient cohorts provides a principled path toward trustworthy clinical AI.

</details>


### [270] [Ranking-and-Selection with Multiple Correct Answers and Non-Answerable Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21889)
*Qiaoqiao Wang,Wei You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的固定精度排序与选择框架，用于处理答案可能非唯一且噪声估计可能暂时无法提供有效答案的结构化问题。作者提出了ENDS算法（估计、提名、陷阱检测与成本感知信息导向选择），通过答案接受集、受限广义似然比停止准则以及答案-陷阱分解，解决了多保真度排序与选择、从成对比较中识别Condorcet胜者等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化排序与选择问题中，答案可能非唯一（如多个最优解），且由于噪声估计的存在，可能暂时无法得到任何有效答案。这类现象在多保真度排序与选择和Condorcet胜者识别等实际问题中自然出现，现有方法缺乏统一的理论框架和处理机制。

Method: 1) 提出基于答案接受集的统一框架；2) 采用受限广义似然比停止准则；3) 建立答案-陷阱分解，导出最大-最大-最小特征值和通用采样原则；4) 提出ENDS算法，融合估计、提名、陷阱检测和成本感知信息导向选择四个模块。

Result: 通过为多种问题实例推导显式公式，并在大量数值实验中验证了该统一框架在广泛的纯探索问题中表现良好。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个面向非唯一答案和噪声干扰场景的通用实践框架和概念验证算法，能够有效处理多保真度排序与选择、Condorcet胜者识别等多样化问题。

Abstract: We study fixed-precision ranking-and-selection in structured settings where the answer may be non-unique and where noisy estimates may temporarily admit no valid answer at all. This phenomenon arises naturally in problems such as multi-fidelity ranking-and-selection and identifying a Condorcet winner from pairwise comparisons. To address this, we propose a unified framework based on answer-wise acceptance sets, restricted generalized likelihood ratio stopping, and an answer-pitfall decomposition that yields a max-max-min characteristic value and a common sampling principle. We introduce ENDS, a general procedure that combines estimation, nomination, pitfall detection, and cost-aware information-directed selection. We instantiate ENDS for various problems by deriving explicit formulas. Extensive numerical experiments show that this unified recipe performs well across a broad range of pure-exploration problems and offers a practical framework and proof-of-concept algorithmic recipe.

</details>


### [271] [AgroSense 2.0: Cross-Modal Transformer Fusion with Geospatial Raster Integration and Interpretable Multi-Task Learning for Precision Crop Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21892)
*Vishal Pandey,Rishav Tewari,Ruzina Haque Laskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AgroSense 2.0 提出了一种跨模态Transformer融合框架，将七波段土壤 raster 图像与表格养分特征进行多模态融合，并引入多任务学习联合优化土壤分类和作物推荐，同时使用TreeSHAP增强可解释性，在精度农业中实现了更原理化、可解释且地理空间基础更强的作物推荐系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作物推荐系统存在根本性的模态鸿沟：视觉土壤表征和化学养分分析被当作独立的推理问题，特征融合通常仅停留在简单的后期特征拼接，缺乏深层次跨模态交互建模。

Method: (1) 引入覆盖印度的七波段土壤raster（32×32空间块）；(2) 使用跨模态Transformer融合模块，让表格养分特征通过多头注意力机制关注图像表征；(3) 采用多任务目标联合优化土壤分类和作物推荐，共享骨干网络。

Result: TreeSHAP可解释性分析揭示：湿度和降雨量是全球最具影响力的特征；作物特异性特征分布差异显著——水稻主导为降雨，玉米主导为氮和钾，咖啡主导为湿度和氮。

Conclusion: AgroSense 2.0 构建了一个更原理化、可解释且地理空间基础更强的精度农业框架，其跨模态融合和多任务学习策略有效弥补了传统方法的模态鸿沟，可解释性分析既验证了农学一致性，也揭示了需要进一步研究的数据集特定偏差。

Abstract: Crop recommendation systems in precision agriculture have long suffered from a fundamental modality gap: visual soil characterization and chemical nutrient profiling are typically treated as independent inference problems, with fusion often reduced to late-stage feature concatenation. AgroSense~2.0 addresses this limitation through three architectural advances. First, we introduce continental-scale geospatial integration via a seven-band soil raster (\texttt{india\_soil\_7bands.tif}) spanning India, encoding Nitrogen, pH, SOC, Clay, Sand, Silt, and Bulk Density as $32\times32$ spatial patches, a modality entirely absent from prior work. Second, we replace naive feature concatenation with a cross-modal Transformer fusion module, where tabular nutrient features attend over image representations via multi-head attention, enabling richer inter-modal dependency modeling than shallow fusion. Third, we adopt a multi-task objective jointly optimizing soil classification and crop recommendation through a shared backbone, improving generalization via complementary cross-task signal. To enhance interpretability, we apply TreeSHAP to the tabular branch, revealing crop-conditioned nutrient sensitivity: humidity and rainfall emerge as the most influential features globally, while crop-specific profiles diverge meaningfully rainfall dominates rice, nitrogen and potassium dominate maize, and humidity and nitrogen dominate coffee. These explanations provide transparency into model decisions and surface both agronomically consistent patterns and dataset-specific divergences worth further study. Together, these contributions establish AgroSense~2.0 as a more principled, interpretable, and geospatially grounded framework for precision agriculture.

</details>


### [272] [Data Pruning: Redundant, Problematic, and Interdependent Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21916)
*Leon Freese,Marthinus W. Theunissen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了两种主流数据剪枝方法在有无噪声条件下的表现，发现它们在存在显著标签噪声时失效，并指出数据剪枝的成功受到数据集冗余、问题样本存在以及样本间相互依赖三个因素的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究数据剪枝方法在标签噪声条件下的有效性，揭示其失效原因及影响因素。

Method: 在含噪和无噪条件下，对两种主流数据剪枝方法进行实证研究，在标准基准分类数据集和神经网络架构上进行详细调查。

Result: 两种数据剪枝方法在显著标签噪声存在时均失效；数据剪枝的成功受数据集冗余、问题样本存在和样本间相互依赖三个因素影响；该发现在不同数据分布和训练协议下一致。

Conclusion: 数据剪枝方法在标签噪声场景下不可靠，其效果依赖于数据冗余度、问题样本和样本间依赖关系。

Abstract: The performance of deep learning models is affected by not only data quantity but also data quality. Data pruning is a process by which practitioners can reduce the size of a dataset by only keeping the most important training data points, thereby achieving similar test set performance. We empirically investigate two popular data pruning methods under noisy and noiseless conditions and show that these methods fail in the presence of significant label noise. We highlight that the success of data pruning is distinctly affected by three factors: redundancy in the dataset, the presence of problematic samples, and interdependence between samples. We perform a detailed investigation on commonly used benchmark classification datasets and neural network architectures. We find that our observations are consistent across data distributions and training protocols.

</details>


### [273] [Selective Ensemble Based on Preference-Directed Multi-Objective Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21929)
*Lanjihong Ma,Zhen-Yu Zhang,Masashi Sugiyama,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出偏好导向多目标赌博机（PDMOB）框架，形式化处理在部分偏好指定下的大模型选择性集成问题，并设计PrefUCB算法，在理论和实验上均取得最优对数遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习中的选择性集成需要在不完全评估预算下选择有前景的模型候选，而下游任务通常只指定对准确性、鲁棒性、推理等能力的部分偏好，这自然构成了部分指定线性偏好下的序贯决策问题。

Method: 形式化为偏好导向多目标赌博机（PDMOB），用多面体偏好锥表示可接受的权衡；定义Pareto C-最优性（统一Pareto最优与单权重标量化）；提出PrefUCB算法，维护方向置信区间引导探索。

Result: 建立了基于指标和基于间隙加权的遗憾分析，对两个准则均得到实例相关的对数遗憾界，在经典特例中恢复对时间T的最优对数依赖关系。

Conclusion: 在大模型预训练选择性集成任务和机构授权下的在线资产配置实验中验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Selective ensemble for modern machine learning systems requires choosing promising model candidates under limited evaluation budgets, while downstream tasks often specify only partial preferences over capabilities such as accuracy, robustness, and reasoning. This setting naturally gives rise to a sequential decision problem under partially specified linear preferences. We formalize it as preference-directed multi-objective bandits (PDMOB), where admissible trade-offs are represented by a polyhedral preference cone. Based on this formulation, we introduce Pareto $C$-optimality, which recovers standard Pareto optimality and single-weight scalarization as special cases. We then propose the preference-directed upper confidence bound (PrefUCB) algorithm, which maintains directional confidence intervals to guide exploration. We analyze both indicator-based and gap-weighted regret, and establish instance-dependent logarithmic bounds for both criteria, recovering the optimal logarithmic dependence on the horizon $T$ in classical special cases. Experiments on large pre-trained model selective ensemble tasks and online asset allocation under institutional mandates validate the efficacy of our method.

</details>


### [274] [DevoTG: Temporal Graph Neural Networks for Modeling C. elegans Developmental Connectomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21940)
*Jayadratha Gayen,Bradly Alicea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DevoTG，一个基于时序图神经网络（TGN）的框架，用于分析秀丽隐杆线虫神经系统的发育过程。该框架在细胞谱系预测任务上（AUC 0.839）显著优于静态GNN（AUC 0.577），并在突触连接组动态图中识别出三类连接稳定性，揭示了枢纽命令中间神经元的持续中心性及其在幼虫阶段的逐步强化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经系统从出生到成年的布线机制是发育神经科学的基本挑战。现有方法缺乏对发育过程中时间动态的建模能力，需要一种能够同时处理细胞分裂事件和突触连接组时间演化的计算框架。

Method: 提出DevoTG框架，包含两个互补的时序图表示：(1) 基于细胞谱系数据的连续时间动态图（CTDG）用于建模细胞分裂事件；(2) 基于8个重建电子显微镜数据集的离散时间动态图（DTDG）用于建模突触连接组的发育过程。采用时序图神经网络（TGN）进行学习和预测。

Result: 在细胞谱系预测任务上，TGN达到平均测试AUC 0.839±0.007（验证AUC 0.937±0.001），比相同架构的静态GNN（0.577±0.080）高出26个AUC点。在连接组DTDG上，识别出三类连接稳定性（稳定、发育、可变），覆盖225个神经元和858-2496个连接。对AVA、AVB、AVE枢纽命令中间神经元的分析显示其持续的中心性及幼虫阶段的逐步强化整合作用。

Conclusion: DevoTG证明了时序记忆是神经系统发育建模的决定性因素，为神经发育研究提供了时序图理论工具，可与个体变异分类方法互补。该框架开源且可扩展至其他发育中的神经系统。

Abstract: Understanding how a nervous system wires itself from birth to adulthood is a fundamental challenge in developmental neuroscience. We present DevoTG, a temporal graph framework that applies Temporal Graph Neural Networks (TGNs) to two complementary representations of C. elegans neural development: a Continuous-Time Dynamic Graph (CTDG) of cell division events derived from cell lineage data, and a Discrete-Time Dynamic Graph (DTDG) of the developing synaptic connectome spanning eight reconstructed electron-microscopy datasets. On the lineage prediction task, our TGN achieves a mean test AUC of 0.839 +/- 0.007 (5 seeds; validation AUC 0.937 +/- 0.001), outperforming a static GNN with the identical architecture by 26 AUC points (0.577 +/- 0.080), demonstrating that temporal memory is the decisive factor. Applied to the connectome DTDG, DevoTG identifies three connection stability classes (stable, developmental, and variable) across 225 neurons and 858 to 2,496 connections over development (L1 birth to adult), providing a temporal-graph-theoretic complement to the individual-variability classification of Witvliet et al. Analysis of hub command interneurons AVA, AVB, and AVE reveals their persistent centrality and how their integration roles are progressively reinforced across larval stages. Accompanying interactive visualizations (3D animated networks, centrality heatmaps, and a spatiotemporal lineage graph) make developmental dynamics accessible for biological hypothesis generation. DevoTG is open-source and designed for extension to other developing nervous systems. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/DevoLearn/DevoGraph/tree/main/DevoTG.

</details>


### [275] [Modularized Reinforcement Learning on LLMs: From MDP Creation to Exploration and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21943)
*Zhao Yang,Yuxuan Jiang,Ting-Chih Chen,Lincen Yang,Annie Wong,Chao Gao,Jacob E. Kooi,Zhong Li,Jiayang Shi,Kevin Qiu,Qi Huang,Xinrui Zu,Shiping Yang,Hengyuan Zhang,Ngai Wong,Filip Ilievski,Shujian Yu,Aske Plaat,Zhaochun Ren,Mark Hoogendoorn,Vincent François-Lavet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理了RL算法构建的三个阶段（MDP设计、探索策略、学习维度），并基于此分类法映射LLM后训练中的RL应用，揭示了当前研究在无评论家策略梯度、蒙特卡洛信用分配等领域密集，而基于值的方法、离策略演员-评论家、引导式信用分配等方向存在明显空白，为跨领域研究者提供了理解现状与识别未来方向的共享框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM后训练中PPO和GRPO等RL方法占据主导地位，但这些方法仅代表了RL领域的一小部分。作者希望通过系统性地审视RL算法设计决策，揭示哪些方法已被充分探索、哪些存在空白，从而为RL和LLM研究者提供理解当前实践和识别未来方向的共享框架。

Method: 论文围绕RL算法构建的三个阶段组织分析：(1) MDP创建——奖励函数、状态空间、动作空间、终止条件和折扣因子的定义；(2) 探索策略——温度采样、熵正则化、内在动机、树搜索和课程学习；(3) 学习维度——无模型vs有模型、基于值vs基于策略vs演员-评论家、在策略vs离策略、以及信用分配（蒙特卡洛方法和引导方法）。通过将LLM文献映射到这一分类法，识别研究分布的不均匀性。

Result: 研究发现当前LLM后训练中的RL研究分布极不均匀：无评论家策略梯度和蒙特卡洛信用分配被密集研究，而基于值的方法、离策略演员-评论家训练和基于引导的信用分配在很大程度上未被探索，尽管这些方法在经典RL中已有成熟对应。

Conclusion: 这些空白代表了将经过验证的RL技术迁移到LLM训练中的具体机会。该综述通过明确这些空白与已证明有效的方法，为RL和LLM领域的研究者提供了理解当前实践和识别未来方向的共享框架。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to LLM post-training, yet the methods that dominate current pipelines, PPO and GRPO, represent only a narrow slice of what RL offers. Understanding why these methods prevail, and what alternatives exist, requires a principled examination of the design decisions that underlie any RL algorithm.
  This survey organizes that examination around three stages of algorithm construction. We begin with MDP creation: how the reward function, state space, action space, termination condition, and discount factor are, or could be, defined for LLM training. We then turn to exploration, covering temperature sampling, entropy regularization, intrinsic motivation, tree search, and curriculum learning. Finally, we address learning along four classical RL dimensions: model-free versus model-based, value-based versus policy-based versus actor-critic, on-policy versus off-policy, and credit assignment, including both Monte Carlo methods, which rely on full return estimates, and bootstrapping methods, which update estimates using other learned predictions.
  Mapping the LLM literature onto this taxonomy reveals a strikingly non-uniform distribution of research effort. Critic-free policy gradients and Monte Carlo credit assignment are densely populated, while value-based methods, off-policy actor-critic training, and bootstrapping-based credit assignment remain largely unexplored despite well-established counterparts in classical RL. These gaps represent concrete opportunities for transferring proven RL techniques to LLM training.
  By making these gaps explicit alongside the methods that have proven effective, this survey offers researchers in both RL and LLMs a shared framework for understanding current practice and identifying promising directions for future work.

</details>


### [276] [On the Curse of Dimensionality in Private Sparse Covariance Estimation and PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21951)
*Syamantak Kumar,Shourya Pandey,Purnamrita Sarkar,Kevin Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高维差分隐私下稀疏协方差矩阵（k-行列稀疏）的协方差估计（算子范数）和主成分分析问题。研究发现，非私有设置下poly(k, log d)个样本足够，但差分隐私下存在维度诅咒。上界方面，若假设主导特征向量稀疏，PCA可实现poly(k, log d)样本复杂度；下界方面，证明稀疏协方差估计和PCA均需poly(d)样本，与私有前版本存在指数级差距（k=polylog(d)时）。这是首个在高维稀疏估计问题中展示此类分离的工作，且技术可推广至标准DP PCA，得到更强的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究差分隐私下稀疏协方差估计任务中维度诅咒是否固有存在，即非私有设置中poly(k, log d)样本足以解决的问题，在差分隐私下是否必然需要Ω(d)样本。

Method: 上界：在假设主导特征向量稀疏的条件下，设计DP PCA算法达到poly(k, log d)样本复杂度。下界：构造信息论下界，证明稀疏协方差估计和PCA均需要poly(d)个样本，揭示私有与非私有设置之间的指数级差距。

Result: 当k=polylog(d)时，稀疏协方差估计和PCA在DP下存在指数级样本复杂度差距；技术可推广至标准DP PCA，得到更强的下界结果。

Conclusion: 高维差分隐私下稀疏协方差估计存在固有的维度诅咒，私有与非私有版本之间存在指数级样本复杂度分离，这是首个在高维稀疏估计问题中展示此类分离的工作。

Abstract: We study high-dimensional differentially private (DP) covariance estimation in the operator norm, and principal component analysis (PCA), under $k$-row-column sparsity ($k$-RCS) of the covariance matrix. In the non-private setting, it is known that $\mathsf{poly}(k, \log d)$ samples suffice to solve both of these problems. However, the only comparable result known under DP (Wang et al. 2021) requires $Ω(d)$ samples under standard parameterizations of the problem. We investigate when this curse of dimensionality is inherent for sparse covariance estimation tasks under DP.
  On the upper bound front, we show that a $\mathsf{poly}(k, \log d)$ sample complexity for PCA is possible under DP, if we also posit sparsity of the leading eigenvector. We complement this result with $\mathsf{poly}(d)$ lower bounds under DP for both sparse covariance estimation and PCA, establishing an exponential gap between the private and non-private variants of these problems when $k = \mathsf{polylog}(d)$. To our knowledge, no such separation has previously been demonstrated for any sparse estimation problems in private high-dimensional statistics. Our techniques are flexible enough that they imply stronger lower bounds even for the well-studied problem of standard DP PCA, without sparsity assumptions.

</details>


### [277] [Learning by Shifting: Temporal View Construction for Time Series Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21957)
*Abdul-Kazeem Shamba,Kerstin Bach,Gavin Taylor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ShiFT方法，通过显式编码时间平移不变性这一简单、确定性的视图构建策略，在时间序列对比学习中达到SOTA性能，同时减少训练时间，并系统分析了对比学习动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列对比学习方法依赖手工数据增强和掩码启发式策略，嵌入强领域假设，限制了跨不同时间模式的泛化能力并可能引入虚假相关性。作者旨在挑战这一范式，探索更简单有效的方法。

Method: 提出Shift Invariant Feature Training (ShiFT)，通过显式编码时间平移不变性，利用时间结构进行确定性视图构建，无需复杂的数据增强或掩码策略。

Result: 在六个多样化真实世界时间序列基准数据集以及UCR和UEA档案上取得SOTA性能，同时降低了训练时间。此外，系统分析了批量大小和负样本数量对下游性能的影响。

Conclusion: 简单、确定性的时间平移不变性编码足以学习强表征，为设计高效的时间序列对比学习框架提供了实用见解。

Abstract: Supervised learning demands large quantities of labeled data, a bottleneck that is expensive and reliant on domain-specific expertise. Self-supervised learning, particularly contrastive learning, has emerged as a compelling alternative, enabling rich representation learning directly from unlabeled data. Yet its success hinges critically on the design of positive and negative sample pairs. Existing approaches for time series rely on hand-crafted augmentations and masking heuristics that embed strong domain assumptions, often limiting generalization across diverse temporal patterns and potentially introducing spurious correlations.
