Abstract: Sensors are critical components of modern intelligent devices. The proliferation of the Internet of Things (IoT) and wearable mobile devices has enabled the integration of such sensors to monitor the environment and enable users to take predictive actions. Human activity recognition (HAR) is a popular application in which Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU)-based sensors, such as accelerometers and gyroscopes, are used to provide insights into health, training, and medical diagnosis. However, the accuracy of such a model is hindered by the lack of data. The diffusion model-based technique has proven successful in generating synthetic data for training HAR models. In this paper, we propose a backdoor training technique, IMU-DM-CLIP, that leverages a diffusion model to enable trigger-based attacks on HAR models. Our empirical analysis shows that the attack is successful even with a very small backdoor injection rate of 10\% and 10\% of the data guided for the diffusion model.

</details>


### [352] [RLM-Cascade: Response-Level Speculative Decoding for Cost-Efficient LLM API Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22840)
*Haifeng Wu,Srinivasan Manoharan,Fangbo Tu,Junhua Zhao,Jian Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RLM-Cascade 是一个代理层系统，通过在响应级别应用推测解码来降低 LLM API 成本。在 Claude Code 真实场景中，草稿使用率达 88.8%，成本降低 45.8%，中位延迟从 3,698ms 降至 2,026ms（1.83 倍加速），质量上 100% 通过率（基线 95%）。系统包含基于规则的复杂度路由器和混合工具调用策略，已开源并部署在生产环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低 LLM API 调用成本，同时不牺牲响应质量和端到端延迟，尤其针对 agentic 工作负载中大量简单请求场景。

Method: 采用代理层架构，包含三个核心组件：(1) 快速廉价的草稿模型（DeepSeek）生成候选响应；(2) 轻量级复杂度路由器根据规则选择 SKIPPED（仅草稿）、ACCEPT（草稿+验证）或 BYPASS（直接调用验证模型）路径；(3) 能力更强的验证模型（Opus）在必要时验证/增强响应。此外设计了混合工具调用策略，对 schema 关键的工具选择步骤绕过推测流水线。

Result: 在 Claude Code 生产环境的 125 个请求上，草稿使用率 88.8%，API 成本降低 45.8%；中位延迟 2,026ms 对比基线 3,698ms（1.83 倍加速）；20 任务基准测试 100% 通过率（基线 95%）。

Conclusion: RLM-Cascade 证明了在响应级别应用推测解码可以有效降低成本和延迟，同时保持或提升质量。系统已开源并在企业 AI 基础设施中生产部署。

Abstract: We present RLM-Cascade, a proxy-layer system that applies speculative decoding at the response level to reduce LLM API costs without requiring model architecture access or a shared vocabulary. A fast, inexpensive draft model generates a candidate response; a capable verify model accepts, enhances, or is bypassed entirely depending on a lightweight complexity router. On a real-world agentic coding workload (Claude Code), RLM-Cascade achieves a draft-use rate of 88.8% across 125 production requests, reducing API cost by 45.8% relative to a direct Opus baseline. Counter-intuitively, the proxy also reduces end-to-end latency: median response time is 2,026 ms versus 3,698 ms for Native Opus -- a 1.83X speedup at p50 -- because the SKIPPED path (DeepSeek only, no Opus call) dominates the workload distribution. Quality matches or exceeds the Opus baseline: 100% pass rate on a 20-task Code/Math/Instruct benchmark versus 95% for Native Opus. We further describe a rule-based complexity router that selects the SKIPPED path for simple agentic turns and a hybrid tool-call strategy that bypasses the speculative pipeline for schema-critical tool-selection turns. RLM-Cascade is deployed in production as an enterprise AI infrastructure component and published as open source with a live metrics dashboard and Prometheus endpoint.

</details>


### [353] [The Unseen Hand: Manipulating Model Fairness and SHAP with Targeted Identity Re-Association Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22858)
*Sannaan Khan,Muhammad U. S. Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TIRA攻击（包括PMiS和PRSMP两种算法），能够在无需访问模型内部的情况下，通过概率性微小扰动操纵模型输出，使公平性指标趋近理想值，并成功混淆SHAP解释，使受保护特征的归因几乎为零，比先前方法更具隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型日益强大且不透明，算法公平性和可解释性对确保问责制至关重要。然而，这些审计机制本身易受隐蔽操纵，现有数据无关攻击虽暴露了此漏洞，但会留下可检测痕迹。本文旨在设计更隐蔽的攻击方法，在不留下伪影的情况下混淆公平性审计和可解释性分析。

Method: 提出TIRA（Targeted Identity Re-Association）攻击家族，包含两种算法：(1) PMiS（Probabilistic Micro-Shuffling），通过局部相邻交换进行概率性微扰；(2) PRSMP（Probabilistic Rank-Shift Micro-Perturbation），引入微小随机排序偏移。两种方法均无需访问模型内部结构或特征表示，以迭代概率方式操纵模型输出。

Result: TIRA攻击能高效地将公平性指标推向理想值；在混淆SHAP解释方面，使受保护特征的归因残留近乎为零，显著优于先前工作。

Conclusion: TIRA攻击揭示了现有公平性与可解释性审计机制在面对精心设计的隐蔽攻击时的脆弱性，表明需要开发更鲁棒的审计方法以抵御此类操纵。

Abstract: As machine learning models grow more influential and opaque, algorithmic fairness and explainability are critical for ensuring accountability. However, we demonstrate that these auditing mechanisms are themselves vulnerable to subtle manipulation, camouflaging the influence of protected features. While prior work on data-agnostic attacks has exposed this vulnerability, they leave behind detectable artifacts that compromise their stealth. We introduce Targeted Identity Re-Association (TIRA) attacks, a novel family of attacks that iteratively and probabilistically manipulate a model's outputs without requiring access to the model's internals or feature representations. We formalize two algorithms: Probabilistic Micro-Shuffling (PMiS), which applies localized adjacent swaps, and Probabilistic Rank-Shift Micro-Perturbation (PRSMP), which introduces small, randomized rank shifts. We empirically demonstrate that TIRA attacks are highly effective at pushing fairness metrics towards ideal values. Crucially, TIRA attacks successfully confound SHAP-based explanations, leaving effectively zero residual attribution for protected features, a major improvement over prior work.

</details>


### [354] [When AUC 0.998 Is Not Enough: A Candidate Evaluation Protocol for Hidden-State Probes of Indirect Prompt Injection in Multimodal Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22864)
*Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以Qwen2.5-VL-7B在Mind2Web上的实验表明，基于隐藏状态探针的高AUC值本身不能作为恶意内容检测的证据，并提出了配套诊断方法作为控制集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑"高AUC=恶意内容检测"这一隐含假设，指出在视觉-语言模型上对间接提示注入的探测结果可能被过度解读。

Method: 采用单骨干案例研究（Qwen2.5-VL-7B + Mind2Web），通过教师强制回放方式；提出两种事后诊断方法：文本侧注入的配对构造标量基线和覆盖表面的同步骤噪声匹配视觉对照。

Result: 高AUC不能独立作为恶意内容检测的证据；提出的诊断方法允许部分语义解读但不支持无条件的恶意内容解释。

Conclusion: 将诊断方法打包为候选控制集，附带报告启发式规则，阐明高AUC的含义边界；标注类别是注入表面存在性而非攻击成功性，跨骨干和基准的泛化仍为推测。

Abstract: Hidden-state probing -- a linear classifier on a frozen vision-language model's internal activations -- has emerged as an attractive evaluation tool for flagging indirect prompt injection (IPI) in multimodal computer-use agents before the agent emits a corrupted action. We argue, on a single-backbone cautionary case study (Qwen2.5-VL-7B on Mind2Web, teacher-forced replay), that a high probing AUC on a clean-vs-attack split is not, on its own, evidence of malicious-content detection. Two post-hoc diagnostics -- a paired-construction scalar baseline on text-side injections, and same-step nuisance-matched visual controls on the overlay surface -- do not license an unqualified malicious-content interpretation of the headline while leaving room for partly-semantic readings. We package the diagnostics as a candidate control set with reporting heuristics for what a high clean-vs-attack AUC does and does not license. Labels are injection-surface-present, not attack success; generalisation beyond this backbone and benchmark is a conjecture.

</details>


### [355] [Discovering Crystal Structure Prediction Algorithms with an AI Co-Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22866)
*Kiyoung Seong,Nayoung Kim,Sungsoo Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出人机共发现系统HACO，通过跨领域搜索和稀疏人工引导，从视觉领域的MaskGIT模型出发，结合晶体学对称性知识，开发出掩码生成晶体变换器MaskGXT，在晶体结构预测任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学算法发现依赖人工试错，跨领域迁移能力有限。作者希望构建一个AI科学家系统，能够自主跨领域搜索可迁移的建模思想，并结合人类领域知识实现高效的算法发现。

Method: HACO系统首先跨领域搜索生成式建模方法，识别出视觉领域的MaskGIT作为晶体结构预测的候选框架；然后将其实例化为离散token模型；在稀疏人工引导下，依次引入晶体学对称性token、空间群分层采样和子坐标细化模块，最终形成MaskGXT模型。

Result: 在MP-20多晶型划分上，MaskGXT的METRe准确率达79.06%，远超最强基线70.87%；同时在标准MP-20和MPTS-52基准上取得最佳匹配率。

Conclusion: 在验证成本低、速度快且对齐良好的领域，迁移引导的交互式AI共发现系统能够通过识别可迁移的建模原则并结合人类领域引导，有效促进科学算法发现。

Abstract: We introduce Human-AI Co-discovery system (HACO) for scientific algorithm discovery through cross-domain search and sparse human steering. Starting from the goal of generating crystal structures from chemical compositions, HACO searched across generative modeling methodologies from multiple fields and identified MaskGIT, a masked generative model from vision, as a promising framework for crystal structure prediction (CSP). HACO instantiated this masked formulation as a discrete token model of crystal structure; guided by sparse high-level human objectives, it then added crystallographic symmetry tokens, space group stratified sampling for polymorph coverage, and sub-bin coordinate refinement, yielding the Masked Generative Crystal Transformer (MaskGXT). On the MP-20 polymorph split, MaskGXT reaches 79.06% match-everyone-to-reference (METRe) accuracy, compared with 70.87% for the strongest evaluated baseline. MaskGXT also attains the best match rate on standard MP-20 and MPTS-52 CSP benchmarks. These results provide evidence that, in domains offering cheap, fast, and well-aligned validation, transfer-guided interactive AI co-scientists can contribute to scientific algorithm discovery by identifying transferable modeling principles and combining them with targeted human domain guidance.

</details>


### [356] [SpotAttention: Plug-In Block-Sparse Routing for Pretrained Long-Context Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22874)
*Huzama Ahmad,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpotAttention 通过轻量级选择器蒸馏预训练 Transformer 的注意力分布，实现 top-K 稀疏注意力，在 128K 上下文长度下匹配密集注意力的精度，解码速度比 FlashAttention 快 3.9 倍，并将 KV 缓存量化至 INT4/FP4 无精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文预训练 LLM 的 prefill 计算随序列长度二次增长，且每个 decode 步骤需重新读取线性增长的 KV 缓存，导致推理成本过高。现有稀疏注意力虽能降低开销，但选择相关子集的过程本身代价高昂。

Method: 提出 SpotAttention，包含一个轻量级选择器，通过 KL 散度蒸馏冻结预训练 Transformer 的注意力分布来学习估计。选择器为每个查询选取 top-K 键，并采用 dual top-p 规则从校准分布中直接读取每查询每层的预算。

Result: 在 Qwen3（密集，4B-32B）和 Qwen3.5（混合线性/全注意力，4B-9B）上，SpotAttention 在 128K 上下文（训练长度的 8 倍）下匹配密集注意力精度。L=128K 解码比 FlashAttention 快 3.9 倍，比最强无训练基线 Twilight 快 1.8 倍。选择器的 K-cache 量化至 INT4 或 FP4 微尺度可缩小 3.5 倍且无精度损失。

Conclusion: SpotAttention 通过轻量级蒸馏选择器实现了高效且准确的长上下文稀疏注意力，显著加速推理并支持极低精度的 KV 缓存量化，为长序列 LLM 部署提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Long contexts have become standard in pretrained LLMs, yet they remain expensive to run: prefill compute grows quadratically with sequence length, and every decode step re-reads a key-value cache that grows linearly with it. Sparse attention cuts these costs by attending only to a relevant subset of past tokens, but selecting that subset is itself expensive. We present SpotAttention, a lightweight selector that attaches to a frozen pretrained transformer and learns by KL distillation to estimate its attention distribution. The selector picks the top-K keys each query attends to, and because its estimate is a calibrated distribution, a dual top-p rule reads the per-query, per-layer budget directly from it. Across Qwen3 (dense, 4B-32B) and Qwen3.5 (hybrid linear/full attention, 4B-9B), SpotAttention matches dense accuracy at contexts up to 128K tokens, eight times the training length. Decode at L=128K runs 3.9x faster than FlashAttention and 1.8x faster than Twilight, the strongest training-free baseline. Quantizing the selector's K-cache to INT4 or FP4 microscale shrinks it 3.5x at no accuracy cost.

</details>


### [357] [Priority-Aware Learning-Unlearning Correction for Dynamic Decentralized LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22878)
*Nuocheng Yang,Yechen He,Sihua Wang,Zihan Chen,Tony Q. S. Quek,Changchuan Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于正交LoRA的优先级感知学习-遗忘修正框架，通过拓扑调整实现去中心化联邦学习中设备动态加入/离开后的高效参数修正，在无线资源约束下优先处理主导残差项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化边缘网络中设备动态加入或离开导致全局微调参数中设备贡献深度纠缠，现有方法难以高效进行连续修正，需要一种能够自适应调整并选择性移除历史贡献的机制。

Method: 1) 设计正交LoRA机制，生成训练后贡献坐标，实现无历史依赖的投影添加/删除；2) 分析修正瓶颈，开发优先级感知策略，根据主导残差项在拓扑细化、局部修正、近端阻尼和同步调度中选择操作；3) 提出资源分配算法，在每轮无线约束下按层组分配有限通信资源优先处理主要瓶颈。

Result: 实验表明所提框架在设备加入和离开事件中均实现了稳健的后事件修正，验证了不同残差区域需要不同的修正操作。

Conclusion: 通过正交LoRA与优先级感知策略结合，有效解决了去中心化联邦学习中动态成员变化后的参数修正难题，为边缘部署LLM提供了一种高效的持续学习方法。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed at the network edge to provide pervasive generative AI services, decentralized federated learning (DFL) provides a vital mechanism for privacy-preserving, domain-specific fine-tuning through peer-to-peer exchanges of parameter-efficient updates. However, the dynamic nature of practical decentralized edge networks, where devices may dynamically join or leave the collaborative training process, requires the system to continuously adapt to new data while selectively removing prior contributions. This correction process remains a significant bottleneck, as individual device updates become deeply entangled within the global fine-tuned parameters. To address this challenge, we propose a priority-aware learning-unlearning correction framework based on orthogonal LoRA that can enhance the knowledge evaluation through topology adjustment. Specifically, we first design an orthogonal LoRA mechanism that yields post-training contribution coordinates, enabling history-free projection addition and deletion in response to membership changes. We then analyze the correction bottleneck and develop a priority-aware policy that selects among topology refinement, local correction, proximal damping, and synchronization scheduling according to the dominant residual term. A resource allocation algorithm is further developed to allocate limited communication across layer groups, prioritizing the primary bottlenecks within per-round wireless constraints. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves robust post-event correction for both device join and leave events and validate that different residual regimes necessitate distinct correction actions.

</details>


### [358] [Physiology-Aware CNN and Zero-Shot Multimodal LLMs for ECG Image Classification: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22889)
*Khalil Ahammad,Derek Abbott,Mohsen Dorraki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对比了零样本多模态大语言模型（GPT-5.2、GPT-4.1、Gemini-2.5 Pro）与基于CNN的模型（包括提出的LeadGroupECG）在12导联心电图图像正常/异常二分类任务上的表现。CNN模型在内部测试集（ROC-AUC 0.92-0.94）和外部PTB-XL数据集（ROC-AUC 0.85-0.86）上表现稳定，LeadGroupECG在保持泛化能力的同时显著提升了骨干网络性能。而零样本LLMs的判别能力近乎随机（ROC-AUC约0.5），即使网格校准背景能略微改善PR-AUC。结论：当前多模态LLMs的心电图零样本诊断判别能力有限，仍需基于临床知识的专用架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型被越来越多地用于解读12导联心电图图像，但其解读结果往往缺乏验证。心电图图像理解依赖于精确的波形形态、导联关系和准确的间期测量，与通用图像理解有本质区别。本研究旨在系统评估零样本多模态LLMs能否可靠区分正常与异常心电图，并与基于CNN的临床参考模型进行对比。

Method: 将标准12导联心电图记录渲染为单页图像，构建正常/异常二分类任务。测试GPT-5.2、GPT-4.1和Gemini-2.5 Pro三个LLMs，使用固定零样本提示词多次运行。同时开发了生理学感知的CNN模型LeadGroupECG，具备从预定义解剖导联组聚合特征的能力，并与ResNet18、DenseNet121、VGG16基线模型在内部测试集和外部PTB-XL数据集上进行对比评估。

Result: CNN模型在不同随机种子下表现稳定，内部ROC-AUC为0.92-0.94，外部ROC-AUC为0.85-0.86。提出的LeadGroupECG在内部数据集上显著优于其骨干网络，且不牺牲外部泛化能力，与其他基线模型竞争力相当，并能一致地突出解剖导联组的贡献。相比之下，零样本LLMs的判别能力接近随机（ROC-AUC约0.5），使用网格校准背景的心电图相比无网格背景的心电图，PR-AUC略有改善。

Conclusion: 尽管多模态LLMs能生成合理的电图叙述文本，但其零样本诊断判别能力仍然有限。因此，基于临床框架的领域专用架构仍然是AI心电图解读所必需的。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (LLMs) are increasingly adopted to interpret 12-lead ECG images, though the interpretations often lack validation. However, ECG image understanding significantly differs from general images as it depends on precise waveform morphology, lead relationships and accurate interval measurements. This study investigated whether zero-shot multimodal LLMs can reliably distinguish normal and abnormal ECG images and, in parallel, evaluated CNN-based models for clinically grounded references. Standard 12-lead ECG recordings were rendered as single-page images for a binary normal-abnormal classification task. Three prominent LLMs (GPT-5.2, GPT-4.1, and Gemini-2.5 Pro) were tested using a fixed zero-shot prompt across multiple runs. In parallel, a physiology-aware CNN-based model was developed with the capability to aggregate features from the predefined anatomical lead groups. The model was compared with ResNet18, DenseNet121, VGG16 baselines, and all the models were evaluated on an internal test set and external PTB-XL dataset. Across seeds, CNN-based models demonstrated stable discrimination, with average internal ROC-AUC of 0.92-0.94, and external ROC-AUC of 0.85-0.86. The proposed LeadGroupECG model significantly improved over its backbone internally without compromising external generalization. It remained competitive with other baselines, while consistently highlighting anatomical lead-group contributions. In contrast, zero-shot LLM discrimination remained near-chance (ROC-AUC around 0.5). The PR-AUC improved slightly when ECGs used a grid-based calibration background compared with the grid-free ECGs. Although multimodal LLMs can generate reasonable ECG narratives, their zero-shot diagnostic discrimination remains limited. Therefore, clinically framed, domain-specific architectures remain essential for AI-based ECG interpretation.

</details>


### [359] [Learning Graphs through Continuous Information Entropy Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22895)
*Hui Cong,Bo Sun,Ziheng Jiao,Yisheng An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出场信息图网络(FGN)，将图视为潜在连续信息熵场的离散实例化，通过标量场调节消息传递，实现结构与场平滑度的自强化学习，在节点和图分类任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图论将边视为原始构造，只能描述关系存在与否，无法解释关系为何产生。作者希望建立一种能够揭示关系背后成因的解释性图学习框架。

Method: 提出Field-informed Graph Network (FGN)，从节点特征学习标量场，利用场调制权重影响消息传递，同时通过信息论目标平衡结构保真度与场平滑度，形成自强化循环：场调节信息扩散，更新后的节点表示迭代优化场。

Result: 在节点分类和图分类基准上取得优越性能，对扰动具有鲁棒性，并生成结构连贯的场表示。

Conclusion: FGN通过学习模拟自身共演化过程，成功将图的离散结构与潜在的连续信息熵场联系起来，为图学习提供了新的解释性范式。

Abstract: Graph theory is inherently descriptive, capturing what relationships exist but not why they arise, because it treats edges as primitive constructs. This paper proposes a new explanatory framework for graph learning, where relationships emerge from latent continuous information entropy fields, and a graph becomes a discrete instantiation of an underlying field. To formalize this field, we introduce the Field-informed Graph Network (FGN). It learns a scalar field from node features and leverages it to modulate message passing. The information-theoretic objective balances structural fidelity with field smoothness, forming a self-reinforcing loop. In this loop, the field modulates information diffusion through field-modulated weighting, and the updated node representations iteratively refine the field. As a result, FGN learns by simulating its own co-evolution. Extensive experiments on node classification and graph classification benchmarks demonstrate superior performance, robustness to perturbations, and structurally coherent field representations.

</details>


### [360] [PromptDyG: Test-Time Prompt Adaptation on Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22914)
*Guoguo Ai,Chaoxi Niu,Hui Yan,Joey Tianyi Zhou,Yew-Soon Ong,Guansong Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PromptDyG提出了一种基于无监督测试时提示适配（test-time prompt adaptation）的框架，用于离散时间动态图（DTDG）的在线学习，在冻结的骨干网络上通过学习特征级无标签熵最小化的动态图提示，持续建模演化模式，并在六个基准数据集上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DTDG方法专注于离线学习设置，模型用历史快照训练一次后便不再更新，无法捕捉图快照间不断演化的复杂性以及测试图快照中的分布偏移。

Method: 提出PromptDyG框架，在冻结的骨干网络上学习表达性动态图提示，通过最小化特征级无标签熵（feature-wise, label-free entropy）实现无监督的测试时提示适配，从而在在线设置下高效连续地建模演化模式。

Result: 理论上证明该无监督提示适配能保证正负样本对之间更大的相似性间隔；在六个基准数据集上的实验结果显示PromptDyG持续且显著优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 无监督测试时提示适配是一种有效的在线动态图学习范式，能够在不更新骨干网络的情况下适应分布偏移，实现更准确的动态预测。

Abstract: Activities in numerous evolving systems can be represented as dynamic graphs in snapshot form at different time intervals, i.e., discrete-time dynamic graphs (DTDGs). Existing methods show impressive advances in capturing historical temporal evolution patterns in DTDGs, but they focus on addressing an offline learning setting, where models are trained using historical snapshots once and then evaluated to all subsequent graph snapshots without further updating. This fails to capture 1) the nature of evolving complexities across graph snapshots and 2) the distribution shift in the testing graph snapshots. To address these problems, we propose PromptDyG, a novel framework that leverages unsupervised test-time Prompt adaptation for Dynamic Graph learning under a live-update online setting. The key insight is that an expressive dynamic graph prompt can be learned on a frozen backbone via minimization of feature-wise, label-free entropy to efficiently and continuously model the evolving patterns. We show theoretically that this unsupervised prompt adaptation can guarantee a larger similarity margin between positive and negative pairs, facilitating more accurate dynamic predictions. It is further confirmed by our extensive empirical results on six benchmark datasets that show consistent and significant improvements of PromptDyG over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [361] [GRAIN: Group Aggregation via Min-Norm Objective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22917)
*Nghia Bui,Jiarui Yao,Lijing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GRAIN 算法，通过最小范数凸组合替代均值聚合来优化小批量梯度，解决梯度冲突问题，在大规模预训练模型上实现更低的训练方差和更优的均值性能，且收敛速度与 SGD 相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习尤其在过参数化大模型微调场景下存在严重的训练不稳定性问题：随机因素导致解空间差异大，训练成本高且下游数据少，重复实验降方差不可行。

Method: 提出 GRAIN 算法，用最小范数凸组合替代传统均值聚合来处理批内和批间梯度，确保聚合更新与每个组梯度内积非负，消除梯度冲突。

Result: 理论保证 O(1/T) 收敛率，均匀稳定性界严格优于 SGD；实验在生成、分类、回归等大模型任务上均提升均值性能并降低方差，无额外训练开销。

Conclusion: GRAIN 是一种轻量、高效、即插即用的训练算法，能有效缓解大模型训练不稳定性，且无需额外存储或计算成本。

Abstract: Learning instability is a long-standing problem across machine learning, but it is especially acute in the overparameterized regime that defines modern deep learning: large models fine-tuned or trained on limited data traverse flat loss landscapes with many nearly-equivalent minima, and stochastic factors (initialization, data order, dropout, hardware non-determinism) can route optimization to very different solutions. The rise of large pretrained models (LPMs) makes the problem more urgent: training cost is high, downstream data is often small, and repeated runs for variance reduction are prohibitive. We introduce \textbf{GRAIN} (\textbf{G}roup \textbf{A}ggregation via m\textbf{IN}-norm objective), a lightweight training algorithm that replaces the mean aggregation used in mini-batch optimization (both across mini-batches and within a mini-batch) with a min-norm convex combination of group-wise gradients. \mName guarantees a non-negative inner product between the aggregated update and every group gradient, resolving intra- and inner-batch gradient conflict, and retains an $\mathcal{O}(1/T)$ convergence rate comparable to SGD. Under mild smoothness and absolute-continuity assumptions, the min-norm solution differs almost surely from the arithmetic mean, which yields a uniform-stability bound for \mName strictly tighter than the standard bound for SGD. Empirically across generation, classification, and regression at LPM scale, \mName delivers consistent improvements in mean performance and reductions in run-to-run variance over a broad suite of tasks, with no extra training-time or storage cost beyond a single backward pass.

</details>


### [362] [Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Sparse-Reward Search in Commutative Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22922)
*Giorgi Butbaia,Paul Orland,Coco Huang,Davide Passaro,Lucas Fagan,Michele Tarquini,Hailong Dao,David Eisenbud,Ali Shehper,Sergei Gukov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于层级强化学习（HRL）的框架，利用等变图神经网络策略和约束选项机制，将Kalai代数Hirsch猜想的反例构建转化为稀疏奖励的强化学习问题，首次将HRL应用于交换代数领域，并在多个维度上优于经典RL算法和贪心搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长期存在的数学猜想（如Kalai代数Hirsch猜想）时，机器学习面临极端的奖励稀疏性问题，传统方法难以有效探索和发现反例。

Method: 提出一种基于约束选项的层级强化学习（HRL）框架，结合等变图神经网络策略，将反例构建问题建模为图上的稀疏奖励RL问题，通过学习有用的时间抽象来分层探索。

Result: 在多个维度上，所提方法一致优于经典强化学习算法和贪心搜索方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用问题的层级结构，成功将HRL首次应用于交换代数问题，为机器学习解决数学猜想提供了新范式。

Abstract: Applying machine learning techniques to solving long-standing mathematical conjectures can be particularly challenging due to their extreme reward sparsity. As an illustrative example, we consider Kalai's algebraic Hirsch conjecture and recast the construction of its counterexamples as a sparse-reward reinforcement learning problem on graphs. We propose a constrained options-based HRL framework with an equivariant graph neural network policy, which allows us to learn useful temporal abstractions for this task. We evaluate our approach over a wide range of degrees and demonstrate that it consistently outperforms classical RL algorithms as well as greedy search. By exploiting the hierarchical structure of the problem, we effectively provide a first-of-its-kind application of HRL to a problem in commutative algebra.

</details>


### [363] [EEG Benchmarking Needs a Task Specification Layer: NeuroDoc for Rulebook-Guided, Executable Benchmark Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22925)
*Chengxuan Qin,Zhige Chen,Shu Peng,Rui Yang,Jiping Cui,Yikai Dong,Jun Li,Liu Peng,Zhida Shang,Mingze Tang,Kay Chen Tan,Jibin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化任务规范语言（NeuroDoc）和共享规则手册（NeuroAudit），将异构公共EEG数据集标准化为可复用、可审计、可执行的基准测试单元，并发布了包含53个条目、245个任务定义的社区审阅EEG基准语料库，在四个基础模型骨干上验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共EEG数据集缺乏共享的任务规范层，无法将异构记录转化为可复用的基准单元；现有标准仅组织文件、元数据和溯源信息，但未以通用语言和规则手册定义EEG任务，导致关键任务语义分散在论文、代码和人工解释中。

Method: 每个基准条目由一个与可执行任务内核同步的任务文档（NeuroDoc）表示；规则手册（NeuroAudit）定义任务字段、证据要求、文档-内核对齐、审阅状态和机器可检查约束；通过社区审阅流程进行管理。

Result: 发布了社区审阅的EEG基准语料库，包含53个已完成和审阅的条目、245个任务定义，涵盖多种范式；在四个EEG基础模型骨干上验证了基准单元的可实例化性，提供了基于执行的证据。

Conclusion: NeuroDoc和NeuroAudit作为操作支持层，能够实现规则手册指导的起草、升级、审阅、修订和发布管理，为可复用、可审计、可执行的EEG基准测试基础设施提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models increasingly rely on multi-dataset training and evaluation, yet public EEG datasets still lack a shared task specification layer that can turn heterogeneous recordings into reusable benchmark units. Existing standards organize files, metadata, and provenance, but they do not specify EEG tasks under a common language and rulebook, leaving critical task semantics scattered across papers, code, and manual interpretation. We investigate whether heterogeneous public EEG datasets can be standardized through a structured task specification language paired with a shared rulebook. Our methodology represents each benchmark entry as a task document synchronized with an executable task kernel, with the rulebook defining task fields, evidence requirements, document-kernel alignment, review states, and machine-checkable constraints. Using this methodology, we release a community-reviewed EEG benchmark corpus centered on 53 completed and reviewed entries with 245 task definitions spanning diverse paradigms, and we introduce NeuroDoc and NeuroAudit as the operational support layer for rulebook-guided drafting, upgrading, review, amendment, and release management. We further examine whether the resulting benchmark units can be instantiated in a shared downstream setting across four EEG foundation model backbones, providing execution-based evidence for reusable, auditable, and executable EEG benchmarking infrastructure.

</details>


### [364] [FORGE: Fused On-Register Gradient Elimination for Memory-Efficient LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22932)
*Dikshant Kukreja,Kritarth Prasad,Avinash Anand,Zhengkui Wang,Erik Cambria,Timothy Liu,Aik Beng Ng,Simon See,Bapi Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FORGE通过将优化器步骤折叠到反向传播中，以逐tile方式在寄存器中即时消费梯度，消除了传统训练中所有梯度同时存活的显存瓶颈，在保持优化器更新数学等价性的前提下，将梯度内存开销减半以上，并实现约1.5倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反向传播采用两阶段调度：先计算并写入所有梯度到内存，优化器再读取更新。这导致在阶段衔接处所有层的梯度同时存活，形成训练内存天花板。作者认为这种物化梯度是微分阶段划分的人为产物，并非学习所需的必要量，因此旨在消除它。

Method: FORGE将优化器步骤融合到反向传播中，以tile粒度逐块处理：每个梯度tile在寄存器中生成后立即被消费（应用优化器更新），从不写出为完整张量。在全精度下该融合步骤与标准优化器更新数学等价；在bf16和8-bit精度下通过随机舍入实现无偏的保真更新。该方法适用于任何逐元素优化器规则和线性层，兼容张量并行和序列并行。

Result: FORGE将优化器步骤的梯度内存开销减半以上；在微调和持续预训练常用的小批量场景下运行速度约提升1.5倍；集成到张量并行的Megatron-LM中时，在相同GPU上支持4倍于标准优化器的微批量大小来训练8B参数模型。

Conclusion: 梯度物化并非训练的必要条件，通过将优化器更新融合到反向传播的寄存器级tile消费中，可以显著降低内存开销并提升速度，同时保持更新的数学等价性或可控的保真度偏差。

Abstract: Reverse-mode differentiation computes every weight gradient, writes it to memory, and only then lets the optimizer read it back. This two-phase schedule sets the memory ceiling of modern training: at the seam between the phases, every layer's gradient is live at once. We argue that this materialized gradient is an artifact of how differentiation is staged, not a quantity that learning requires -- and we eliminate it. FORGE folds the optimizer step into the backward pass and applies it one tile at a time, entirely in registers, so each gradient tile is consumed the instant it is produced and never becomes a tensor. The fusion changes only when the update happens, not what it computes: in full precision the fused step is provably exact -- the identical optimizer update, for every element-wise rule -- and that exactness survives tensor- and sequence-parallel sharding; in the bf16 and 8-bit regimes used in practice it is faithful rather than bit-identical, its deviation bounded and, for the weight store, rendered unbiased by stochastic rounding. Because each gradient tile is born and consumed in the same registers, it is never converted down to bf16 to be stored and read back; FORGE thus preserves the full-precision fidelity that both bf16 and 8-bit optimizers lose to that conversion. Nor is the method tied to one architecture or one optimizer: linear layers are ubiquitous, and FORGE reclaims the gradient memory of any of them under any element-wise rule. Empirically FORGE more than halves the memory of an optimizer step and, at the small batch sizes typical of fine-tuning and continued pretraining, runs about 1.5x faster; integrated into tensor-parallel Megatron-LM it fits 8B training at four times the micro-batch a standard optimizer allows on the same GPUs.

</details>


### [365] [Provable Benefits of RLVR over SFT for Reasoning Models: Learning to Backtrack Efficiently](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22938)
*Stanley Wei,Juno Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上分析了强化学习（RLVR）相比监督微调（SFT）能提升推理能力的原因，证明SFT因缺乏负样本而无法学会高效回溯，而RLVR可以学会从死胡同回溯，导致两者在推理计算量上存在指数级差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为何强化微调比纯监督微调能带来更好的推理性能，从理论层面解释这一经验现象。

Method: 将思维链推理建模为图上的路径寻找问题，分别分析SFT（仅训练最优路径，无负样本）和RLVR（使用可验证奖励的强化学习）两种方法的推理行为，进行理论证明。

Result: 证明SFT无法学习高效回溯，而RLVR可以通过仅使用结果奖励学会从死胡同回溯；两者在推理时计算量上存在指数级分离；RLVR模型学会识别推理链中困难决策的位置，从而更好分配推理计算；RLVR的推理轨迹可蒸馏到基础模型使其也能高效回溯。

Conclusion: 强化微调通过学会回溯机制带来了推理性能的显著提升，且这种能力可以通过蒸馏迁移，为提升LLM推理能力提供了理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated that reinforcement fine-tuning of pretrained base models can lead to significant gains in reasoning performance at inference time. In this work, we theoretically analyze why reinforcement fine-tuning induces better reasoning ability than purely supervised fine-tuning (SFT) methods. We model chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning as a pathfinding problem on graphs and compare the popular method of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) against traditional SFT. We prove that SFT, when trained on golden shortest paths without negative examples, fails to learn how to efficiently backtrack. In contrast, an RLVR-trained model can learn how to efficiently backtrack from dead ends using only outcome reward. This leads to an exponential separation in inference-time compute between the two methods, and demonstrates that RLVR leads the model to learn the location of difficult decisions in a reasoning chain, ultimately allowing for better allocation of inference-time compute. Finally, we show that the reasoning traces of an RLVR model can be distilled to train a base model to backtrack efficiently as well.

</details>


### [366] [Neural Operator Processes for Probabilistic Operator Learning under Partial Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22946)
*Jose Miguel Lara-Rangel,Serge Guillas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经算子过程（NOPs）框架，统一神经过程条件化与神经算子解码，实现从稀疏、不规则观测中预测完整输出场，支持确定性与概率性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子通常需要稠密输入-输出训练数据并在推理时观测完整输入，但许多科学问题需要从不稀疏、不规则或部分观测中预测解场，且存在不确定性。

Method: NOPs框架：采用编码器-解码器架构，编码器通过卷积池化摘要或查询对齐注意力对稀疏联合输入-输出观测进行条件化，解码器进行神经算子解码；引入潜在随机变量处理不确定性。

Result: 稀疏条件算子学习在多种设定下可行，可匹配稠密网格性能；局部上下文-查询几何在非周期设定中至关重要，但在谱平滑周期设定中作用较小；不确定性感知算子学习在潜在条件化补充而非覆盖局部几何路径时效果最佳。

Conclusion: 为部分观测下的概率算子学习提供了基础，有助于桥接算子学习与函数空间中的概率元学习。

Abstract: Neural operators learn mappings between function spaces, but are typically developed with dense input-output training fields and fully observed inputs at inference. Many scientific problems require instead predicting solution fields from sparse, irregular, or partial observations under uncertainty. We introduce Neural Operator Processes (NOPs), a framework that unifies neural-process conditioning with neural-operator decoding to predict full output fields from limited context. NOPs condition on sparse joint input-output observations and support deterministic and probabilistic prediction within a shared encoder-decoder architecture. We study two conditioning strategies, convolutional pooled summaries and query-aligned attention, and analyze how their interaction with latent stochastic variables depends on PDE geometry. Across function regression and three PDE benchmarks, we find that sparse conditional operator learning is viable and can match dense-grid behavior in several regimes, that preserving local context-query geometry is essential in non-periodic settings but less so in spectrally smooth periodic regimes, and that uncertainty-aware operator learning succeeds when latent conditioning complements rather than overwrites the local geometric pathway. These results provide a basis for probabilistic operator learning under partial observations and help bridge operator learning and probabilistic meta-learning in function space.

</details>


### [367] [DT-GOL: Dual-Track Geometric Online Learning in Nonstationary Environment with Label Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22950)
*Yulin Wang,Yi He,Dianlong You,Di Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DT-GOL是一种双轨几何在线学习框架，通过将标签延迟建模为半监督任务，利用特征拓扑演化作为几何代理，并设计动态证据校准与解耦双轨架构，在概念漂移场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线学习研究忽视标签延迟问题，新数据因标注过程缓慢昂贵无法及时获得标签，阻碍了模型对动态环境的快速适应。

Method: 提出DT-GOL框架：(1) 将延迟挑战建模为半监督任务，利用特征实时拓扑演化作为几何代理；(2) 引入动态证据校准机制，将几何信息蒸馏为感知不确定性的软标签；(3) 设计解耦双轨架构：主学习器由延迟真实标签严格更新作为稳定锚点，瞬态分支利用软几何知识进行低风险前向适应。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上的大量实验表明，DT-GOL显著优于现有最先进基线方法，尤其在概念漂移场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: DT-GOL通过从时间补偿转向空间推理，有效弥合了监督延迟差距，实现了延迟窗口内的主动监督适应，同时缓解了确认偏差和稳定性-可塑性困境。

Abstract: Online learning is crucial for handling complex data streams in big data applications. Recent research has begun to focus on dynamic scenarios, i.e., non-stationary environments. However, a crucial yet often overlooked aspect is label latency, where new data may not receive labels in time due to the slow and expensive labeling process, thus hindering rapid adaptation to dynamic environments.
  To resolve this impasse, we propose Dual-Track Geometry Online Learning (DT-GOL), a novel framework that shifts from temporal compensation to spatial reasoning to bridge the supervised latency gap. By modeling the delay challenge as a semi-supervised task, we leverage real-time topological evolution of features as a reliable geometric surrogate for unobservable conceptual changes to achieve proactive supervised adaptation within the delay window. Unlike rigid self-training, we introduce a dynamic evidence calibration mechanism that distills geometric information into soft labels that perceive uncertainty, effectively mitigating the confirmation bias inherent in hard pseudo-labels. Furthermore, to resolve the stability-plasticity dilemma, we design a decoupled dual-track architecture in which a master learner serves as a stable anchor, updated strictly from delayed ground truth, while a transient branch leverages soft geometric knowledge for low-risk forward adaptation. Extensive experiments on real and synthetic datasets demonstrate that DT-GOL significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art baseline methods, especially in scenarios with concept drift.

</details>


### [368] [PG-MAP: Joint MAP Optimization for Inference-Time Alignment of Diffusion and Flow-Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22958)
*Ruolan Sun,Pawel Polak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PG-MAP框架，通过在推理时对条件c和潜变量z_t进行联合轨迹级Gibbs-MAP/近端能量优化，实现无需训练的文本到图像模型对齐，同时兼容扩散模型和流匹配模型，显著提升PickScore、Aesthetic等对齐指标，在SD3.5-medium上达到91.9% PickScore胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐方法仅沿单一控制轴（如分类器无梯度引导、注意力编辑或基于奖励的潜变量扰动）操作，无法建模条件变量与潜变量之间的联合依赖关系，且难以跨生成传输机制迁移。

Method: 提出PG-MAP框架，将推理时对齐建模为轨迹级Gibbs-MAP/近端能量优化问题，通过前向一致性耦合对条件c和潜状态z_t进行联合更新，可选地由冻结的偏好奖励引导。针对扩散模型和流匹配模型分别进行传输特定适配。

Result: 在SD1.5、SDXL上持续提升PickScore和Aesthetic指标，可与调优后的CFG结合达到最佳性能。在SD3.5-medium上实现91.9% PickScore和75.7% HPS胜率，消融实验排除噪声伪影。人类评估确认优于强基线。Oracle路由分析显示条件和潜变量优化的相对重要性取决于提示类型。

Conclusion: PG-MAP通过联合条件-潜变量优化有效提升文本到图像生成的对齐质量，且无需额外训练。不同提示类型对优化策略的偏好差异表明，按提示选择器的进一步优化仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Inference-time alignment of pretrained text-to-image models is typically performed along a single control axis, such as classifier-free guidance, attention editing, or reward-based latent perturbations. This limitation prevents modeling joint dependencies between conditioning and latent variables and hinders transfer across generative transports. We propose PG-MAP, a training-free framework that formulates inference-time alignment as a trajectory-level Gibbs-MAP / proximal energy optimization over the conditioning $c$ and latent state $z_t$ via a forward-consistency coupling, optionally guided by a frozen preference reward. This joint formulation enables coordinated updates across modalities while remaining compatible with both diffusion and flow-matching models through transport-specific adaptations. Across diffusion backbones (SD~1.5, SDXL), PG-MAP consistently improves alignment metrics such as PickScore and Aesthetic, and can be effectively combined with tuned classifier-free guidance to achieve the strongest overall performance. On flow-matching models (SD3.5-medium), the framework reduces to a latent-only variant, achieving $\mathbf{91.9\%}$ PickScore and $75.7\%$ HPS win rates against a static baseline, with controlled experiments ruling out noise-related artifacts. Human evaluations further confirm consistent preference over strong baselines, including tuned CFG and compute-matched universal guidance. Finally, an oracle-routing analysis shows that the relative importance of conditioning and latent optimization depends on prompt types, surfacing further headroom that a per-prompt selector could exploit.

</details>


### [369] [Attacking the Trusted Imagination: Oracle-Level Integrity Attacks on Imagine-then-Act World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22966)
*Linghan Chen,Kaiyan Ji,Minyu Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现VLA策略中的世界-动作模型(WAM)的"想象"模块是暴露的攻击面。攻击者通过有界观测扰动轻易破坏潜在轨迹z~，使其偏离自然流形，导致下游安全门/MPC规划器/验证器失效。非目标攻击强度约为随机的60倍（AUC 1.0检测），而定向攻击受限。自适应攻击者只能放弃攻击来逃避检测。原生MPC在epsilon=0.01时成功率从0.70降至0.05（Fisher p<10^-4）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略采用"先想象后行动"的设计，但其鲁棒性研究中忽略了下游系统（安全门、MPC规划器等）依赖于WAM生成的未来想象轨迹z~这一关键事实。本文旨在揭示WAM的想象过程作为攻击面的脆弱性及其不对称性。

Method: 采用基于能力的威胁模型，L-infinity有界观测扰动。攻击者通过全可微的观测到想象的映射执行投影梯度下降。利用离流形特性设计无参数去噪检测器。评估三个VLA模型：RynnVLA-002、LingBot-VA和LaDi-WM。

Result: 非目标破坏强度约为随机的60倍，AUC 1.0完全检测。定向控制攻击受限。自适应攻击者只能放弃攻击来逃避检测。原生MPC在epsilon=0.01时成功率从0.70降至0.05（Fisher p<10^-4）。

Conclusion: WAM的想象模块是对抗攻击的关键脆弱点；恶意想象会导致依赖该想象的下游系统出现任务失败；反应式策略对受损想象保持鲁棒，但想象驱动的MPC则不然；离流形特性可用于有效检测非目标攻击。

Abstract: Many recent vision-language-action (VLA) policies adopt an imagine-then-act design. A world-action model (WAM) first imagines a short future as a latent trajectory z~, on which the action is then conditioned. We identify this trusted imagination, rather than the reactive policy, as the exposed attack surface. A downstream oracle, such as a safety gate, a visual model-predictive-control (MPC) planner, or an imagine-then-check verifier, consumes z~ as a prediction of the future. The robustness of the policy therefore does not entail the robustness of systems that rely on the WAM. The underlying phenomenon is an asymmetry. Corrupting the imagination is easy, since it requires only displacing z~ from its natural-future manifold. Steering it precisely is hard, since it must reach a specified on-manifold target. We adopt a capability-based threat model with an L-infinity-bounded observation perturbation. The attacker applies projected gradient descent through the fully differentiable observation-to-imagination map. The same off-manifold property motivates a parameter-free denoiser detector. We evaluate three targets: RynnVLA-002, LingBot-VA, and LaDi-WM. Untargeted corruption is roughly 60x stronger than random and is detected at AUC 1.0. Targeted control remains bounded. An adaptive attacker evades detection only by forgoing corruption. The reactive policy remains robust to corrupted imagination. A native imagination-driven MPC, however, exhibits the first adversary-specific task failure (at epsilon=0.01, success 0.70 versus 0.05; Fisher p < 10^-4).

</details>


### [370] [Topological Out-of-Domain Generalization in Dynamical Systems Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22969)
*Georg Trede,Charlotte Ricarda Doll,Elias Weber,Daniel Durstewitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了现有动态系统重建（DSR）模型在跨动态区域零样本预测中的局限性，提出特征分裂等改进方法，实现了对训练数据外的临界点等新动态区域的准确预测，并推导了可靠外推范围的闭式界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态系统行为预测是科学ML中的核心未解问题，现有层次化/超网络方法虽能同时训练多个DS并提取与关键控制参数相关的潜在特征，但在真正跨动态区域（如临界点）的预测能力有限，通常需要微调或重新训练。本文旨在解决这一局限性。

Method: 数学分析先前模型公式的缺陷，识别出重建模型的结构假设与物理系统典型属性之间的三个核心不匹配问题；提出特征分裂（feature splitting）等综合补救措施；推导出可靠外推范围的闭式界。

Result: 所提技术在零样本（zero-shot）场景下实现对训练动态区域外（如临界点）的新动态区域的准确预测。

Conclusion: 通过识别和修复结构假设与物理系统属性之间的不匹配，可以显著提升DSR模型的跨动态区域外推能力，为科学ML中真正的零样本泛化提供了理论依据和实践方案。

Abstract: Predicting the behavior of dynamical systems (DS) beyond the dynamical and parameter regimes observed in training is a pivotal and essentially unresolved problem in scientific ML. It is central to any good scientific theory, which we expect to be able to make predictions about regimes not covered by currently available data. Recent hierarchical and hyper-network guided approaches for DS reconstruction (DSR) enable training on many DS simultaneously, and revealed that extracted latent features are often related to crucial control parameters of the underlying DS that varied across the training corpus. However, true out-of-domain forecasting abilities of these models, e.g., across tipping points, remain limited, and fine-tuning, or even full model retraining, on time series from the new dynamical regime is usually required. Here, we mathematically analyze the root of these limitations in previous model formulations and identify three core shortcomings rooted in a mismatch between structural assumptions of the reconstruction model and typical properties of physical systems. We propose a combination of remedies for these shortcomings, most importantly feature splitting, and furthermore derive a closed-form bound on the reliable extrapolation range. We demonstrate empirically that our techniques allow for accurate zero-shot prediction into new dynamical regimes, outside the observed training regime, as, e.g., encountered across tipping points.

</details>


### [371] [TaLK: Text-attributed Graph Dataset Distillation via Coupling Language Model with Graph-Aware Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22975)
*Yeongho Kim,Yeonje Choi,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TaLK是一种针对文本属性图的高效数据集蒸馏方法，通过将语言模型与图感知神经正切核耦合，避免在完整数据集上重复训练LM-GNN模型，仅用1%合成数据即可达到97%的全数据集性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本属性图上的学习需要联合建模文本语义和图结构，标准做法是结合语言模型和图神经网络，但联合训练计算成本高且难以扩展。现有数据集蒸馏方法要么针对单模态设计，要么在蒸馏过程中仍需在完整数据集上重复训练昂贵的LM-GNN模型，不适合文本属性图。

Method: 提出TaLK方法，将语言模型与图感知神经正切核耦合，实现高效数据集蒸馏，避免在完整数据集上重复联合训练，同时保留文本和结构信息。

Result: 在多个文本属性图基准上，TaLK持续优于现有基线方法，仅用1%合成数据即可达到全数据集97%的性能。

Conclusion: TaLK通过耦合LM与图感知神经正切核，有效解决了文本属性图数据集蒸馏的效率和效果问题，是一种实用且可扩展的方法。

Abstract: Text-attributed graphs (TAGs) are widely used in many real-world domains, and learning on TAGs requires jointly modeling text semantics and graph structure. A standard approach for modeling TAGs is to combine a language model (LM) and a graph neural network (GNN), but joint training is computationally expensive and difficult to scale. Dataset distillation is a promising way to reduce training costs, but existing methods are not well suited to TAGs because they are typically designed for a single modality or still require repeatedly training expensive LM-GNN models on the full dataset during distillation. To address this, we propose TaLK, an effective dataset distillation method for TAGs that couples an LM with a graph-aware neural tangent kernel.This design enables efficient dataset distillation, avoiding repeated joint training on the full dataset while reflecting both textual and structural information for effective TAG learning.Experiments on multiple TAG benchmarks show that TaLK consistently outperforms existing baselines and achieves up to 97% of full-dataset performance with only 1% synthetic data.

</details>


### [372] [Understanding Parallel Samplers in Masked Diffusion via Random Walks on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22976)
*Vansh Bansal,Cho Cholyeon,Syamantak Kumar,Sujay Sanghavi,Purnamrita Sarkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用图上的随机游走作为可验证的沙盒环境，研究掩码扩散模型中的不同并行采样策略。通过理论证明和实验验证，发现最优采样策略依赖于图的结构，并开发了一种新的二分采样器，在对数步内实现精确采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的并行采样策略（如最低熵解掩码）在不同场景下性能不一，缺乏可控制、可定量评估的基准环境来系统比较这些策略。作者希望建立一个类似数独验证机制的可控框架来研究并行采样方法。

Method: 使用固定图上的随机游走构建可验证沙盒：图或转移核不显式暴露给模型。理论分析证明最低熵并行解掩码并非普遍优于随机采样器，其性能依赖于图结构。提出二分采样器，在序列长度对数步内完成采样，且在完美训练下可证明精确。在多种图游走任务和OpenWebText预训练MDM上进行实验。

Result: 理论证明并行采样策略的性能关键依赖于底层图结构，不存在统一最优策略。二分采样器在对数步内实现精确采样。实验表明不同图需要不同并行采样器。在语言生成任务中，二分风格采样器改善了速度-质量权衡。

Conclusion: 图随机游走可作为掩码扩散模型并行采样器诊断和设计的机械化基准，为理解和改进并行采样策略提供了可控的定量评估框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose using random walks on graphs as a verifiable sandbox to study different parallel sampling strategies in masked diffusion models (MDMs). We train an MDM on random walk samples from a fixed graph. The graph or the transition kernel is never shown to the model explicitly and plays the role of latent structure in the sequences, albeit one that is controllable and can be used for quantitative evaluation. Thus, this framework enjoys a Sudoku-like validity check: verifying that an output is a valid walk and estimating the Markov kernel from the walks to measure distribution fidelity.
  Using simple graphs, we theoretically prove that parallel unmasking via widely used scores like lowest entropy is not uniformly better than a random parallel sampler; the performance critically depends on the structure of the underlying graph. We develop a new bisection sampler for random walks, which takes logarithmic steps in the sequence length and is provably exact under perfect training. Experiments on various graph walk tasks show that different parallel samplers are better for different graphs even in practice. Our initial experiments on a pretrained OpenWebText MDM show that the bisection-style samplers improve speed-quality tradeoffs even for language generation. Together, these results position graph random walks as a mechanistic benchmark for diagnosing and designing parallel samplers for masked diffusion models.

</details>


### [373] [Neural Architecture Search of Sample Reweighting Networks for Complex Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22991)
*Keisuke Sugawara,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MW-Net在同时面临标签噪声和类别不平衡时性能退化的问题，本文引入神经架构搜索（NAS）来优化MW-Net的网络结构（隐藏层数、节点数）及其输入层选择，使用树结构Parzen估计器进行搜索，在修改后的CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MW-Net在单一分布偏移（标签噪声或类别不平衡）下表现良好，但在两者同时存在时，仅靠分类损失和简单网络难以确定合适的样本权重，导致性能退化。因此需要更复杂的网络结构和更优的输入特征来提升MW-Net在复合分布偏移下的表现。

Method: 提出使用神经架构搜索（NAS）优化MW-Net，具体采用树结构Parzen估计器（TPE）搜索最优的隐藏层数、节点数，并选择分类模型中最合适的中间层作为MW-Net的输入。

Result: 在同时包含标签噪声和类别不平衡的CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上，经过NAS优化的MW-Net相比原始MW-Net取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: 神经架构搜索能够有效提升MW-Net在复合分布偏移场景下的样本重加权性能，验证了通过搜索优化元网络结构和输入特征的有效性。

Abstract: Sample reweighting is a major approach to addressing distribution shifts, such as label noise and class imbalance. Meta-Weight-Net (MW-Net) is a promising sample reweighting network that computes weights based on classification loss. Although MW-Net improves prediction performance under a single type of distribution shift using a simple neural network, its performance degrades when facing both label noise and class imbalance, where it is hard to determine appropriate weights solely from classification loss and using a simple network. In this study, we introduce neural architecture search to MW-Net to mitigate such performance degradation. Using the tree-structured Parzen estimator, we explore the optimal number of hidden layers and nodes and select the most suitable intermediate layer in the classification model to serve as the input for MW-Net. Experimental results on the CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 datasets that were modified to include both label noise and class imbalance demonstrate the effectiveness of neural architecture search for MW-Net.

</details>


### [374] [Do Sparse Autoencoders Learn Meaningful Concept Hierarchies?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22994)
*Nils Grandien,David Steinmann,Felix Friedrich,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对稀疏自编码器（SAE）在无监督概念发现中产生的层次化特征空间缺乏统一评估标准的问题，基于语义网络和分类学研究推导出一组泛化/特化层次结构的关键要求，并据此制定具体的评估协议。将该协议应用于视觉数据的SAE方法后发现，虽然特征空间通常能为合理层次结构提供基础，但建立良好的层次结构仍具挑战性；特别是特征吸收（包括硬吸收和连续的软吸收形式）会系统性损害层次质量，揭示了未来方法需要解决的根本性矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SAE层次化特征空间缺乏统一的评估标准和方法，已有工作多基于定性展示和碎片化的定量指标，难以进行严格比较。

Method: 从语义网络和分类学研究以及近期SAE工作中推导泛化/特化层次结构的关键要求，并据此制定具体的评估协议，应用于视觉数据SAE方法进行实证分析。

Result: 特征空间虽能为合理层次结构提供基础，但建立良好层次结构仍具挑战；特征吸收（硬吸收和连续软吸收）系统性损害层次质量。

Conclusion: 层次化SAE面临特征吸收这一根本性矛盾，未来方法需要在该问题上进行突破。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become an important tool for unsupervised concept discovery in large models. To make the resulting feature spaces more interpretable and manageable, recent approaches have begun imposing hierarchical structure, either explicitly or as an implicit effect of training constraints, yet rigorous comparison remains difficult. There are no agreed-upon requirements for what a meaningful feature hierarchy should satisfy, and evaluation has largely relied on qualitative illustrations with fragmented quantitative protocols. To address this, we derive a set of key requirements for generalization/specialization hierarchies in unsupervised concept discovery, drawing on semantic net and taxonomy research alongside recent SAE work, and use them to derive a concrete evaluation protocol. Applying this protocol to current SAE approaches trained on visual data, we find that while feature spaces generally provide a basis for sensible hierarchies, establishing good hierarchical structure remains challenging. In particular, feature absorption, both in its well-known hard form and in a continuous, soft form, systematically compromises hierarchy quality, pointing to a fundamental tension that future approaches will need to navigate.

</details>


### [375] [Group-Graph Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22995)
*Yunan Wang,Minghui Song,Zihan Zhang,Shaohan Huang,Haizhen Huang,Furu Wei,Weiwei Deng,Feng Sun,Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出G2PO，一种基于图结构的组强化学习算法，通过将线性交互轨迹转化为全局状态转移图，采用组聚合状态值估计和边中心优势估计策略，解决长程智能体强化学习中的奖励稀疏、延迟和信用分配粗粒度问题，在WebShop等基准上相比GRPO提升最高22.2%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于组的强化学习在智能体场景中虽已从轨迹级转向步级训练以实现更细粒度的策略更新，但长程任务中仍存在严重的奖励稀疏与延迟问题。此外，现有步级框架的信用分配仍是粗粒度的，将智能体探索视为孤立的线性轨迹，忽略了状态转移的固有图结构，导致状态值估计高方差和局部的短视信用分配。

Method: 提出Group-Graph Policy Optimization (G2PO)，核心包括：(1) 将线性交互轨迹显式转化为全局状态转移图；(2) 跨不同轨迹聚合相同观测，引入组聚合状态值估计，降低采样方差和轨迹依赖偏差；(3) 将智能体动作重新定义为状态节点间的边，提出边中心优势估计策略，通过全局标准化整个图上的TD误差，显式识别并优先驱动绝对任务进展的关键转移。

Result: 在WebShop、ALFWorld和AppWorld三个长程基准上，G2PO显著优于现有最先进的基于提示和基于RL的基线方法，相比GRPO实现了最高22.2%的成功率提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入图结构视角和全局标准化机制，G2PO有效解决了长程智能体RL中的奖励稀疏、延迟和信用分配问题，为基于组的强化学习在复杂多轮智能体任务中的应用提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Group-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) has significantly enhanced Large Language Models (LLMs) in agentic scenarios. To achieve finer-grained policy updates, recent agentic RL frameworks have shifted from trajectory-level to step-level training. However, long-horizon agentic RL suffers from severe reward sparsity and delay, as feedback is often deferred for dozens of interaction steps. While existing step-level frameworks refine training granularity, their credit assignment remains coarse-grained and still treats agent exploration as isolated, linear trajectories. This oversimplified perspective ignores the inherent graph structure of state transitions, leading to high-variance state-value estimation and myopic, localized credit assignment. To overcome these critical bottlenecks, we propose Group-Graph Policy Optimization (G2PO), a novel group-based RL algorithm tailored for multi-turn agentic tasks. G2PO explicitly transforms linear interaction trajectories into a global state-transition graph. By aggregating identical observations across different trajectories, we introduce group-aggregation state-value estimation that reduces sampling variance and trajectory-dependent bias. Furthermore, we redefine agent actions as transitions between state nodes and propose an edge-centric advantage estimation strategy. By globally standardizing Temporal Difference (TD) errors across the entire graph, G2PO explicitly identifies and prioritizes critical transitions that drive absolute task progress. Extensive experiments on representative long-horizon benchmarks-WebShop, ALFWorld, and AppWorld-demonstrate that G2PO substantially outperforms state-of-the-art prompt-based and RL baselines, achieving remarkable success rate improvements of up to 22.2% over GRPO.

</details>


### [376] [A Novel Approach to Temporal QoS Estimation via Extended Kalman Filter-Incorporated Latent Feature Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23010)
*Ye Yuan,Song Wang,Hongxun Zhou,Ling Wang,Xin Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展卡尔曼滤波增强的潜在特征分析（EKL）模型，通过双向模型-数据驱动学习，实现对非平稳时序QoS数据的高效准确预测。该模型结合模型驱动的时序特征生成器（基于EKF）和数据驱动的时不变特征生成器（基于ALS），并采用密度导向并行策略提升计算效率，在真实数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流方法纯数据驱动的特性限制了其捕捉非平稳时序模式的能力，当时序QoS数据出现波动时预测精度下降。为克服这一局限，需要一种能同时利用模型驱动和数据驱动优势的新方法。

Method: 提出EKL模型，包含三部分：(a) 基于扩展卡尔曼滤波设计模型驱动的特征生成器，获取时序潜在特征以捕捉复杂时序模式；(b) 基于交替最小二乘算法构建数据驱动的特征生成器，识别描述用户-服务固有特征的时间不变潜在特征；(c) 采用密度导向并行策略，按服务调用密度排序用户以实现负载均衡，提升计算效率。同时提供严格理论分析证明模型收敛性。

Result: 在真实时序QoS数据集上的实验评估表明，EKL在计算效率和预测精度两方面均优于现有最先进模型。

Conclusion: EKL通过双向模型-数据驱动学习有效解决了纯数据驱动方法在非平稳时序QoS预测中的局限性，兼顾了预测准确性与计算效率。

Abstract: Predicting temporal Quality of Service (QoS) data is critical for optimizing network services and rationalizing resource allocation in cloud computing and service-oriented systems. Existing mainstream methods have achieved promising predictive performance. However, their purely data-driven manner limits their ability to capture non-stationary temporal patterns, thereby leading to accuracy degradation when temporal QoS data exhibits fluctuations. To tackle this limitation, we propose a novel Extended Kalman Filter-Enhanced Latent Feature Analysis (EKL) model to perform efficient and accurate temporal QoS prediction from the perspective of bidirectional model-data-driven learning. Its main idea is three-fold: a) designing a model-driven feature producer to obtain the temporal latent features to capture the intricate temporal pattern following the principle of an Extended Kalman Filter; b) building a data-driven feature producer based on the alternating least squares algorithm to identify time-invariant latent features describing intrinsic user-service characteristics; c) exploiting a density-oriented parallel strategy that achieves workload balancing by sorting users in accordance with their service invocation density, which effectively elevates computational efficiency. In addition, we provide a rigorous theoretical analysis to formally prove the convergence of the proposed EKL. Experimental evaluations conducted on real-world temporal QoS datasets reveal that our proposed EKL surpasses existing state-of-the-art models with respect to both computational efficiency and prediction accuracy for missing temporal QoS data.

</details>


### [377] [Counterfactual learning of new adaptive instructional policies using logged data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23015)
*Samuel Girard,Sein Minn,Amel Bouzeghoub,Jill-Jênn Vie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种离线上下文赌博机框架，利用Rasch模型将学生-题目交互映射到连续潜在能力-难度空间，通过优化"心流"奖励函数，仅从历史交互数据中学习新的自适应教学策略，在四个真实大规模数据集上实现了策略改进，计算时间仅需数秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能辅导系统（ITS）中优化教学策略通常需要昂贵的在线实验或可能无法捕捉真实动态的学生模拟器，因此需要一种仅从历史交互日志中直接学习新自适应策略的方法，以降低优化成本并提高可扩展性。

Method: 首先使用Rasch模型将学生-题目交互映射到连续潜在能力-难度空间，将教学过程建模为连续随机赌博机问题；然后设计一种优化"心流"（平衡任务挑战性与学生成功率）的新型奖励函数；最后提出回合特定行为策略估计方法，同时作为离线评估的倾向性模型和ITS自适应性的诊断工具。

Result: 在四个大规模真实数据集上，所提方法相比日志中的行为策略实现了一致的策略改进，且计算仅需数秒即可完成策略学习和可视化。

Conclusion: 有效教学策略可以在无需额外数据收集的情况下，以极低的计算成本从历史数据中学习和可视化，为改进自适应学习系统提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Optimizing instructional policies in Intelligent Tutoring Systems (ITS) typically requires costly online experimentation or student simulators that may fail to capture real-world dynamics. This paper introduces an offline contextual bandit framework that learns new adaptive policies directly from logged interaction data. By mapping student-item interactions onto a continuous latent proficiency-difficulty scale using a Rasch model, we cast the tutoring process as a continuous stochastic bandit problem. We propose a novel reward function designed to optimize ''flow'' by balancing task challenge with student success. Our approach includes a round-specific behavior policy estimation that serves as both a propensity model for off-policy evaluation and a diagnostic tool for ITS adaptivity. We demonstrate the efficacy of this framework across four large-scale real-world datasets, achieving consistent policy improvements over the logged behavior policy. The results show that effective instructional policies can be learned and visualized within seconds of computation, providing a scalable path for improving adaptive learning systems without further data collection.

</details>


### [378] [EvoRubrics: Dynamic Rubrics as Rewards via Adversarial Co-Evolution for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23038)
*Hongxin Ding,Baixiang Huang,Yue Fang,Weibin Liao,Zheng Li,Jinyang Zhang,Zhijing Wu,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EvoRubrics，一种策略LLM与评分生成器在RL训练中协同进化的框架，解决静态评分标准与持续进化策略之间的失配问题，在多项基准上超越静态和动态评分基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于评分的奖励在强化学习中提供可解释的细粒度优化信号，但预构建的评分标准在训练过程中保持静态，与持续进化的策略之间存在根本性不匹配：固定标准逐渐丧失区分能力，导致奖励饱和和潜在的奖励破解问题。现有动态评分方法依赖外部前沿模型或真实答案，且更新粒度粗糙。

Method: 提出EvoRubrics共进化框架，使Policy LLM和Rubric Generator在每个训练步骤内通过对抗性交互共同提升。策略在评分生成器引导下改进的同时，评分生成器自适应更新其标准以保持区分性和信息性，使评估能够实时追踪策略，自然诱导自动课程学习。

Result: 在多项基准上持续优于静态和动态评分基线，学习到的评分生成器可泛化为可迁移的奖励模型。完全自监督变体（无外部监督）也能实现有意义的性能提升。

Conclusion: 生成与评估之间的协同进化本身就足以提供丰富的学习信号，为开放任务中的强化学习提供了更高效、可解释且可迁移的奖励设计范式。

Abstract: Rubric-based rewards offer interpretable and fine-grained optimization signals for reinforcement learning in open-ended tasks where verifiable answers are unavailable. However, pre-constructed rubrics remain static throughout training, creating a fundamental mismatch with the evolving policy: fixed criteria gradually lose discriminative power as the model improves, leading to reward saturation and potential hacking. Recent dynamic rubric methods partially address this but rely on external frontier models or ground-truth answers, and update rubrics only at coarse granularity. We propose EvoRubrics, a co-evolutionary RL framework where a Policy LLM and a Rubric Generator jointly improve through adversarial interaction within each training step. As the policy improves under the rubric generator's guidance, the rubric generator adapts its criteria to remain discriminative and informative, enabling evaluation to track the policy in real time and naturally inducing an automatic curriculum. Experiments show that EvoRubrics consistently outperforms static and dynamic rubric baselines across benchmarks. The learned Rubric Generator further generalizes as a transferable reward model. Notably, even a fully self-supervised variant without any external supervision achieves meaningful gains, suggesting that co-evolution between generation and evaluation alone can provide sufficiently rich learning signals. Our code is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/EvoRubrics-2155/.

</details>


### [379] [Prime Fourier Embeddings: A Principled Basis for Modular Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23044)
*Hyunsang Hwang,Suhyun Bae,Donghun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出素数傅里叶嵌入（PFE），将整数编码为素数索引的正余弦对，使模运算简化为选择相关素数通道而非从头学习代数结构。理论证明线性映射的块对角化性质，实验验证了通道特异性比率超过500倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经嵌入难以揭示数字的代数结构（如模运算），需要一种预结构化的表示方法来编码整数间的代数关系。

Method: 基于Q上的调和分析，将整数编码为素数索引的(cos, sin)对；利用舒尔引理证明任何与乘积群作用等变的线性映射必为块对角化，每块对应一个素数；中国剩余定理预测复合模数下哪些素数通道与任务相关。

Result: 消融实验显示任务相关通道与无关通道间的特异性比率超过500倍；在所有测试的平方自由复合模数上实现完美分布内测试准确率。

Conclusion: PFE为神经网络的代数推理提供了预结构化先验，理论上保证了表示的可分解性，实证验证了该方法的有效性和高效性。

Abstract: Numbers have algebraic structure that standard neural embeddings often fail to expose. We introduce Prime Fourier Embeddings (PFE), which encode integers as prime-indexed (cos, sin) pairs derived from the harmonic analysis of Q, providing a pre-structured representation in which modular arithmetic reduces to selecting the relevant prime channel rather than discovering algebraic structure from scratch. We prove that any linear map equivariant with respect to the product group action on PFE must be block-diagonal with one independent block per prime -- a consequence of Schur's lemma applied to the resulting character decomposition. For square-free composite moduli, the Chinese Remainder Theorem predicts which prime channels are task-relevant. Both predictions are confirmed empirically: ablation studies show specialization ratios exceeding 500x between task-relevant and task-irrelevant channels, with perfect in-distribution test accuracy across all square-free composite moduli tested.

</details>


### [380] [PeLAP-A: Adaptive Latent Pruning for Lightweight Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23086)
*Kissa Zahra,Zaib Un Nisa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PeLAP-A框架，通过可学习的通道重要性预测器对潜在扩散模型中的潜在通道进行自适应剪枝。实验发现，即使在极端稀疏正则化下，所有潜在通道被抑制到接近零，去噪UNet仍能获得比未剪枝基线更低的扩散损失和重建MSE，作者将其称为"稀疏崩溃"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究潜在扩散模型中各潜在通道是否冗余，以及是否所有通道对扩散过程同等重要。

Method: 轻量级框架PeLAP-A：在标准潜在扩散流程中增加可学习的通道重要性预测器（两层MLP，输入为全局池化的潜在特征），输出软掩码抑制不重要的潜在通道，然后送入去噪UNet。联合优化扩散损失、重建损失和稀疏损失。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，强稀疏正则化（λ=0.01）下，重要性预测器将所有潜在通道驱动至接近零，但去噪UNet的扩散损失（0.0236 vs 0.0240）和VAE重建MSE（22.59 vs 24.67）均优于未剪枝基线。

Conclusion: 发现了"稀疏崩溃"现象并给出分析，表明去噪UNet对潜在通道的抑制具有显著鲁棒性，揭示了潜在扩散模型的信息需求特性。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models achieve strong generative performance by operating in a compressed latent space produced by a variational autoencoder (VAE). However, it remains unclear whether all latent channels contribute equally to the diffusion process, or whether significant redundancy exists. We introduce PeLAP-A (Adaptive Latent Pruning for Diffusion), a lightweight framework that augments a standard latent diffusion pipeline with a learnable channel-wise importance predictor. A two-layer MLP operating on globally pooled latent features produces a soft mask that suppresses unimportant latent channels before they enter the denoising UNet. The entire system is trained jointly on CIFAR-10 under a combined diffusion, reconstruction, and sparsity loss. Experiments reveal a striking result: under aggressive sparsity regularization (lambda = 0.01), the importance predictor drives all latent channels to near-zero yet the denoising UNet achieves lower diffusion loss (0.0236 vs. 0.0240) and lower VAE reconstruction MSE (22.59 vs. 24.67) compared to the unpruned baseline. We term this the sparsity collapse phenomenon and provide an analysis of why it occurs and what it reveals about the information requirements of latent diffusion models. These findings constitute an exploratory study of sparsity dynamics in latent diffusion training, and demonstrate that denoising UNets can remain remarkably robust to latent channel suppression even under aggressive regularization. Code is available at: https://github.com/kissasium/PeLAP-A.git.

</details>


### [381] [FlowTrain: Flow-Based Decoupled Training for Industrial-Grade Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23087)
*Zhida Jiang,Zhaolong Xing,Yang Pei,Xiaolong Chen,Yuanhang Xiao,Chengzhi Huang,Xiyu Liu,Haopeng Liu,Qingyuan Sang,Lingfeng Zhou,Jiaxing Wang,Zicheng Zhang,Wenzhe Wang,Xinyu Liu,Yan Li,Zhen Chen,Ke Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowTrain是一个基于数据流解耦的VLM训练框架，通过生产者-消费者模型和统一内存池实现编码器与骨干网络的独立执行，采用异构并行分配器和动态打包调度器，在真实负载上达到50%+ MFU和1.7x吞吐提升，缩小了与纯LLM训练的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级VLM分布式训练效率远低于单模态LLM训练，现有方案（单体设计或分离部署的批同步流水线）仍不够高效，需要进一步解耦VLM训练流程。

Method: 提出FlowTrain流式解耦训练框架：1) 将VLM训练重构为生产者-消费者数据流，通过统一内存池协调；2) 编码器和骨干网络在全局虚拟地址空间上独立推进；3) 异构并行分配器通过求解吞吐匹配问题分配模块特定并行策略；4) 动态打包调度器根据LLM端实际计算成本在运行时构建平衡微批次。

Result: 在真实工作负载上实现超过50%的模型算力利用率（MFU），吞吐量提升最高达1.7倍，缩小了与纯LLM训练的效率差距。

Conclusion: FlowTrain通过流式解耦和模块级异构并行策略有效提升了VLM分布式训练效率，证明了VLM训练可以进一步解耦以提高效率。

Abstract: Industrial-grade distributed training of vision-language models (VLMs) remains far less efficient than that of unimodal LLMs. Existing solutions either follow a monolithic design that assigns uniform parallelism to heterogeneous modules or adopt a disaggregated deployment that separates modules while executing them as a batch-synchronized pipeline. In this paper, we highlight that the above solutions are still not sufficient, and VLM training can be further decoupled. To this end, we present FlowTrain, a flow-based decoupled training framework that reformulates VLM training as a producer-consumer dataflow coordinated through a unified memory pool. The encoder and backbone can progress independently over a global virtual address space. Since this execution decoupling fundamentally changes the optimization objective of allocation and scheduling, FlowTrain further introduces a heterogeneous parallel allocator that assigns module-specific parallelism strategies by solving a throughput matching problem. The dynamic packing scheduler is used to construct balanced microbatches at runtime according to the actual LLM-side computation cost. Extensive experiments on real-world workloads show that FlowTrain achieves over 50% MFU and up to 1.7x throughput improvement, narrowing the efficiency gap to LLM-only training.

</details>


### [382] [FLFL: Federated Latent Factor Learning for Private Recovery of Spatio-Temporal Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23091)
*Chengjun Yu,Di Wu,Yi He,Jia Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联邦潜在因子学习（FLFL）模型，用于无线传感器网络中时空信号的隐私保护恢复。每个传感器仅上传梯度信息而非原始数据，并结合时空相关性正则化约束，在保护数据隐私的同时实现高精度缺失数据恢复。在四个真实数据集上的实验表明，FLFL在恢复精度上显著优于八种前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在因子学习（LFL）模型要求将传感器采集的感知信号集中存储在中央服务器进行处理，这在数据所有者隐私意识日益增强的背景下变得不可接受，亟需一种既能保护原始数据隐私又能实现高精度信号恢复的方法。

Method: 提出FLFL模型，核心包括两点：1）基于LFL设计传感器级联邦学习框架，各传感器仅上传梯度信息而非原始数据，训练隐私保护的恢复模型；2）将时空相关性作为正则化约束融入联邦学习框架，提升恢复精度。

Result: 在四个真实WSN数据集上的大量实验表明，FLFL在恢复精度上显著优于八种最先进的联邦和非联邦信号恢复模型，同时实现了隐私保护。

Conclusion: FLFL模型能够同时实现WSN缺失数据的高精度恢复和原始数据所有者的隐私保护，为隐私敏感场景下的传感器数据恢复提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Wireless sensor network (WSNs) stands out as a burgeoning and promising domain in intelligent sensing. Owing to various factors such as sudden sensor malfunctions or deliberate shutdown of partial nodes to save energy, the collected sensing signals from WSNs commonly have massive missing data, leading to adverse effects on subsequent analysis or decision-making. Latent factor learning (LFL) has proven to be highly effective in recovering the missing data for WSNs. However, the existing LFL models require the collected sensing signals to be maintained in one central place like a central server, which is becoming unacceptable for data owners who are getting increasingly privacy-sensitive. To address this issue, this paper innovatively proposes a federated latent factor learning (FLFL) model for privacy-preserving spatio-temporal signal recovery. Its main idea is two-fold: 1) it designs a sensor-level federated learning framework based on LFL, where each sensor only needs to upload gradient information rather than raw data for training a privacy-preserving recovery model, and 2) it incorporates the spatio-temporal correlation into the designed federated learning framework as the regularization constraint to improve its recovery accuracy. With such designs, FLFL can not only accurately recover the missing data of WSNs but also ensure data owners' privacy-preserving of raw data. To evaluate the proposed FLFL model, extensive experiments have been conducted on four real-world WSN datasets. The results demonstrate that FLFL significantly outperforms eight state-of-the-art federated and non-federated signal recovery models in terms of recovery accuracy with privacy-preserving.

</details>


### [383] [Minimax Quantile Lower Bounds for Interactive Statistical Decision Making with Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23096)
*Raghav Bongole,Amirreza Zamani,Tobias J. Oechtering,Mikael Skoglund*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对交互式统计决策制定的δ-显式极小极大分位数理论，以捕捉罕见但严重的失败事件。通过建立极小极大分位数与风险之间的结构关系，并开发了高概率交互式Fano方法和Le Cam方法，为带隐私约束的决策问题提供了显式下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的极小极大风险和遗憾基于期望准则，无法捕捉罕见但后果严重的失败事件。为了在交互式统计决策中应对这种极端风险，需要建立基于分位数的理论框架。

Method: 1) 建立了极小极大分位数、下极小极大分位数与极小极大风险之间的结构关系，包括分位数到期望的转换以及严格与下极小极大分位数在可数置信水平集外的等价性。2) 开发了两种高概率逆工具：交互式Fano方法和交互式Le Cam方法。3) 通过限制可接受决策类，将互信息隐私纳入同一框架。4) 针对坐标高斯私有化，提出隔离隐私诱导方差膨胀的两点模板，并应用于高斯均值估计和两臂高斯Bandit。5) 推导了K臂高斯Bandit问题的极小极大分位数下界。

Result: 得到了在置信水平δ下显式的下界：平方误差高斯均值估计的log(1/δ)/n缩放；两臂有界均值高斯Bandit的√(T log(1/δ))缩放；K臂Bandit的√(KT log(1/δ))型缩放。隐私预算通过高斯方差膨胀因子体现。

Conclusion: 提出的极小极大分位数理论能够系统性地刻画交互式统计决策中的高概率失败风险，并通过显式下界展示了隐私约束对决策精度的影响，为隐私保护下的统计决策提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Minimax risk and regret are expectation-based criteria and do not capture rare but consequential failures. To address this concern, we develop a $δ$-explicit minimax-quantile theory for interactive statistical decision making (ISDM). We first provide structural relations between minimax quantiles, lower minimax quantiles, and minimax risk. This includes a quantile-to-expectation conversion and an equivalence between strict and lower minimax quantiles outside a countable set of confidence levels. We then derive two converse tools for ISDM: a high-probability interactive Fano's method and a high-probability interactive Le Cam's method. Then, we show that mutual-information (MI) privacy can be handled in the same framework by restricting the admissible decision class. For coordinatewise Gaussian privatization, we derive a two-point template that isolates the privacy-induced variance inflation. We instantiate this template for Gaussian mean estimation, and use the same two-point strategy directly for two-armed Gaussian bandits. We then derive a minimax quantile lower bound for the $K$-armed Gaussian bandit problem, showing that the interactive Fano method captures the exploration cost over multiple possible best arms. The resulting lower bounds are explicit in the confidence level $δ$ and in the privacy budget for the private problems. They yield $\log(1/δ)/n$ scaling for squared-error Gaussian mean estimation, $\sqrt{T\log(1/δ)}$ scaling for two-armed bounded-mean Gaussian bandits, and $\sqrt{KT\log(1/δ)}$-type scaling for the $K$-armed bandits, with privacy appearing through a Gaussian variance-inflation factor for the private problems.

</details>


### [384] [ReNIO: Reweighting Negative Trajectory Importance for LLM On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23104)
*Chen Lin,Kedi Chen,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReNIO方法，通过重加权负轨迹重要性来改进大语言模型的同策略蒸馏（OPD），无需依赖最终答案正确性即可识别并强化错误推理轨迹中的有用信号，在数学推理和代码生成任务上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准同策略蒸馏（OPD）平等对待所有学生生成输出，但实验发现训练仅使用错误输出比仅使用正确输出效果更好，表明错误输出中包含未被充分利用的探索性推理信号。需要一种无需最终答案标签即可利用这一信号的方法。

Method: 提出ReNIO方法，利用学生到教师的概率比（student-to-teacher probability ratio）识别导致错误推理轨迹的关键token，聚合其信息为归一化的样本权重，从而在不观察最终答案正确性的前提下，自动对可能的负轨迹赋予更大权重。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务上，ReNIO同时改进了OPD和OPSD，代表性相对提升：Qwen3-1.7B提升8.90%，R1-Distill-Qwen-7B提升10.00%。

Conclusion: 错误推理轨迹中包含有利于模型探索能力边界的有用信号，通过token级概率比对负轨迹进行重加权可以有效提升蒸馏性能，且该方法保留了OPD的前缀训练优势。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves LLM reasoning by training a student model on its own generated outputs, but standard OPD treats all student-generated outputs (SGOs) equally regardless of their informativeness. We observe a consistent asymmetry in controlled filtering experiments: in both OPD and on-policy self distillation (OPSD), training only on incorrect SGOs outperforms training only on correct ones. Our further analysis suggests that models trained on correct-only SGOs tend to generate shorter reasoning traces and show weaker reflection behavior, while incorrect SGOs better preserve exploratory reasoning near the model's capability boundary. To exploit this signal without requiring full answer-containing rollouts, we introduce ReNIO, which Reweights Negative trajectory Importance for LLM On-policy distillation. By using the student-to-teacher probability ratio, ReNIO identifies pivotal tokens leading to wrong reasoning traces and aggregates their information into a normalized sample weight, inherently assigning larger weights to likely negative trajectories without observing the correctness of final-answer. Since Re-NIO only uses prefix-conditioned token probabilities, it preserves OPD's prefix training advantage over full-rollout reinforcement learning. Across both mathematical reasoning and code generation tasks, ReNIO improves both OPD and OPSD, with representative relative gains of up to 8.90% for Qwen3-1.7B and 10.00% for R1-Distill-Qwen-7B on mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Code repo: https://github.com/BDML-lab/ReNIO.

</details>


### [385] [Self-Evolution for Multi-Turn Tool-Calling Agents via Divergence-Point Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23112)
*Jiaqiang Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ToolGraph通过结合模式拓扑、过渡权重和历史感知控制来结构化工具选择，并利用DPO在相同上下文中进行偏好优化。在375个tau2-bench任务中，加权平均奖励从0.304提升至0.338（ToolGraph），ToolGraph+DPO达到0.355（+16.8%），增益集中在航空和零售领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将推理时编排与参数级学习分离，导致工具选择缺乏结构化，且偏好更新易受训练-部署提示不匹配影响。

Method: 1) 构建ToolGraph：基于模式的拓扑结构、成功轨迹估计的过渡权重、历史感知控制（写前置条件、重复搜索循环）；2) 构造161个偏好对：通过状态匹配和前缀对齐定位分歧点，经动作正确性注释过滤；3) 在ToolGraph上下文中进行DPO训练。

Result: 在375个tau2-bench任务中，ToolGraph将加权平均奖励从0.304提升至0.338（+11.2%），ToolGraph+DPO达到0.355（+16.8%），DPO增益集中在航空和零售领域。

Conclusion: ToolGraph有效结构化工具选择，DPO在相同上下文中带来额外收益，但约半数电信轨迹在动作执行前耗尽步骤预算，且选择奖励正值是最有用的检查点信号。

Abstract: Multi-turn tool-using agents must coordinate long-horizon tool sequences while tracking dialogue state and policy constraints. Existing approaches often separate inference-time orchestration from parameter-level learning, leaving tool selection weakly structured and preference updates vulnerable to train--deployment prompt mismatch. For within-benchmark self-improvement, ToolGraph combines schema-derived topology, transition weights estimated from successful rollouts, and history-aware controls for write prerequisites and repeated-search loops. We then construct 161 preference pairs by locating divergence points via state-based matching and prefix-based alignment, filtered through action-correctness annotations, and train DPO under the same ToolGraph context used at inference. Across 375 tau2-bench tasks, ToolGraph raises the weighted average reward from 0.304 to 0.338 (+11.2% relative), while ToolGraph+DPO reaches 0.355 (+16.8% over the baseline), with the DPO gain concentrated in airline and retail. Fine-grained diagnostics further show that roughly half of telecom trajectories exhaust the step budget before action execution and that chosen reward positivity is the most useful checkpoint signal across our 16 evaluated DPO configurations.

</details>


### [386] [Temporal-Spectral Alignment with Frequency Adaptation for Source-Free Time-Series Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23120)
*Shichang Meng,Linquan Wu,Xuan Ai,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAFA方法，通过时频对齐和频率自适应模块解决源自由时间序列域适应中的频谱偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有源自由域适应方法主要关注时间动态特性，但忽略了时间序列数据中固有的频谱偏移问题。

Method: 1) 多尺度联合建模源域的时序依赖和频谱特征；2) 引入可训练的频域自适应模块，通过调制目标信号在频域的相位和幅度来对齐源分布。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上验证了SAFA的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SAFA通过结合时频对齐与频率自适应，有效解决了源自由时间序列域适应中的特征偏移和时序漂移问题。

Abstract: The goal of source-free domain adaptation (SFDA) for time-series data is to transfer knowledge from a pre-trained source model to an unlabeled target domain without requiring access to source data, while addressing feature shift and temporal drift inherent in the signals. Although existing approaches have explored temporal dynamics in unsupervised source-free adaptation, they largely overlook spectral shifts in time-series data. Towards this end, we propose a novel approach termed temporal-Spectral Alignment with Frequency Adaptation (SAFA) for source-free time-series domain adaptation. Specifically, we first model the source domain at multiple scales by jointly capturing temporal dependencies and spectral characteristics. To adapt time-series data in the target domain, we introduce a trainable frequency adaptation module that modulates the phase and amplitude of target signals in the frequency domain to align them with the source distribution. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate the efficacy and robustness of SAFA.

</details>


### [387] [The Fractal Neural Operator: Overcoming Spectral Bias in Chaotic Attractors via Prime-Harmonic Weierstrass Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23123)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fractal Neural Operator (FNO)，利用非共振素数基（Harmonic Weierstrass Encoder）替代传统几何编码，将Lorenz-63系统的有效预测范围提升至347 Lyapunov时间，超越现有基线2.3倍，证明混沌系统的“不可预测性”源于网络嵌入流形的选择而非混沌本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型（Transformer、Neural Operator）存在谱偏置，即低通滤波特性，在流体动力学中可接受，但对混沌动力系统（其奇异吸引子具有分形几何和无穷谱密度）是灾难性的。现有方法无法捕捉高频信息，导致对混沌系统的预测能力严重不足。

Method: 提出Fractal Neural Operator (FNO)，核心创新包括：(1) 使用非共振素数基（non-resonant prime number basis）替代传统$2^k$几何编码；(2) Harmonic Weierstrass Encoder将无穷谱分辨率注入潜空间，避免谱间隙和共振问题；(3) 架构支持非可微的分形嵌入流形。

Result: 在Lorenz-63系统上，FNO将有效预测时域扩展至347 Lyapunov时间，超过SOTA Reservoir Computing基线2.3倍。

Conclusion: 混沌系统并非本质上对神经网络不可预测，其预测瓶颈在于嵌入流形的选择——需要非可微分的分形嵌入流形而非传统平滑嵌入。

Abstract: Deep learning models, particularly Transformers and Neural Operators, exhibit a well-documented "spectral bias," effectively acting as low-pass filters that smooth out high-frequency information. While benign in fluid dynamics, this bias is catastrophic for Chaotic Dynamical Systems, where the underlying strange attractor is characterized by fractal geometry and infinite spectral density. We introduce the Fractal Neural Operator (FNO), a novel architecture that utilizes a non-resonant prime number basis to approximate continuous dynamical systems. Unlike geometric encodings ($2^k$), which suffer from spectral gaps and resonance, our Harmonic Weierstrass Encoder injects infinite spectral resolution into the latent space. We demonstrate that FNO extends the valid prediction horizon of the Lorenz-63 system to 347 Lyapunov times, exceeding state-of-the-art Reservoir Computing baselines by a factor of 2.3x.
