Our main result is a high-probability exact-recovery theorem for the binary Contextual Stochastic Block Model after \(k=O(\log n)\) propagation steps in the dense polylogarithmic regime \(p\ge C\log^B n/n\), for any fixed \(B>4\), under explicit graph-signal and feature-SNR conditions. We also establish a multi-class partial recovery theorem showing contraction toward class centers for most nodes. Synthetic and real node-classification experiments are included as empirical checks of the theory's predicted dependence on depth, graph signal, and feature noise.

</details>


### [426] [Solve for the Hyperparameter, Skip the Search: Kolmogorov-Optimal Scaling Laws for Spline Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23575)
*Yong Yi Bay,Kathleen A. Yearick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KORE方法，用闭式解替代网格搜索进行最优超参数选择，通过经典逼近理论、ANOVA分解和PRESS恒等式，在样条回归中实现高效优化，计算量仅为传统方法的1/8，在36个真实数据集上综合排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超参数调优通常依赖网格搜索和交叉验证，计算成本高昂。作者希望证明对于样条回归，这种搜索是不必要的，可以通过解析方法直接求解最优分辨率。

Method: 利用经典逼近理论将平方偏置确定为分辨率G的幂函数（精确到Kolmogorov n-width）；将基维度表示为G的显式多项式；利用PRESS恒等式从单次拟合获得留一法误差。通过ANOVA分解将输入维度替换为交互阶数，得到缩放律。KORE算法仅需拟合两个试点分辨率，求解2×2线性系统得到偏置和噪声尺度，然后计算闭式最优分辨率，并附带留一法验证。

Result: 在高达80维的加性及稀疏成对目标上，KORE与穷举3折交叉验证及经典准则（GCV、Cp、AIC、BIC）匹配，模型拟合次数减少约8倍；在36个真实表格数据集上，KORE在单位计算量的精度排名中位列21种方法之首。

Conclusion: 当复杂度存在于低交互阶数时，求解分辨率优于搜索分辨率。

Abstract: Hyperparameter tuning almost always means search: fit the model at every value on a grid, score each by cross-validation, and keep the winner. For spline regression that search is unnecessary. The optimal resolution can be solved for in closed form, to the accuracy an exhaustive search reaches, at a fraction of the compute. Three ingredients make this possible: classical approximation theory pins the squared bias to a known power of the resolution G, exactly the Kolmogorov n-width of the smoothness class; the basis dimension is an explicit polynomial in G; and leave-one-out error follows from a single fit via the PRESS identity. Balancing the two known curves gives the minimizer analytically. We extend this calculus to many coordinates by replacing ambient input dimension with interaction order, the number of active low-order components in an ANOVA decomposition, yielding a scaling law in which the optimal resolution and error are power functions of the effective density (sample size per active component), with input dimension absent from the exponent. The law becomes an algorithm. KORE (Kolmogorov-optimal Order-aware Resolution Estimation) fits two pilot resolutions, solves a leverage-calibrated 2x2 system for the bias and noise scales, and evaluates the closed-form plug-in resolution with a tiny leave-one-out certificate: about a dozen fits instead of a full grid sweep, with a consistency guarantee as the sample grows. Across additive and sparse pairwise targets up to 80 input dimensions, KORE matches exhaustive 3-fold cross-validation and the full classical ladder (GCV, Mallows' Cp, AIC, BIC) while fitting roughly 8x fewer models; on 36 real tabular datasets it ranks first among 21 methods in accuracy per unit of compute, ahead of tuned boosters and kernel machines. When complexity lives in low interaction order, solving for the resolution beats searching for it.

</details>


### [427] [It's Much Easier for Neural Networks to learn Game of Life Dynamics with the Right Activation Function: Polynomial Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23587)
*Tashin Ahmed,Q. Tyrell Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经网络学习康威生命游戏时，通过使用合适的激活函数（特别是二次多项式激活函数），极小网络也能可靠学习该任务，挑战了默认使用大规模网络的做法，并倡导将元胞自动机作为机器学习研究的测试领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究发现，能可靠学习康威生命游戏的神经网络规模与能硬编码表示该元胞自动机的最小网络之间存在巨大差距。从搜索视角看，这依赖于足够大的网络包含幸运初始化的子网络（"中奖彩票"）。本文希望重新将问题定位为学习问题，通过引入合适的归纳偏置，使极小网络也能有效学习。

Method: 探索多种替代激活函数（如2次多项式激活函数），在极小网络架构下测试学习康威生命游戏动态的能力，并与默认的ReLU激活函数进行对比。

Result: 多种替代激活函数显著优于ReLU，其中2次多项式激活函数在有无学习神经权重的情况下均能稳定学习生命游戏动态。

Conclusion: 匹配任务的学习策略（合适的归纳偏置）能极大提升极小网络的学习能力，挑战了"默认使用大规模网络"的简易选择。倡导将元胞自动机作为科学机器学习、物理深度学习及可解释机器学习发展的简单测试领域。

Abstract: Previous work has found a gap between the scale of neural networks that reliably learn Conway's Game of Life, and minimal networks capable of representing the classic cellular automaton with hard-coded parameter values. Viewing neural network learning as a search process suggests a dependence on networks large enough to contain sub-networks with lucky initializations (sometimes known as 'winning tickets') that actually learn the task. In this work, we reorient our perspective from discovering Life rules as a search problem back to a learning problem, and reason that with fitting inductive biases, the problem should be much more amenable to minimal networks. We find that network variants with several alternative activation functions meaningfully outperform the default choice of Rectified Linear Units, and in particular, that a 2nd degree polynomial activation function consistently learns Life dynamics with or without the benefit of learning neural weights. Our results provide an informative demonstration of the benefits of matching learning to the task at hand and challenge the easy default choice of scale for all problems. In particular, we advocate for the use of cellular automata as simple test domains for developing strategies that can benefit machine learning for science, physics-based deep learning, and interpretable machine learning.

</details>


### [428] [Quantifying the Agreement Between Data-Influence and Data-Similarity to Understand LLM Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23591)
*Christopher J. Anders,Henrique Da Silva Gameiro,Nico Daheim,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了两种用于追溯LLM输出到训练数据的方法——数据相似度（data-similarity）和数据影响（data-influence），发现两者排序高度一致但存在不对称性：数据相似度排序靠前的文档在数据影响下排名更稳定。基于此不对称性，提出用高成本的数据影响来精炼低成本的数据相似度结果，从而在成本与准确性之间取得更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数据相似度和数据影响是两种常用的输出追溯方法，且已有研究在真实任务上对两者进行比较，但在输出追溯这一具体场景下缺乏系统性的定量对比。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对每个训练文档分别按数据相似度和数据影响两种度量排序，然后计算两个排序之间的重叠度（rank overlap），并通过在多种模型（OLMo2-1B, Qwen3-1.7B, LlaMa3.2-1B, Gemma3-1B, GPT2）上进行实验来验证结论。

Result: 两种排序显著一致，但存在不对称性：数据相似度排序的顶部文档在数据影响度量下排名更一致（即更稳定），反之则不成立。

Conclusion: 利用这种不对称性，可以用低成本的数据相似度进行初步筛选，再用高成本的数据影响对结果进行精炼，从而在成本与准确性之间取得更好的平衡。

Abstract: One way to understand LLM behavior is to trace its output back to the training data. Two types of measures are commonly used for output tracing: data-similarity and data-influence. The former is cheaper while the latter is believed to be more accurate. Even though many works have compared them for ground-truth tasks, no such comparisons exist for output tracing. Here, we fill this gap and precisely quantify the commonalities and differences between the two measures. We do this by first ranking the training documents according to each measure and then computing the overlap between the two rankings. Our main finding is that the two rankings agree significantly, but there is an asymmetry between them: The top documents of data-similarity are assigned more consistent ranks by data-influence than the other way around. This result is valid across a range of experiments involving OLMo2-1B, Qwen3-1.7B, LlaMa3.2-1B, Gemma3-1B, and GPT2. We exploit the asymmetry to obtain a favorable cost-accuracy trade-off by using the costly data-influence to refine the results of data-similarity.

</details>


### [429] [MORL-A2C: Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning Reranker for Optimizing Healthiness in MOPI-HFRS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23603)
*Aarya Vasantlal,Joshua Zolla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MORL-A2C，一种基于优势Actor-Critic的序列决策扩展框架，用于多目标个性化食品推荐，在轻微牺牲排序质量（Recall@20从25.64%降至23.61%）的前提下，大幅提升健康对齐度（H-Score@20从46.05%提升至69.57%），并修正了MOPI-HFRS基线评估中的bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食品推荐系统仅优化用户偏好，忽视营养健康；而MOPI-HFRS虽采用Pareto优化联合优化偏好、健康与多样性，但其静态逐步权衡无法捕捉饮食决策的序列性，亟需一种能动态建模序列依赖的推荐方法。

Method: 提出MORL-A2C框架，在MOPI-HFRS基础上沿健康-偏好轴扩展；(1) 利用冻结的GNN嵌入；(2) 将推荐建模为K步重排序问题；(3) 采用优势Actor-Critic算法，结合标量化相关/健康奖励；(4) 通过行为克隆（模仿冻结嵌入的点积排序器）对策略进行热启动；(5) 识别并修正了MOPI-HFRS评估管线中低估基线性能的bug。

Result: 在宏营养素基准上：Recall@20从25.64%降至23.61%（下降约2%），NDCG@20从23.52%降至20.64%（下降约2.9%），而H-Score@20从46.05%提升至69.57%（提升约23.5%）；全营养素基准上趋势一致。

Conclusion: 基于策略驱动的序列优化能够有效导航多目标食品推荐中健康-偏好之间的权衡，在可接受的排序质量损失下显著提升健康对齐度，验证了序列决策方法在多目标推荐中的有效性。

Abstract: Unhealthy dietary behavior continues to be a persistent public health issue in the United States, exacerbated by recommendation systems that prioritize user preference without considering nutritional health. The Multi-Objective Personalized Interpretable Health-aware Food Recommendation System (MOPI-HFRS), from which this work extends, addresses this by jointly optimizing preference, health, and diversity through Pareto-based optimization. However, this approach relies on static, per-step tradeoff solutions that fail to capture the sequential nature of dietary decision-making. We introduce MORL-A2C, a sequential decision-making extension to MOPI-HFRS targeting the health-preference axis. Leveraging frozen GNN embeddings, MORL-A2C formulates recommendation as a K-step reranking problem using an Advantage Actor-Critic algorithm with a scalarized relevance/health reward. The policy is warm-started via behavior cloning against a dot-product ranker derived from frozen embeddings. We also identify and correct a non-trivial bug in the MOPI-HFRS evaluation pipeline that understated baseline performance; all results are reported against the corrected baseline. On the macro-nutrient benchmark, MORL-A2C achieves a modest reduction in ranking quality (Recall@20: 25.64% to 23.61%, NDCG@20: 23.52% to 20.64%) in exchange for a substantial improvement in health alignment (H-Score@20: 46.05% to 69.57%), with consistent trends on the full-nutrient benchmark. These findings validate that policy-driven sequential optimization can effectively navigate the health-preference trade-off in multi-objective food recommendation.

</details>


### [430] [Scaling Linear Mode Connectivity and Merging to Billion Parameter Pretrained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23607)
*Tianyi Li,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的双向学习框架，通过对功能等价的模型施加保功能的参数变换，使十亿参数级的预训练Transformer能够通过简单的线性插值路径实现近零损失屏障的合并，在语言模型和视觉模型上均取得了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性模式连通性方法通常仅从单个模型端点优化插值路径，限制了其在大规模预训练Transformer上的可扩展性和有效性。

Method: 提出双学习框架，对两个功能等价的模型施加保功能的参数变换，并通过双向优化使两个模型共同学习各自变换以朝向共享线性插值路径对齐。

Result: 在中等参数规模的语言模型上实现了近零损失屏障；ViT-L在插值路径上保持69%以上ImageNet top-1准确率；十亿参数级LLM仅表现出很小的损失屏障。

Conclusion: 正确解决参数对称性能够使大规模预训练Transformer通过简单的线性路径实现连接与合并，显著提升插值性能。

Abstract: Linear mode connectivity (LMC) provides a promising foundation for understanding and merging independently trained neural networks, but existing methods typically optimize the interpolation path from only one model endpoint, limiting their scalability and effectiveness for large pretrained transformers. We propose a novel and scalable framework for enabling LMC-based model merging to {\em billion-parameter pretrained transformers}. Our method applies properly parameterized functionality-preserving weight transformations to align functionally equivalent solutions, and introduces a dual learning procedure in which both models jointly learn their corresponding transformations toward a shared linear interpolation path. This bidirectional optimization substantially reduces interpolation barriers and enables more reliable merging across large-scale architectures. Empirically, we show that our approach achieves near-zero loss barriers on WikiText for language models with medium-sized parameters, representing, to our knowledge, the first demonstration of near-barrier-free linear connectivity at this scale. In the vision domain, ViT-L maintains above 69\% ImageNet top-1 accuracy throughout the interpolation path, while modern billion-parameter LLMs exhibit only small loss barriers. These results suggest that properly resolving parameter symmetries enables large pretrained Transformers to be connected and merged through simple linear paths with substantially improved interpolation performance. Code: https://github.com/VILA-Lab/Dual-Learned-Matching .

</details>


### [431] [Discovering Latent Groups for Robust Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23609)
*Ankur Garg,Ulrich Aïvodji,Samira Ebrahimi Kahou,Vincent Michalski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经分类树（NCT）框架，通过树形架构编码子组结构，利用样本路由机制和伪标签迭代，在不依赖子组标注的情况下解耦冲突子组，在保持可解释性的同时实现与SOTA相当的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过调整网络参数来应对虚假相关性，但在推理时仅能输出类别预测，无法提供样本潜在子组的信息，缺乏可解释性。此外，这些方法要么依赖子组标注，要么依赖推断的伪组标签。

Method: 提出神经分类树（NCT）框架，其树形架构编码子组结构。通过路由机制将每个样本分配至"简单"或"困难"节点（基于预测正确性），并将这些路由路径作为下一轮迭代的伪标签，从而解耦冲突子组，无需子组监督。

Result: 在五个涵盖二分类和多分类虚假相关性的基准上，NCT学习的树拓扑结构能够一致地隔离少数子组，提供模型架构与数据潜在组结构之间的透明映射，同时鲁棒性与SOTA方法相当。

Conclusion: NCT通过树形架构有效解耦冲突子组，在无需子组标注的情况下实现强可解释性和竞争性鲁棒性，为处理虚假相关性提供了一种透明且有效的方案。

Abstract: Machine learning models exploit spurious correlations, achieving high average accuracy but failing disproportionately on underrepresented subgroups. Existing methods address this by adjusting network parameters, guided either by subgroup annotations or inferred pseudo-group labels. Yet at inference, these methods produce only a class prediction, with no insight into a sample's latent subgroup. We propose neural classification trees (NCT), a framework that achieves robustness by encoding subgroup structure in its tree-shaped architecture. By routing each sample to an "easy" or "hard" node of this tree -- based on prediction correctness -- and reusing these routes as pseudo-labels for the next iteration, NCT disentangles conflicting subgroups, without requiring subgroup supervision. We evaluate NCT on five benchmarks spanning binary and multi-class spurious correlations. Our experiments show that the learned tree topology provides strong interpretability by consistently isolating minority subgroups, which provides a transparent mapping between the model architecture and the data's latent group structure, while yielding competitive robustness with state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [432] [DiT-Reward: Generative Representations for Text-to-Image Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23626)
*Yuanming Yang,Guoqing Ma,Bo Wang,Yuan Zhang,Wei Tang,Chenyi Li,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiT-Reward，利用预训练文本到图像扩散Transformer（DiT）的中间层表征构建奖励模型，在偏好预测基准上超越HPSv3，并支持生成策略优化和加速推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生成式表征学习（用于图像生成）是否也能服务于生成图像的评估（奖励预测），从而统一生成与评估的表示学习。

Method: 将预训练文本到图像DiT转换为奖励模型（DiT-Reward）：处理近干净图像潜变量，跨Transformer层聚合文本条件图像表征；采用轻量级头部网络；通过Flow-GRPO优化生成策略。

Result: 在相同训练数据混合下，DiT-Reward在所有四个偏好基准上超越HPSv3，HPDv2达85.6%，HPDv3达77.6%；生成骨干冻结时轻量头部仍有效；中间到后期层表征最强；生成骨干容量正向扩展；推理速度比HPSv3提升1.65倍，峰值内存相当。

Conclusion: 预训练生成式DiT不仅可用于图像生成，其表征也能有效迁移至奖励建模和策略优化，统一生成与评估的表示学习。

Abstract: Can representations learned for image generation also support the evaluation of generated images? We study text-to-image reward prediction as a downstream task of generative representation learning. To this end, we introduce DiT-Reward, which converts a pretrained text-to-image Diffusion Transformer into a reward model by processing near-clean image latents and aggregating text-conditioned image representations across transformer layers. Under the same training data mixture as HPSv3, DiT-Reward outperforms HPSv3 on all four evaluated preference benchmarks, reaching 85.6% on HPDv2 and 77.6% on HPDv3. When the generative backbone is frozen, a lightweight learned head can still extract meaningful preference predictions from its representations. Probing across depth further reveals that downstream reward performance is strongest in the middle-to-late layers and benefits from combining representations across different stages. We also observe consistent positive scaling with generative backbone capacity. Finally, when used to optimize Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large with Flow-GRPO, DiT-Reward outperforms HPSv3 along the matched training trajectory, with particularly clear gains in realism. Direct latent scoring also achieves a 1.65x inference speedup over HPSv3 with comparable peak memory. These results show that pretrained generative DiTs provide transferable representations for reward modeling and policy optimization.

</details>


### [433] [Muown Implicitly Performs Angular Step-size Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23637)
*Florian Hübler,Kai Lion,Antonio Orvieto,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AngularMuown优化器，将Muown的方向更新显式化为黎曼流形上的优化，引入可调角步长超参数，在多个规模模型上取得优于Muown的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Muown优化器中方向更新的几何本质，揭示其步长稳定性来源，并基于此改进算法以提升性能。

Method: 将Muown的方向更新等价转化为归一化方向上的黎曼梯度步，解耦角步长与径向幅度更新，显式引入可调角步长超参数。

Result: AngularMuown在modded nanoGPT速度竞赛中领先per-optimizer类别，在Qwen2-0.5B和1.1B MoE模型上验证了可扩展性。

Conclusion: 显式化角步长能提升优化器性能，AngularMuown在大规模模型训练中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Matrix-aware optimizers such as Muon and Muown have recently shown strong empirical performance for pre-training Transformers. In particular, Muown separates each weight matrix into row magnitudes and an un-normalized direction variable, updating the former with Adam and the latter with Muon. We show that the directional update of Muown is equivalent to a Riemannian step on the normalized directions, while the magnitude of the un-normalized parameterization only modulates the angular step size. This explains the step-size stability of Muown and suggests making the angular step size explicit. The resulting method, AngularMuown, optimizes directly over the normalized directions and uses a schedulable angular multiplier decoupled from the radial magnitude update. AngularMuown improves over Muown and, at the time of writing, a preliminary version is leading the per-optimizer category of the modded nanoGPT speedrunning competition. Further experiments on Qwen2-0.5B, and 1.1B parameter mixture-of-experts models confirm the algorithm scales beyond small models. An implementation of the algorithm is available at https://github.com/fhueb/angular-muown

</details>


### [434] [Learning Process Rewards via Success Visitation Matching for Efficient RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23640)
*Raymond Tsao,Andrew Wagenmaker,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将稀疏结果奖励转化为密集过程奖励的简单方法，通过训练判别器区分成功与不成功的过往回合，激励策略匹配成功回合的状态-动作访问模式，从而加速强化学习微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中任务的自然奖励通常是稀疏的（仅在任务完成时给予+1奖励），导致信用分配困难，训练缓慢或无效。需要将稀疏奖励转化为密集反馈以加速学习。

Method: 训练一个判别器来区分历史成功和失败的回合，利用该判别器生成密集奖励信号，激励当前策略模仿成功回合的状态-动作访问分布，同时避免失败回合的访问模式。该方法不改变最优策略。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的机器人操控任务微调中，该方法相比直接最大化稀疏结果奖励，显著加快了强化学习微调速度。

Conclusion: 通过判别器将稀疏结果奖励转化为密集过程奖励是一种简单且有效的方法，能够在保持最优策略不变的前提下，显著加速机器人控制策略的微调。

Abstract: In many modern applications of reinforcement learning (RL), the natural reward for a task of interest is inherently sparse: a reward of 0 is given everywhere except when the task is completed, when a reward of +1 is given. Training a policy to maximize such a sparse reward requires solving a challenging credit assignment problem, leading to slow or ineffective RL improvement. We propose a simple approach to transform a sparse outcome reward into a dense process reward. Our approach relies on training a discriminator to distinguish between previous successful and unsuccessful episodes, and using this discriminator to incentivize the RL-learned policy to match the state-action visitations of successful episodes, while avoiding those of unsuccessful episodes. By incentivizing the policy to match the visitations over all states, not just those that correspond to task success, this reward provides dense feedback on whether progress is being made towards task completion, and, we show, provably achieves this without changing the optimal policy. Focusing on finetuning of robotic control policies, we demonstrate that our approach leads to significantly faster RL finetuning performance on both simulated and real-world manipulation tasks, as compared to simply maximizing the sparse outcome reward.

</details>


### [435] [Dynamic estimation of slowly varying sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23655)
*Prashant Gokhale,Mikhail Khodak,Sandeep Silwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用框架，用于高效逼近缓慢变化序列中的每个元素的函数值。该框架通过利用相邻元素间的小差异（α_i）重用查询，实现了对矩阵幂、谱密度、蒙特卡洛积分和偏微分方程边值问题的顺序估计，并开发了一种自适应算法将估计预算与α_i局部缩放，获得了O(∑α_i)的路径长度型变分界，优于之前O(m·maxα_i)的最坏情况界。此外，在未知变化量的情况下，可以近乎零额外成本地在线估计变化量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列逼近方法（如隐式迹估计）虽能通过重用查询降低计算成本，但其预算分配基于最坏情况α_i，对含有罕见突变的稳定序列效率低下。亟需一种能够自适应地根据实际变化量动态分配计算资源的通用框架，以处理更广泛的线性和非线性函数及多种向量空间上的序列估计问题。

Method: 提出一个通用框架，将查询重用策略扩展到多种线性和非线性函数及不同向量空间；开发一种局部缩放估计预算的自适应算法，使预算与α_i成比例，从而实现O(∑α_i)的路径长度型变分界；对于未知变化量的情况，设计在线估计机制，以近乎零额外成本估计α_i。

Result: 获得了更紧的O(∑_{i=1}^m α_i)路径长度型变分界，优于此前O(m·max_i α_i)的界；在矩阵幂、谱密度、蒙特卡洛积分和偏微分方程边值问题等多种场景下验证了框架的有效性；实现了对未知变化量的在线估计且代价极低。

Conclusion: 该框架将顺序逼近工具推广为通用且自适应的工具，在动态迹估计问题上取得了优于现有技术的保障，通过自适应预算分配显著提高了对缓慢变化序列的计算效率。

Abstract: We consider the problem of sequentially approximating functions of each element in a slowly-varying sequence, i.e. one where the magnitude $α_i$ of the difference between the elements at positions $i$ and $i-1$ is small. Recent work on implicit trace estimation shows that when $α_t$ is small, reusing queries to past sequence elements can reduce the overall cost [Dharangutte \& Musco, NeurIPS~2021; Woodruff et al., NeurIPS~2022]. We introduce a framework generalizing this to a variety of linear and nonlinear functions on diverse vector spaces, obtaining novel sequential estimation results for matrix powers, spectral densities, Monte Carlo integration, and a boundary value problem from partial differential equations~(PDEs). Furthermore, we develop a novel algorithm for use with this framework that locally scales the estimation budget with $α_t$, obtaining sharper path-length-style variation bounds of form $\mathcal O(\sum_{i=1}^mα_i)$ on the cost of estimating a sequence of length $m$. This improves upon the previous implicit trace estimation bound of $\mathcal O(m\cdot\max_iα_i)$ [Dharangutte \& Musco, NeurIPS~2021], which is achieved by fixing the query budget using the worst-case $α_i$ and is thus inefficient for stable sequences with rare bursts. Lastly, while all past work assumes a known bound on $α_i$, we show in certain cases how the changes can be estimated on-the-fly with (nearly) no added cost. In summary, our framework makes the sequential approximation toolkit general-purpose and adaptive while improving upon state-of-the-art-guarantees for dynamic trace estimation.

</details>


### [436] [MAS-PromptBench: When Does Prompt Optimization Improve Multi-Agent LLM Systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23664)
*Juyang Bai,Laixi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多智能体系统（MAS）中系统提示词优化的效果，发现其能带来显著性能提升，但优化效果高度依赖任务、工作流、通信协议和团队规模等系统配置，并揭示了搜索空间指数增长等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管提示词优化在单LLM中已展现巨大潜力，但多智能体系统（MAS）中系统提示词的优化面临搜索空间指数级增长等独特挑战。目前尚不清楚提示词优化在MAS中是否有效、何时有效以及提升幅度有多大，且对系统配置的敏感性未知。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在广泛的多智能体系统配置（涵盖不同任务、工作流、通信协议和团队规模）下，采用两种自然扩展自单智能体最优方法的提示词优化器，进行系统性的对比实验研究。

Result: 提示词优化在MAS中能够带来显著性能提升，但增益幅度高度依赖于任务类型、工作流设计、通信协议和团队规模等系统配置；同时暴露出搜索空间指数增长等开放挑战。

Conclusion: 提示词优化是提升MAS性能的有效途径，但其效果具有显著的配置依赖性，需根据具体系统设置进行针对性优化；当前方法仍面临搜索空间等挑战，有待进一步研究。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) offer a scalable path forward for agentic AI, comprising multiple LLM-based agents, each assigned a system prompt and a position within a workflow that governs inter-agent coordination and output aggregation. System prompts thus form a critical and accessible optimization surface: they specify agents' roles and behaviors, enabling system-level improvements without model finetuning. Although prompt optimization has shown substantial potential for single LLMs, extending it to MAS poses distinct challenges, notably an exponentially growing search space. It remains unclear whether, when, and by how much prompt optimization improves MAS performance, and how sensitive such gains are to system configuration. In this work, we systematically study system-prompt optimization across a broad range of MAS setups varying in task, workflow, communication protocol, and team size, benchmarking two prompt optimizers that naturally extend state-of-the-art single-agent methods. The results reveal its potential to unlock significant gains while exposing open challenges, characterizing when and how much prompt optimization helps across diverse MAS settings.

</details>


### [437] [On the Limits of Prompt-Conditioned Language Models as General-Purpose Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23668)
*David Mguni,Julian Ma,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过廉价谈话博弈模型和PAC-Bayes界，从理论上证明了提示条件化的大语言模型（LLM）并非通用问题求解器。语言作为容量受限的通信通道，当任务族的信息复杂度超过通道容量时，会产生不可消除的误差下界（表达能力下限）；同时，对齐约束将用户理想分布排除在可行类之外，导致不可约的畸变（目标错位下限）。这些结果表明，即使拥有无限数据，LLM也无法通过纯提示方法正确求解所有任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战LLM被广泛视为通用求解器的流行观点，揭示其根本性局限：语言是容量受限的压缩接口，无法承载任意复杂任务的全部信息。

Method: 将用户-系统交互建模为双层廉价谈话博弈，引入任务推理与执行的分离式分解，利用PAC-Bayes界限推导有限样本估计误差与不可约结构局限，从理论上建立表达能力下限和目标错位下限。

Result: （1）语言通道容量限制导致任务不可区分，产生严格为正的误差下限，无法通过更多数据、优化或模型缩放消除；（2）对齐约束导致用户理想分布超出可行模型类，产生不可消除的畸变。

Conclusion: 提示条件化LLM通过纯提示无法实现通用问题求解，存在理论上不可达的正确行为。多模态观测和外部记忆等超越自然语言的接口可能缓解这些固有限制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are frequently portrayed as general-purpose solvers capable of solving arbitrary tasks. We argue that this view overlooks a fundamental constraint: language is a compressed and capacity-limited interface for conveying task information. Modelling User--System interaction as a bilevel \emph{cheap-talk} game, we analyse how latent tasks are encoded into prompts and reinterpreted under alignment and safety constraints. We introduce a conceptual decomposition separating task inference from execution and derive PAC-Bayes bounds that distinguish finite-sample estimation error from irreducible structural limitations. Our first main result establishes an \emph{expressivity floor}: language acts as a capacity-limited communication channel, and whenever the informational complexity of a task family exceeds the capacity of that channel, distinct tasks become unavoidably indistinguishable to the Solver, inducing a strictly positive error floor that cannot be eliminated by additional data, optimisation, or model scaling alone. We then establish an \emph{objective-misalignment floor}: when alignment constraints restrict the admissible output set, the User-ideal distribution may lie outside the feasible class, inducing an irreducible distortion. Together, these results yield a formal negative conclusion: prompt-conditioned LLMs are not universal problem solvers through prompting alone, as there exist task families for which correct behaviour is provably unattainable even in the infinite-data regime. More broadly, our analysis shows the limits of prompt-based generalisation arise from information-constrained communication and alignment-constrained objectives. This suggests that interfaces beyond natural language, including multimodal observations and, external memory, may reduce the inherent LLM limitations by increasing the task-relevant information available to the System.

</details>


### [438] [Tapered Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23670)
*Reza Bayat,Ali Behrouz,Aaron Courville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"锥形语言模型"(TLM)的架构原则：在固定预算下，将模型容量（尤其是MLP宽度）从浅层到深层单调递减分配，相比均匀分配能一致提升困惑度和下游任务性能，且不增加参数或计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型（包括Transformer、RNN、记忆增强模型等）在深度上均匀分配参数，但已有证据表明各层对最终输出的贡献不均匀——浅层更关键，深层主要细化残差流而非转换它。本文探究参数容量是否应反映这种非对称性。

Method: 通过控制变量实验，在固定总参数预算下，将MLP宽度按照平滑余弦调度函数从浅层到深层单调递减（锥形化）。在三种模型规模和四种架构（Transformer、Gated Attention、Hope-attention、Titans）上进行验证。

Result: 浅层分配更多容量、深层分配更少容量能提升困惑度，反向分配则损害性能。锥形化MLP宽度在三种规模、四种架构上一致优于均匀基线，且不增加参数或计算开销。

Conclusion: 深度感知的容量分配是一种简单、与架构无关的语言模型设计轴，是在固定预算下免费提升性能的有效手段。

Abstract: Modern language models, including transformer, recurrent, and memory-based variants, share a common chassis: a stack of identical layers in which parameters are allocated uniformly across depth. This is a default inherited from the original transformer and largely unchanged since, yet a growing body of evidence suggests that layers contribute non-uniformly to the final output, with later layers refining the residual stream rather than transforming it. We ask whether parameter capacity should reflect this asymmetry. Our controlled experiment shows that, under a fixed budget, allocating more capacity to earlier layers and less to later layers improves perplexity over a uniform-width baseline, while the reverse allocation hurts. Building on this result, we introduce Tapered Language Models (TLMs), an architectural principle in which a parameter-bearing component is monotonically tapered across depth under a fixed total budget. MLPs are the natural site for this instantiation: they dominate parameter count across all modern LM families and expose width as a single, clean axis of variation. Across three model scales and four architectures (Transformer, Gated Attention, Hope-attention, and Titans), tapering MLP width via a smooth cosine schedule consistently improves perplexity and downstream benchmark performance over uniform baselines, at no additional parameter or compute cost. These findings establish depth-aware capacity allocation as a simple, architecture-agnostic axis of language model design, a free lever hidden in plain sight.

</details>


### [439] [Open Problem: Is AdamW Effective Under Heavy-Tailed Noise?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23676)
*Dingzhi Yu,Hongyi Tao,Yuanyu Wan,Luo Luo,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出AdamW在重尾噪声下的收敛性作为一个开放问题，证明了加权度量下的正基准结果，并展示了分母记忆如何隐藏大梯度的下界机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练广泛使用AdamW优化器，但其收敛理论主要局限于有限方差场景。实证表明LLM预训练中的随机梯度噪声通常具有重尾特性，而AdamW在重尾噪声下的收敛理论尚未建立，这构成了理论与实践之间的显著鸿沟。

Method: 论文将该问题形式化为一个开放问题，通过构建加权度量下的正基准结果来证明收敛可能性，同时设计了下界机制（"走廊下界"）来揭示AdamW的二阶矩累积器（分母记忆）如何可能阻碍收敛——即分母记忆会掩蔽大梯度信息。

Result: 给出了一个正面的加权度量收敛基准，以及一个负面的下界结果：证明了AdamW的分母记忆机制在重尾噪声下可能导致大梯度被隐藏，从而构成收敛障碍。

Conclusion: AdamW在重尾噪声下的收敛性尚未解决，既有收敛潜力（加权度量下）也存在根本性障碍（分母记忆导致梯度信息丢失），这为后续理论分析指明了方向。

Abstract: AdamW is the de facto optimizer for training large language models (LLMs), yet the theory behind it still lives mostly in finite-variance regimes. This is increasingly unsatisfying, as empirical evidence indicates that stochastic gradient noise in LLM pretraining is typically heavy-tailed. Recent work shows that sign-based optimizers such as Lion and Muon achieve sharp heavy-tailed rates, and that AdaGrad can also converge under heavy-tailed noise. However, no rigorous convergence theory for AdamW has yet been established in this regime. Can AdamW converge under the same heavy-tailed assumptions, or does its second-moment accumulator create a genuine obstruction? We formulate this as an open problem, prove a positive weighted-metric benchmark, and give a corridor lower-bound mechanism showing how denominator memory can hide large gradients.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [440] [Total-Lagrangian vectorial lattice Boltzmann method for finite-strain hyperelasticity with curved boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20646)
*Jingsen Feng,Xu Chu*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种总拉格朗日矢量格子玻尔兹曼方法，用于在弯曲嵌入域上求解有限应变超弹性动力学问题。该方法通过矢量值分布函数、水平集嵌入边界和相容性投影，在笛卡尔网格上实现了对二维和三维弯曲边界的处理，并通过数值算例验证了与精确解和有限元参考结果的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限应变超弹性在弯曲嵌入域上的几何挑战：格子玻尔兹曼方法在跨越嵌入材料表面进行流式传播后，需要在物理切割连接点处恢复缺失的分布函数，而该点处的格子方向、表面法向和切向变形方向通常不一致，存在几何失配问题。

Method: 开发了一种总拉格朗日矢量格子玻尔兹曼方法：(1) 将连续方程写为关于材料速度和变形梯度的守恒一阶系统；(2) 选择矢量值分布函数，使其矩恢复状态和物质坐标Piola通量，得到D2Q4×6和D3Q6×12格式；(3) 通过水平集嵌入弯曲边界，利用对向分布函数矩恒等式、切割连接点插值和局部几何信息进行逐连接点重构；(4) 结合相容性投影，使恢复的位移与演化的变形梯度在嵌入活动节点图上保持一致。

Result: 该方法将先前的网格对齐二维公式扩展到弯曲域和三维格子，同时保留笛卡尔网格上的显式碰撞-流式更新。二维和三维数值算例表明，结果与精确有限应变场、非线性径向边值问题以及有限元参考结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: 成功实现了格子玻尔兹曼方法在弯曲嵌入域上的有限应变超弹性动力学模拟，为处理复杂几何边界下的非线性弹性问题提供了一种有效的显式数值框架。

Abstract: Finite-strain hyperelasticity on curved embedded domains poses a geometric challenge for lattice Boltzmann methods. After streaming across an embedded material surface, the missing population is recovered at the physical cut-link point, where the lattice direction, surface normal, and tangential deformation directions are generally distinct. We develop a total-Lagrangian vectorial lattice Boltzmann method that resolves this geometric mismatch for two- and three-dimensional hyperelastic dynamics. The continuum equations are written as a conservative first-order system for material velocity and deformation gradient. Vector-valued populations are chosen so that their moments recover the state and the material-coordinate Piola fluxes, giving D2Q4\(\times\)6 and D3Q6\(\times\)12 schemes from one \(D\)-dimensional construction. Curved boundaries are embedded by a level set and closed link by link through opposite-population moment identities, cut-link interpolation, and local geometric information at the boundary point. The reconstruction is coupled to a compatibility projection that keeps the recovered displacement aligned with the evolved deformation gradient on embedded active-node graphs. The resulting method extends the previous grid-aligned two-dimensional formulation to curved domains and three-dimensional lattices while retaining explicit collide-stream updates on Cartesian grids. Benchmarks in two and three dimensions show agreement with exact finite-strain fields, nonlinear radial boundary-value problems, and finite-element references.

</details>


### [441] [Input-schema identifiability limits in physics-informed surrogates for mechanics-governed flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20655)
*Daniel Cieslak,Andrzej Czyzewski*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种输入模式可辨识性验证方法，用于在训练前判断计算代理模型是否能够从输入变量中物理可辨识地预测目标流场。通过将目标场分解为几何可测分量、边界条件依赖分量和对称性导致的模糊分量，并在不可压缩管流（基于Cosserat-rod约化）中验证了该框架，实验表明方向符号模糊无法通过改变模型架构或数据量消除，且传统聚合误差指标会隐藏此类失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理信息驱动和数据驱动的代理模型在近似力学主导的流场时，目标量并不总是能从预测时的输入变量中可辨识，导致模型可能学习到错误或模糊的映射关系。需要一种预训练审计方法来判断建模任务是否物理可辨识。

Method: 从约化物理模型出发，将目标场分解为几何可测分量、边界条件依赖分量和仅能对称性商辨识的分量。以不可压缩管流为例，基于Cosserat-rod约化将管腔速度分解为网格可测的切向方向、依赖边界条件的幅度和符号取向模糊。在患者特异性主动脉CFD几何、解析Womersley流和对流-扩散传输问题上进行实验验证。

Result: 提供方向符号可消除角度误差至Oracle水平，而仅提供幅度不提供方向时，预测的符号模糊导致16-33%的节点符号翻转。聚合误差指标会隐藏这类失败模式。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种基于力学的诊断方法，可在训练前判断代理建模任务的物理可辨识性，揭示了传统聚合误差指标可能掩盖的失败模式。

Abstract: Physics-informed and data-driven surrogates are increasingly used to approximate mechanics-governed flow fields, but the target quantities assigned to such models are not always identifiable from the input variables available at prediction time. We introduce an input-schema identifiability certificate for computational surrogates. Starting from a reduced physical model, the certificate decomposes a target field into components that are measurable from geometry, components that require boundary-condition information, and components identifiable only up to a symmetry quotient. This yields a pre-training audit: it predicts which oracle-channel interventions should reduce error, which should fail, and which ambiguity cannot be removed by changing the architecture, loss, optimizer, or sample size. We instantiate the framework for incompressible tubular flow using a Cosserat-rod reduction, where lumen velocity separates into a mesh-measurable tangent direction, a boundary-condition-dependent magnitude, and a signed-orientation ambiguity. Controlled experiments on patient-specific aortic CFD geometries, analytic Womersley flows, and an advection-diffusion transfer problem confirm the predicted pattern: supplying signed direction collapses angular error to the oracle regime, whereas supplying magnitude without orientation leaves the predicted sign ambiguity and yields 16-33 percent per-node sign flips. The results provide a mechanics-based diagnostic for deciding whether a surrogate modelling task is physically identifiable before training, and expose failure modes that aggregate error metrics can hide.

</details>


### [442] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for coupled stiff transport systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21236)
*Laetitia Laguzet,Gabriel Turinici*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对标准PINNs在刚性输运方程（以Marshak波方程为例）中因不稳定性、损失不平衡和物理一致性违反而失败的问题，提出了三项改进：缩放Sigmoid激活函数、对数MSE损失函数以及全局守恒律约束，成功在30分钟内恢复了Marshak波动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PINNs在处理刚性、变 regimes 的输运方程时存在不稳定、损失不平衡和物理一致性违反等问题，本文以Marshak波方程（初始和边界条件相差高达12个数量级）为基准，旨在识别并解决这些失效模式。

Method: (1) ScaledSigmoid激活函数确保物理有界性和未知量正性；(2) 对数MSE损失替代标准二次损失处理极端尺度差异；(3) 显式施加全局守恒律作为额外物理损失项；结合蒙特卡洛采样与指数时间加权。

Result: 成功恢复了Marshak波动力学（包括热区、冷区和波前区域），与参考隐式蒙特卡洛解一致，运行时间低于30分钟。消融实验表明每项改进均不可或缺。

Conclusion: 有界激活函数、尺度感知损失函数和守恒律约束的组合构成了新颖且经过实践验证的框架，可推广至辐射输运及其他耦合刚性PDE系统。

Abstract: Purpose: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) struggle with stiff, regime-changing transport equations due to instability, loss imbalance, and violations of physical consistency. This paper investigates these failures through the Marshak wave equations - a canonical benchmark from radiative transport - where initial and boundary conditions differ by up to 12 orders of magnitude, and proposes targeted modifications to the standard PINN framework to overcome them.
  Design/methodology/approach: Three modifications are introduced: (1) a ScaledSigmoid final activation enforcing physical bounds and positivity of the unknowns; (2) a logarithmic MSE loss replacing the standard quadratic loss for initial and boundary conditions, enabling training across extreme scale disparities; and (3) explicit enforcement of global conservation laws derived from the governing equations as an additional physics loss term. Monte Carlo sampling with exponential time weighting is used throughout.
  Findings: The proposed framework successfully recovers the Marshak wave dynamics - including the hot, cold, and wave-front regions - in agreement with a reference Implicit Monte Carlo solution, with run times under 30 minutes. Ablation studies confirm that each ingredient is essential: linear activation, absence of the logarithmic loss, or removal of the PDE term each independently cause the method to fail qualitatively.
  Originality/value: This work identifies and resolves three concrete failure modes of standard PINNs on stiff hyperbolic systems with nonlinear coupling. The combination of bounded activations, scale-aware loss functions, and conservation law enforcement constitutes a novel and practically validated framework, with applicability to radiative transport and other coupled stiff PDE systems in engineering.

</details>


### [443] [AI-accelerated metallized $σ$-bonding screening for superconductor discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21251)
*Zechen Tang,Wen-Han Dong,Baochun Wu,Jian-Feng Zhang,Yuxiang Wang,Yang Li,Honggeng Tao,Qiyu Zeng,Chong Wang,Chen Si,Zhong-Yi Lu,Wenhui Duan,Tao Xiang,Yong Xu*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于σ键态密度（σDOS）的高效物理描述符，结合深度学习加速的DFT方法，无需昂贵的密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT）即可识别高温超导体。通过对200万种材料的筛选，发现了环境压力下Tc>40K的超导体候选材料B13Se及B13X家族，验证了该策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声子介导超导体的计算发现受到密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT）高昂计算成本的严重制约，亟需一种更高效的筛选方法。

Method: 基于金属化σ键图像，引入σ键态密度（σDOS）作为物理描述符，仅需DFT级别的电子结构即可评估超导转变温度；进一步结合深度学习DFT哈密顿量方法加速σDOS计算，实现大规模高通量筛选。

Result: 筛选200万种材料后，发现环境压力下的超导体候选材料B13Se（预测Tc>40K）以及B13X系列高温超导体家族。

Conclusion: 通过将物理先验知识与AI加速相结合，该研究提供了一种高效且可推广的计算材料发现路线，适用于人工智能时代的材料高通量筛选。

Abstract: The computational discovery of phonon-mediated superconductors is hindered by the prohibitive cost of density functional perturbation theory (DFPT). Here, guided by the metallized $σ$-bonding picture, we introduce the $σ$-bonding density of states ($σ$DOS) as an efficient physical descriptor to identify high-transition-temperature ($T_{\mathrm{c}}$) superconductors from density functional theory (DFT)-level electronic structure without explicit DFPT calculations. The evaluation of $σ$DOS can be further accelerated by a deep-learning DFT Hamiltonian method, enabling efficient large-scale screening for superconductors. Screening 2 million materials, we identify B$_{13}$Se as an ambient-pressure superconductor candidate with predicted $T_{\mathrm{c}} > 40$~K, together with a family of high-$T_{\mathrm{c}}$ B$_{13}X$ candidates, supporting the effectiveness of this discovery strategy. By bridging physics priors with AI acceleration, this study delivers an efficient and generalizable route for computational materials discovery in the AI era.

</details>


### [444] [MADField: Multi-fidelity Amortized Density Field for Adsorption in Nanoporous Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21284)
*Yoonho Kim,Seongsu Kim,Sungsoo Ahn,Honghui Kim*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: MADField是一种多保真度摊销密度场模型，通过结合cDFT和GCMC两种互补保真度的数据，实现纳米多孔材料中气体吸附的快速准确预测，在甲烷工作容量筛选中比最强基线精度提高56倍，推理速度比GCMC快五个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量计算筛选纳米多孔材料进行气体存储和分离需要快速且准确地表征吸附平衡，但现有基于粒子的GCMC和基于密度的cDFT方法在速度与精度之间的权衡不足以满足大规模筛选需求。

Method: 提出多保真度摊销密度场模型MADField，将吸附预测重构为平衡密度场估计问题，从两种互补保真度中学习：结合广泛可扩展的cDFT密度监督与高保真度GCMC密度标签，通过积分预测的密度场恢复气体吸附量。

Result: MADField在吸附量精度上分别比cDFT和GCMC最强基线提升6.0倍和15.4倍；预测的密度场可使cDFT求解器迭代步数减少2.0倍，并恢复42%的标准设置下失败的案例。在ARC-MOF数据库的CH4工作容量筛选中，平均精度比最强基线高56倍，推理速度比GCMC快五个数量级；选取前1.7%的候选结构可恢复95%的目标，前6%可实现100%召回。

Conclusion: MADField通过多保真度学习策略有效解决了纳米多孔材料吸附预测中速度与精度的权衡问题，在超大规模材料筛选中展现出极高的效率和精度，尤其擅长识别罕见的高容量目标。

Abstract: High-throughput computational screening of nanoporous materials for gas storage and separation requires fast and accurate characterization of adsorption equilibrium. Particle-based grand canonical Monte Carlo (GCMC) and density-based classical density functional theory (cDFT) provide simulation-based estimates of gas uptake and adsorbate density fields, but their speed-accuracy tradeoff remains insufficient for large-scale screening. In this work, we address this gap with Multi-fidelity Amortized Density Field for Adsorption in Nanoporous Materials (MADField), which reframes adsorption prediction as equilibrium density-field estimation. MADField learns from two complementary fidelities, combining broad and scalable cDFT density supervision with higher-fidelity GCMC density labels, and recovers gas uptake by integrating the predicted density field. MADField improves uptake accuracy over the strongest baselines by 6.0x for cDFT and 15.4x for GCMC, and its predicted fields accelerate cDFT solvers with 2.0x fewer iteration steps while recovering 42 percent of cases that fail under standard settings. Finally, we evaluate MADField for conventional CH4 working capacity screening on the 270k-structure ARC-MOF database. Within this space of extremely rare high-capacity targets, 167 in total, the model achieves 56x higher average precision than the strongest baseline and accelerates inference by five orders of magnitude compared to GCMC. By prioritizing the MADField rankings, selecting the top 1.7 percent of candidates recovers 95 percent of all targets, while the top 6 percent ensures 100 percent recall.

</details>


### [445] [Observations Regarding the Construction of Multipoint Kinetics Models from Observational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21741)
*K. G. Howey,Giorgio Valocchi,Dean Price*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了从瞬态数据中恢复多点点动力学（MPK）耦合系数矩阵K的问题，通过解析求解Kobayashi MPK公式（无先驱核和单先驱核族情况），分析了特征模态行为与参数可观测性，发现延迟中子先驱核虽延缓特征模态发散但不足以实现K的实际恢复，关键在于在不过度采样主导特征模态的情况下捕捉非主导特征模态的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPK方程通过跟踪集总反应堆区域的中子种群来扩展点动力学模型，以适中的计算成本提供更好的空间分辨率。然而，从瞬态数据确定耦合系数矩阵K仍是一个开放问题，缺乏成熟的实验程序。本文旨在为从瞬态可观测正向通量测量中恢复K这一任务提供评述。

Method: 对Kobayashi MPK公式在无先驱核和单先驱核族情况下推导了谱形式的解析解；采用模态灵敏度分析和谱恢复问题的Hessian条件数来研究耦合系数可恢复性。

Result: 延迟中子先驱核会延缓特征模态的发散，但在许多场景下可能不足以实现K的实际恢复；捕获非主导特征模态的行为对K的可恢复性至关重要，前提是不能过度采样主导特征模态。

Conclusion: 从瞬态数据中恢复耦合系数矩阵K的关键在于在不过度采样主导特征模态的条件下，有效捕捉非主导特征模态的行为。

Abstract: The multipoint kinetics (MPK) equations extend point kinetics models by tracking neutron populations in lumped reactor regions, offering improved spatial resolution at modest computational cost. However, determining the coupling coefficient matrix $K$ from transient data remains an open problem without established experimental procedures. This work provides some commentary on the task of recovering $K$ from observable forward flux measurements during reactor transients.
  Analytical solutions to the Kobayashi MPK formulation in the case of no precursors and with a single precursor family are derived in spectral form, enabling a mathematically rigorous investigation of eigenmode behavior and parameter observability. Coupling coefficient recoverability is investigated using modal sensitivity analysis and the Hessian condition number of the spectral recovery problem. The inclusion of delayed neutron precursors delays this eigenmode divergence but may be insufficient for enabling practical recovery of $K$ in many scenarios. In conclusion, capturing the behavior of nondominant eigenmodes without oversampling the dominant eigenmode is critical to the recoverability of $K$ through transient data.

</details>


### [446] [False Summit and Silent Drift: A Failure Taxonomy and Efficiency Analysis of LLM-Assisted Multiphysics Simulation in an Open-Source Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21841)
*Chi-Chun Chiang,Shih-Ming He*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM辅助和文献指导能否降低多物理场仿真开发门槛，以MOOSE框架下石墨烯LPCVD模型开发为案例，发现阶段性开发虽能产生收敛仿真，但存在"虚假顶峰"和"无声漂移"两类隐蔽错误，且文献指导可显著减少交互负担（GPT减少91%，Claude减少64%），由此提出八类失效分类、人工监督策略和阶段门控工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多物理场半导体工艺仿真要求研究者同时掌握控制方程、数值方法、软件实现和工艺物理知识，为新手设置了极高的入门门槛。本文探索LLM和文献指导能否降低这一障碍。

Method: 在MOOSE框架下开发石墨烯LPCVD模型，对比GPT和Claude在直接提示、无文献阶段性开发、有文献阶段性开发三种方式下的表现，分析仿真收敛性和错误模式。

Result: 阶段性开发成功产生收敛仿真，但发现两类隐蔽错误：虚假顶峰（收敛且物理上看似合理但实际物理错误）和无声漂移（未经验证的假设在开发阶段中无声传播）。非守恒的物质输运公式产生了与真实表面消耗视觉上无法区分的虚假甲烷消耗；遗漏的自限表面反应项导致LLM反复为之辩护的物理错误行为。文献指导在最具挑战的开发阶段将GPT的提示次数减少91%，Claude减少64%。

Conclusion: LLM可以降低多物理场仿真开发的门槛，但严格的物理验证仍然不可或缺，因为表面上合理的解可能隐藏着关键缺陷。提出了八类失效分类、模型特定的人工监督策略和阶段门控工作流以提高隐蔽建模错误的可检测性。

Abstract: Multiphysics simulation of semiconductor processes requires simultaneous command of governing equations, numerical methods, software implementation, and process physics, creating a substantial entry barrier for newcomers. We investigate whether large language model (LLM) assistance and curated literature guidance can reduce this barrier during the development of a low-pressure chemical vapor deposition (LPCVD) model for graphene growth using the open-source Multiphysics Object-Oriented Simulation Environment (MOOSE). We compare GPT and Claude across direct prompting, staged development without literature, and staged development with literature guidance. Staged development successfully produced converged simulations but revealed two recurring failure classes. We define a false summit as a simulation that converges and appears physically plausible while implementing incorrect physics, and silent drift as the undetected propagation of unverified assumptions through successive development stages. In our case study, a non-conservative species-transport formulation generated spurious methane depletion that was visually indistinguishable from genuine surface consumption, while an omitted self-limiting surface-reaction term produced physically incorrect growth behavior that the LLM repeatedly rationalized as valid. Literature guidance substantially reduced interaction burden during the most challenging development stage, decreasing prompt counts by 91% for GPT and 64% for Claude. Based on these observations, we propose an eight-category failure taxonomy, model-specific human oversight strategies, and a stage-gated workflow to improve the detectability of hidden modeling errors. The results demonstrate that LLMs can lower the barrier to multiphysics simulation development, but rigorous physical validation remains essential because apparently reasonable solutions may conceal critical defects

</details>


### [447] [Inverse Identification of Surface Elastic Parameters in Soft Solids Using GA-ANN Surrogate Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22120)
*Md. Ayaz,Abhishek Ghosh,Andrew McBride,M. Rashid Zafar,Md. Irfan Ansari*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于遗传算法(GA)和人工神经网络(ANN)替代前向模型的优化框架，用于从圆柱试样的力-位移响应中高效识别软固体的表面弹性参数，在高达5%噪声下识别误差小于8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面弹性在微米尺度软固体力学中至关重要，但由于与体积弹性和非线性的复杂相互作用，准确确定表面弹性参数仍然极具挑战性。

Method: 开发了GA优化框架，采用ANN替代前向模型，ANN基于非线性有限元模型生成的力-位移数据（表面张力0-5，表面剪切模量0-50无量纲范围）进行训练，替代了依赖理想化边界条件的解析前向模型。

Result: 所有情况下的参数识别最大误差小于8%；基于多次噪声实现的重现性和不确定性分析显示出窄置信区间，证明了方法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为表面参数的有限元逆识别提供了一种新颖、高效且鲁棒的替代方案，特别适用于实验相关的边界条件。

Abstract: Surface elasticity plays a crucial role in the mechanics of soft solids at the scale of micrometers and may become significant even at the scale of millimeters in exceptional cases. However, despite the efforts made for the identification of surface elastic parameters, accurately determining these values remains challenging due to the complex interplay with bulk elasticity and nonlinearity. To address this issue, a novel GA-based optimization framework is developed by employing an ANN-based surrogate forward model for efficient identification of surface parameters from the force-displacement response of a cylindrical specimen. The ANN is trained on force-displacement data of a soft cylindrical specimen, generated by nonlinear FE model predictions incorporating model elastic surfaces. The data used for training is generated for non-dimensional values of surface tension in the range of 0-5 and surface shear modulus in the range of 0-50. The accuracy and reliability of the trained ANN are established. The key novelty lies in replacing analytical forward models, which rely on idealized boundary conditions, with a data-driven surrogate trained on FE simulations under realistic constraints. The parameter identification is performed for a number of surface parameter sets using numerically generated force-displacement data as well as for noisy data obtained by adding 5% error in simulated data. The maximum error in identified values of parameters in all the cases is less than 8%. Repeatability and uncertainty analyses based on multiple noisy realizations further demonstrate the robustness of the approach, yielding narrow confidence intervals for the predicted parameters. The proposed framework provides a novel, efficient, and robust alternative to FE-based inverse identification of surface parameters, particularly for experimentally relevant boundary conditions.

</details>


### [448] [Properties of the matrix functions arising in exponential integrators with applications to stochastic simulations of PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23227)
*Elliot J. Carr*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了矩阵phi函数在本质非负矩阵和转移率矩阵下的非负性和随机性等性质，并利用这些性质分析线性和半线性ODE系统的确定性解，进而生成随机模拟以量化模型预测的变异性，最后通过扩散、对流-扩散、Fisher-KPP和Allen-Cahn等PDE空间离散化后的ODE系统进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵指数在ODE求解中具有基础性作用，其性质已被充分研究。本文旨在将矩阵指数的非负性和随机性等关键性质推广到矩阵phi函数上，以便更好地理解和分析线性和半线性ODE系统，并为随机模拟提供理论支撑。

Method: 首先理论推导矩阵phi函数在本质非负矩阵和转移率矩阵下的性质；然后利用这些性质分析线性与半线性ODE系统的确定性解；最后通过扩散、对流-扩散、Fisher-KPP和Allen-Cahn方程经空间离散化后的ODE系统进行数值验证。

Result: 建立了矩阵phi函数在本质非负矩阵下的非负性以及转移率矩阵下的随机性等性质；阐明了这些性质在确定性解分析中的应用；展示了如何利用这些解生成随机模拟来量化预测变异性。

Conclusion: 矩阵phi函数继承了矩阵指数的重要性质，为线性和半线性ODE系统的分析提供了新视角，并为基于这些解的随机模拟方法奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: The matrix exponential and the closely related matrix phi-functions play a fundamental role in the solution of first-order systems of ordinary differential equations (ODEs). In particular, they appear in exact solutions of certain linear ODE systems, and in exponential integrators, a class of explicit time discretisation methods for computing approximate solutions of stiff semi-linear ODE systems. Fundamental properties of the matrix exponential include that it is nonnegative if the matrix is essentially nonnegative and stochastic if the matrix is a transition-rate matrix. In this paper, we study related properties for the matrix phi-functions. Using these properties, we then provide insights into deterministic solutions of linear and semi-linear ODE systems and outline how such solutions can be used to generate stochastic simulations to quantify variability in model predictions. The paper concludes with some illustrative examples exploring the theory for some ODE systems arising from spatial discretisation of PDEs of diffusion, advection-diffusion, Fisher-KPP and Allen-Cahn type.

</details>


### [449] [Parallelized contraction of tensor trains or matrix product operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23274)
*Simone Foderà,Marc K. Ritter,Hiroshi Shinaoka,Jan von Delft*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种加速张量训练中MPO-MPO收缩拟合算法的策略：基于MPI的分布式内存并行化（采用逆规范或位点规范两种规范选择）和随机投影技术，前者实现近理想并行加速，后者将局部更新从2-site成本降至1-site成本同时保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPO-MPO收缩是张量训练中最昂贵的操作之一，现有的fit算法虽然精确但计算成本高（涉及2-site更新），且其并行化研究不足。

Method: (1) 基于MPI的分布式内存并行化，采用逆规范规范（近理想加速）或位点规范规范（避免奇异值求逆，需额外全局一致性计算）；(2) 随机投影技术，将局部更新从2-site成本降至1-site成本，同时保持2-site精度，并加速环境张量与MPO张量的收缩。

Result: 逆规范规范在所有问题规模上实现近理想并行加速；位点规范规范仅在大规模问题（需多次扫描才能收敛）上表现优异；随机投影在降低计算成本的同时保持了2-site的精度。

Conclusion: 两种策略可组合使用，显著加速MPO-MPO收缩的fit算法，为高维数据和算子的张量训练提供更高效的计算方案。

Abstract: Tensor Trains (TT), also known as Matrix Product States (MPS) and Matrix Product Operators (MPO), provide a compact and structured representation for high-dimensional data and operators. One of the most expensive manipulations involving tensor trains is the contraction of two MPOs. A popular and accurate method for mitigating this cost is the fit algorithm. However, it is still comparatively costly since it involves 2-site updates. Moreover, the parallelization of the fit algorithm when used for MPO-MPO contractions has received comparatively little attention. In this work, we present two strategies for accelerating the fit algorithm, usable in combination: (1) We use MPI-based distributed-memory parallelization tailored for MPO-MPO contractions, employing one of two MPO gauge choices: (1a) the inverse canonical gauge, which yields near-ideal parallelization speedup across all problem sizes; and (1b) the site-canonical gauge, which avoids the inversion of singular values but requires extra computations to ensure global consistency, thus yielding excellent parallelization speedup only for large problems requiring several sweeps before convergence. (2) We use randomized projections to reduce the cost of local updates from 2-site to 1-site costs while retaining 2-site accuracy, and to speed up contractions of environment tensors with MPO tensors.

</details>
